## VII / 3769–37,478,326,894,6 号星——塔尔索

===Original===
Wade sat staring moodily at the screen for some time, while Zezdon Afthen, sunk in his own reveries, continued.

“Our race was too highly psychic, and too little mechanically curious. We learned too little of the world about, and too much of our own processes. We are a peaceful race, for, while you and the Ancient Masters learned the rule of existence in a world of strife, where only the fittest, the best fighters survived, we learned life in a carefully tended world, where the Ancient Masters taught us to live, where the one whose social instincts were best developed, where he who would most help the others, and the race, was permitted to live. Is it not natural that our race will not fight among themselves? We are careful to suppress tendencies toward criminality and struggle. The criminal and the maniac, or those who are permanently incurable as determined by careful examination, are ‘removed’ as the Leaders put it. Lethal gas.

“At any rate, we know so pitiably little of natural science. We were hopelessly helpless against an attacking science.”

“I promise you, Afthen, that if Earth survives, Ortol shall survive, for we have given you all the weapons we know of and we will give your people all the weapons we shall learn of.” Morey spoke from the doorway. Arcot was directly behind him.

They talked for a short while, then Wade retired for some needed sleep, while Morey and Arcot started further work on the time fields.

===Chinese===
韦德闷闷地盯着屏幕出了半晌神，泽兹登·阿夫森（Zezdon Afthen）则沉浸在自己的遐思里，继续说了下去。

“我们这个种族过于耽于心灵，对机械又太缺乏好奇。关于周遭的世界，我们学得太少；关于自身的心性历程，又钻得太深。我们是个爱好和平的种族——你和远古宗师（Ancient Masters）是在一个弱肉强食的世界里学会生存法则的，那里唯有适者、最强的斗士才能活下来；而我们却是在一个被精心照拂的世界里学会生活的：远古宗师在那里教我们如何生存，在那里，社会本能发展得最好的人、最能造福他人与全族的人，才被允许活下来。我们的种族内部不起争斗，岂不是自然而然？我们时刻警惕地压制犯罪与争斗的倾向。罪犯与狂人，或者经缜密检查判定永久无可救药之人，便照首领们的说法，被‘移除’。用的是毒气。”

“总而言之，我们对自然科学所知少得可怜。面对一门进攻性的科学，我们全然束手无策。”

“我向你保证，阿夫森：只要地球存续，奥托尔（Ortol）必存续。我们已经把所知的全部武器交给了你们，今后学到的任何武器，也都会交给你们的人民。”莫里（Morey）的声音从舱门口传来，阿科特（Arcot）就站在他正后方。

三人又谈了片刻，随后韦德退下去补足必需的睡眠，莫里和阿科特则着手继续研究时间场（time-field）。

===Original===
Hour after hour the ship sped on through the dark of space, weirdly distorted, glowing spots of light before them, wheeling suns that moved and flashed as their awesome speed whirled them on.

They had to move slower soon, as the changing stars showed them near the space-marks of certain locating suns. Finally, still moving close to fifteen thousand miles per second, they saw the sun they knew was sun 3769–37,478,-326,894, twice as large as Sol, two and a half times as massive and twenty-six times as brilliant.

Thirteen major planets they counted as they searched the system with their powerful telectroscope, the outermost more than ten billion miles from the parent sun, while planet six, the one indicated by the world number, was at a distance of five hundred million miles, nearly as far from the sun as Jupiter is from ours, yet the giant sun, giving more than twenty-five times as much heat and light in the blue-white range, heated the planet to approximately the same temperature Earth enjoys. Spectroscopy showed that the atmosphere was well supplied with oxygen, and so the inhabitants were evidently oxygen-breathing men, unlike those of the Negrian people who live in an atmosphere of hydrogen.

Arcot threw the ship toward the planet, and as it loomed swiftly larger, he shut off the space-control, and set the coils for full charge, while the ship entered the planet’s atmosphere in a screaming dive, still at a speed of better than a hundred miles a second. But this speed was quickly damped as the ship shot high over broad oceans to the dull green of land ahead in the daylit zone. Observations made from various distances by means of the space-control, thus going back in time, show that the planet had a day of approximately forty hours, the diameter was nearly nine thousand miles, which would probably mean an inconveniently high gravity for the terrestrians and a distressingly high gravity for the Ortolians, used to their world even smaller than Earth, with scarcely 80 percent of Earth’s gravity.

Wade made some volumetric analysis of the atmosphere, and with the aid of a mouse, pronounced it “Q.A.R.” (quite all right) for human beings. It had not killed the mouse, so probably humans would find it quite all right.

===Chinese===
一小时又一小时，飞船疾驰着穿过太空的黑暗，船身怪异地扭曲变形；前方光点莹莹闪烁，一轮轮太阳旋转明灭，惊人的速度裹挟着他们一路向前。

用不了多久，他们就得放慢速度了——流变的星象显示，他们已临近某几颗定位恒星的太空标界。终于，在仍接近每秒一万五千英里的速度下，他们望见了那颗认得的恒星——3769–37,478,-326,894 号恒星：它是太阳的两倍大，质量为其两倍半，亮度为其二十六倍。

两人用强劲的电视望远镜（telectroscope）搜扫这个行星系，数出了十三颗大行星：最外侧的一颗距母星一百多亿英里；而第六颗行星——世界编号所指的那一颗——距离为五亿英里，几乎与木星到太阳的距离相当。然而这颗巨星在蓝白光波段发出的热与光超过太阳二十五倍，把这颗行星烘得与地球温度大致相仿。光谱分析表明，大气中氧的供给十分充足，因此居民显然是呼吸氧气的类人种族，不同于生活在氢气大气中的内格里安人（Negrian）。

阿科特驾船朝行星冲去。行星迅速胀大逼近之际，他关掉空间控制装置（space-control），把线圈调到满充，飞船便以一记尖啸的俯冲扎入行星大气——速度仍在每秒一百英里以上。不过这速度很快被遏止：飞船掠过广袤的大洋上空，朝着昼光照耀下前方陆地那片暗绿飞去。借助空间控制装置从不同距离所作的观测——也因此回溯了时间——表明：这颗行星一昼夜约为四十小时，直径近九千英里；这对地球人（terrestrian）而言多半意味着高得碍事的重力，对奥托尔人（Ortolian）而言更是高得难熬——他们住惯的那个世界比地球还小，重力不过地球的百分之八十。

韦德对大气做了些体积分析，又借一只小老鼠之助，宣布它对人类而言“Q.A.R.”——完全没问题（quite all right）。老鼠没被毒死，那么人类多半也会觉得完全没问题。

===Original===
“We’ll land at the first city that comes into view,” suggested Arcot. “Afthen, you be the spokesman; you have a very considerable ability with the mental communication, and have a better understanding of the physics we need to explain than has Zezdon Fentes.”

They were over land, a rocky coast that shot behind them as great jagged mountains, tipped with snow, rose beneath. Suddenly, a shining apparition appeared from behind one of the neighboring hills, and drove down at them with an unearthly acceleration. Arcot moved just enough to dodge the blow, and turned to meet the ship. Instantly, now that he had a good view of it he was certain it was a Thessian ship. Waiting no longer to determine that it was not a ship of this world, he shot a molecular beam at it. The beam exploded into a coruscating panoply of pyrotechnics on the Thessian shield. The Thessian replied with all beams he had available, including an induction-beam, an intensely brilliant light-beam, and several molecular cannons with shells loaded with an explosive that was very evidently condensed light. This was no exploration ship, but a full-fledged battleship.

The *Ancient Mariner* was blinded instantly. None of the occupants were hurt, but the combined pressure of the various beams hurled the ship to one side. The induction beam alone was dangerous. It passed through the outer lux-metal wall unhindered, and the perfectly conducting relux wall absorbed it, and turned it into power. At once, all the metal objects in the ship began to heat up with terrific rapidity. Since there were no metallic conductors on the ship, no damage was done.

Arcot immediately hid behind his perfect shield—the space-distortion.

===Chinese===
“我们就在看见的第一座城市降落，”阿科特提议道，“阿夫森，你来做发言人。你的心灵交流能力相当可观，而且对我们需要讲解的物理学，你比泽兹登·芬特斯（Zezdon Fentes）理解得更透。”

他们已飞至陆地上空，一道多岩的海岸飞速退向身后，顶着积雪的嶙峋高山从脚下升起。突然，一个亮闪闪的幻影从邻近一座山丘背后闪出，以非人间的加速度直扑他们而来。阿科特只侧移了恰好避过这一击的距离，随即转身迎向那艘船。此刻他已看得真切，当即断定那是一艘塞特人（Thessian）的船。他不再耽搁去查证它并非此界之船，径直朝它射出一道分子射线。射线在塞特人的护盾上炸开一片炫目的烟火。塞特人以其手头全部射线还击：包括一具感应射线（induction beam）、一具炫目至极的光束炮，还有几门分子炮（molecular cannon），炮弹里装填的炸药显然是固化光（condensed light）。这绝不是什么勘探船，而是一艘正牌战列舰。

*老水手号*（Ancient Mariner）顷刻被致盲。船上人员无一人受伤，但各路射线合成的压力把飞船猛推向一侧。唯有感应射线是危险的：它毫无阻碍地穿透外层卢克斯（lux）金属壁，导电性完美的雷卢克斯（relux）壁将它吸收，转化为电能。刹那间，船内所有金属物件都开始以骇人的速度升温。好在船上没有金属导体，损伤倒是没有。

阿科特立即躲进了他那份完美护盾——空间畸变（space-distortion）——的后面。

===Original===
“That’s no mild dose,” he said in a tense voice, working rapidly. “He’s a real-for-sure battleship. Better get down in the power room, Morey.”

In a few moments the ship was ready again. Opening the shield somewhat, Arcot was able to determine that no rays were being played on it, for no energy fields disclosed as distorting the opened field, other than the field of the sun and planet.

Arcot opened it. The battleship was searching vainly about the mountains, and was now some miles distant. His last view of Arcot’s ship had been a suddenly contracting ship, one that vanished in infinite distance, the infinite distance of another space, though he did not know it.

Arcot turned three powerful heat beams on the Thessian ship, and drove down toward it, accompanying them with molecular rays. The Thessian shield stopped the moleculars, but the heat had already destroyed the eyes of the ship. By some system of magnetic or electrostatic locating devices, the enemy guns and rays replied, and so successfully that Arcot was again blinded.

He had again been driving in a line straight toward the enemy, and now he threw in the entire power of his huge magnetic field-rays. The induction ray disappeared, and the heat, light and cannons stopped.

“Worked again,” grinned Arcot. A new set of eyes was inserted automatically, and the screen again lighted. The Thessian ship was spinning end over end toward the ground. It landed with a tremendous crash. Simultaneously from the rear of the *Ancient Mariner* came a terrific crash, an explosion that drove the terrestrian ship forward, as though a giant hand had pushed it from behind.

===Chinese===
“这可不是小剂量。”他一边飞快地操作，一边绷紧了声音说，“他是艘如假包换的战列舰。莫里，你最好下到动力室去。”

不消片刻，飞船便已再度就绪。阿科特把护盾略开一线，便判明已没有射线打在船上——除了太阳与行星的场之外，没有任何能量场使开启的护盾发生畸变。

阿科特敞开护盾。那艘战列舰正在群山间徒劳地搜索，此刻已在数英里之外。它对阿科特这艘船的最后印象，是一艘骤然收缩而去的船——一艘消失于无穷远处的船，另一个空间的无穷远处，尽管它自己并不知情。

阿科特把三道强力的热射线（heat ray）对准塞特人的战舰，向下俯冲直逼过去，同时辅以分子射线。塞特人的护盾挡住了分子射线，但热射线早已毁掉舰上的观测眼。敌舰凭着某种磁性或静电定位装置还击——炮火与射线齐发，而且打得极准，阿科特又一次被致盲。

他仍是一路直线朝敌舰冲去，此刻他把巨型磁场束（magnetic field-rays）的全部功率都压了上去。感应射线消失了，热射线、光束和火炮也全都哑了。

“又奏效了。”阿科特咧嘴一笑。新的观测眼自动换入，屏幕重新亮起。塞特人的战舰正一头接一头地翻滚坠向地面，轰然砸落。与此同时，*老水手号*尾部传来一声惊天动地的巨响——一场爆炸把地球人的飞船猛推向前，仿佛有一只巨手从背后推了它一把。

===Original===
The *Ancient Mariner* spun like a top, facing the direction of the explosion, though still traveling in the direction it had been pursuing, but backward now. Behind them the air was a gigantic pool of ionization. Tremendous fragments of what obviously had been a ship were drifting down, turning end over end. And those fragments of the wall showed them to be fully four feet of solid relux.

“Enemy got up behind somehow while the eyes were out, and was ready to raise merry hell. Somebody blew them up beautifully. Look at the ground down there—it’s red hot. That’s from the radiated heat of our recent encounter. Heat rays reflected, light bombs turned off, heat escaping from ions—nice little workout—and it didn’t seriously bother our defenses of two-inch relux. Now tell me: what will blow up four-foot relux?” asked Arcot, looking at the fragments. “It seems to me those fellows don’t need any help from us; they may decline it with thanks.”

“But they may be willing to help us,” replied Afthen, “and we certainly need such help.”

“I didn’t expect to come out alive from that battleship there. It was luck. If they knew what we had, they could insulate against it in an hour,” added Arcot.

===Chinese===
*老水手号*像陀螺般打起转来，船头对准爆炸的方向，却仍沿着原先的航向行进——只不过成了倒退。他们身后的空气已成了一大片电离的池沼。显然曾是一艘船的巨大残骸翻滚着飘坠而下。而那些舱壁残片显示，其厚度足有整整四英尺的实心雷卢克斯。

“趁观测眼失明的当口，敌人不知怎么摸到了我们背后，正准备大闹一场。结果有谁把他们炸了个漂亮。瞧瞧下面的地面——都烧红了。那是咱们刚才交手时辐射热的功劳：热射线被反射回来，光炸弹被荡开，热量从电离区里逸散——好一场小小的热身操——却没能真正撼动我们两英寸雷卢克斯的防御。现在告诉我：什么东西能炸穿四英尺厚的雷卢克斯？”阿科特望着残骸问道。“依我看，那帮家伙根本用不着我们帮忙；他们没准还会客气地谢绝呢。”

“可他们或许愿意帮我们，”阿夫森答道，“而我们确实急需这样的帮助。”

“刚才对付那艘战列舰，我本来没打算活着出来。全凭运气。他们要是摸清了我们的家底，一个钟头内就能找到绝缘对策。”阿科特补充道。

===Original===
“Let’s finish those fellows over there—look!” From the wreck of the ship they had downed, a stream of men in glistening relux suits were filing. Any men comparable to humans would have been killed by the fall, but not Thessians. They carried peculiar machines, and as they drove out of the ship in dive that looked as though they had been shot from a cannon, they turned and landed on the ground and proceeded to jump back, leaping at a speed that was bewildering, seemingly impossible in any living creature.

They busied themselves quickly. It took less than thirty seconds, and they had a large relux disc laid under the entire group and machines. Arcot turned a molecular ray down. The rock and soil shot up all about them, even the ship shot up, to fall back into the great pit its ray had formed. But the ionization told of the ray shield over the little group of men. A heat ray reached down, while the men still frantically worked at their stubby projectors. The relux disc now showed its purpose. In an instant the soil about them was white hot, bubbling lava. It was liquid, boiling furiously. But the deep relux disc simply floated on it. The enemy ship began sinking, and in a moment had fallen almost completely beneath the white hot rock.

A fountain of the melted lava sprung up, and under Arcot’s skillful direction, fell in a cloud of molten rock on the men working. The suits protected, and the white hot stuff simply rolled off. But it was sinking their boat. Arcot continued hopefully.

Meanwhile a signaling machine was frantically calling for help and sending out information of their plight and position.

Then all was instantly wiped out in a single terrific jolt of the magnetic beam. The machines jumped a little, despite their weight, and the ray shield apparatus slumped suddenly in blazing white heat, the interior mechanism fused. But the men were still active, and rapidly spreading from the spot, each protected by a ray shield pack.

A brilliant stab of molecular ray shot at each from either of two of the *Ancient Mariner*’s projectors as Morey aided Arcot. Their little packs flared brilliantly for an instant under the thousands of horsepower of energy lashing at the screen, then flashed away, and the opalescent relux yielded a moment later, and the figure went twisting, hurtling away. Meanwhile Wade was busy with the magnetic apparatus, destroying shield after shield, which either Arcot or Morey picked off. The fall from even so much as half a mile seemed not sufficient to seriously bother these supermen, for an instant later they would be up tearing away in great leaps on their own power as their molecular suits, blown out by the magnetic field, failed them.

===Chinese===
“先把那边那帮家伙料理完——瞧！”从他们击落的战舰残骸里，一长列身着闪亮雷卢克斯服的人正鱼贯而出。换作任何与人类相近的种族，这样的坠落早已丧命，塞特人却安然无恙。他们携带着奇特的机器，以仿佛被大炮射出炮膛般的俯冲姿势冲出军舰，随即转身落地，又纵身跃回，跳跃的速度令人目眩，看似绝非任何生物所能办到。

他们手脚麻利地忙了起来。不到三十秒，他们便在那群人和机器的整个下方铺好了一大块雷卢克斯圆盘。阿科特把一道分子射线压向地面。他们四周的岩石泥土冲天飞起，连那艘战舰也被掀上半空，又落回射线轰出的大坑里。但电离现象显示，那一小群人头顶罩着射线护盾（ray-shield）。一道热射线随即探了下去，而那些人仍在发狂般摆弄他们那些粗短的发射器。雷卢克斯圆盘的用途此刻显露出来：转眼间，他们四周的土壤已烧成白热的、咕嘟冒泡的熔岩，成了剧烈翻滚沸腾的液体。而那块厚重的雷卢克斯圆盘只管稳稳浮在上面。敌舰开始下沉，不一会儿便几乎完全没入白热的岩层之下。

一股熔岩喷泉腾空而起，经阿科特娴熟的引导，化作一片熔岩之云，倾泻在那些正在作业的人身上。护盾服护住了他们，白热的岩浆只是从上面滚滚滑落。可这一来却把他们的“艇”往下压沉。阿科特满怀希望地继续。

与此同时，一台信号机正发狂似地呼救，把他们的困境与方位不断发送出去。

随后，一记磁场束猛然一击，刹那间把这一切扫荡干净。那些机器虽重，也微微跳了一下；射线护盾装置轰然瘫倒，烧成炽亮的白热，内部机构熔作一团。但那些人依然活跃，迅速从原地四散开来，人人背着一具射线护盾背包（ray shield pack）。

莫里前来助战，*老水手号*两台发射器轮番出射，一道道炫目的分子射线直刺每一个目标。那些小背包在鞭笞护盾的数千马力能量下炽亮地闪了一瞬，随即光华敛去；片刻之后，乳白色的雷卢克斯终于不支，那道身影便打着旋被掀飞出去。与此同时，韦德忙着操纵磁场装置，接连废掉一面又一面护盾；护盾一倒，那人便由阿科特或莫里顺手点掉。即便从半英里高处坠落，似乎也难不倒这些超人分毫：片刻之后他们便会爬起来，全凭自身气力大步跃走——他们的分子动力服（molecular suit）已被磁场打灭，失效了。

===Original===
It was but a matter of minutes before the last had been chased down either by the rays or the ship. Then, circling back, Arcot slowly settled beside the enemy ship.

“Wait,” called Arcot sharply as Morey started for the door.

“Don’t go out yet. The friends who wrecked that little sweetheart who crept up behind will probably show up. Wait and see what happens.” Hardly had he spoken, when a strange apparition rose from behind a rock scarcely a quarter of a mile away. Immediately Arcot intensified the vision screen covering him. He seemed to leap near. There was one man, and he held what was obviously a sword by the blade, above his head, waving it from side to side.

“There they are—whatever they are. Intelligent all right—what more universally obvious peace sign than a primitive weapon such as a knife held in reverse position? You go with Zezdon Afthen. Try holding a carving knife by the blade.”

Morey grinned as he got into his power suit, on Wade’s OK of the atmosphere. “They may mistake me for the cook out looking for dinner, and I wouldn’t risk my dignity that way. I’ll take the baseball bat and hold it wrong way instead.”

===Chinese===
不过几分钟工夫，最后一个不是被射线就是被飞船撂倒就擒。随后，阿科特兜了个圈子回来，缓缓降落在敌舰旁边。

莫里朝舱门走去时，阿科特厉声喝道：“等等。”

“先别出去。把那个从背后摸上来的小宝贝送上天的那帮朋友，多半就要露面了。等着瞧吧。”话音未落，一块相距不过四分之一英里的岩石后面，升起了一个奇怪的身影。阿科特立即加强罩住那人的视屏（vision screen），那人仿佛倏地跃至近前。只有一个人，手里高举着一件显然是剑的东西——握在剑刃上——左右挥动。

“他们来了——不管他们究竟是什么。肯定有智慧——还有什么比把刀这样的原始武器倒握在手更为通行易懂的和平信号呢？你和泽兹登·阿夫森一起去。试试倒握着切肉刀的刀刃吧。”

韦德确认大气无碍后，莫里笑着钻进动力服（power suit）。“他们没准会把我当成出门找晚饭的厨子，我可爱不了那种丢脸。我还是拿根棒球棒，倒着握算了。”

===Original===
Nevertheless, as he stepped from the ship, with Afthen close behind, he held the long knife by the blade, and Afthen, very awkwardly operating his still rather unfamiliar power suit, followed.

Into the intensely blue sunlight the men stepped. Their skin and clothing took on a peculiar tint under the strange sunlight.

The single stranger was joined by a second, also holding a reversed weapon, and together they threw them down. Morey and Zezdon Afthen followed suit. The two parties advanced toward each other.

The strangers advanced with a swift, light step, jumping from rock to rock, while Morey and Afthen flew part way toward them. The men of this world were totally unlike any intelligent race Morey had conceived of. Their head and brain case was so small as to be almost animalish. The nose was small and well formed, the ears more or less cup-shaped with a remarkable power of motion. Their eyes were seemingly huge, probably no larger than a terrestrian’s, though in the tiny head they were necessarily closely placed, protected by heavy bony ridges that actually projected from the skull to enclose them. Tiny, childlike chins completed the head, running down to a scrawny neck.

They were short, scarcely five feet, yet evidently of tremendous strength for their short, heavy arms, the muscle bulging plainly under the tight rubber-like composition garments, and the short legs whose stocky girth proclaimed equal strength were members of a body in keeping with them. The deep, broad chest, wide, square shoulders, heavy broad hips, combined with the tiny head seemed to indicate a perfect incarnation of brainless, brute strength.

===Chinese===
话虽如此，他跨出船舱时，手里仍倒握着那把长刀的刀刃；阿夫森跟在后面，他那套还相当不趁手的动力服，被他操纵得十分笨拙。

两人走进那浓烈湛蓝的阳光里。皮肤和衣着在这陌生的日照下泛出一种奇特的色泽。

那个孤单的陌生人随后有第二个同伴会合，后者同样倒持着武器，两人一齐把武器掷在地上。莫里和泽兹登·阿夫森如法炮制。两拨人相向而行。

陌生人们迈着轻捷迅疾的步伐，在岩石间纵跳而来，莫里和阿夫森则飞着迎出一程。这个世界的人，与莫里想象过的任何智慧种族都截然不同。他们的头部和颅腔小得近乎兽类。鼻子小巧端正；耳朵多少呈杯状，转动的灵活劲儿惊人。他们的眼睛看似巨大——其实未必大过地球人，只是在小小的脑袋里必然挨得很近，且有厚重凸出的骨质眉脊护住：那眉脊自颅骨直接鼓出，把双眼环抱其中。小小的、孩童似的下巴收束了头型，往下连着一根瘦骨嶙峋的脖子。

他们个子很矮，不足五英尺，力气却显然大得惊人：短粗的手臂上，肌肉在紧绷绷的类橡胶合成衣料下根根鼓起；短腿那粗壮的围度也宣示着同等的力气，与整个身躯恰相匹配。深厚的宽胸、方正的宽肩、沉甸甸的宽胯，配上那颗小小的脑袋，活脱脱是徒具蛮力、不存心智的完美化身。

===Original===
“Strangers from another planet, enemies of our enemies. What brings you here at this time of troubles?” The thoughts came clearly from the stocky individual before them.

“We seek to aid, and to find aid. The menace that you face, attacks not alone your world, but all this star cluster,” replied Zezdon Afthen steadily.

The stranger shook his head with an evident expression of hopelessness. “The menace is even greater than we feared. It was just fortune that permitted us to have our weapon in workable condition at the time your ship was attacked. It will be a day before the machine will again be capable of successful operation. When in condition for use, it is invincible, but—one blow in thirty hours—you can see we are not of great aid.” He shrugged.

An enemy with evident resources of tremendous power, deadly, unknown rays that wiped out entire cities with a single brief sweep—and no defense save this single weapon, good but once a day! Morey could read the utter despair of the man.

“What is the difficulty?” asked Morey eagerly.

“Power, lack of power. Our cities are going without power, while every electric generator on the planet is pouring its output into the accumulators that work these damnable, hopeless things. Invincible with power—helpless without.”

===Chinese===
“来自另一颗行星的陌生人，我们之敌的敌人。值此多难之秋，是什么把你们引到这里的？”清晰的思维自面前那个矮壮者传来。

“我们既想施以援手，也来寻求援助。你们面临的威胁，攻击的不只是你们的星球，而是整个星团。”泽兹登·阿夫森沉着地答道。

陌生人摇了摇头，满面显而易见的绝望。“那威胁比我们担心的还要可怕。你们飞船遇袭之时，我们的武器恰好还能开动，纯属侥幸。这台机器得再过一天才能重新正常运转。一旦可堪使用，它便不可战胜——可三十小时方能一击——你们看，我们帮不上什么大忙。”他耸了耸肩。

敌人显然握有庞大到惊人的力量储备，那致命的未知射线短短一扫便能抹平整座城市——而防御手段只有这一件一天只能用一次的武器！莫里读得出此人深深的绝望。

“症结在哪里？”莫里急切地问。

“能量，缺能量。我们的城市都在断电，而全行星每一台发电机发出的电力，全都灌进了驱动这些该死的、叫人绝望的玩意儿的蓄能器里。有能量便不可战胜——没有便一筹莫展。”

===Original===
“Ah!” Morey’s face shone with delight—invincible weapon—with power. And the *Ancient Mariner* could generate unthinkable power.

“What power source do you use—how do you generate your power?”

“Combining oxidizing agent with reducing agents releases heat. Heat used to boil liquid and the vapor runs turbines.”

“We can give you power. What wattage have you available?”

Only Morey’s thoughts had to translate “watts” to “How many man-weights can you lift through your height per time interval, equal to this.” He gave the man some impression of a second, by counting. The man figured rapidly. His answer indicated that approximately a total of two billion kilowatts were available.

===Chinese===
“啊！”莫里的脸因欣喜而发亮——不可战胜的武器——只要有能量。而*老水手号*所能发出的能量，多到不可想象。

“你们用的是什么能源——能量是怎么发出来的？”

“使氧化剂与还原剂相化合，便释放出热。热用来把液体煮沸，蒸汽再推动涡轮机。”

“我们可以给你们能量。你们现有多少瓦特的功率可用？”

莫里还得以思维把“瓦特”翻译过去：“在与此相等的时间间隔里，你能把多少个成人的体重举过你自己身高的高度？”他靠逐一计数，给了那人约一秒钟的印象。那人飞快地心算了一番。答案表明，他们可用的总功率约有二十亿千瓦。

===Original===
“Then the weapon is invincible hereafter, if what you say is true. Our ship alone can easily generate ten thousand times that power.

“Come, get in the ship, accompany us to your capital.”

The men turned, and retreated to their position behind the rocks, while Morey and Zezdon Afthen waited for them. Soon they returned, and entered the ship.

“Our world,” explained the leader rapidly, “is a single unified colony. The capital is ‘Shesto,’ our world we call ‘Talso.’ ” His directions were explicit, and Arcot started for Shesto, on Talso.

===Chinese===
“那么从今往后，这武器便不可战胜了——只要你所言属实。单是我们这艘船，就能轻而易举地发出一万倍于此的功率。

“走吧，上船来，陪我们一同去你们的首都。”

那几个人转身退回岩石后面原先的位置，莫里和泽兹登·阿夫森在一旁等候。不多时他们便回来了，登上了船。

“我们的世界，”那领头的急急解释道，“是一个统一的整体殖民地。首都是‘谢斯托’（Shesto），我们的星球则叫‘塔尔索’（Talso）。”他的指引一清二楚，阿科特便朝着塔尔索上的谢斯托进发了。

## III A Quarter of a Million Light Years / III 二十五万光年

===Original===
“Our civilization,” continued Zezdon Afthen, “is built largely on the knowledge of the mind. We cannot have criminals, for the man who plots evil is surely found out by his thoughts. We cannot have lying politicians and unjust rulers.
“It is a peaceful civilization. The Ancient Masters feared and hated War with a mighty aversion. But they did not make our race cowards, merely peaceful intelligence. Now we must fight for our homes, and my race will fight mightily. But we need weapons.
“But my story has little to do with our race. I will tell the story of our civilization and of the Ancient Ones later when the time is more auspicious.
“Four months ago, our mental vibration instruments detected powerful emanations from space. That could only mean that a new, highly intelligent race had suddenly appeared within a billion miles of our world. The directional devices quickly spotted it as emanating from the third planet of our system. Zezdon Fentes, with my aid, set up some special apparatus, which would pick up strong thoughts and make them visible. We had used this before to see not only what an enemy looked upon, but also what he saw in that curious thing, the eye of the mind, the vision of the past and the future. But while the thought-amplification device was powerful, the new emanations were hard to separate from each other.
“It was done finally, when all but one man slept. That one we were enable to tune sharply to. After that we could reach him at any time. He was the commander. We saw him operate the ship, we saw the ship, saw it glide over the barren, rocky surface of that world. We saw other men come in and go out. They were strange men. Short, squat, bulky men. Their arms were short and stocky. But their strength was enormous, unbelievable. We saw them bend solid bars of steel as thick as my arm. With perfect ease!

===Chinese===
“我们的文明，”泽兹登·阿夫森（Zezdon Afthen）继续说道，“在很大程度上奠基于对心灵的认知。我们不可能有罪犯，因为图谋作恶的人必然会被自己的思想出卖。我们不可能有满口谎言的政客，也不可能有不义的统治者。
“那是一个和平的文明。远古宗师（Ancient Masters）对战争怀着深切的畏惧与憎恶，但他们并没有把我们的种族变成懦夫，只是塑造了爱好和平的智慧。如今我们必须为家园而战，我的种族必将奋勇作战。但我们需要武器。
“不过，我要讲的故事与我们的种族关系不大。关于我们的文明与远古先民（Ancient Ones）的历史，且等时机更合适时我再细说。
“四个月前，我们的心灵振动仪器探测到来自太空的强大发散波。那只可能意味着：一个高度智慧的新种族，突然出现在了我们世界十亿英里的范围之内。定向装置很快锁定，发散波来自我们这个星系的第三颗行星。泽兹登·芬特斯（Zezdon Fentes）在我的协助下架起了一套特殊装置，能捕捉强烈的思维，并把它们化为可见的影像。我们以前就用它看到过敌人注视着什么，甚至看到他在那件奇妙之物——心灵之眼——中所见的景象：对过去与未来的洞视。只是思维放大装置虽然强大，这些新的发散波却很难彼此分离。
“这项工作最终完成时，船上除一人之外全都睡了。我们得以与那一个人精确调谐，此后随时都能联系上他。他就是指挥官。我们看到他操纵飞船，看到了那艘船，看它在那个世界遍布岩石的荒芜表面上滑行。我们看到其他人进进出出。他们是一群怪异的人：矮壮、敦实、粗大。手臂短而粗壮，力气却大得惊人，简直难以置信。我们看到他们把和我手臂一样粗的实心钢条拗弯——轻而易举！

===Original===
“Their brains were tremendously active, but they were evil, selfishly evil. Nothing that did not benefit them counted. At one time our instruments went dead, and we feared that the commander had detected us, but we saw what happened a little later. The second in command had killed him.
“We saw them examine the world, working their way across it, wearing heavy suits, yet, for all the terrific gravity of that world, bouncing about like rubber balls, leaping and jumping where they wanted. Their legs would drive out like pistons, and they soared up and through the air.
“They were tired while they made those examinations, and slept heavily at night.
“Then one night there was a conference. We saw then what they intended. Before we had tried desperately to signal them. Now we were glad that we had failed.
“We saw their ship rise (in the thoughts of the second in command) and sail out into space, and rush toward our world. The world grew larger, but it was imperfectly sketched in, for they did not know our world well. Their telescopes did not have great power as your electric telescopes have.
“We saw them investigate the planet. We saw them plan to destroy any people they found with a ray which was as follows: ‘the ray which makes all parts move as one.’ We could not understand and could not interpret. Thoughts beyond our knowledge have, of course, no meaning, even when our mental amplifiers get them, and bring them to us.”

===Chinese===
“他们的大脑活跃得惊人，人却很邪恶——一种自私的邪恶。凡对他们无益的东西，一概不入他们的眼。有一次，我们的仪器突然中断了信号，我们担心指挥官发现了我们，但不久之后我们看到发生了什么：是副指挥官杀了他。
“我们看到他们勘察那个世界，一步一步横穿过去。他们穿着厚重的防护服，可尽管那个世界引力惊人，他们却像皮球一样四处蹦跳，想跳到哪里就跳到哪里。他们的双腿像活塞一样弹出，整个人便腾空而起，掠过空中。
“勘察的时候，他们会感到疲惫，夜里睡得很沉。
“后来一天夜里，他们开了一场会议。我们由此看清了他们的意图。此前我们曾不顾一切地向他们发出信号，如今却庆幸没有成功。
“我们看到他们的飞船升起（这是从副指挥官的思想里看到的），驶入太空，朝我们的世界疾驰而来。我们的世界在视野里越来越大，只是勾画得并不完整，因为他们对我们的世界所知不多。他们的望远镜放大倍率不高，比不上你们的电子望远镜。
“我们看到他们勘察这颗行星，看到他们计划用一种射线消灭所发现的任何民族。那射线在他们心中的图景是这样一句话：‘能使一切部分齐一共动的射线。’我们无法理解，也无法诠释。超出我们知识范围的思想自然无从索解，就算我们的精神放大器捕获了它们、传送到我们面前，也是一样。”

===Original===
“The Molecular ray!” gasped Morey in surprise. “They will be an enemy.”
“You know it! It is familiar to you! You have it? You can fight it?” asked Zezdon Afthen excitedly.
“We know it, and can fight it, if that is all they have.”

===Chinese===
“分子射线（Molecular ray）！”莫里（Morey）惊得倒吸一口气，“他们会是劲敌。”
“你们认识它！你们熟悉它！你们拥有它吗？你们能对抗它吗？”泽兹登·阿夫森兴奋地追问。
“我们认识它，也能对抗它——前提是他们只有这一手。”

===Original===
“They have more—much more I fear,” replied Zezdon Afthen. “At any rate, we saw what they intended. If our world was inhabited, they would destroy everyone on it, and then other men of their race were to float in on their great ships, and settle on that largest of our worlds.
“We had to stop them so we did what we could. We had powerful machines, which would amplify and broadcast our thoughts. So we broadcast our thought-waves, and implanted in the mind of their leader that it would be wise to land, and learn the extent of the civilization, and the weapons to be met. Also, as the ship drew nearer, we made him decide on a certain spot we had prepared for him.
“He never guessed that the thoughts were not his own. Only the ideas came to him, seeming to spring from his own mind.
“He landed—and we used our one weapon. It was a thing left to one group of rulers when the Ancient Masters left us to care for ourselves. What it was, we never knew; we had never used it in the fifteen thousand years since the Great Masters had passed—never had to. But now it was brought out, and concealed behind great piles of rock in a deep canyon where the ship of the enemy would land. When it landed, we turned the beam of the machine on it, and the apparatus rotated it swiftly, and a cone of the beam’s ray was formed as the beam was swung through a small circle in the vertical plane. The machine leaped backward, and though it was so massive that a tremendous amount of labor had been required to bring it there, the push of the pencil of force we sent out hurled it back against a rocky cliff behind it as though it were some child’s toy. It continued to operate for perhaps a second, perhaps two. In that time two great holes had been cut in the enemy ship, holes fifteen feet across, that ran completely through the hull as though a die had cut through the metal of the ship, cutting out a disc of metal.
“There was a terrific concussion, and a roar as the air blasted out of the ship. It did not take us long to discover that the enemy were dead. Their terrible, bloated corpses lay everywhere in the ship. Most of the men we were able to recognize, having seen them in the mentovisor. But the colors were distorted, and their forms were peculiar. Indeed, the whole ship seemed strange. The only time that things ever did seem normal about that strange thing, when the angles of it seemed what they were, when the machines did not seem out of proportion, out of shape, twisted, was when on a trial trip we ventured very close to our sun.”

===Chinese===
“他们还有更多——我怕是多得多，”泽兹登·阿夫森答道，“总之，我们看清了他们的意图。倘若我们的世界有人居住，他们就会把上面的人斩尽杀绝，然后他们种族的其他人便乘着巨舰飘然而来，在我们最大的那颗星球上定居。
“我们必须阻止他们，于是竭尽了全力。我们有能把思维放大并向外播送的强力机器，于是播发思维波，在敌方首领心中植入了这样一个念头：降落到行星表面，探明文明的规模和将要遭遇的武器，方为明智。此外，随着飞船逐渐接近，我们还让他决定降落在某个我们早已为他备好的地点。
“他从未疑心那些念头并非出自他自己。那些想法只是自行浮现，仿佛源自他本人的头脑。
“他降落了——于是我们动用了唯一的那件武器。那是远古宗师放手让我们自理之时，留给一脉统治者的东西。它究竟是什么，我们从来不知道；自大宗师（Great Masters）离去之后的一万五千年里，我们从未动用过它——也从不需要。但这一次，它被运了出来，藏在敌人将降临的那道深谷里、大堆巨岩之后。飞船一着陆，我们便把机器的射束对准它，装置令射束飞速旋转，当射束在垂直面上扫过一个很小的圆周时，一道锥形的射线便形成了。机器猛地向后一弹——它重达千钧，当初运到那里费尽了人力，可我们射出的那道力束一推，竟把它掀得撞上身后的岩壁，仿佛它不过是件儿童玩具。它持续运转了大约一秒钟，也许两秒。就在这段时间里，敌舰上被切出了两个大洞，直径十五英尺，把船壳彻底贯穿，仿佛冲模切穿舰体金属，剜走了一块圆盘。
“随即是一声惊天动地的震荡，舱内的空气夺路喷涌，发出轰鸣。没过多久，我们便查明敌人已悉数毙命。他们那可怖而臃肿的尸体横陈全船。大多数人我们都认得出来——在思维视镜（mentovisor）里见过他们。只是颜色失真，形体也透着古怪。说真的，整艘船都显得陌生。唯一一次，那件怪物的种种显得正常——棱角看上去恰如其分，机器不再比例失调、不再变形扭曲——是在一次试航中，我们冒险逼近了自己的恒星。”

===Original===
Arcot whistled softly and looked at Morey. Morey nodded. “Probably right. Don’t interrupt.”
“That you thought something, I understood, but the thoughts themselves were hopelessly unintelligible to me. You know the explanation?” asked Zezdon Afthen eagerly.
“We think so. The ship was evidently made on a world of huge size. Those men, their stocky, block legs and arms, their entire build and their desire for the largest of your planets, would indicate that. Their own world was probably even larger—they were forced to wear pressure suits even on that large world, and could jump all over, you said. On so huge a sphere as their native world seems to be, the gravity would be so intense as to distort space. Geometry, such as yours seems to be, and such as ours was, could never be developed, for you assume the existence of a straight line, and of an absolute plane surface. These things cannot exist in space, but on small worlds, far from the central sun’s mass, the conditions approach that without sufficient discrepency to make the error obvious. On so huge a globe as their world the space is so curved that it is at once obvious that no straight line exists, and that no plane exists. Their geometry would never be like ours. When you went close to your sun, the attraction was sufficient to curve space into a semblance of the natural conditions on their home planet, then your senses and the ship met a compromise condition which made it seem more or less normal, not so obviously strange to you.
“But continue.” Arcot looked at Afthen interestedly.

===Chinese===
阿科特（Arcot）轻轻吹了声口哨，望向莫里。莫里点点头。“多半猜得没错。别打断他。”
“我知道你们刚才想到了什么，可那些思想本身，我全然无法索解。你们知道这是什么缘故？”泽兹登·阿夫森急切地问。
“我们认为是知道。那艘船显然出自一个体积巨大的世界。那些人粗短如块的腿臂、整体的体型，再加上他们对你们星系最大那颗行星的觊觎，都指向这一点。他们自己的世界多半还要更大——你说过，即便在那么大的一颗行星上，他们也不得不穿着耐压服，还能四处跳跃。在像他们母星看来那样巨大的球体上，引力会强到令空间扭曲。像你们如今这样、也像我们昔日那样的几何学，在那里根本无从发展，因为这种几何学假定直线存在、假定绝对平面存在，而这些东西在空间中并不存在。只有在远离中心恒星质量的小世界上，种种条件才近似于这些假定，偏差也不至于让错误显露。而在他们那颗星球那样巨大的球体上，空间弯曲得太厉害，直线并不存在、平面也不存在，这是一望即知的。他们的几何学绝不会同我们的相似。当你们驶近自己的恒星时，引力足以把空间弯曲成与他们母星自然条件相似的模样，于是你们的感官与飞船便遭逢了一种折中状态，令它显得大体正常，不再那么明晃晃地怪异。
“请继续讲。”阿科特兴致盎然地望着阿夫森。

===Original===
“There were none left in their ship now, and we had been careful in locating the first hole, that it should not damage the propulsive machinery. The second hole was accidental, due to the shift of the machine. The machine itself was wrecked now, crushed by its own reaction. We forgot that any pencil of force powerful enough to do what we wanted, would tear the machine from its moorings unless fastened with great steel bolts into the solid rock.
“The second hole had been far to the rear, and had, by ill-luck, cut out a portion of the driving apparatus. We could not repair that, though we did succeed at last in lifting the great discs into place. We attempted to cut them, and put them back in sections. Our finest saws and machines did not nick them. Their weight was unbelievable, and yet we finally succeeded in lifting the things into the wall of the ship. The actual missing material did not represent more than a tiny cut, perhaps as wide as one of your credit-discs. You could slip the thin piece of metal in between them, but not so much as your finger.
“Those slots we welded tight with our best steel, letting a flap hang over on each side of the cut, and as the hot metal cooled, it was drawn against the shining walls with terrific force. The joints were perfectly airtight.
“The machines proper were repaired to the greatest possible extent. It was a heartbreaking task, for we must only guess at what machines should be connected together. Much damage had been done by the rushing air as it left, for it filled the machines, too, and they were not designed to resist the terrific air pressure that was on them when the pressure in the ship escaped. Many of the machines had been burst open, and these we could repair when we had the necessary elements and knew their construction from the remnants, or could find unbroken duplicates in the stock rooms.

===Chinese===
“那时他们船上已无一活口。第一个洞的位置我们选得十分谨慎，不致伤及推进机械；第二个洞则纯属意外，是机器移位所致。机器本身这下也毁了——被自己的反冲力压垮。我们忘了一件事：任何强到能达成目的的力束，若不用巨大的钢螺栓锚死在实心岩石里，都会把机器从固定座上生生扯走。
“第二个洞开在远离舰尾的地方，不巧切掉了驱动装置的一部分。那一部分我们修不了，不过两块大圆盘最终总算被我们抬回了原位。我们试过把圆盘切开、分块装复，可我们最好的锯机和机床连个豁口都留不下。那些东西重得难以置信，而我们最终竟把它们抬起来嵌进了船壁。真正缺失的材料不过是细细一条切缝，宽大约有你们的一枚信用币（credit-disc）。切下来的那片薄金属可以插回两壁之间，但连一根手指也休想伸进去。
“那些切缝，我们用最好的钢材焊死了，让焊料在切口两侧各垂搭一片，滚热的金属冷却收缩时，便以惊人的力量箍紧光亮的舱壁。接缝密不透风。
“机器本体也尽最大可能修复了。这是件令人心碎的差事，因为我们只能凭猜测判断哪些机器应当彼此相连。夺舱而出的疾风也造成了不小的破坏——空气同样灌进了机器里，而这些机器的设计根本经受不住舱内泄压时压在它们身上的骇人气压。许多机器被胀裂开来；这些机器，只有在备齐必需的元素、能从残骸推知其构造、或能在储藏室里找到完好复件时，才修得好。

===Original===
“Once we connected the wrong things. This will show you what we dealt with. They were the wrong poles—two generators, connected together in the wrong way. There was a terrific crash when the switch was thrown, and huge sheets of electric flame leaped from one of them. Two men were killed, incinerated in an instant, even the odors one might expect were killed in that flash of heat. Everything save the shining metal and clear glass within ten feet of it was instantly wiped out. And there was a fuse link that gave. The generator was ruined. One was left, and several small auxiliary generators.
“Eventually, we did the job. We made the machine work. And we are here.
“We have come to warn you, and to ask aid. Your system also has a large planet, slightly smaller than the largest of our system, but yet attractive. There are approximately 50,000 planetary systems in this universe, according to the records of the Invaders. Their world is not of this system. It is the World Thett, sun Antseck, Universe Venone. Where that is, or even what it means, we do not know. Perhaps you understand.
“But they investigated your world, and its address, according to their records, was World 3769–8482730–3. This, I believe, means, Universe 3769, sun 8482730, world 3. They have been investigating this system now for nearly three centuries. It was close to 200 years ago that they visited your world—two hundred years of your time.”

===Chinese===
“有一次我们接错了线。这足以让你们明白我们对付的是什么：极性接反——两台发电机用错误的方式连在了一起。合闸的一瞬轰然巨响，大片的电焰从其中一台里蹿起。两个人当场丧命，刹那间烧成灰烬，连本该弥漫的焦糊气味都被那阵热闪烧了个干净。十英尺之内，除了光亮的金属和透明的玻璃，一切都在瞬间湮灭。另有一根保险熔链烧断了。那台发电机报废了。剩下完整的只有一台，外加几台小型辅助发电机。
“终于，我们把活干完了。我们让机器运转了起来。于是，我们来到了这里。
“我们此来，是要向你们示警，并请求援助。你们的星系也有一颗大行星，比我们星系最大的那颗稍小，却同样具有吸引力。据入侵者（Invaders）的档案记载，这个宇宙大约有 50,000 个行星系。他们的世界并不属于这个星系，而是：塞特星（Thett），安塞克恒星（Antseck），维诺恩宇宙（Venone）。那在哪里，甚至那意味着什么，我们都无从知晓。或许你们能明白。
“而他们勘察过你们的世界。按他们的档案，你们世界的地址是：3769–8482730–3 号世界。我想，这意为宇宙 3769、恒星 8482730、世界 3。他们勘察这个星系，至今已近三个世纪。大约 200 年前，他们就造访过你们的世界——按你们的时间算，是两百年前。”

===Original===
“This is 2129—which makes it about the year 1929–30 that they floated around here investigating. Why haven’t they done anything?” Arcot asked him.
“They waited for an auspicious time. They are afraid now, for recently they visited your world, and were utterly amazed to find the unbelievable progress your people have made. They intend to make an immediate attack on all worlds known to be intelligently populated. They had made the mistake of letting one race learn too much; they cannot afford to let it happen again.
“There are only twenty-one inhabited worlds known, and their thousands of scouts have already investigated nearly all the central mass of this universe, and much of the outer rings. They have established a base in this universe. Where I do not know. That, alone, was never mentioned in the records. But of all peoples, they feared only your world.
“There is one race in the universe far older than yours, but they are a sleeping people. Long ago their culture decayed. Still, now they are not far from you, and perhaps it will be worth the few days needed to learn more about them. We have their location and can take you there. Their world circles a dead star—”

===Chinese===
“今年是 2129 年——这么说，他们大约在 1929–30 年前后就在这一带飘荡侦察了。那他们怎么一直没动静？”阿科特问他。
“他们在等待合适的时机。如今他们害怕了——因为最近他们又造访了你们的世界，万分惊愕地发现你们的人民取得了难以置信的进步。他们打算立即进攻所有已知栖居着智慧生灵的世界。他们犯过一个错误：放任一个种族懂得太多。这个错误，他们再也无法承受第二次。
“已知有生灵居住的世界只有二十一个，他们数以千计的侦察船已经勘察过这个宇宙几乎整个中央主体地带和外围星环的大部分。他们在这个宇宙里建立了一处基地，位置我不知道——唯独这件事，档案里从未提起。可在所有民族当中，他们畏惧的只有你们的世界。
“这个宇宙里有一个比你们古老得多的种族，但他们是一群沉睡的民族，久远以前文化便已朽坏。不过，他们如今离你们不远，或许值得花上几天工夫，去对他们多作些了解。我们有他们的方位，可以带你们去。他们的世界绕着一颗死星运转——”

===Original===
“Not any more,” laughed Morey grimly. “That’s another surprise for the enemy. They had a little jog, and they certainly are wide awake now. They are headed for big things, and they are going to do a lot.”
“But how do you know these things? You have ships that can go from planet to planet, I know, but the records of the enemy said you could not leave the system of your sun. They alone knew that secret.”
“Another surprise for them,” said Morey. “We can—and we can move faster than your ship, if not faster than they. The people of the dead star have moved to a very live star—Sirius, the brightest in our heavens. And they are as much alive now as their new sun. They can move faster than light, also. We had a little misunderstanding a while back, when their star passed close to ours. They came off second best, and we haven’t spoken to them since. But I think we can make valuable allies there.”

===Chinese===
“现在不是了，”莫里冷冷地笑道，“这是给敌人的又一个惊喜。他们被轻轻撞了一下，如今肯定彻底醒了。他们正走向大场面，也注定要大干一场。”
“可你们怎么会知道这些事？我知道你们拥有能在行星之间往返的飞船，但敌人的档案说，你们无法离开自己恒星的星系。那个秘密，只有他们知道。”
“这又是给他们的一个惊喜，”莫里说，“我们能离开——而且我们能飞得比你们的船快，就算未必快得过他们。死星的居民已经迁往一颗极富生机的恒星——天狼星（Sirius），我们天空里最亮的一颗。如今他们也同他们的新太阳一样生气勃勃。他们的速度同样能超越光速。不久前我们之间有过一点小误会，那时他们的恒星从我们恒星近旁经过。他们落了下风，此后我们再没跟他们打过交道。不过我想，在那里我们能结交到极有价值的盟友。”

===Original===
For all Morey’s jocular manner, he realized the terrible import of this announcement. A race which had been able to cross the vast gulf of intergalactic space in the days when Terrestrians were still developing the airplane—and already they had mapped Jupiter, and planned their colonies! What developments had come? They had molecular rays, cosmic rays, the energy of matter, then—what else had they now? Lux and Relux, the two artificial metals, made of solidified light, far stronger than anything of molecular structure in nature, absolutely infusible, totally inert chemically, one a perfect conductor of light and of all radiation in space, the other a perfect reflector of all radiations—save molecular rays. Made into the condition of reflection by the action of special frequencies in its formation from light, molecular frequencies were, unfortunately, able to convert it into perfectly transparent lux metal, when the protective value was gone.
They had that. All Earth had, perhaps.
“There was one other race of some importance, the others were semi-civilized. They rated us in a position between these races and the high races—yours, those of the dead star, and those of world 3769–37:478:326:894–6. Our science had been investigated two hundred or so years ago.

===Chinese===
尽管莫里满口打趣，他心里却清楚这番宣告意味着何等可怕之事。一个在地球人（Terrestrians）还在研制飞机的年代便能横越星系际浩瀚鸿沟的种族——而那时他们已经测绘过木星、规划好了殖民地！此后又会有怎样的发展？那时他们已掌握分子射线、宇宙射线、物质能量——如今还会有什么别的？卢克斯（Lux）与雷卢克斯（Relux）——两种人造金属，以固化之光制成，远比自然界一切分子结构的材料坚固，绝对不可熔融，化学上完全惰性：其一，是光与太空中一切辐射的完美导体；其二，是一切辐射的完美反射体——唯独分子射线例外。后者在由光凝成之时，经特殊频率的作用而进入反射态；不幸的是，分子频率又能将它转变成全透明的卢克斯金属——防护价值便随之荡然无存。
那种射线，他们是有的。整个地球，或许也有。
“另外还有一个较为重要的种族，其余的都属半开化。他们把我们评在两者之间：一边是那些半开化种族，一边是高等种族——你们，死星的居民，还有 3769–37:478:326:894–6 号世界的居民。我们的科学，他们在两百年左右以前就勘察过了。

===Original===
“This other race was at a great distance from us, greater than yours, and apparently not feared as greatly as yours. They cannot cross to other worlds, save in small ships driven solely by fire, which the Thessians have called a ‘hopelessly inefficient and laughably awkward thing to ride in.’ ”
“Rockets,” grinned Morey. “Our first ship was part rocket.”
Zezdon Fentes smiled. “But that is all. We have brought you warning, and our plea. Can you help us?”

===Chinese===
“这另一个种族离我们十分遥远，比你们更远，显然也不像你们那样令他们忌惮。他们跨不出自己的世界，除非乘坐仅靠燃烧推进的小小飞船——塞特人（Thessians）管那叫‘一种效率低得无可救药、乘坐起来滑稽笨拙的玩意儿’。”
“火箭，”莫里咧嘴一笑，“我们的第一艘船就有几分是火箭。”
泽兹登·芬特斯莞尔一笑。“话就到此为止。我们带来了警告，也带来了恳求。你们能帮助我们吗？”

===Original===
“We cannot answer that. The Interplanetary Council must act. But I am afraid that it will be all we can do to protect our own world if this enemy attacks soon, and I fear they will. Since they have a base in this universe, it is impossible to believe that all ships did not report back to the home world at stated intervals. That one is missing will soon be discovered, and it will be sought. War will start at once. Three months it took you to reach us—they should come soon.
“Those men who left will be on their way back from the home world from which they came. What do you call your planet, friend?”
“Ortol is our home,” replied Zezdon Inthel.

===Chinese===
“这个问题我们无法作答，必须由行星际理事会（Interplanetary Council）定夺。但我担心，倘若敌人很快来犯——我担心他们一定会——我们能保住自己的世界就已竭尽全力了。既然他们在这个宇宙设有基地，就不可能相信他们的舰船不按规定周期向母星回报。少了一艘，很快就会被发现，随后就会有人来搜寻。战争会立刻打响。你们用了三个月才到我们这里——他们应该很快就会来。
“那些离去的人，此刻正在归途上，从他们的母星返回。朋友，你们的行星叫什么？”
“奥托尔（Ortol）是我们的家园，”泽兹登·因特尔（Zezdon Inthel）答道。

===Original===
“At any rate, I can only assure you that your world will be given weapons that will permit your people to defend themselves and I will get you to your home within twenty-four hours. Your ship—is it in the system?”
“It waits on the second satellite of the fourth planet,” replied Zezdon Afthen.
“Signal them, and tell them to land where a beacon of intense light, alternating red and blue, reaches up from—this point on the map.” Arcot pointed out the spot in Vermont where their private lake and laboratory were.

===Chinese===
“无论如何，我只能向你们保证：你们的世界将得到武器，让你们的人民足以自卫；而二十四小时之内，我会送你们回到家乡。你们的飞船——在本星系里吗？”
“它停在第四颗行星的第二颗卫星上，”泽兹登·阿夫森答道。
“给他们发信号，让他们降落到一道红蓝交替的强光信标向上射出的地方——地图上这一点。”阿科特指了指佛蒙特州他们那片私人湖泊与实验室所在的地点。

===Original===
He turned to the others, and in rapid-fire English, explained his plans. “We need the help of these people as much as they need ours. I think Zezdon Fentes will stay here and help you. The others will go with us to their world. There we shall have plenty of work to do, but on the way we are going to stop at Mars and pick up that valuable ship of theirs and make a careful examination for possible new weapons, their system of speed-drive, and their regular space-drive. I’m willing to make a bet right now, that I can guess both. Their regular drive is a molecular drive with lead disintegration apparatus for the energy, cosmic ray absorbers for the heating, and a drive much like ours. Their speed drive is a time distortion apparatus, I’ll wager. Time distinction offers an easy solution of speed. All speed is relative—relative to other bodies, but also to time-speed. But we’ll see.
“I’m going to hustle some workmen to installing the biggest spare power board I can get into the storerooms of the *Ancient Mariner*, and pack in a ray-screen. It will be useful. Let’s move.”
“Our ship,” said Zezdon Afthen, “will land in three of your hours.”

===Chinese===
他转向同伴们，用连珠炮似的英语说明自己的计划。“我们和这些人是彼此需要的。我想让泽兹登·芬特斯留在这里帮助你们，其余的跟我们一道去他们的世界。在那里我们有的是工作要做；而路上，我们打算在火星停靠，把他们那艘宝贵的飞船接上，仔细检验一番——看看有什么新式武器，看看他们的速度驱动系统，还有他们那套常规的空间驱动。我现在就敢打赌，这两样我全能猜中。他们的常规驱动是一种分子驱动，以铅蜕变装置供能，用宇宙射线吸收器散热，推进方式与我们的大同小异。至于速度驱动，我敢断言是一种时间扭曲装置。时间畸变是解决速度问题的捷径。一切速度皆是相对——相对于其他物体，也相对于时间的流速。不过，到时便见分晓。
“我这就催工人们动手，把能弄到的最大一块备用配电板装进老水手号（Ancient Mariner）的储藏舱，再塞进一面射线屏障——迟早派得上用场。出发。”
“我们的飞船，”泽兹登·阿夫森说，“再过你们的三个小时就降落。”

## XV / 全能之神

===Original===
Again there was a period of intense labor, while the ship drifted through time, following Earth in its mad careening about the sun, and the sun as it rushed headlong through space. At the end of a thirty-day period, they had reached no definite position in their calculations, and the Talsonian reported, as a medium between the two parties of scientists, that the work of the Ortolian had not reached a level that would make a scientific understanding possible.

As the ship needed no replenishing, they determined to finish their present work before landing, and it was nearly forty thousand years after their first arrival that they again landed on Earth.

It was changed now; the ice caps had retreated visibly, the Nile delta was far longer, far more prominent, and cities showed on the Earth here and there.

===Chinese===
又是一段紧张的劳作。飞船在时间中漂流，追随着地球绕太阳的疯狂疾转，也追随着太阳在太空中的夺路狂奔。三十天期满，他们的计算仍未得出确定的结果。那位塔尔索人充任两拨科学家之间的居间传话人，报告说：那位奥托尔人的研究，尚未进展到能作科学理解的地步。

飞船既无需补充给养，他们便决定先把手头的工作做完再降落；再度踏上地球时，距他们初次到来，已将近四万年。

地球变了模样；冰盖明显地退缩了，尼罗河三角洲延伸得更远，也更加突出醒目，大地上星星点点，已有了城市的踪影。

===Original===
Greece, they decided would be the next stop, and to Greece they went, landing on a mountain side. Below was a village, a small village, a small thing of huts and hovels. But the villagers attacked, swarming up the hillside furiously, shouting and shrieking warnings of their terrible prowess to these men who came from the “shining house,” ordering them to flee from them and turn over their possession to them.

“What'll we do?” asked Morey. He and Arcot had come out alone this time.

“Take one of these fellows back with us, and question him. We had best get a more or less definite idea of what time-age we are in, hadn't we? We don't want to overshoot by a few centuries, you know!”

===Chinese===
他们决定下一站去希腊，于是到了希腊，降落在山坡上。山下有个村庄，小小的村落，不过是一堆棚屋陋舍。村民们却发动了攻击，怒气冲冲地漫上山坡，冲着这些从“闪光的房子”里出来的人高喊尖叫，夸耀着自己何等骁勇可怕，喝令他们快滚，把财物统统交出来。

“咱们怎么办？”莫里问。这一次，他和阿科特是独自出来的。

“抓一个这号人带回去盘问盘问。咱们最好多少弄清一点，自己正处在哪个时代，是不是？总不想一冲就冲过头几个世纪吧，你知道的！”

===Original===
The villagers were swarming up the side of the hill, armed with weapons of bronze and wood. The bronze implements of murder were rare, and evidently costly, for those that had them were obviously leaders, and better dressed than the others.

“Hang it all, I have only a molecular pistol. Can't use that, it would be a plain massacre!” exclaimed Arcot.

But suddenly several others, who had come up from one side, appeared from behind a rock. The scientists were wearing their power suits, and had them on at low power, leaving a weight of about fifty pounds. Morey, with his normal weight well over two hundred, jumped far to one side of a clumsy rush of a peasant, leaped back, and caught him from behind. Lifting the smaller man above his head, he hurled him at two others following. The three went down in a heap.

===Chinese===
村民们漫上山坡，手里握着铜器和木器。这些用来杀人的铜家伙颇为稀罕，显然价格不菲——凡持铜器者，一看便知是头目，衣着也比旁人体面。

“真见鬼，我只带了一把分子手枪。这可不能用，那简直是场屠杀！”阿科特大呼。

可就在这时，另有几个人从侧面绕上来，突然出现在一块岩石后面。两位科学家穿着动力服，只调在低功率上，身上还压着大约五十磅的份量。莫里本人体重足有两百多磅，见一个庄稼汉笨拙地直冲过来，便远远跳向一旁，随即回身跃起，从背后将他拿住，把这个小个子举过头顶，朝后面追来的两个人掷去。三个人摔作一团。

===Original===
Most of the men were about five feet tall, and rather lightly built. The “Greek God” had not yet materialized among them. They were probably poorly fed, and heavily worked. Only the leaders appeared to be in good physical condition, and the men could not develop to large stature. Arcot and Morey were giants among them, and with their greater skill, tremendous jumping ability, and far greater strength, easily overcame the few who had come by the side. One of the leaders was picked up, and trussed quickly in a rope a fellow had carried.

“Look out,” called Wade from above. Suddenly he was standing beside them, having flown down on the power suit. “Caught your thoughts—rather Zezdon Afthen did.” He handed Arcot a ray pistol. The rest of the Greeks were near now, crying in amazement, and running more slowly. They didn't seem so anxious to attack. Arcot turned the ray pistol to one side.

“Wait!” called Morey. A face peered from around the rock toward which Arcot had aimed his pistol. It was that of a girl, about fifteen years old in appearance, but hard work had probably aged her face. Morey bent over, heaved on a small boulder, about two hundred pounds of rock, and rolled it free of the depression it rested in, then caught it on a molecular ray, hurled it up. Arcot turned his heat ray on it for an instant, and it was white hot. Then the molecular ray threw it over toward the great rock, and crushed it against it. Three children shrieked and ran out from the rock, scurrying down the hillside.

===Chinese===
这些人大多身高五英尺上下，体格也相当单薄。“希腊神祇”那样的体魄，在他们中间还没有出现。他们多半吃得粗劣，劳作又重。只有头目们看起来体魄强健，寻常人根本长不出高大身量。阿科特和莫里在他们中间俨然巨人，凭着更娴熟的身手、惊人的弹跳力和大得多的力气，轻易便撂倒了从侧面绕上来的那几个。一个头目被拎了起来，用旁人带着的一条绳子飞快捆了个结实。

“当心！”韦德在上面喊道。转眼间他已站到两人身旁，是驾着动力服飞下来的。“接到你们的念头了——不如说，是泽兹登·阿夫森接到的。”他递给阿科特一把射线手枪（ray pistol）。其余的希腊人已经逼近，惊叫连连，脚步却慢了下来，似乎不那么急着动手了。阿科特把射线手枪转向一侧。

“等等！”莫里喊道。一张脸从阿科特枪口所指的那块岩石旁探了出来，是个姑娘，看上去十五岁上下，只是繁重的劳作多半已叫她未老先衰。莫里俯下身，抱住一块小小的巨砾——约莫两百磅重的石头——把它从凹坑里滚了出来，随即用分子射线托住，猛地抛上半空。阿科特的热射线扫过一瞬，石头烧得白热。分子射线又把它掷向那块巨岩，撞得粉碎。三个孩子尖叫着从岩石后面逃出来，慌慌张张奔下山坡。

===Original===
The soldiers had stopped. They looked at Morey. Then they looked at the great rock, three hundred yards from him. They looked at the rock fragments.

“They think you threw it,” grinned Arcot.

“What else—they saw me pick it up, saw me roll it, and it flew. What else could they think?”

===Chinese===
那些士兵停住了。他们看看莫里，又看看距他三百码开外的巨岩，再看看那些碎石。

“他们以为石头是你扔的。”阿科特咧嘴笑道。

“不然呢——他们亲眼看见我搬起它，看见我把它滚了出来，然后它就飞了。不这么想，还能怎么想？”

===Original===
Arcot's heat ray hissed out, and the rocks sputtered and cracked, then glowed white. There was a dull explosion, and chips of rock flew up. Water, imprisoned, had been turned into steam. In a moment the whistle and crackle of combined heat and molecular rays stabbing out from Arcot's hands had built a barrier of fused rocks.

Leisurely Arcot and Morey carried their now revived prisoner back to the ship, while Wade flew ahead to open the locks.

Half an hour later the prisoner was discharged, much to his surprise, and the ship rose. They had been able to learn nothing from him. Even the Greek Gods, Zeus, Hermes, Apollo, all the later Greek gods, were unknown, or so greatly changed that Arcot could not recognize them.

===Chinese===
阿科特的热射线嘶嘶射出，岩石噼啪爆裂，继而烧得白亮。一声闷响，石屑纷飞——封在石中的水化作了蒸汽。顷刻之间，热射线与分子射线从阿科特双手间交错刺出，嘶鸣爆响，已筑起一道熔石凝成的屏障。

阿科特和莫里不紧不慢地抬着已然苏醒的俘虏返回飞船，韦德则飞在前面，先去开舱门。

半小时后，俘虏被放了——这大出他的意料——飞船随即升起。从他嘴里，他们什么也没能问出来。就连希腊诸神，宙斯（Zeus）、赫尔墨斯（Hermes）、阿波罗（Apollo），所有后世的希腊神祇，他也一概不知；或是形貌变了几多，阿科特根本认不出来。

===Original===
“Well,” he said at length, “it seems all we know is that they came before any historical Greeks we know of. That puts them back quite a bit, but I don't know how far. Shall we go see the Egyptians?”

They tried Egypt, a few moments across the Mediterranean, landing close to the mouth of the Nile. The people of a village near by immediately set out after them. Better prepared this time, Arcot flew out to meet them with Zezdon Afthen and Stel Felso Theu. Surely, he felt, the sight of the strange men would be no more terrifying than the ship or the men flying. And that did not seem to deter their attack. Apparently the proverb that “Discretion is the better part of valor,” had not been invented.

Arcot landed near the head of the column, and cut off two or three men from the rest with the aid of his ray pistol. Zezdon Afthen quickly searched his mind, and with Arcot's aid they determined he did not know any of the Gods that Arcot suggested.

===Chinese===
“唉，”他终于开口，“看来咱们只知道一点：他们来过，而且早在我们所知的任何有史时代的希腊人之前。这一来是把他们往前推了不少，可究竟推到多早，我说不准。要不要去会会埃及人？”

他们转赴埃及，几分钟便横越地中海，降落在尼罗河口近旁。邻近一座村庄的人立刻倾巢而出，追踪而至。这一次有了准备，阿科特带着泽兹登·阿夫森和斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤飞出去迎敌。他心想，几个怪人的现身，总不至于比那艘飞船或那些飞在空中的人更吓人。可这似乎丝毫没能吓退对方的攻势。显然，“谨慎总比逞勇强”这句谚语，这时还没有被发明出来。

阿科特降落在队伍前端附近，借助射线手枪把两三个人从大队里截了下来。泽兹登·阿夫森迅速探查此人的心灵，在阿科特协助下确认：阿科特提示的那些神祇，他一概不知。

===Original===
Finally they had to return to the ship, disappointed. They had had the slight satisfaction of finding that the Sun God was Ralz, the later Egyptian Ra might well have been an evolved form of that name.

They restocked the ship, fresh game and fruits again appearing on the menu, then once again they launched forth into space to wait for their own time.

“It seems to me that we must have produced some effect by our visit,” said Arcot, shaking his head solemnly.

===Chinese===
最后，他们只得失望地回到船上。唯一的些许安慰，是发现此地的太阳神名叫拉尔兹（Ralz）——后来的埃及神“拉”（Ra），多半就是由这个名字演化而来的。

他们给飞船补充了给养，菜单上又见新鲜的猎物和水果，随后再度启程驶入太空，等待属于他们自己的时代。

“依我看，我们这一趟到访，必定造成了某种影响。”阿科特一本正经地摇着头说。

===Original===
“We did, Arcot,” replied Morey softly. “We left an impress in history, an impress that still is, and an impress that affected countless thousands.

“Meet the Egyptian Gods with their heads strange to terrestrians, the Gods who fly through the air without wings, come from a shining house that flies, whose look, whose pointed finger melts the desert sands, and the moist soil!” he continued softly, nodding toward the Ortolian and the Talsonian.

“Their 'impossible' Gods existed, and visited them. Indubitably some genius saw that here was a chance for fame and fortune and sold 'charms' against the 'Gods.' Result: we are carrying with us some of the oldest deities. Again, we did leave our imprint in history.”

===Chinese===
“我们的确留下了，阿科特。”莫里轻声答道。“我们在历史上留下了印记，一个至今犹存的印记，一个影响过千千万万人的印记。

“看看那些埃及神祇吧——头颅生得让地球人备感陌生，不假翅膀便翱翔长空，从一座会飞的闪光房子里降临；他们的目光、他们指点出去的手指，能熔化沙漠的黄沙，也能熔化湿润的泥土！”他轻声续道，朝那位奥托尔人和那位塔尔索人点了点头。

“他们那些‘绝不可能有’的神祇，确有其神，而且真的造访过他们。毫无疑问，某位天才看出这是成名发财的良机，便兜售起对付‘神祇’的‘符咒’来。结果便是：我们随船带着的，竟是人间最古老的一批神祇。再说一遍，我们确确实实在历史上留下了印记。”

===Original===
“And,” cried Wade excitedly, “meet the great Hercules, who threw men about. I always knew that Morey was a brainless brute, but I never realized the marvelous divining powers of those Greeks so perfectly—now, the Incarnation of Dumb Power!” Dramatically Wade pointed to Morey, unable even now to refrain from some unnecessary comments.

“All right, Mercury, the messenger of the Gods speaks. The little flaps on Wade's flying shoes must indeed have looked like the winged shoes of legend. Wade was Mercury, too brainless for anything but carrying the words of wisdom uttered by others.

“And Arcot,” continued Morey, releasing Wade from his condescending stare, “is Jove, hurling the rockfusing, destroying thunderbolts!”

===Chinese===
“还有，”韦德兴奋地嚷道，“再会会伟大的赫拉克勒斯（Hercules）——专把人抛来掷去的那一位。我一直都说莫里是个没脑子的莽汉，可直到今天，我才把那些希腊人神乎其神的先见之明领教得如此透彻——瞧啊，‘蛮力的化身’！”韦德戏剧性地伸手指向莫里，事到如今，嘴上那几句多余的注脚还是免不了。

“好了好了，墨丘利（Mercury），众神的信使开尊口了。韦德飞行鞋上那两片小襟翼，落在古人眼里，想必真如传说中那双带翼的飞鞋。韦德便是墨丘利——那点脑子，除了传传旁人嘴里吐出的智慧，也别无所用。

“至于阿科特，”莫里说着，把那副居高临下的目光从韦德身上挪开，“便是朱庇特（Jove），专掷那熔石毁物的雷霆！”

===Original===
“The Gods that my friends have been talking of,” explained Arcot to the curious Ortolians, “are legendary deities of Earth. I can see now that we did leave an imprint on history in the only way we could—as Gods, for surely no other explanation could have occurred to those men.”

The days passed swiftly in the ship, as their work approached completion. Finally, when the last of the equation of Time, artificial matter, and the most awful of their weapons, the unlimited Cosmic Power, had been calculated, they fell to the last stage of the work. The actual appliances were designed. Then the completed apparatus that the Ortolian and the Talsonian had been working on, was carefully investigated by the terrestrial physicists, and its mechanism studied. Arcot had great plans for this, and now it was incorporated in their control apparatus.

The one remaining problem was their exact location in time. Already their progress had brought them well up to the nineteenth century, but, as Morey sadly remarked, they couldn't tell what date, for they were sadly lacking in history. Had they known the real date, for instance, of the famous battle of Bull Run, they could have watched it in the telectroscope, and so determined their time. As it was, they knew only that it was one of the periods of the first half of the decade of 1860.

===Chinese===
“我的朋友们方才谈起的那些神，”阿科特向满心好奇的奥托尔人解释道，“是地球传说中的神祇。如今我明白了：我们确实在历史上留下了印记——而且是以我们唯一可能的方式，作为神。在那时的古人想来，除此之外，断无别的解释。”

船上的日子飞逝而过，各项工作渐近尾声。终于，时间、人工物质，与他们最可怕的武器——无穷的宇宙之力（Cosmic Power）——之间的最后一段方程演算完毕，工作便转入最后阶段：实际的装置设计出来了。接着，那位奥托尔人和那位塔尔索人一直在研制的成品装置，经地球物理学家们细细查验，其机构原理也被一一吃透。阿科特对此早有宏图，如今这装置已并入他们的控制装置之中。

只剩一个问题：他们确切身处时间的哪一点。一路推进，他们已稳稳来到十九世纪，可正如莫里苦恼地所说，具体年月无从知晓——他们缺的恰恰是历史。比方说，倘若他们知道著名的布尔河战役（Battle of Bull Run）的确切日期，本可以用电视望远镜观上一战，从而定出自己的时间。可实际上，他们只知道，如今是 1860 年代上半期里的某段时日。

===Original===
“As historians, we're a bunch of first-class kitchen mechanics. Looks like we're due for another landing to locate the exact date,” agreed Arcot.

“Why land now? Let's wait until we are nearer the time to which we belong, so we won't have to watch so carefully and so long,” suggested Wade.

They argued this question for about two hundred years as a matter of fact. After that, it was academic anyway.

===Chinese===
“要论当历史学家，咱们就是一伙头等的厨房修理工。看来又得降落一回，把确切日期查明白。”阿科特附和道。

“何必现在就下去？不如等到离我们自己的时代更近了再降，省得盯得那么紧、耗得那么久。”韦德提议。

事实上，为这个问题，他们前前后后足足争论了约莫两百年。而两百年过后，它反正也已成了纸上空谈。

## XIII / 遇袭

===Original===
The *Ancient Mariner* stirred, and rose lightly from its place beside the city. Visible over the horizon now, and coming at terrific speed, was a fleet of seven Thessian ships.

They must do their best to protect that city. Arcot turned the ship and called his decision to Morey. As he did so, one of the Thessian ships suddenly swerved violently, and plunged downward. The attractive ray was in action. It struck the rocks of Neptune, and plunged in. Half buried, it stopped. Stopped—and backed out! The tremendously strong relux and lux had withstood the blow, and these strange, inhumanly powerful men had not been injured!

Two of the ships darted toward him simultaneously, flashing out molecular rays. The rays glanced off of Arcot’s screen already in place, but the tubes were showing almost at once that this could not be sustained. It was evident that the swiftly approaching ships would soon break down the shields. Arcot turned the ship and drove to one side. His eyes went dead.

===Chinese===
老水手号微微一震，轻盈地从那座城市旁停泊的位置上浮起。此刻，地平线之外已经望得见一支塞特人的舰队——七艘飞船，正以骇人的速度逼近。

他们必须竭尽全力保护那座城市。阿科特调转船头，把这个决定告知了莫里。就在这时，一艘塞特飞船猛然剧烈偏转，直坠而下——引力射线已经发动。那艘船撞上海王星的岩石，一头扎了进去，半截埋进岩体，停住了。停住了——又倒退着挣了出来！无比强韧的雷卢克斯与卢克斯扛住了这一撞，那些奇异而非人般强悍的家伙也毫发无伤！

两艘飞船同时朝他疾冲而来，射出分子射线。射线在阿科特早已张开的射线护盾上偏折滑开，但功率管几乎立刻就显示，这样的防御撑不下去了。显而易见，疾扑而来的飞船很快就会击穿护盾。阿科特调转船头，向一侧驶开。他的观测眼骤然熄灭。

===Original===
He cut into artificial space, waited ten seconds, then cut back. The scene before him changed. It seemed a different world. The light was very dim, so dim he could scarcely see the images on the view plate. They were so deep a red that they were very near to black. Even Sirius, the flaming blue-white star was red. The darting Thessian ships were moving quite slowly now, moving at a speed that was easy to follow. Their rays, before ionizing the air brilliantly red, were now dark. The instruments showed that the screen was no longer encountering serious loading, and, further, the load was coming in at a frequency harmlessly far down the radio spectrum!

Arcot stared in wide-eyed amazement. What could the Thessians have done that caused this change? He reached up and increased the amplification on the eyes to a point that made even the dim illumination sufficient. Wade was staring in amazement, too.

“Lord! What an idea!” suddenly exclaimed Arcot.

Wade was staring at Arcot in equally great amazement. “What’s the secret?” he asked.

===Chinese===
他切入人造空间，等了十秒钟，再切回来。眼前的景象变了，仿佛换了一个世界。光线极为暗淡，暗得他几乎看不清视屏上的影像。影像红得极深，几近于黑。连天狼星那颗炽燃的蓝白色恒星，也成了红色。疾掠的塞特飞船此刻移动得相当缓慢，慢得尽可从容追踪。它们的射线先前把空气电离得红光耀眼，如今却黯淡无光。仪表显示，护盾承受的负载已不再严重，而且，袭来的负载频率已远远落到无线电频谱的低端，无害无碍！

阿科特瞪大双眼，惊愕地凝视着。塞特人究竟做了什么，才造成了这样的变化？他抬手调高观测眼的放大倍率，直到连这点昏暗的光亮也足以看清。韦德也在惊愕地凝望。

“天哪！这主意真是绝了！”阿科特突然叫出声来。

韦德也怀着同样的惊愕望着阿科特。“诀窍是什么？”他问。

===Original===
“Time, man, time! We are in an advanced time plane, living faster than they, our atoms of fuel are destroyed faster, our second is shorter. In one second of our earthly time our generators do the same amount of work as usual, but they do many, many times more work in one second, of the time we were in! We are under the advanced time field.”

Wade could see it all. The red light—normal light seen through eyes enormously speeded in all perceptions. The change, the dimness—dim because less energy reached them per second of their time. Then came this blue light, as they reached the X-ray spectrum of Sirius, and saw X-rays as normal light—shielded, tremendously shielded by the atmosphere, but the enormous amplification of the eyes made up for it.

The remaining Thessians seemed to get the idea simultaneously, and started for Arcot in his own time field. The Thessian ship appeared to be actually leaping at him. Suddenly, his speed increased inconceivably. Simultaneously, Arcot’s hand, already started toward the space-control switch, reached it, and pushed it to the point that threw the ship into artificial Space. The last glimmer of light died suddenly, as the Thessian ship’s bow loomed huge beside the *Ancient Mariner*.

There was a terrific shock that hurled the ship violently to one side, threw the men about inside the ship. Simultaneously the lights blinked out.

===Chinese===
“时间，老兄，是时间！我们正处在一个超前的时间平面（advanced time plane）上，活得比他们快，燃料原子的耗损比他们快，我们的一秒钟也更短。在我们地球时间的一秒钟里，发电机做出的功与往常一样多，可换算到先前所处时间的一秒钟里，它们做出的功就要多出许许多多倍！我们此刻就在超前时间场（advanced time field）的作用之下。”

韦德全明白了。那红光——本是无异样的光，只是透过一双一切感知都极度加速的眼睛，才成了这般模样。变化，昏暗——暗，是因为在他们的一秒钟里，抵达他们的能量少了。而后来那片蓝光，则是在他们探入天狼星的X射线频段时到来的——X射线被看成了寻常的光。大气对它的屏蔽极重，可观测眼巨大的放大倍率补足了这一点。

其余的塞特人似乎同时悟出了其中道理，随即进入阿科特所处的时间场，朝他扑来。那艘塞特飞船看上去简直是在向他跃扑。蓦地，速度以难以想象的程度激增。与此同时，阿科特那早已伸向空间控制装置开关的手恰好到位，一推到底，将飞船抛进了人造空间。最后一线微光骤然熄灭——塞特飞船的船首已在老水手号身侧赫然逼近。

一阵剧烈的震撞把飞船狠狠甩向一侧，把船里的人抛来掷去。与此同时，灯光尽数熄灭。

===Original===
Light returned as the automatic emergency incandescent lights in the room, fed from an energy store coil, flashed on abruptly. The men were white-faced, tense in their positions. Swiftly Morey was looking over the indicators on his remote-reading panel, while Arcot stared at the few dials before the actual control board.

“*There’s an air pressure outside the ship!*” he cried out in surprise. “High oxygen, very little nitrogen, breathable apparently, provided there are no poisons. Temperature ten below zero C.”

“Lights are off because relays opened when the crash short circuited them.” Morey and the entire group were suddenly shaking.

“Nervous shock,” commented Zezdon Afthen. “It will be an hour or more before we will be in condition to work.”

“Can’t wait,” replied Arcot testily, his nerves on edge, too.

“Morey, make some good strong coffee if you can, and we’ll waste a little air on some smokes.”

===Chinese===
灯光回来了——舱内那几盏自动应急白炽灯由储能线圈供电，此刻骤然亮起。众人面色煞白，僵在各自的姿势里。莫里正飞快查验遥测读数面板（remote-reading panel）上的各路指示器，阿科特则凝视着主控制台前仅剩的几只表盘。

“飞船外面竟然有气压！”他惊呼出声。“氧含量很高，氮气极少，看起来可以呼吸——只要不含毒质。温度零下十摄氏度。”

“灯灭，是因为撞击造成短路，继电器跳开了。”这时，莫里和所有人都不由自主地颤抖起来。

“神经受了震荡，”泽兹登·阿夫森评道。“要过一个钟头或者更久，我们才能恢复到能干活的状态。”

“等不及，”阿科特没好气地答道，他自己也一样神经紧绷。

“莫里，能弄的话，煮点够劲的浓咖啡来，咱们也浪费一点儿空气，抽支烟。”

===Original===
Morey rose and went to the door that led through the main passage to the galley. “Heck of a job—no weight at all,” he muttered. “There is air in the passage, anyway.” He opened the door, and the air rushed from the control room to the passage till the pressure was equalized. The door to the power room was shut, but it was bulged, despite its two-inch lux metal, and through its clear material he could see the wreckage of the power room.

“Arcot,” he called. “Come here and look at the power room. Quintillions of miles from home, we can’t shut off this field now.”

Arcot was with him in a moment. The tremendous mass of the nose of the Thessian ship had caught them full amidship, and the powerful ram had driven through the room. Their lux walls had not been touched; only a sledgehammer blow would have bent them under any circumstances, let alone breaking them. But the tremendously powerful main generator was split wide open. And the mechanical damage was awful. The prow of the ship had been driven deep into the machine, and the power room was a wreck.

===Chinese===
莫里起身，走向那扇穿过主走廊通往厨房的门。“这活儿真见鬼——一点儿重量都没有，”他嘀咕道。“好在走廊里还有空气。”他推开门，空气从控制室涌进走廊，直到两处压力持平。动力室的门关着，却已鼓凸变形——那可是两英寸厚的卢克斯金属——透过透明的门体，他望见了动力室里的一片残骸。

“阿科特，”他喊道，“过来看看动力室。离家几百亿亿英里，眼下这个场可万万关不得。”

阿科特立刻赶了过来。塞特飞船那庞大的船首质量不偏不倚，正撞在他们船身中段，强劲的冲角（ram）贯穿了这个房间。卢克斯舱壁分毫未损——无论何时，想让它变形都得大锤猛砸，砸破更是免谈。可那台威力惊人的主发电机却被豁然劈开，机械损伤惨不忍睹：船首深深楔进机器，动力室成了一片废墟。

===Original===
“And,” pointed out Morey, “we can’t handle a job like that. It will take a tremendous amount of machinery back on a planet to work that stuff, and we couldn’t bend that bar, let alone fix it.”

“Get the coffee, will you please, Morey? I have an idea that’s bound to work,” said Arcot looking fixedly at the machinery.

Morey turned and went to the galley.

Five minutes later they returned to the corridor, where Arcot stood still, looking fixedly at the engine room. They were carrying small plastic balloons with coffee in them.

They drank the coffee and returned to the control room, and sat about, the terrestrians smoking peacefully, the Ortolian and the Talsonian satisfying themselves with some form of mild narcotic from Ortol, which Zezdon Afthen introduced.

===Chinese===
“再说，”莫里指出，“这样的活儿我们干不了。要加工这种材料，得回到行星上动用大批机械才行。那根大梁我们连弯都掰不动，更别说修复了。”

“去把咖啡弄来，行不行，莫里？我有个主意，保管行得通，”阿科特说道，目光一动不动地钉在机器上。

莫里转身去了厨房。

五分钟后，他们回到走廊。阿科特仍站在原地，目不转睛地望着动力室。他们手里都端着盛咖啡的小塑料球囊。

喝完咖啡，他们回到控制室各自坐下：地球人安然自得地吞云吐雾，奥托尔人和塔尔索人则享用起奥托尔出产的一种温和麻醉物——那是泽兹登·阿夫森拿出来的。

===Original===
“Well, we have a lot more to do,” Arcot said. “The air-apparatus stopped working a while back, and I don’t want to sit around doing nothing while the air in the storage tanks is used up. Did you notice our friends, the enemy?” Through the great pilot’s window the bulk of the Thessian ship’s bow could be seen. It was cut across with an exactitude of mathematical certainty.

“Easy to guess what happened,” Morey grinned. “They may have wrecked us, but we sure wrecked them. They got half in and half out of our space field. Result—the half that was in, stayed in. The half that was out stayed out. The two halves were instantaneously a billion miles apart, and that beautifully exact surface represents the point our space cut across.

“That being decided, the next question is how to fix this poor old wreck.” Morey grinned a bit. “Better, how to get out of here, and down to old Neptune.”

===Chinese===
“好了，要做的事还多着呢，”阿科特说。“制气装置方才已经停摆，我可不想干坐着，眼看着储备罐里的空气耗光。你们注意到我们的朋友——敌人了吗？”透过巨大的驾驶窗，可以望见塞特飞船那庞大的船首。切口齐整无比，带着数学必然般的精确。

“不难猜出发生了什么，”莫里咧嘴一笑。“他们或许撞毁了咱们，可咱们也确确实实撞毁了他们。他们一半进了我们的空间场，一半还在外头。结果——在里面的一半留在了里面，在外面的一半留在了外面。两半在一瞬之间相隔十亿英里，那道漂亮齐整的切面，正是我们的空间切过他们的位置。

“这个搞清楚了，下一个问题就是怎么修好这条可怜的老破船。”莫里又咧了咧嘴。“更要紧的是——怎么离开这儿，下到老海王星上去。”

===Original===
“Fix it!” replied Arcot. “Come on; you get in your space suit, take the portable telectroscope and set it up in space, motionless, in such a position that it views both our ship and the nose of the Thessian machine, will you, Wade? Tune it to—seven-seven-three.” Morey rose with Arcot, and followed him, somewhat mystified, down the passage. At the airlock Wade put on his space suit, and the Ortolian helped him with it. In a moment the other three men appeared bearing the machine. It was practically weightless, though it would fall slowly if left to itself, for the mass of the *Ancient Mariner* and the front end of the Thessian ship made a considerable attractive field. But it was clumsy, and needed guiding here in the ship.

Wade took it into the airlock, and a moment later into space with him. His hand molecular-driving unit pulling him, he towed the machine into place, and with some difficulty got it practically motionless with respect of the two bodies, which were now lying against each other.

“Turn it a bit, Wade, so that the *Ancient Mariner* is just in its range,” came Arcot’s thoughts. Wade did so. “Come on back and watch the fun.”

===Chinese===
“修好它！”阿科特答道。“来吧——你穿上太空服（space suit），带上便携式电视望远镜，到太空里把它架好，一动不动地放在既能望见我们这艘船、又能望见塞特机船首的位置上行不行，韦德？调到——七七三。”莫里随阿科特起身，跟着他走下走廊，心里几分摸不着头脑。到了气闸舱（airlock），韦德穿上太空服，奥托尔人在一旁帮他。转眼间，另外三人抬着那台机器出现了。它几乎没有分量——不过若听其自然，还是会缓缓坠落，因为老水手号与塞特飞船前端的巨大质量，已经形成了一个相当可观的引力场。只是这家伙笨手笨脚，在船里得有人引导着走。

韦德带着它进了气闸舱，片刻之后便连人带机器一同到了太空里。他靠手持分子推进装置牵引自身，把机器拖到位，又费了些气力，才让它相对那两个紧贴在一起的庞然大物基本静止下来。

“转一点儿，韦德，让老水手号正好落进它的视野，”阿科特的思维讯息传了过来。韦德照办了。“回来吧，来看好戏。”

===Original===
Wade returned. Arcot and the others were busy placing a heavy emergency lead from the storeroom in the place of one of the broken leads. In five minutes they had it fixed where they wanted it.

Into the control room went Arcot, and started the power-room teleview plate. Connected into the system of view plates, the scene was visible now on all the plates in the ship. Well off to one side of the room, prepared for such emergencies, and equipped with individual power storage coils that would run it for several days, the view plate functioned smoothly.

“Now, we are ready,” said Arcot. The Talsonian proved he understood Arcot’s intentions by preceding him to the laboratory.

Arcot had two viewplates operating here. One was covering the scene as shown by the machine outside, and the other showed the power room.

===Chinese===
韦德回来了。阿科特和其他人正忙着把一根从储藏室取来的粗大应急导线，接在一根断线的位置上。五分钟后，他们把它固定到了预定的位置。

阿科特走进控制室，启动了动力室的遥视屏（teleview plate）。由于接入了视屏系统，此刻全船每一块视屏上都能看到那边的景象。那块视屏装在房间一侧很远的地方，本就是为这类突发情况预备的，还配有足以让它独立运转数日的专用储能线圈，此刻运转顺畅。

“好了，一切就绪，”阿科特说。塔尔索人显然领会了阿科特的意图，先行一步，往实验室去了。

在这里，阿科特开着两块视屏。一块显示外面那台机器传回的景象，另一块显示动力室。

===Original===
Arcot stepped over to the artificial-matter machine, and worked swiftly on it. In a moment the power from the storage coils of the ship was flowing through the new cable, and into the machine. A huge ring appeared about the nose of the Thessian ship, fitting snugly over it. A terrific wrench—and it was free of the *Ancient Mariner*. The ring contracted and formed a chunk of the stuff free of the broken nose of the ship.

It was carried over to the wall of the *Ancient Mariner*, a smaller piece snipped off as before, and carried inside. A piece of perhaps half a ton mass. “I hope they use good stuff,” grinned Arcot. The piece was deposited on the floor of the ship, and a disc formed of artificial matter plugged the hole in its side. Another took a piece of the relux from the broken Thessian ship, pushed it into the hole on the ship. The space about the scene of operation was a crackling inferno of energy breaking down into heat and light. Arcot dematerialized his tremendous tools, and the wall of the *Ancient Mariner* was neatly patched with relux smoothed over as perfectly as before. A second time, using some of the relux he had brought within the ship, and the inner wall was rebuilt. The job was absolutely perfect, save that now, where there had been lux, there was an outer wall of relux.

===Chinese===
阿科特走到人工物质机器旁，迅速操作起来。转眼间，飞船储能线圈里的电能便顺着新接的导线涌进机器。一个巨大的圆环出现在塞特飞船的船首四周，严丝合缝地箍住它。猛力一扳——船首便从老水手号上脱了出来。圆环随即收紧，凝成一块那种材质的紧实料块，与破损的船首分离开来。

料块被移到老水手号的舱壁前，照方才那样剪下一小块，送进船里——约莫半吨重的一块。“但愿他们用料地道，”阿科特咧嘴笑道。这块料被搁在船内甲板上，随后，一块由人工物质铸成的圆盘堵住了船侧的破洞；另有一块人工物质从破毁的塞特飞船上取下一片雷卢克斯，压进船身的洞口。作业区四周，能量分解为热与光，噼啪炸响，恍若一片烈焰炼狱。阿科特解除了他那几件庞大工具的物质形态，老水手号的舱壁便已修补得整整齐齐，雷卢克斯抹得同原先一般平滑。第二遍，他用先前带进船内的一些雷卢克斯如法炮制，把内壁也重建起来。活儿做得尽善尽美——只是原先镶卢克斯的地方，如今外层换成了雷卢克斯。

===Original===
The main generator was crumpled up, and torn out. The auxiliary generators would have to carry the load. The great cables were swiftly repaired in the same manner, a perfect cylinder forming about them, and a piece of relux from the store Arcot had sliced from the enemy ship, welding them perfectly under enormous pressure, pressure that made them flow perfectly into one another as heat alone could not.

In less than half an hour the ship was patched up, the power room generally repaired, save for a few minor things that had to be replaced from the stores. The main generator was gone, but that was not an essential. The door was straightened and the job done.

In an hour they were ready to proceed.

===Chinese===
主发电机被揉作一团，整个拖了出去；负载只能交给辅助发电机来扛。那些巨大的电缆也以同样的方式迅速修复：一个规整的圆柱套在电缆之外凝成，再把一片从敌舰上削下存着的雷卢克斯压入其中，巨大压力之下，彼此完美地熔合为一——那样的熔流合一，单凭加热绝难做到。

不出半个钟头，飞船便修补完毕，动力室大体修复，只剩几样小部件需要从备件中更换。主发电机是没了，但那并非必不可少。舱门校正归位，活儿就此完工。

一小时后，他们已经整装待发。

## II / 犬族之人

===Original===
"And that," said Arcot between puffs, "will certainly be a great boon to the Rocket Patrol, you must admit. They don't like dueling with these space-pirates using the molecular rays, and since molecular rays have such a tremendous commercial value, we can't prohibit the sale of ray apparatus. Now, if you will come into the 'workshop,' Fuller, I'll give a demonstration with friend Morey's help."

The four friends rose, Morey, Wade and Fuller following Arcot into his laboratory on the thirty-seventh floor of the Arcot Research Building. As they went, Arcot explained to Fuller the results and principles of the latest product of the ingenuity of the "Triumvirate," as Arcot, Morey and Wade had come to be called in the news dispatches.

"As you know, the molecular rays make all the molecules of any piece of matter they are turned upon move in the desired direction. Since they supply no new energy, but make the body they are turned upon supply its own, using the energy of its own random molecular motion of heat, they are practically impossible to stop. The energy necessary for molecular rays to take effect is so small that the usual type of filter lets enough of it pass. A ship equipped with filters is no better off when attacked than one without. The rays simply drove the front end into the rear, or vice versa, or tore it to pieces as the pirates desired. The Rocket Patrol could kill off the pirates, but they lost so many men in the process, it was a Phyrric victory.

===Chinese===
“这一点，”阿科特吞云吐雾之际说道，“对火箭巡逻队（Rocket Patrol）来说无疑是一大福音，你们得承认。他们可不喜欢用分子射线（molecular ray）跟那帮太空海盗捉对厮杀，而分子射线又有着巨大的商业价值，我们没法禁止射线装置出售。好了，富勒（Fuller），请到‘工坊’里来，有莫里老兄帮忙，我给你演示一番。”

四位朋友起身，莫里（Morey）、韦德（Wade）和富勒跟着阿科特，走进他位于阿科特研究大厦（Arcot Research Building）三十七层的实验室。一路上，阿科特向富勒讲解这件“三巨头”（Triumvirate）最新智慧结晶的成果与原理——阿科特、莫里和韦德在新闻电讯里已经得了“三巨头”这个名号。

“你们知道，分子射线能让被它照射的任何物质的所有分子都朝指定方向运动。它并不提供新的能量，而是让被照射的物体自己出力——利用物体自身分子热运动的随机动能——所以实际上根本挡不住。分子射线生效所需的能量极小，寻常的过滤装置都拦不住足以奏效的量，装了过滤器的飞船遇袭时并不比没装的强。射线只会把船头压进船尾，或者反其道而行，再不然就照海盗的心意把整艘船撕成碎片。火箭巡逻队固然能剿灭海盗，可自己折损的人手太多，成了皮洛士式的惨胜。

===Original===
"For some time Morey and I have been working on something to stop the rays. Obviously it can't be by means of any of the usual metallic energy absorption screens.

"We finally found a combination of rays, better frequencies, that did what we wanted. I have such an apparatus here. What we want you to do, of course, is the usual job of rearranging the stuff so that the apparatus can be made from dies, and put into quantity production. As the Official Designer for the A.A.L. you ought to do that easily." Arcot grinned as Fuller looked in amazement at the apparatus Arcot had picked up from the bench in the "workshop."

"Don't get worried," laughed Morey, "that's got a lifting unit combined—just a plain ordinary molecular lift such as you see by the hundreds out there." Morey pointed through the great window where thousands of those lift units were carrying men, women and children through the air, lifting them hundreds, thousands of feet above the streets and through the doors of buildings.

===Chinese===
“前一阵子，我和莫里一直在琢磨能挡住射线的东西。显然，靠寻常那些金属吸能屏是行不通的。

“我们最后找到了一种射线组合，频率更合适，达到了我们想要的效果。我这里就有这样一台装置。当然，我们要你做的，还是老一套：把东西重新排布一番，让装置能开模制造、批量生产。你是 A.A.L. 的官方设计师，这活儿对你来说轻而易举。”富勒正惊讶地打量着阿科特从“工坊”台面上拿起的那台装置，阿科特咧嘴笑了。

“别担心，”莫里笑道，“那玩意儿组合了一个升降器——就是外头成百上千的那种普普通通的分子升降器。”莫里指向巨大的窗外：成千上万台升降器正载着男男女女穿行空中，把人们托到街道上空几百、几千英尺的高度，送进一栋栋楼宇的门里。

===Original===
"Here's an ordinary molecular pistol. I'm going to put the suit on, and rise about five feet off the floor. You can turn the pistol on me, and see what impression it makes on the suit."

Fuller took the molecular ray pistol, while Wade helped Arcot into the suit. He looked at the pistol dubiously, pointed it at a heavy casting of iron resting in one corner of the room, and turned the ray at low concentration, then pressed the trigger-button. The casting gave out a low, scrunching grind, and slid toward him with a lurch. Instantly he shut off the power. "This isn't any ordinary pistol. It's got seven or eight times the ordinary power!" he exclaimed.

"Oh yes, I forgot," Morey said. "Instead of the fuel battery that the early pistols used, this has a space-distortion power coil. This pistol has as much power as the usual A-39 power unit for commercial work."

===Chinese===
“这是一把普通的分子手枪。我穿上这套衣服，升到离地五英尺左右的高度。你可以用枪对我射击，看看它对这套衣服能有什么作用。”

富勒接过分子射线枪，韦德帮阿科特穿上那套衣服。富勒狐疑地端详着手枪，把它对准屋角一块沉重的铁铸件，把射线调到低强度，然后按下扳机按钮。铸件发出低沉的嘎吱研磨声，猛地朝他滑了过来。他立刻切断了电源。“这可不是什么普通手枪。功率足有普通枪的七八倍！”他惊呼道。

“哦对，我忘了说，”莫里道，“这枪没配早期手枪用的燃料电池，装的是空间畸变功率线圈。它的功率抵得上商用领域常见的 A-39 功率单元。”

===Original===
By the time Morey had explained the changes to Fuller, Arcot had the suit on, and was floating five or six feet in the air, like a grotesque captive balloon. "Ready, Fuller?"

"I guess so, but I certainly hope that suit is all it is claimed to be. If it isn't—well I'd rather not commit murder."

"It'll work," said Arcot. "I'll bet my neck on that!" Suddenly he was surrounded by the faintest of auras, a strange, wavering blue light, like the hazy corona about a 400,000-volt power line. "Now try it."

===Chinese===
等莫里给富勒讲完这些改动，阿科特已经穿好衣服，飘在五六英尺高的空中，活像一只怪模怪样的系留气球。“准备好了吗，富勒？”

“大概好了。不过我真心希望这套衣服名不虚传。要是不灵——那我可不想背上杀人的罪名。”

“管用的，”阿科特说，“我拿脖子担保！”刹那间，他周身泛起一圈极淡的光晕，一种奇怪摇曳的蓝光，仿佛 400,000 伏高压输电线周围朦胧的电晕。“来吧，开枪。”

===Original===
Fuller pointed the pistol at the floating man and pushed the trigger. The brilliant blue beam of the molecular ray, and the low hum of the air, rushing in the path of the director beam, stabbed out toward Arcot. The faint aura about him was suddenly intensified a million times till he floated in a ball of blue-white fire. Scarcely visible, the air about him blazed with bluish incandescence of ionization.

"Increase the power," suggested Morey. Fuller turned on more power. The blue halo was shot through with tiny violet sparks, the sharp odor of ozone in the air was stifling; the heat of wasted energy was making the room hotter.
