The power increased further, and the tiny sparks were waving streamers, that laced across the surface of the blue fire. Little jets of electric flame reached out along the beam of the ray now. Finally, as full power of the molecular ray was reached, the entire halo was buried under a mass of writhing sparks that seemed to leap up into the air above the man's head, wavering up to extinction. The room was unbearably hot, despite the molecular ray coolers absorbing the heat of the air, and blowing cooled air into the room.

Fuller snapped off the ray, and put the pistol on the table beside him. The halo died, and went out a moment later, and Arcot settled to the floor.

===Chinese===
富勒把枪口对准飘浮着的人，扣下扳机。分子射线炽亮的蓝色光束，连同空气冲入导向光束路径时发出的低鸣，一齐刺向阿科特。他周身那圈淡光骤然增强了百万倍，直到他整个人悬浮在一团蓝白色的火焰之中。他周身的空气电离生热，泛出淡蓝色的炽光，隐隐约约，几乎难以察觉。

“加大功率。”莫里提议。富勒把功率开得更大。蓝色光晕中窜动着细小的紫色火花，空气中臭氧的刺鼻气味令人窒息，白白耗散的能量烤得屋子越来越热。功率继续加大，细小的火花变成摇曳的电焰流，在蓝色火焰表面交织穿梭。此时，小小的电焰喷流已顺着射线光束向外伸展。最后，当分子射线达到全功率时，整个光晕都被一大团翻腾扭动的火花吞没，那些火花似乎要跃升到那人头顶的空中，摇曳着升腾、熄灭。尽管分子射线制冷器正吸收空气中的热量、把冷风送进屋里，房间还是热得让人难以忍受。

富勒啪的一声关掉射线，把手枪放在身旁桌上。光晕黯淡下去，片刻后熄灭，阿科特落回地面。

===Original===
"This particular suit will stand up against anything the ordinary commercial sets will give. The system now: remember that the rays are short electrical waves. The easiest way to stop them is to interpose a wave of opposite phase, and cause interference. Fine, but try to get in tune with an unknown wave when it is moving in relation to your center of control. It is impossible to do it before you yourself have been rayed out of existence. We must use some system that will automatically, instantly be out of phase.

"The Hall effect would naturally tend to make the frequency of a wave through a resisting medium change, and lengthen. If we can send out a spherical wave front, and have it lengthen rapidly as it proceeds, we will have a wave front that is, at all points, different. Any entering wave would, sooner or later, meet a wave that was half a phase out, no matter what the motion was, nor what the frequency, as long as it lies within the comparatively narrow molecular wave band. What this apparatus, or ray screen, consists of, is a machine generating a spherical wave front of the nature of a molecular wave, but of just too great a frequency to do anything. A second part generates a condition in space, which opposes that wave. After traveling a certain distance, the wave has lengthened to molecular wave type, but is now beyond the machine which generated it, and no longer affects it, or damages it. However, as it proceeds, it continues to lengthen, till eventually it reaches the length of infra-light, when the air quickly absorbs it, as it reaches one of the absorption bands for air molecular waves, and any molecular wave must find its half-wave complement somewhere in that wedge of waves. It does, and is at once choked off, its energy fighting the energy of the ray screen, of course. In the air, however, the screen is greatly helped by the fact that before the half-wave frequency is met in the ray-wedge, the molecular ray is buried in ions, leaving the ray screen little work to do.

"Now your job is to design the apparatus in a form that machines can make automatically. We tried doing it ourselves for the fun of it, but we couldn't see how we could make a machine that didn't need at least two humans to supervise."

===Chinese===
“这一套特制的衣服，足以抵挡普通商用装备所能发出的任何攻击。至于这套系统的原理——记住，射线是短电磁波。挡住它最省事的办法，是叠加一列相位相反的波，让它发生干涉。道理不错，可你想跟一列未知的波调到同频，而它还在相对你的控制中心运动——没等你调好，你自己早被射线消灭了。我们必须用一套能自动、即时错开相位的系统。

“霍尔效应（Hall effect）天然会使波在穿过阻性介质时频率改变、波长变长。如果我们能发出一个球形波前，让它在传播中迅速变长，得到的就是一个在所有点上频率各不相同的波前。任何射进来的波，无论处于什么运动状态、什么频率——只要落在分子波那个相对狭窄的波段里——迟早都会撞上一列相位恰好差半拍的波。这台装置，或者叫射线屏障，其构成是一台机器，产生一个性质如同分子波的球形波前，只是频率刚好高到起不了任何作用。第二个部分在空间中生成一种态势，与那列波相对抗。波行进一定距离后，波长已拉长到分子波的类型，但此时它已越过了产生它的机器，不再影响机器，也不会损伤机器。而它继续前进，波长继续拉长，直到最终达到亚光波（infra-light）的长度——此时它进入了空气分子波的某个吸收带，空气便迅速将它吸收，而任何分子波都必然能在那片波楔中的某处找到自己的半波配对。事实正是如此，它当即被扼断，其能量自然要同射线屏障的能量相抗衡。不过在大气中，屏障还有一大助力：在射线波楔中遇上半波频率之前，分子射线早已被埋进离子堆里，留给射线屏障的活儿没多少了。

“现在你的活儿，就是把这台装置设计成机器能够自动制造的形式。我们本来出于好玩自己试过，可怎么看也做不出一台至少不用两个人照看的机器。”

===Original===
"Well," grinned Fuller, "you have it all over me as scientists, but as economic workers—two human supervisors to make one product!"

"All right—we agree. But no, let's see you—Lord! What was that?" Morey started for the door on the run. The building was still trembling from the shock of a heavy blow, a blow that seemed much as though a machine had been wrecked on the armored roof, and a big machine at that. Arcot, a flying suit already on, was up in the air, and darting past Morey in an instant, streaking for the vertical shaft that would let him out to the roof. The molecular ray pistol was already in his hand, ready to pull any beams off unfortunate victims pinned under them.

In a moment he had flashed up through the seven stories, and out to the roof. A gigantic silvery machine rested there, streamlined to perfection, its hull dazzingly beautiful in the sunlight. A door opened, and three tall, lean men stepped from it. Already people were collecting about the ship, flying up from below. Air patrolmen floated up in a minute, and seeing Arcot, held the crowd back.

===Chinese===
“行啊，”富勒咧嘴一笑，“论科学你们比我强得多，可论经济账——造一件产品要配两名人工监工！”

“好吧——我们服气。不对——还是看你的本事吧——天哪！那是什么？”莫里拔腿朝门口冲去。整栋大楼还在一阵重击的余震里发抖，那声响就像是有一台机器砸毁在装甲楼顶上，还是台大机器。阿科特早已穿上飞行服，一跃升空，刹那间从莫里身边掠过，直奔通往楼顶的垂直升降井。他手里已经握着分子射线枪，好随时把压住倒霉蛋的横梁吊开。

转眼间他已疾冲而上，穿过七层楼，到了楼顶。一台巨大的银色机器停在那里，流线型完美无缺，船身在阳光下美得令人目眩。一扇门开了，走出三个瘦高的男人。人群已从下方纷纷飞上来，聚拢在飞船四周。空警片刻间便漂浮而至，一见是阿科特，便替他拦住了人群。

===Original===
The strange men were tall, eight feet or more in height. Great, round, soft brown eyes looked in curiosity at the towering multicolored buildings, at the people floating in the air, at the green trees and the blue sky, the yellowish sun.

Arcot looked at their strangely blotched and mottled heads, faces, arms and hands. Their feet were very long and narrow, their legs long and thin. Their faces were kindly; the mottled skin, brown and white and black, seemed not to make them ugly. It was not a disfigurement; it seemed oddly familiar and natural in some reminiscent way.

"Lord, Arcot—queer specimens, yet they seem familiar!" said Morey in an undertone.

===Chinese===
这些陌生的来客身材高大，足有八英尺上下。一双又大又圆、温和的褐色眼睛好奇地打量着高耸入云的彩色楼宇，打量着悬浮在空中的人们，打量着绿树、蓝天和淡黄的太阳。

阿科特端详着他们那布满奇异斑块的脑袋、面孔、手臂和双手。他们的脚又长又窄，腿又细又长。他们面容和善；棕、白、黑三色相间的斑纹皮肤并不显得丑陋。那不算什么缺陷，反倒透着一种莫名的亲切与自然，令人隐隐想起什么似的。

“天哪，阿科特——好古怪的家伙，可又觉得眼熟！”莫里低声道。

===Original===
"They are. Their race is that of man's first and best friend, the dog! See the brown eyes? The typical teeth? The feet still show the traces of the dog's toe-step. Their nails, not flat like human ones but rounded? The mottled skin, the ears—look, one is advancing."

One of the strangers walked laboriously forward. A lighter world than Earth was evidently his home. His great brown eyes fixed themselves on Arcot's. Arcot watched them. They seemed to expand, grow larger; they seemed to fill all the sky. Hypnotism! He concentrated his mind, and the eyes suddenly contracted to the normal eyes of the stranger. The man reeled back, as Arcot's telepathic command to sleep came, stronger than his own will. The stranger's friends caught him, shook him, but he slept. One of the others looked at Arcot; his eyes seemed hurt, desperately pleading.

Arcot strode forward, and quickly brought the man out of the trance. He shook his head, smiled at Arcot, then, with desperate difficulty, he enunciated some words in English, terribly distorted.

===Chinese===
“是眼熟。他们的种族，正是人类最早也最好的朋友——狗！看见那双褐色的眼睛了吗？那典型的牙齿？他们的脚上还留着犬类趾行的痕迹。他们的指甲不是人那种扁平的，而是浑圆的，对吧？还有斑纹皮肤、那对耳朵——瞧，有一个过来了。”

一个陌生人步履艰难地走上前来。显然，他的家园是一颗比地球更轻的行星。他那双褐色大眼定定地盯住了阿科特的眼睛。阿科特注视着那双眼。它们似乎在扩张、变大，似乎要占满整个天空。催眠术！他凝神定念，那双眼骤然缩回陌生人正常的模样。那人一个踉跄向后倒去——阿科特命其入睡的心灵感应令已经压过了他自己的意志。陌生人的同伴们扶住他、摇晃他，他却沉沉睡去。另一个陌生人望向阿科特，眼神里像是受了伤，在绝望地恳求。

阿科特大步上前，很快让那人脱离了恍惚状态。那人摇摇头，冲阿科特笑了笑，随后拼尽全力、咬字艰难地挤出几句英语，发音扭曲得厉害。

===Original===
"Ahy wizz tahk. Vokle kohds ron. Tahk by breen."

Distorted as it was, Arcot recognized the meaning without difficulty. "I wish (to) talk. Vocal cords wrong. Talk by brain." He switched to communication by the Venerian method, telepathically, but without hypnotism.

"Good enough. When you attempted to hypnotize me, I didn't known what you wanted. It is not necessary to hypnotize to carry on communication by the method of the second world of this system. What brings you to our system? From what system do you come? What do you wish to say?"

===Chinese===
“哎维什·塔克。沃克尔·科兹·荣。塔克·拜·布林。”

尽管发音扭曲，阿科特还是毫不费力地听出了意思：“我希望（要）谈话。声带不对。用脑谈话。”他改用金星人（Venerian）的交流法，直接心灵感应，但不再借催眠。

“这样就好。你方才想对我催眠时，我不明白你的用意。用本星系第二颗行星的方法交流，本不必先行催眠。是什么风把你们吹到我们这个星系来的？你们来自哪个星系？想说些什么？”

===Original===
The other, not having learned the Venerian system, had great difficulty in communicating his thoughts, but Arcot learned that they had machines which would make it easier, and the terrestrian invited them into his laboratory, for the crowd was steadily growing.

The three returned to their ship for a moment, coming out with several peculiar headsets. Almost at once the ship started to rise, going up more and more swiftly, as the people cleared a way for it.

Then, in the tiniest fraction of a second, the ship was gone; it shrank to a point, and was invisible in the blue vault of the sky.

===Chinese===
对方没学过金星人的那套方法，传递思想十分吃力，不过阿科特得知，他们带着能让交流变得容易的机器。这位地球人（terrestrian）便邀请他们进自己的实验室，因为围观的人越聚越多。

三人回了一趟飞船，拿出来几副奇特的头戴装置。几乎与此同时，飞船开始上升，人们纷纷给它让路，它越升越快。

接着，在短到无法再短的一瞬间，飞船不见了——它缩成一个点，消失在蔚蓝的天穹里。

===Original===
"Apparently they intend to stay a while," said Wade. "They are trusting souls, for their line of retreat is cut off. We naturally have no intention of harming them, but they can't know that."

"I'm not so sure," said Arcot. He turned to the apparent leader of the three and explained that there were several stories to descend, and stairs were harder than a flying unit. "Wrap your arms about my legs, when I rise above you, and hold on till your feet are on the floor again," he concluded.

The stranger walked a little closer to the edge of the shaft, and looked down. White bulbs illuminated its walls down its length to the ground. The man talked rapidly to his friends, looking with evident distaste at the shaft, and the tiny pack on Arcot's back. Finally, smiling, he evinced his willingness. Arcot rose, the man grasped his legs, and then both rose. Over the shaft, and down to his laboratory was the work of a moment.

===Chinese===
“看样子他们打算待上一阵子。”韦德说，“真是轻信的家伙，退路都断了。我们自然无意伤害他们，可他们没法知道这一点。”

“那可说不准。”阿科特道。他转向三人中显然的首领，解释说下面还有好几层楼，爬楼梯可比乘飞行器费劲。“等我升到你身边，就用双臂抱住我的腿，抓紧了，直到你的双脚重新踩到地板为止。”他最后交代道。

陌生人走近升降井口，朝下望去。白色灯泡把井壁自上而下一直照到地面。他飞快地同同伴们说着什么，望着那口深井，还有阿科特背上的小小背囊，神色分明很嫌恶。最后，他微笑着表示愿意一试。阿科特升起，那人抱住他的双腿，两人一同升了起来。越过井口，降到实验室，不过一瞬的工夫。

===Original===
Arcot led them into his "consultation room," where a number of comfortable chairs were arranged, facing each other. He seated them together, and his own friends facing them.

"Friends of another world," began Arcot, "we do not know your errand here, but you evidently have good reason for coming to this place. It is unlikely that your landing was the result of sheer chance. What brought you? How came you to this point?"

"It is difficult for me to reply. First we must be *en rapport*. Our system is not simple as yours, but more effective, for yours depends on thought ideas, not altogether universal. Place these on your heads, for only a moment. I must induce temporary hypnotic coma. Let one try first if you desire." The leader of the visitors held out one of the several headsets they had brought, caplike things, made of laminated metal apparently.

===Chinese===
阿科特把他们领进自己的“会商室”，里面摆着几把舒适的椅子，两两相对。他让三位客人坐在一侧，自己和朋友们坐在他们对面。

“异星的朋友们，”阿科特开口道，“我们不知道各位此来的使命，但你们显然有充分的理由来到此地。你们在此降落，恐怕绝非出于偶然。是什么召你们前来？你们又是怎么找到这里的？”

“这让我很难作答。首先，我们必须先达成心灵相通（en rapport）。我们的交流系统不像你们的那样简单，但更有效，因为你们的系统依赖思维意象，那并非人人共有。把这些戴在头上，只需一小会儿。我必须先引入暂时性的催眠昏睡。你们若不放心，可以先让一个人试试。”来客的首领递出他们带来的几副头戴装置中的一副——帽子似的物件，看来是层压金属制成的。

===Original===
Arcot hesitated, then with a grin slipped it on.

"Relax," came a voice in Arcot's head, a low, droning voice, a voice of command. "Sleep," it added. Arcot felt himself floating down an infinite shaft, on some superflying suit that did not pull at him with its straps, just floating down lightly, down and down and down. Suddenly he reached the bottom, and found to his surprise that it led directly into the room again! He was back. "You are awake. Speak!" came the voice.

Arcot shook himself, and looked about. A new voice spoke now, not the tonelessly melodious voice, but the voice of an individual, yet a mental voice. It was perfectly clear, and perfectly comprehensible. "We have traveled far to find you, and now we have business of the utmost import. Ask these others to let us treat them, for we must do what we can in the least possible time. I will explain when all can understand. I am Zezdon Fentes, First Student of Thought. He who sits on my right is Zezdon Afthen, and he beyond him, is Zezdon Inthel, of Physics and of Chemistry, respectively."

===Chinese===
阿科特迟疑了一下，还是咧嘴一笑，把那东西扣在了头上。

“放松。”一个声音在阿科特脑海里响起，低沉、单调，带着命令的意味。“睡吧。”那声音补充道。阿科特觉得自己正顺着一口无穷深的竖井飘落，身上穿着某种超级飞行服，没有带子勒着他，只是轻飘飘地往下落，落啊，落啊，落个不停。忽然他触到了井底，并且惊讶地发现，井底直接通回了那个房间！他回来了。“你醒了。说吧！”那声音又响起。

阿科特一激灵，环顾四周。此刻响起的是一个新的声音，不是那种不带感情的悦耳嗓音，而是一个个独有的声音——却是心灵的声音。它清晰无比，也让人理解得透彻无比。“我们远道跋涉来寻找你们，如今有万分紧要的事。请让其他几位也接受处理，因为我们必须在最短的时间里尽力而为。等人人都能听懂了，我自会解释。我是泽兹登·芬特斯（Zezdon Fentes），思想学首席学生（First Student of Thought）。坐在我右边的是泽兹登·阿夫森（Zezdon Afthen），再过去那位是泽兹登·因特尔（Zezdon Inthel），分别主攻物理与化学。”

===Original===
And now Arcot spoke to his friends.

"These men have something of the greatest importance to tell us, it seems. They want us all to hear, and they are in a hurry. The treatment isn't at all annoying. Try it. The man on the extreme right, as we face them, is Zezdon Fentes of Thought, Zezdon apparently meaning something like professor, or 'First Student of.' Those next him are Zezdon Afthen of Physics and Zezdon Inthel of Chemistry."

Zezdon Afthen offered them the headsets, and in a moment everyone present was wearing one. The process of putting them en rapport took very little time, and shortly all were able to communicate with ease.

===Chinese===
这时阿科特转对朋友们说话。

“这几位似乎有万分紧要的事要告诉我们。他们要我们所有人都听听，而且事情很急。那种处理一点也不难受，试试吧。我们面对他们时，最右边那位是思想学的泽兹登·芬特斯——‘泽兹登’显然是个类似教授的头衔，意为‘某某学的首席学生’。他旁边两位，是物理学的泽兹登·阿夫森和化学学的泽兹登·因特尔。”

泽兹登·阿夫森把头戴装置递了过来，片刻之间，在场每个人都戴上了一副。建立心灵相通的过程费时极短，很快人人都能自如交流了。

===Original===
"Friends of Earth, we must tell our strange story quickly for the benefit of your world as well as ours, and others, too. We cannot so much as annoy. We are helpless to combat them.

"Our world lies far out across the galaxy; even with incalculable velocity of the great swift thing that bore us, three long months have we traveled toward your distant worlds, hoping that at last the Invaders might meet their masters.

"We landed on this roof because we examined mentally the knowledge of a pilot of one of your patrol ships. His mind told us that here we would find the three greatest students of Science of this Solar System. So it was here we came for help.

===Chinese===
“地球的朋友们，我们必须赶快讲出我们那离奇的经历，这关系到你们的世界，也关系到我们自己的世界，还有别的世界。我们连叫他们皱一下眉头都做不到。我们对付不了他们，毫无办法。

“我们的世界远在银河系的另一端；即便凭着载我们前来的那艘巨大而迅捷的飞行器那难以估量的速度，我们也走了整整三个月，才朝你们遥远的诸世界赶到这里，只盼入侵者（Invaders）最终能遇上制服他们的人。

“我们降落在那片楼顶，是因为我们用心灵探查了你们一艘巡逻舰上一名驾驶员的所知。他的心智告诉我们，在这座太阳系里最杰出的三位科学研究者会在这里。于是我们为求助而来。

===Original===
"Our race has arisen," he continued, "as you have so surely determined from the race you call canines. It was artificially produced by the Ancient Masters when their hour of need had come. We have lost the great science of the Ancient Ones. But we have developed a different science, a science of the mind."

"Dogs are far more psychic than are men. They would naturally tend to develop such a civilization," said Arcot judiciously.

===Chinese===
“我们的种族，”他继续说道，“正如你们已然准确断定的那样，正是出自你们所称的犬类。那是远古宗师（Ancient Masters）在大难临头之时人工培育出来的。远古先民（Ancient Ones）的伟大科学，我们已经失传了。但我们发展出了另一门科学——心灵的科学。”

“狗的灵性远胜于人类。它们自然而然会倾向于发展出这样一种文明。”阿科特审慎地评道。

## XX / 毁灭

===Original===
Some time later, Arcot spoke. “I have just received a message from Zezdon Fentes that he has an important communication to make, so I will go down to New York instead of to Chicago, if you gentlemen do not mind. Morey will take you to Chicago in the tender, and I can find Zezdon Fentes.”

Zezdon Fentes’ message was brief. He had discovered from the minds of several who had been killed by the magnetic field Arcot had used, and not destroyed, that they had a base in this universe. Thett’s base was somewhere near the center of the galaxy, on a system of unusually large planets, circling a rather small star. But what star their minds had not revealed.

“It’s up to us then to locate said star,” said Arcot, after listening to Zezdon Fentes’ account: “I think the easiest way will be to follow them home. We can go to your world, Zezdon Fentes, and see what they are doing there, and drive them off. Then to yours, Stel Felso. I place your world second as it is far better able to defend itself than is Ortol. It is agreeable?”

===Chinese===
过了一阵，阿科特（Arcot）开了口。“我刚收到泽兹登·芬特斯（Zezdon Fentes）的一条消息，说他有要事相告。所以，只要诸位不介意，我就不去芝加哥，改下纽约了。莫里（Morey）可以乘交通艇送你们去芝加哥，我去接泽兹登·芬特斯。”

泽兹登·芬特斯的消息很简短。他从几名死者——他们死于阿科特动用的那道磁场，尸身却未遭毁坏——的心智中查明：他们在这个宇宙设有一处基地。塞特星（Thett）的基地就在银河中心附近的某处，位于一个行星大得出奇的星系里，绕着一颗相当小的恒星运转。可那是哪颗恒星，他们的心智却不曾透露。

“那么，找到那颗恒星就是我们的事了。”阿科特听完泽兹登·芬特斯的陈述后说道，“我看最省事的办法，就是跟着他们打回家去。我们先去你的星球，泽兹登·芬特斯，看看他们在那儿干些什么，再把他们赶走。然后去你的星球，斯特尔·费尔索（Stel Felso）。我把你的星球排在第二位，因为它自保的能力远比奥托尔（Ortol）强。二位意下如何？”

===Original===
It was, and the ship which had been hanging in the atmosphere over New York, where Zezdon Afthen, Fentes and Inthel had come to it in a taxi-ship, signaled for the crowd to clear away above. The enormous bulk of the shining machine, the savior of Earth, had attracted a very great amount of attention, naturally, and thousands on thousands of hardy souls had braved the cold of the fifteen mile height with altitude suits or in small ships. Now they cleared away, and as the ship slowly rose, the tremendous concentrated mental well-wishing of the thousands reached the men within the ship. “That,” observed Morley, “is one thing cosmium won’t stop. In some ways I wish it would—because the mental power that could be wielded by any great number of those highly advanced Thessians, if they know its possibilities, is not a thing to neglect.”

“I can answer that, terrestrian,” thought Zezdon Afthen. “Our instruments show great mental powers, and great ability to concentrate the will in mental processes, but they indicate a very slight development of these abilities. Our race, despite the fact that our mental powers are much less than those of such men as Arcot and yourself, have done, and can do many things your greater minds cannot, for we have learned the direction of the will. We need not fear the will of the Thessians. I feel confident of that!”

The ship was in space now, and as Arcot directed it toward Ortol, far far across the Island, he threw on, for the moment, the combined power of space distortion and time fields. Instantly the sun vanished, and when, less than a second later, he cut off the space field, and left only the time, the constellations were instantly recognizable. They were within a dozen light years of Ortol.

===Chinese===
众人自无异议。那艘船一直悬在纽约上空的大气层里——泽兹登·阿夫森（Zezdon Afthen）、泽兹登·芬特斯和泽兹登·因特尔（Zezdon Inthel）正是乘出租飞船来到船上的——此刻它发出信号，要上方的人群散开。这台光华璀璨的庞然巨物、地球的救星，自然吸引了极大的关注；成千上万胆气过人的男女，穿着高空服或驾着小型飞船，硬闯十五英里高空的严寒前来围观。此刻人群纷纷散去；飞船缓缓升起，数千人凝聚成一股的强大精神祝愿涌进船内。“这个，”莫里说，“倒是科斯米姆（cosmium）挡不住的东西。从某种意义上说，我倒希望它挡得住——因为那么多高度发达的塞特人（Thessian），倘若他们知晓自己精神力量的种种潜能，那股力量绝不是可以忽视的。”

“这个我来回答，地球人。”泽兹登·阿夫森以思维说道，“我们的仪器能测出强大的精神力量、在思维过程中凝聚意志的强大能力，但仪器也表明，他们这些能力的发展极为有限。我们这个种族，尽管精神力量远逊于阿科特和你这样的强者，却已经做到、并且能够做到许多你们更强大的心智也做不到的事，因为我们学会了为意志指引方向。塞特人的意志，我们无须畏惧。这一点我有充分的信心！”

此刻飞船已入太空。阿科特把航向指向远在空间之岛极远彼端的奥托尔，霎时间同时开动了空间畸变与时间场两重力量。太阳倏然隐没；不到一秒之后，他切断空间场，只留下时间场，四周的星座已可一眼辨认——他们距奥托尔已不足十二光年。

===Original===
“Morey, may I ask what you call this machine?” asked Torlos.

“You may, but I can’t answer,” laughed Morey. “We were so anxious to get it going that we didn’t name it. Any suggestions?”

For a moment none of them made any suggestions, then slowly came Arcot’s thoughts, clear and sharp, the thoughts of carefully weighed decision.

===Chinese===
“莫里，请教一句，你们管这台机器叫什么？”托洛斯（Torlos）问。

“尽管问，可我答不上来。”莫里笑道，“我们光顾着让它赶紧跑起来，连名字都没起。诸位有什么提议？”

一时间谁也没有作声。随后，阿科特的思维缓缓传来，清晰而锋利，那是反复权衡之后拿定的主意。

===Original===
“The swiftest thing that ever was *Thought*! The most irresistible thing, *thought*, for nothing can stop its progress. The most destructive thing, *thought*. Thought, the greatest constructor, the greatest destroyer, the product of mind, and producer of powers, the greatest of powers. Thought is controlled by the mind. Let us call it *Thought*!”

“Excellent, Arcot, excellent. The *Thought*, the controller of the powers of the cosmos!” cried Morey.

“But the *Thought* has not been christened, save in battle, and then it had no name. Let us emblazen its name on it now,” suggested Wade.

Stopping their motion through space, but maintaining a time field that permitted them to work without consuming precious time, Arcot formed some more cosmium, but now he subjected it to a special type of converted field, and into the cosmium, he forced some light photons, half bound, half free. The fixture he formed into the letters, and welded forever on the gigantic prow of the ship, and on its huge sides. *Thought*, it stood in letters ten feet high, made of clear transparent cosmium, and the golden light photons, imprisoned in it, the slowly disintegrating lux metal, would cause those letters to shine for countless aeons with the steady golden light they now had.

===Chinese===
“世间至快者，思想！至不可当者，思想，因为没有什么能拦住它的行进。至能毁灭者，也是思想。思想，最伟大的建造者，最伟大的毁灭者，心灵的产物，诸力的创造者，力量中之至大者。思想受心灵驾驭。就叫它思想号吧！”

“妙极，阿科特，妙极！思想号——宇宙诸力的驾驭者！”莫里高声喊道。

“不过，思想号还从没正式受过命名之礼，只在战斗中受过洗礼，而那时它连名字都没有。趁现在把它的名字铭刻到它身上吧。”韦德（Wade）提议道。

阿科特停驻了飞船在空间中的航程，却维持着一层时间场，让他们既能工作，又不耗损宝贵的光阴。他再度凝造出一些科斯米姆，这一回却让它经受一种特殊类型的转换场，并把若干光子强压进科斯米姆之中，一半受缚，一半自由。他将这具构件塑成字母，永久地熔焊在巨舰的船首与庞大的两舷之上。*Thought*——以澄澈透明的科斯米姆铸成的十英尺高字母巍然而立；囚于其间的金色光子，在这缓缓解体的卢克斯（lux）金属之中，将令那几个字母以如今这般恒定的金色光辉，闪耀无数亿万年。

===Original===
The *Thought* continued on now, and as they slowed their progress for Ortol, they saw that messengers of Thett had barely arrived. The fort here too had been razed to the ground, and now they were concentrating over the largest city of Ortol. Their rays were beating down on the great ray screen that terrestrial engineers had set up, protecting the city, as Earth had been protected. But the fleet that stood guard was small, and was rapidly being destroyed. A fort broke free, and plunged at last for the ray screen. Its relux walls glowed a thousand colors as the tremendous energy of the ray-screen struck them—but it was through!

A molecular ray reached down for the city—and stopped halfway in a tremendous coruscating burst of light and energy. Yet there was none of the sheen of the ray screen. Merely light.

The fort was still driving downward. Then suddenly it stopped, and the side dented in like the side of a can someone has stepped on, and it came to sudden rest against an invisible, impenetrable barrier. A molecular reached down from somewhere in space, hit the ray screen of Ortol, which the Thessians had attacked for hours, and the screen flashed into sudden brilliance, and disappeared. The ray struck the Thessian fort, and the fort burst into tremendous opalescence, while the invisible barrier the ray had struck was suddenly a great sheet of flaming light. In less than half a second the opalescence was gone, the fort shuddered, and shrieked out of the planet’s atmosphere, a mass of lux now, and susceptible to the moleculars. And everything that lived within that fort had died instantly and painlessly.

The fleet which had been preparing to follow the leading fort was suddenly stopped; it halted indecisively.

===Chinese===
思想号继续前进。当他们为抵近奥托尔而减速时，只见塞特星的使者舰队才刚刚赶到。这里的要塞同样已被夷为平地，此刻敌人正集结在奥托尔最大城市的上空。他们的射线正不停轰击着地球工程师们架设的巨型射线屏障（ray screen）——如同地球当年那样，这道屏障护住了城市。但担任守卫的舰队为数甚少，正在迅速覆灭。一座要塞挣脱出来，终于朝射线屏障直扑而下。射线屏障的庞大能量砸在它的雷卢克斯（relux）壁上，激起千般异彩——可它还是闯过来了！

一道分子射线朝城市探下——却在半途被一阵极尽炫目的光与能量的爆闪拦住。然而那里面没有射线屏障的光泽，只有纯粹的光。

要塞仍在向下冲压。忽然它停住了，一侧像被人踩过的罐头一样凹陷进去，猛地抵在一层看不见、不可逾越的屏障上。一道分子射线从太空深处某处探下，击中奥托尔的射线屏障——塞特人已围攻它数小时之久——屏障猝然一亮，随即消失。射线击中那座塞特要塞，要塞顿时迸发出强烈的乳白光焰，而射线所撞上的那道无形屏障，也刹那间化作一整片燃烧的光。不到半秒，乳白光焰散尽，要塞一阵战栗，尖啸着冲出行星的大气——它如今已只剩一团卢克斯，任凭分子射线宰割。而要塞中一切活物，都已在瞬间无痛地死去。

那支正准备跟着头一座要塞跟进的舰队骤然止步，迟疑不决地悬停在那里。

===Original===
Then the *Thought* became visible as its great golden letters showed suddenly, streaking up from distant space. Every ship turned cosmic and moleculars on it. The cosmic rebounded from the cosmium walls, and from the artificial matter that protected the eyes. The moleculars did not affect either, but the invisible protective sheet that the *Thought* was maintaining in the Ortolian atmosphere became misty as it fought the slight molecular rebounds.

The *Thought* went into action. The fort which remained was the point of attack. The fort had turned its destructive ray on the cosmium ship with the result that, as before, the cosmium slowly disintegrated into puffs of cosmic rays. The vapor seemed to boil out, puff suddenly, then was gone. Arcot put up a wall of artificial matter to test the effect. The ray went right through the matter, without so much as affecting it. He tried a sheet of pure energy, an electromagnetic energy stream of tremendous power. The ray bent sharply to one side. But in a moment the Thessians had realigned it.

“It’s a photonic stream, but of some type that doesn’t affect ordinary matter, but only artificial matter such as lux, relux, or cosmium. If the artificial matter would only fight it, I’d be all right.” The thought running through Arcot’s mind reached the others.

===Chinese===
随后，思想号显出了形迹——船身上那几个巨大的金色字母骤然显现，从遥远的太空疾射而来。每一艘敌船都把宇宙射线和分子射线对准了它。宇宙射线从科斯米姆壁上、从护住观察窗的人工物质（artificial matter）上弹了回去；分子射线对两者皆无可奈何，只是思想号维持在奥托尔大气中的那层无形护膜，在抗御分子射线微弱回波的侵扰时，泛起了一层雾翳。

思想号投入战斗。硕果仅存的那座要塞便是攻击目标。要塞已把毁灭射线转向这艘科斯米姆飞船，结果一如先前：科斯米姆缓缓解体，化作一蓬蓬宇宙射线。那蒸气仿佛沸腾般涌出，猛地一鼓，随即消散无踪。阿科特竖起一面人工物质之墙试探效果。射线径直穿透那物质，自己连丝毫影响都未受。他又试了一层纯粹的能量——一股威力巨大的电磁能量流。射线猛地折向一侧。但片刻之后，塞特人又把它校了回来。

“那是一种光子流，只是类型特殊，对普通物质毫无作用，只对卢克斯、雷卢克斯、科斯米姆这类人工物质起效。要是人工物质能与它相抗，我就不用愁了。”——这段掠过阿科特心头的念头，传到了其余人的心里。

===Original===
A tremendous burst of light energy to the rear announced the fact that a Thessian had crashed against the artificial matter wall that surrounded the ship. Arcot was throwing the Thessian destructive beam from side to side now, and twice succeeded in misdirecting it so that it hit the enemy machines.

The *Thought* sent out its terrific beam of magnetic energy. The ray was suddenly killed, and the fort cruised helplessly on. Its driving apparatus was dead. The diffused cosmic reached out, and as the magnetic field, the relux and the cosmics interacted, the great fort was suddenly blue-white—then instantly a dust that scattered before an enormous blast of air.

From the *Thought* a great shell of artificial matter went, a visible, misty wall, that curled forward, and wrapped itself around the Thessian ships with a motion of tremendous speed, yet deceptive, for it seemed to billow and flow.

===Chinese===
船尾后方一声光能的剧烈迸爆，宣告有一艘塞特战舰撞上了环绕本船的人工物质之墙。阿科特此刻正把塞特人的毁灭射线束左右甩动，两次成功让它偏转而去，反打到敌人自己的机器上。

思想号射出它那可怖的磁能射线束。要塞的射线骤然熄灭，只能无助地继续滑行——它的推进装置已经死透。扩散的宇宙射线随即罩下；磁场、雷卢克斯与宇宙射线相互激荡，巨大的要塞猛然亮成蓝白色，转瞬间化作一蓬尘埃，在一阵狂暴的气浪冲击下四散飘飞。

思想号掷出一具巨大的人工物质外壳——一面肉眼可见的雾墙，向前翻卷，以快得惊人的速度把塞特战舰团团裹住；那速度却又带着欺骗性，因为它看去仿佛只是在汹涌、在流淌。

===Original===
A Thessian warship decided to brush it away—and plowed into inconceivable strength. The ship crumpled to a mass of broken relux.

The greater part of the Thessian fleet had already fled, but there remained half a hundred great battleships. And now, within half a million miles of the planet, there began a battle so weird that astronomers who watched could not believe it.

From behind the *Thought*, where it hung motionless beyond the misty wall, a Thing came.

===Chinese===
一艘塞特战舰想把它拂开——一头扎进了难以想象的力量之中。那艘船顿成一堆碎裂的雷卢克斯。

塞特舰队的大部已经遁逃，但仍有五十艘巨型战舰留下。此刻，在这颗行星五十万英里之内，一场怪诞莫名的战斗开场了，观战的天文学家们简直不敢相信自己的眼睛。

思想号一动不动地悬停在雾墙之外；它的身后，一件巨物（Thing）来了。

===Original===
The Thessian ships had realized now that the misty sphere that walled them in was impenetrable, and their rays were off, for none they now had would penetrate it. The forts were gone.

But the Thing that came behind the *Thought* was a ship, a little ship of the same misty white, and it flowed into, and through the wall, and was within their prison. The Thessian ships turned their rays toward it, and waited. What was this thing?

The ovaloid ship which drifted so slowly toward them suddenly seemed to jerk, and from it reached pseudopods! An amoeba on a titanic scale! It writhed its way purposefully toward the nearest ship, and while that ship waited, a pseudopod reached out, and suddenly drove through the four foot relux armor! A second pseudopod followed with lightning rapidity, and in an instant the ship had been split from end to end!

Now a hundred rays were leaping toward the thing, and the rays burst into fire and gouts of light, blackened, burned pseudopods seemed to fall from the thing and hastily it retreated from the enclosure, flowing once more through the wall that stopped their rays.

===Chinese===
塞特战舰这时已经明白，那面把他们圈禁其中的雾球无法穿透，于是射线尽数歇了火，因为他们现有的任何射线都钻不透它。要塞已经完蛋。

但从思想号身后而来的这件巨物是一艘船，一艘同样雾白的小船。它流入雾墙、透墙而过，进了囚禁他们的牢笼。塞特战舰将射线转向它，屏息以待。这是什么东西？

那艘卵形船缓缓朝他们漂来，忽然像被什么扯了一下，船身探出了伪足！一只放大到骇人规模的变形虫！它扭动着身躯，目标明确地逼近最近的一艘战舰；就在那艘船愣神的当口，一条伪足倏然探出，猛地洞穿了四英尺厚的雷卢克斯装甲！第二条伪足以闪电般的速度跟进，转瞬间，那艘船已被从头到尾撕成两半！

顷刻间，上百道射线朝那东西跃击而至，纷纷爆作火焰与团团光斑；烧焦灼烂的伪足似乎纷纷自它身上脱落，它仓皇撤出这道围垣，再度流过那面能挡住他们射线的墙。

===Original===
But another Thing came. It was enormous, a mile long, a great, shining scaly thing, a dragon, and on its mighty neck was mounted an enormous, distorted head, with great flat nose and huge flapping nostrils. It was a Thessian head! The mouth, fifty feet across, wrinkled into an horrific grin, and broken, stained teeth of iron showed in the mouth. Great talons upraised, it rent the misty wall that bound them, and writhed its awful length in. The swish of its scales seemed to come to the watchers, as it chased after a great battleship whose pilot fled in terror. Faster than the mighty spaceship the awful Thing caught it in mighty talons that ripped through solid relux. Scratching, fluttering enormous, bloodred wings, the silvery claws tore away great masses of relux, sending them flying into space.

Again rays struck at it. Cosmic and moleculars with blinding pencils of light. For now in the close space of the Wall was an atmosphere, the air of two great warships, and though the space was great, the air in the ships was dense.

The rays struck its awful face. The face burst into light, and black, greasy smoke steamed up, as the thing writhed and twisted horribly, awful screams ringing out. Then it was free, and half the face was burned away, and a grinning, bleeding, half-cooked face writhed and screamed in anger at them. It darted at the nearest ship, and ripped out that ray that burned it—and quivered into death. It quivered, then quickly faded into mist, a haze, and was gone!

===Chinese===
接着又来了一件巨物。它庞大无比，长约一英里，一身鳞甲闪闪生光，是一条龙；巨颈上安着一颗扭曲变形的硕大头颅，生着扁平的巨鼻和翕张鼓动的巨大鼻孔。那是一颗塞特人的头颅！那张宽达五十英尺的嘴皱起一个狰狞可怖的狞笑，口中露出残断染污的铁齿。它高举巨爪，撕开困住他们的雾墙，把可怖的长躯扭动着塞了进来。它追逐着一艘巨型战舰——那驾驶员吓得魂飞魄散，夺路而逃——鳞片的簌簌擦响仿佛一直传到观者耳中。可怖的巨物比强大的飞船还要快，巨爪一攫，洞穿坚实的雷卢克斯，将它拿住。它抓挠着、扑扇着硕大的血红双翼，银色的利爪撕下大块大块的雷卢克斯，打得四散飞入太空。

射线又一次朝它打来。宇宙射线与分子射线，化作道道炫目的光锥。因为此刻，这堵墙围拢的封闭空间里已有大气——那是两艘巨型战舰内的空气；这片空间虽说不小，舰中的空气却颇为稠密。

射线打在它那张可怖的脸上。那张脸轰然爆出光焰，黑色的油腻浓烟蒸腾而起，那东西恐怖地翻滚扭动，凄厉的惨叫四下回荡。随后它挣脱了，半张脸已经烧掉，一张狞笑着、淌着血、烧得半熟的脸，冲着他们愤怒地扭动、尖啸。它朝最近的战舰猛扑过去，撕扯那条烧伤它的射线——随即一阵痉挛，就此死去。它抽搐着，很快淡成一片薄雾，一团霭气，消失了！

===Original===
A last awful thing—a thing they had not noticed as all eyes watched that Thing—was standing by the rent in the Sphere now, the gigantic Thessian, with leering, bestial jaws, enormous, squat limbs, the webbed fingers and toes, and the heavy torso of his race, grinning at them. In one hand was a thing—and his jaws munched. Thett’s men stared in horror as they recognized that thing in his hand—a Thessian body! He grinned happily and reached for a battleship—a ray burned him. He howled, and leaped into their midst.

Then the Thessians went mad. All fought, and they fought each other, rays of all sorts, their moleculars and their cosmics, while in their midst the Giant howled his glee, and laughed and laughed—

Eventually it was over, and the last limping Thessian ship drove itself crazily against the wreck of its last enemy. And only wreckage was left.

===Chinese===
还有最后一件可怖之物——当所有目光都盯着那件巨物时，谁也没留意到它——此刻正立在那球体的破口旁：一个巨躯塞特人，生着邪淫兽性的双颚、粗壮矮敦的四肢、带蹼的指趾，还有他一族厚重的躯干，正朝他们咧嘴而笑。他一只手里抓着一样东西——上下颚正大嚼。塞特的众人惊骇地瞪视，认出了他手中的东西——那是一具塞特人的躯体！他欢喜地咧嘴一笑，伸手便去抓一艘战舰——一道射线烧中了他。他长嚎一声，纵身跃入他们中间。

塞特人顿时疯了。所有船都在开火，而且彼此开火，各色射线、他们的分子射线与宇宙射线搅作一团；而在他们中间，那巨人欢快地长嚎，笑了又笑，笑个不停——

终于，一切结束了。最后那艘踉跄苟延的塞特战舰，疯疯癫癫地一头撞上它最后一个敌人的残骸。剩下的唯有断骸残片。

===Original===
“Lord, Arcot! Why in the Universe did you do that—and how did you conceive those horrors?” asked Morey, more than a little amazed at the tactics Arcot had displayed.

Arcot shook himself, and disconnected his controls. “Why—why I don’t know. I don’t know what made me do that, I’m sure. I never imagined anything like that dragon thing—how did—”

His keen eyes fixed themselves suddenly on Zezdon Fentes, and their tremendous hypnotic power beat down the resistance of the Ortolian’s trained mind. Arcot’s mind opened for the others the thoughts of Zezdon Fentes.

===Chinese===
“天哪，阿科特！你到底为什么要那么干——又是从哪儿想出那些恐怖东西的？”莫里问道，他对阿科特展现的这套战术惊愕不已。

阿科特浑身一震，切断了操控。“为什么——为什么，我也不知道。我真的不知道自己怎么会干出那种事来。我做梦都没想象过什么龙一样的怪物——那是怎么——”

他那双锐利的眼睛倏然盯住泽兹登·芬特斯，眼中巨大的催眠之力压垮了这位奥托尔人（Ortolian）受过训练的心智的抵抗。阿科特以自己的心智，为其他人敞开了泽兹登·芬特斯的思绪。

===Original===
He had acted as a medium between the minds of the Thessians, and Arcot. Taking the horror-ideas of the Thessians, he had imprinted them on Arcot’s mind while Arcot was at work with the controls. In Arcot’s mind, they had acted exactly as had the ideas that night on Earth, only here the demonstration had been carried to the limit, and the horror ideas were compounded to the utmost. The Thessians, highly developed minds though they were, were not resistant and they had broken. The Allies, with their different horror-ideas, had been but slightly affected.

“We will leave you on Ortol, Zezdon Fentes. We know you have done much, and perhaps your own mind has given a bit. We hope you recover. I think you agree with me, Zezdon Afthen and Inthel?” thought Arcot.

“We do, heartily, and are heartily sorry that one of our race has acted in this way. Let us proceed to Talso, as soon as possible. You might send Fentes down in a shell of artificial matter,” suggested Zezdon Afthen.

===Chinese===
他充当了塞特人心智与阿科特之间的中介。他取来塞特人心中的恐怖意象，趁阿科特忙于操控之际，把它们印上阿科特的心智。在阿科特心中，这些意象发挥了与地球那一夜的那些念头一模一样的作用，只是这一回，演示被推到了极限，恐怖意象也叠合到了无以复加的地步。塞特人尽管心智高度发达，却毫无抵抗力，就此崩溃。而盟友们怀着的是另一套恐怖意象，只受到了轻微的波及。

“我们就把你留在奥托尔，泽兹登·芬特斯。我们知道你出了很大的力，或许你自己的心智也折损了一点。愿你早日康复。泽兹登·阿夫森、泽兹登·因特尔，我想二位会同意我的看法？”阿科特以思维说道。

“我们由衷赞同，也由衷痛心，我们种族中竟有人做出这等事来。让我们尽快赶往塔尔索（Talso）吧。您可以送芬特斯下去，用一具人工物质的外壳护送。”泽兹登·阿夫森提议道。

===Original===
“Which,” said Arcot, after this had been done, and they were on their way to Talso, “shows the danger of a mad *Thought*!”

===Chinese===
办妥此事，他们踏上前往塔尔索的旅途之后，阿科特说道：“这件事恰恰证明——疯狂的思想，是何等危险！”

## XVIII / 地球的防御

===Original===
“I am sorry, Arcot. I did not know, for I see I might have helped, but to me, with my ideas of horror, it was as you said, amusement,” said Torlos. They were sitting now in Arcot’s study at the cottage; Arcot, his father, Morey, Wade, Torlos, the three Ortolians and the Talsonian.

“I know, Torlos. You see, where I made my mistake, as I have said, was in forgetting that in doing as I did, picturing horror, like a snowball rolling, it would grow greater. The idea of horror, started, my mind pictured one, and it inspired greater horror, which in turn reacted on my all too reactive apparatus. As you said, the things changed as you watched, molding themselves constantly as my mind changed them, under its own initiative and the concentrated thoughts of all those others. It was a very foolish thing to do, for that last Thing—well, remember it *was*, it existed, and the idea of hate and lust it portrayed was caused by my mind, but my mind could picture what it would do, if such were its emotions, and it would do them because my mind pictured them! And *nothing* could resist it!” Arcot’s face was white once more as he thought of the danger he had run, of the terrible consequences possible of that “amusement.”

“I think we had best start on the ship. I’ll go get some sleep now, and then we can go.”

===Chinese===
「实在抱歉，阿科特。我当时并不明白——如今看来，我本来是能帮上忙的——只是对我而言，凭我对恐怖的观感，那正如你所说，不过是一场消遣。」托洛斯说。此刻他们正坐在小屋里阿科特的书房中：阿科特、他的父亲、莫里、韦德、托洛斯、三位奥托尔人，还有那位塔尔索人。

「我明白，托洛斯。你知道，正如我所说，我错就错在忘了一点：像我那样做，把恐怖的景象描绘出来，它就会像滚动的雪球一样越变越大。恐怖的念头一旦开了头，我的心智便构想出一个恐怖之物，它又激起更大的恐怖，反过来再冲击我这台反应过于灵敏的装置。正如你所说，那些东西在你注视时不断变化，随着我的心智自行其是，又加上所有其他人凝聚的意念，不停地把自己塑成新的形态。那样做实在太蠢了——因为最后那个怪物，唉，记住，它可曾是真实的，它确实存在过；它所展现的那份仇恨与贪欲固然出自我的心智，可我的心智既然能构想出那样的情绪会驱使它做什么，它就会去做，因为我的心智构想出了这些！而且没有任何东西能抵挡它！」阿科特想起自己方才经历的险境，想起那场“消遣”可能招致的可怕后果，脸色又一次白了。

「依我看，咱们最好这就准备上船。我现在去睡一觉，醒来咱们就出发。」

===Original===
Arcot led the way to the ship, while Torlos, Morey and Wade and Stel Felso Theu accompanied him. The Ortolians were to work on Earth, aiding in the detection of attacks by means of their mental investigation of the enemy.

“Well—goodbye, Dad. Don’t know when I’ll be back. Maybe twenty-five thousand years from now, or twenty-five thousand years ago. But we’ll get back somehow. And we’ll clean out the Thessians!”

He entered the ship, and rose into space.

“Where are you going, Arcot?” asked Morey.

===Chinese===
阿科特领路走向飞船，托洛斯、莫里、韦德和斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤随行。奥托尔人则留在地球上，以他们对敌人的心灵探查，协助侦测敌方的来袭。

「那么——再见了，爸爸。不知道什么时候才能回来。也许是从两万五千年以后回来，也许是回到两万五千年以前。但不管怎样，我们总会回来的。而且，我们一定会把塞特人清理干净！」

他走进飞船，升入太空。

「阿科特，你要去哪儿？」莫里问。

===Original===
“Eros,” replied Arcot laconically.

“Not if my mind is working right,” cried Wade suddenly. All the others were tense, listening for inaudible sounds.

“I quite agree,” replied Arcot. The ship turned about, and dived toward New York, a hundred thousand miles behind now, at a speed many times that of light as Arcot snapped into time. Across the void, Zezdon Fentes’ call had come—New York was to be attacked by the Thessians, New York and Chicago next. New York because the orbits of their two forts were converging over that city in a few minutes!

They were in the atmosphere, screaming through it as their relux glowed instantaneously in the Heaviside layer, then was through before damage could be done. The screen was up.

Scarcely a minute after they passed, the entire heavens blazed into light, the roar of tremendous thunders crashing above them, great lightning bolts rent the upper air for miles as enormous energies clashed.

===Chinese===
「爱神星（Eros）。」阿科特简短地答道。

「要是我心感不错，就不去那儿！」韦德突然喊道。其他人都绷紧了心弦，倾听着那无声的讯息。

「我完全同意。」阿科特答道。飞船掉转方向，朝纽约俯冲而去——纽约如今已在十万英里之外——阿科特一切入时间，速度便数倍于光速。茫茫空际，泽兹登·芬特斯的呼叫传来：塞特人即将进犯纽约，纽约和芝加哥是接下来的目标。之所以是纽约，是因为敌方那两座要塞的轨道几分钟后就要在这座城市上空交汇！

他们已冲进大气层，呼啸着穿行其间，雷卢克斯船壳在海维赛德层中霎时灼灼发亮，却赶在损伤酿成之前穿透了那层屏障。屏障已经升起。

他们刚过不到一分钟，整片天穹骤然燃成白昼，巨雷的轰鸣在头顶炸响，巨大的闪电将高空撕裂数英里之遥——庞大的能量正在那里激烈相撞。

===Original===
“Ah—they are sending everything they have against that screen, and it’s hot. We have ten of our biggest tube stations working on it, and more coming in, to our total of thirty, but they have two forts, and Lord knows how many ships.

“I think me I’m going to cause them some worrying.”

Arcot turned the ship, and drove up again, now at a speed very low to them but as they had the time-field up, very great. They passed the screen, and a tremendous bolt struck the ship. Everything in it was shielded, but the static was still great enough to cause them some trouble as the time-field and electric field fought. But the time-field, because of its very nature, could work faster, and they won through undamaged, though the enormous current seemed flowing for many minutes as they drifted slowly past it. Slowly—at fifty miles a second.

===Chinese===
「啊——他们正把全部火力朝那道屏障砸去，打得正猛。咱们有十座最大的功率管站在支撑它，还有更多正在赶来，总数将达到三十座；可他们有两座要塞，还有天知道多少艘战舰。

「依我看呐，接下来该轮到他们头疼头疼了。」

阿科特调转船头，再度向上疾驰——以他们的体感，此刻速度极慢，但由于时间场已经开启，实际速度却极为惊人。他们穿过那道屏障，一道巨大的闪电劈中飞船。舱内一切都加了屏蔽，可静电之强仍给他们添了些麻烦——时间场与电场正相互抗衡。不过时间场因其本性使然，作用更快，他们终究安然无损地冲了过去，尽管那股巨大电流仿佛奔涌了好几分钟，他们才缓缓从其中漂过。缓慢——每秒五十英里的缓慢。

===Original===
Out in space, free of the atmosphere, Arcot shot out to the point where the Thessians were congregating. The shining dots of their ships and the discs of the forts were visible from Earth save for the air’s distortion.

They seemed a miniature Milky Way, their deadly beams concentrated on Earth.

Then the Thessians discovered that the terrestrial fleet was in action. A ship glowed with the ray, the opalescence of relux under moleculars visible on its walls. It simply searched for its opponent while its relux slowly yielded. It found it in time, and the terrestrial ship put up its screen.

The terrestrial fleet set to work, everything they had flying at the Thessian giants, but the Thessians had heavier ships, and heavier tubes. More power was winning for them. Inevitably, when the Sun’s interference somewhat weakened the ray shield—

===Chinese===
冲出大气、进入开阔太空之后，阿科特直飞塞特人集结之处。他们那些闪闪发光的战舰，连同圆盘状的要塞，若非大气扭折光线，从地面上本也望得见。

它们宛如一条微缩的银河，致命的光束齐齐汇聚在地球。

这时塞特人才发现地球舰队已经开战。一艘地球战舰被射线罩住，船壁上泛起雷卢克斯在分子射线下的乳白光晕。它只能徒然搜寻对手，而船壳的雷卢克斯正在缓慢熔蚀。它总算及时找到了对手，那艘地球战舰升起了屏障。

地球舰队全面开火，所有火力一齐砸向塞特人的庞然巨舰；然而塞特人的船更重，功率管也更粗，更大的功率正在为他们赢得胜利。不可避免地，当太阳的干扰稍稍削弱了射线护盾之时——

===Original===
About that time Arcot arrived. The nearest fort dived toward the further with an acceleration that smashed it against no less than ten of its own ships before they could so much as move.

When the way was clear to the other fort—and that fort had moved, the berserk fort started off a new tack—and garnered six more wrecks on its side.

Then Thett’s emissaries located Arcot. The screen was up, and the Negrian attractive ray apparatus which Arcot had used was working through it. The screen flashed here and there and collapsed under the full barrage of half the Thessian fleet, as Arcot had suspected it would. But the same force that made it collapse operated a relay that turned on the space control, and Thett’s molecular ray energy steamed off to outer space.

“We worried them, then dug our hole and dragged it in after us, as usual, but damn it, we can’t hurt them!” said Arcot disgustedly. “All we can do is tease them, then go hide where it’s perfectly safe, in artificial—” Arcot stopped in amazement. The ship had been held under such space control that space was shut in about them, and they were motionless. The dials had reached a steady point, the current flow had become zero, and they hung there with only the very slow drain of the Sun’s gravitational field and that of the planet’s field pulling on the ship. Suddenly the current had leaped, and the dials giving the charge in the various coil banks had moved them down toward zero.

===Chinese===
大约就在这时，阿科特赶到了。最近的那座要塞朝远处那座猛扑过去，加速度大得骇人，那些自家战舰还来不及挪动，就被它撞碎了不下十艘。

等到通往另一座要塞的通路清开时——那座要塞已经挪了位置——发狂的要塞又转上新的航向，在自己一侧再收进六艘残骸。

随后，塞特星的使者们锁定了阿科特。屏障已经升起，阿科特先前用过的那套涅格里安引力射线装置正透过屏障运转。屏障东闪西烁，在塞特舰队半数火力的全力齐射下轰然坍塌——正如阿科特预料的那样。但使屏障坍塌的那股力量，同时扳动了一枚继电器，接通了空间控制装置；塞特星的分子射线能量便一股脑儿泄散到外层太空去了。

「咱们先把他们惹火，然后照老规矩挖个洞、连洞一起拖进来藏好——可该死，咱们根本伤不了他们！」阿科特懊恼地说。「咱们能做的只有逗弄他们，然后躲到绝对安全的地方去，躲进人工——」阿科特蓦地收声，惊愕不已。飞船原本被空间控制装置牢牢定住，四周的空间整个闭合起来，他们纹丝不动。仪表读数已经稳定，电流归于零，他们悬在那里，只有太阳引力场和行星引力场对飞船那极其缓慢的牵引消耗。可就在刚才，电流陡然跃升，显示各线圈组电荷量的仪表读数一齐向零滑落。

===Original===
“Hey—they’ve got a wedge in here and are breaking out our hole. Turn on all the generators, Morey.” Arcot was all action now. Somehow, inconceivable though it was, the Thessians had spotted them, and got some means of attacking them, despite their invulnerable position in another space!

The generators were on, pouring enormous power into the coils, and the dials surged, stopped, and climbed ever so slowly. They should have jumped back under that charge, ordinarily dangerously heavy. For perhaps thirty seconds they climbed, then they started down at full speed!

Arcot’s hand darted to the time field, and switched it on full. The dial jerked, swung, then swung back, and started falling in unison with the dials, stopped, and climbed. All climbed swiftly, gaining ever more rapidly. With what seemed a jerk, the time dial flew over, and back, as Arcot opened the switch. They were free, and the dial on the space control coils was climbing normally now.

“By the Nine Planets, did they drink out our energy! The energy of six tons of lead just like that!”

===Chinese===
「喂——他们往这儿塞了一个楔子，正把我们那个洞往外撬。莫里，开动全部发电机。」阿科特此刻全身心扑在了行动上。不可思议，但事实如此：尽管他们藏身于另一个空间、处于无法被攻击的位置，塞特人竟还是发现了他们，而且找到了攻击他们的手段！

发电机开动起来，把巨大的功率灌进线圈。仪表读数猛地一振，停滞，随即极缓慢地爬升。照常理，在这样危险的重负荷充电之下，读数本应猛然回跳。爬升了大约三十秒，读数竟又开始全速下跌！

阿科特的手闪电般伸向时间场开关，将它开到最大。那只仪表猛地一颤，摆动，又摆回来，随即与其他仪表一同下跌，停住，转而攀升。所有读数都迅速爬升，增速越来越快。随着仿佛猛拽似的一荡，时间仪表飞快转过头去，又摆了回来——阿科特断开了开关。他们脱困了，空间控制线圈的仪表此刻也恢复了正常爬升。

「九大行星在上，他们把我们的能量吸了个一干二净！六吨铅蓄的能量，就这么没了！」

===Original===
“How’d they do it?” asked Wade.

Torlos kept silent, and helped Morey replace the coils of lead wire with others from stock.

“Same way we tickled them,” replied Arcot, carefully studying the control instruments, “with the gravity ray! We knew all along that gravitational fields drank out the energy—they simply pulled it out faster than we could pump it in, and used four different rays on us doing it. Which speaks well for a little ship! But they burned off the relux on one room here, and it’s a wreck. The molecs hit everything in it. Looks like something bad,” called Arcot. The room was Morey’s, but he’d find that out himself. “In the meantime, see if you can tell where we are. I got loose from their rays by going on both the high speed time-field and the space control at full, with all generators going full blast. Man, they had a stranglehold on us that time! But wait till we get that new ship turned out!”

===Chinese===
「他们是怎么做到的？」韦德问。

托洛斯默不作声，帮着莫里从备件中取出新线圈，换下烧坏的铅丝线圈。

「跟咱们刚才逗他们的法子一样，」阿科特一面仔细查看控制仪表，一面答道，「用的是引力射线！引力场会吸走能量，这一点我们早就知道——他们只是往外抽的速度比我们往里灌的速度快，而且还同时用四种不同的射线来抽我们。就冲这一点，一条小船能撑成这样也算难得了！不过他们把我们这里一个房间的雷卢克斯衬层烧穿了，那房间彻底报废，分子射线把里面的东西打了个遍。看来够呛。」阿科特扬声说。那是莫里的房间，不过这话留给他自己去发现吧。「眼下，你们先看看能不能弄清咱们到了哪儿。我是靠着高速时间场和空间控制装置全开、所有发电机全功率运转，才从他们的射线里挣脱出来的。好家伙，那一下子他们可把我们卡得死死的！不过等着瞧，等咱们那艘新船造出来！」

===Original===
With the telectroscope they could see what was happening. The terrific bombardment of rays was continuing, and the fleets were locked now in a struggle, the combined fleets of Earth and Venus and of Nansal, far across the void. Many of the terrestrian, or better, Solarian ships, were equipped with space distortion apparatus, now, and had some measure of safety in that the attractive rays of the Thessians could not be so concentrated on them. In numbers was safety; Arcot had been endangered because he was practically alone at the time they attacked.

But it was obvious that the Solarian fleet was losing. They could not compete with the heavier ships, and now the frequent flaming bursts of light that told of a ship caught in the new deadly ray showed another danger.

“I think Earth is lost if you cannot aid it soon, Arcot, for other Thessian ships are coming,” said Stel Felso Theu softly.

From out of the plane of the planetary orbits they were coming, across space from some other world, a fleet of dozens of them. They were visible as one after another leapt into normal time-rates.

===Chinese===
通过电视望远镜，他们能看清外面的战况。猛烈的射线轰击仍在继续，此时双方舰队已陷入鏖战——地球、金星与远隔长空的南萨尔联合舰队。如今许多地球人的——或者更确切地说，太阳系军民（Solarian）的——战舰都装上了空间畸变装置，多少有了些安全保障，因为塞特人的引力射线再也无法那样集中地锁定它们。数量便是安全；阿科特之所以遇险，是因为遭袭之时他几乎孤身一舰。

但显而易见，太阳系舰队正在败退。他们敌不过那些更重的巨舰，而此刻频频炸开的熊熊火光——那意味着又有战舰落入新式致命射线的魔掌——则昭示着另一种危险。

「阿科特，要是你不能尽快驰援，我看地球就要完了，因为塞特人的其他战舰正在赶来。」斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤轻声说。

它们正从行星轨道平面之外驶来——跨越太空，来自某个别的世界——足足几十艘组成的舰队。它们一艘接一艘跃入正常的时间速率，这才显出身形。

===Original===
“Why don’t they fight in advanced time?” asked Morey, half aloud.

“Because the genius that designed that apparatus didn’t think of it. Remember, Morey, those ships have their time apparatus connected with their power apparatus so that the power has to feed the time continuously. They have no coils like ours. When they advance their time, they’re weakened every other way.

“We need that new ship. Are we going to make it?” demanded Arcot.

“Take weeks at best. What chance?” asked Morey.

===Chinese===
「他们为什么不在超前时间里作战？」莫里半是自言自语地问。

「因为设计那套装置的天才压根没想到这一层。记住，莫里，那些船的时间装置和功率装置是联在一起的，功率必须源源不断地喂给时间装置。它们没有咱们这样的线圈。时间一旦超前，它们其他各个方面就全面削弱了。

「咱们需要那艘新船。来得及造出来吗？」阿科特追问。

「最快也得几个星期。那还顶什么用？」莫里问。

===Original===
“Plenty; watch.” As he spoke, Arcot pulled open the time controls, and spun the ship about. They headed off toward a tiny point of light far beyond. It rushed toward them, grew with the swiftness of an exploding bomb, and was suddenly a great, rough fragment of a planet hanging before them, miles in extent.

“Eros,” explained Wade laconically to Torlos. “Part of an ancient planet that was destroyed before the time of man, or life on Earth. The planet got too near the sun when its orbit was irregular, and old Sol pulled it to pieces. This is one of the pieces. The other asteroids are the rest. All planetary surfaces are made up of great blocks; they aren’t continuous, you know. Like blocks of concrete in a building, they can slide a bit on each other, but friction holds them till they slip with a jar and we have earthquakes. This is one of the planetary blocks. We see Eros from Earth intermittently, for when this thing turns broadside it reflects a lot of light; edge on it does not reflect so much.”

It was a desolate bit of rock. Bare, airless, waterless rock, of enormous extent. It was contorted and twisted, but there were no great cracks in it for it was a single planetary block.

Arcot dropped the ship to the barren surface, and anchored it with an attractive ray at low concentration. There was no gravity of consequence on this bit of rock.

===Chinese===
「机会多得很；看着吧。」说话间，阿科特拉开时间控制器，将飞船猛地转了个向。他们朝远方一粒微小的光点飞驰而去。那光点迎面扑来，以炸弹炸裂般的速度急速胀大，倏忽之间，已是一大块嶙峋粗粝的行星残骸悬在眼前，方圆达数英里。

「爱神星。」韦德向托洛斯简略地解释道，「一颗远古行星的一部分。那颗行星早在人类出现之前——早在地球有生命之前——就毁灭了。它的轨道一度不规则，行星离太阳太近，老太阳便把它扯成了碎片。这就是其中一块，其余的小行星都是另外的碎块。所有行星的表层都是由巨大的断块拼成的；它们并不是连成一体的，你知道吗。就像建筑物里的一块块混凝土，彼此之间能稍稍滑动，靠摩擦力维系着，直到某天猛地一错，便有了地震。这就是一块行星断块。从地球上看，爱神星时隐时现，因为这东西正面朝向我们时反射的光很多，侧棱对着我们时反射的光就少了。」

那是一块荒凉的岩石。光秃、无空气、无水，广袤无垠。它扭曲盘结，却没有巨大的裂缝，因为它是一整块行星断块。

阿科特将飞船降落到荒芜的岩面上，用一束低浓度的引力射线将它锚住。这块岩石上没有任何值得一提的引力。

===Original===
“Come on, get to work. Space suits, and rush all the apparatus out,” snapped Arcot. He was on his feet, the power of the ship in neutral now. Only the attractor was on. In the shortest possible time they got into their suits, and under Arcot’s direction set up the apparatus on the rocky soil as fast as it was brought out. In all, less than fifteen minutes were needed, yet Arcot was hurrying them more and more. Torlos’ tremendous strength helped, even on this gravitationless world, for he could accelerate more quickly with his burdens.

At last it was up for operation. The artificial matter apparatus was operated by cosmic power, and controlled by mental operation, or by mathematical formula as they pleased. Immediately Arcot set to work. A giant hollow cylinder drilled a great hole completely through the thin, curved surface of the ancient planetary block, through twelve miles of solid rock—a cylinder of artificial matter created on a scale possible only to cosmic power. The cylinder, half a mile across, contained a huge plug of matter. Then the artificial matter contracted swiftly, compressing the matter, and simultaneously treating it with the tremendous fields that changed its energy form. In seconds it was a tremendous mass of cosmium.

A second smaller cylinder bored a plug from the rock, and worked on it. A huge mass of relux resulted. Now other artificial matter tools set to work at Arcot’s bidding, and cut pieces from his huge masses of raw materials, and literally, quick as thought, built a great framework of them, anchored in the solid rock of the planetoid.

===Chinese===
「来，动手干活。穿上太空服，把所有装置统统搬出去。」阿科特厉声吩咐。他一跃而起，飞船动力此刻置于空挡，只有引力器还在运转。他们以最快的速度穿好太空服，装置一搬出，便在阿科特的指挥下争分夺秒地在岩石地面上架设起来。前后总共用了不到十五分钟，阿科特却还在不断催促。托洛斯那惊人的膂力即便在这个没有引力的世界里也大派用场——负着重物，他能比旁人加速得更快。

装置终于架设完毕，开始运转。人工物质装置以宇宙之力驱动，既可随心以心智操控，也可按数学公式操控。阿科特随即动手。一根巨大的空心圆柱在远古行星断块那薄薄的弧形表层上钻出一个贯通的大洞，穿透十二英里厚的坚实岩石——那是唯有宇宙之力才造得出如此规模的人工物质圆柱。圆柱直径半英里，其中容纳着一大块柱塞状物质。随后人工物质急速收缩，将物质压缩，同时以改变其能量形态的强大场加以处理。短短几秒钟，它便成了一大块科斯米姆（cosmium）。

第二根小些的圆柱从岩石中钻取出一段柱塞，加以处理，得到一大块雷卢克斯。此刻，其余的人工物质工具听候阿科特的号令纷纷开工，从他堆积如山的原料上切下料件，简直快如思想，转眼便搭起一座巨大的构架，牢牢锚定在小行星的坚实岩体之中。

===Original===
On the great framework that had been constructed, four tall shafts of cosmium appeared, and each was a hollow tube, up the center of which ran a huge cable of relux. At the peak of each mile-high shaft was a great globe. Now in the framework below things were materializing as Arcot’s flying thoughts arranged them—great tubes of cosmium with relux element—huge coils of relux conductors, insulated with microscopic but impenetrable layers of cosmium.

Still, for all his swiftness of mind and accuracy of thought, he had to correct two mistakes in all his work. It was nearly an hour before the thing was finished. Then, two hundred feet long, a hundred wide, and fifty in height, the great mechanism was completed, the tall columns rising from four corners of the greater framework that supported it.

Then, into it, Arcot turned the powers of the cosmos. The stars in the airless space wavered and danced as though seen through a thick atmosphere. Tingling power ran through them as it flowed into the tremendous coils. For thirty seconds—then the heavens were as before.

At last Arcot spoke. Through the radio communicators, and through the thought-channels, his ideas came as he took off the headpiece. “It’s done now, and we can rest.” There was a tremendous crash from within the apparatus. The heavens reeled before them, and shifted, then were still, but the stars were changed. The sun shone weirdly, and the stars were altered.

===Chinese===
在那座已然建成的巨大构架上，凭空立起四根高耸的科斯米姆柱，每根都是中空管，管心贯穿着一条粗大的雷卢克斯缆。每根一英里高的立柱顶端都托着一颗巨大的球体。此时，下方构架上的部件正随着阿科特飞驰的思绪逐一成形——嵌着雷卢克斯芯件的科斯米姆大管，一匝匝粗大的雷卢克斯导体线圈，彼此隔着微乎其微却无法穿透的科斯米姆绝缘层。

然而，纵然他心思如电、意念精准，全部工程之中仍不得不纠正两处失误。将近一个小时之后，这台装置方告完工：长两百英尺，宽一百英尺，高五十英尺，四根高柱自承托它的更大构架的四角拔地而起。

随后，阿科特将宇宙的伟力灌入其中。没有空气的太空中，群星摇曳跳动，仿佛隔着一层浓厚的大气。灼灼刺人的伟力涌入巨大的线圈，穿流其间。如此过了三十秒——天空便恢复如初。

阿科特终于开口了。他摘下头环，意念经由无线电通讯器和思维通道传来：「成了，咱们可以歇一歇了。」装置内部响起一声巨响。眼前的天穹天旋地转，摇晃挪移，继而归于静止——但星辰已经变了。太阳的辉光透着诡异，群星也都换了模样。

===Original===
“That is a time shifting apparatus on a slightly larger scale,” replied Arcot to Torlos’ question, “and is designed to give us a chance to work. Come on, let’s sleep. A week here should be a few minutes of Earthtime.”

“You sleep, Arcot. I’ll prepare the materials for you,” suggested Morey. So Arcot and Wade went to sleep, while Morey and the Talsonian and Torlos worked. First Morey bound the *Ancient Mariner* to the frame of the time apparatus, safely away from the four luminous balls, broadcasters of the time field. Then he shut off the attractive ray, and bound himself in the operator’s seat of the apparatus of the artificial matter machine.

A plane of artificial matter formed, and a stretch of rock rose under its lift as it cleft the rock apart. A great cleared, level space resulted. Other artificial matter enclosed the rock, and the fragments cut free were treated under tremendous pressure. In a few moments a second enormous mass of cosmium was formed.

===Chinese===
「这是一台时间搬移装置（time shifting apparatus），只不过规模稍大，」阿科特回答托洛斯的疑问，「造它就是为了给咱们争取干活的时间。来，睡觉吧。在这儿过一个星期，地球时间应该只过去几分钟。」

「你睡吧，阿科特。材料我来替你备。」莫里主动提议。于是阿科特和韦德去睡觉，莫里、那位塔尔索人和托洛斯继续干活。莫里先把老水手号绑在时间装置的构架上，安然避开作为时间场发射器的四颗发光巨球，然后关掉引力射线，把自己固定在人工物质机的操作座上。

一张人工物质平面凭空成形，剖开岩石，一大片岩体在它的抬举下隆起剥离，腾出一大块平整的空地。另一些人工物质包裹住岩体，切下的碎块在强大的压力下接受处理。片刻之后，第二大块科斯米姆宣告成形。

===Original===
For three hours Morey worked steadily, building a tremendous reserve of materials. Lux metal he did not make, but relux, the infusible, perfect conductor, and cosmium in tremendous masses, he did make. And he made some great blocks of oxygen from the rock, transmuting the atoms, and stored it frozen on the plane, with liquid hydrogen in huge tanks, and some metals that would be needed. Then he slept while they waited for Arcot.

Eight hours after he had lain down, Arcot was up, and ate his breakfast. He set to work at once with the machine. It didn’t suit him, it seemed, and first he made a new tool, a small ship that could move about, propelled by a piece of artificial matter, and the entire ship was a tremendously greater artificial matter machine, with a greater power than before!

His thoughts, far faster than hands could move, built up the gigantic hull of the new ship, and put in the rooms, and the brace members in less than twelve hours. A titanic shell of eight-inch cosmium, a space, with braces of the same nonconductor of heat, cosmium, and a two inch inner hull. A tiny space in the gigantic hull, a space less than one thousand cubic feet in dimension was the control and living quarters.

===Chinese===
莫里稳稳当当地干了三个小时，储备起堆积如山的材料。卢克斯金属他不做，但雷卢克斯——那不可熔蚀的完美导体——和大块大块的科斯米姆，他都造了出来。他还从岩石中嬗变原子，造出几大块氧，冷冻着存放在平面上，另有巨罐盛装的液氢，以及一些将会用到的金属。随后他便睡了，众人静候阿科特醒来。

躺下八个钟头后，阿科特起身吃过早饭，立刻开动机器干了起来。那套机器似乎不合他的心意，于是他先造了一件新工具：一艘能四处移动的小飞船，由一块人工物质推进，而整艘船本身就是一台比先前强大得不可同日而语的人工物质机，功率也远胜从前！

他的思绪远比双手迅捷，不到十二个小时便立起新船那巨大的船壳，装好舱室和支撑构件：一层八英寸厚的科斯米姆巨壳，中留间隔，以同样不导热的科斯米姆作支撑，内里再套一层两英寸的内壳。巨大船壳中那一方小小的天地——不足一千立方英尺的空间——便是控制室兼住舱。

===Original===
It was held now on great cosmium springs, but Arcot was not by any means through. One man must do all the work, for one brain must design it, and though he received the constant advice and help of Morey and the others, it was his brain that pictured the thing that was built.

At last the hull was completed. A single, glistening tube, of enormous bulk, a mile in length, a thousand feet in diameter. Yet nearly all of that great bulk would be used immediately. Some room would be left for additional apparatus they might care to install. Spare parts they did not have to carry—they could make their own from the energy abounding in space.

The enormous, shining hull was a thing of beauty through stark grandeur now, but obviously incomplete. The ray projectors were not mounted, but they were to be ray projectors of a type never before possible. Space is the transmitter of all rays, and it is in space that those energy forms exist. Arcot had merely to transfer the enormously high energy level of the space-curvature to any form of energy he wanted, and now, with the complete statistics on it, he was able to do that directly. No tubes, no generators, only fields that changed the energy already there—the immeasurable energy available!

===Chinese===
控制舱此刻由粗大的科斯米姆弹簧托悬着，但阿科特的工程还远远没有完。所有活儿都必须由一个人来干，因为必须由一个头脑来设计一切；尽管莫里等人不断献策出力，但将建成之物构想出来的，始终是他的头脑。

船壳终于完工。一支浑然一体的闪闪长管，体积惊人：长一英里，直径一千英尺。而这庞大的体积几乎立刻就要全部派上用场，只余少许空间，留给日后想要加装的装置。备件无须携带——他们可以从太空中取之不尽的能量自行制造。

那巨大而辉煌的船壳此刻已凭其雄浑气势自成一种壮美，只是显然尚未完工。射线投射器还未安装，但那将是前所未有的新型射线投射器。空间是一切射线的传导者，那些能量形态本就存在于空间之中。阿科特只需把空间曲率那极高的能量级转换成他想要的任何能量形式——如今，掌握了它的完备数据，他已能直接做到。不要功率管，不要发电机，只要几个场，去改变本就存在于那里的能量——那无可估量、取用不竭的能量！

===Original===
The next period of work he started the space distortion apparatus. That must go at the exact center of the ship. One tremendous coil, big enough for the *Ancient Mariner* to lie in easily! Minutes, and flying thoughts had made it—then came thousands of the individual coils, by thinking of one, and picturing it many times! In ranks, rows, and columns they were piled into a great block, for power must be stored for use of this tremendous machine, while in the artificial space when its normal power was not available, and that power source must be tremendous.

Then the time apparatus, and after that the driving apparatus. Not the molecular drive now, but an attraction ray focused on their own ship, with projectors scattered about the ship that it might move effortlessly in every direction. And provision was made for a force-drive by means of artificial matter, planes of it pushing the ship where it was wanted. But with the attraction-drive they would be able to land safely, without fear of being crushed by their own weight on Thett, for all its enormous gravity.

The control was now suspended finally, with a series of attraction drives about it, locking it immovably in place, while smaller attraction devices stimulated gravity for the occupants.

===Chinese===
接下来的一段工作，他着手建造空间畸变装置。它必须安放在船的正中心。一具巨大的线圈，大得足以让老水手号舒舒服服地卧在其中！几分钟，飞驰的思绪便将它造了出来——随后是数以千计的单个线圈：想着一只，反复构画，便可成批显现！它们成排、成列、成行地堆成一大块，因为这台庞然大物必须储备功率，以备常规功率不可得之时在人造空间中驱动，而那功率源必须极为可观。

接着是时间装置，再往后是推进装置。如今用的不再是分子推进装置，而是聚焦于自身飞船的引力射线，投射器遍布全船，使它能毫不费力地向任何方向移动。此外还预备了一套力推进（force-drive），以人工物质作平面，把船推往想去的地方。不过有了引力推进，他们便能安全降落，纵然塞特星引力大得惊人，也不必担心被自己的体重压垮。

控制舱最终悬装到位，四周环绕着一系列引力推进器，将它牢牢锁死在原位，另有几台较小的引力装置为舱内人员模拟出重力。

===Original===
Then finally the main apparatus—the power plant—was installed. The enormous coils which handled, or better, caused space to handle as they directed, powers so great that whole suns could be blasted instantaneously, were put in place, and the field generators that would make and direct their rays, their ray screen if need be, and handle their artificial matter. Everything was installed, and all but a rather small space was occupied.

It had been six weeks of continuous work for them, for the mind of each was aiding in this work, indirectly or directly, and it neared completion now.

“But, we need one more thing, Arcot. That could never land on any planet smaller than Jupiter. What is its mass?” suggested Morey.

“Don’t know, I’m sure, but it is of the order of a billion tons. I know you are right. What are we going to do?”

===Chinese===
最后，主装置——动力装置——安装完毕。那些巨大的线圈所处理的——或者更确切地说，是循着他们的意志驱使空间去处理的——伟力，足以在一瞬间炸毁整颗恒星；它们一一就位，一同就位的还有制造并导引各种射线的场发电机、必要时可张开的射线屏障，以及操控人工物质的装置。一切安装完毕，除了很小一块空处，全船已被占满。

对他们而言，这已是连续不断的六个星期，因为每个人的心智都在直接或间接地为这项工程出力。如今，它已接近完成。

「不过，阿科特，咱们还缺一样东西。这家伙可落在不了任何比木星小的行星上。它的质量有多大？」莫里提醒道。

「这我说不好，不过量级该在十亿吨上下。我知道你说得对。咱们怎么办？」

===Original===
“Put on a tender.”

“Why not the *Ancient Mariner*?” asked Wade.

“It isn’t fitting. It was designed for individual use anyway,” replied Morey. “I suggest something more like this on a small scale. We won’t have much work on that, merely think of every detail of the big ship on a small scale, with the exception of the control cube furnishings. Instead of the numerous decks, swimming pool and so forth, have a large, single room.”

“Good enough,” replied Arcot.

===Chinese===
「给它配一艘交通艇。」

「为什么不用老水手号？」韦德问。

「它不合适。再说它本来就是为个别用途设计的。」莫里答道，「我建议照这个样子造一艘缩小的。那活儿不多，只要把大船的每个细节按比例想小就行，控制舱的陈设除外。那么多层甲板、游泳池等等统统不要，只留一间大屋子。」

「就这么办。」阿科特答道。

===Original===
As if by magic, a machine appeared, a “small” machine of two-hundred-foot length, modified slightly in some parts, its bottom flattened, and equipped with an attractor anchor. Then they were ready.

“We will leave the *Mariner* here, and get it later. This apparatus won’t be needed any longer, and we don’t want the enemy to get it. Our trial trip will be a fight!” called Arcot as he leaped from his seat. The mass of the giant ship pulled him, and he fell slowly toward it.

Into its open port he flew, the others behind him, their suits still on. The door shut behind them as Arcot, at the controls, closed it. As yet they had not released the air supplies. It was airless.

Now the hiss of air, and the quickening of heat crept through it. The water in the tanks thawed as the heat came, soaking through from the great heaters. In minutes the air and heat were normal throughout the great bulk. There was air in power compartments, though no one was expected to go there, for the control room alone need be occupied; vision-screens here viewed every part of the ship, and all about it.

===Chinese===
仿佛变魔术一般，一台机器应念而生——一台“小小的”机器，长两百英尺，几处细节略作修改，底部压平，装上了引力锚。至此，他们万事俱备。

「老水手号就留在这儿，日后再来取。这台装置再也用不着了，可不能落到敌人手里。咱们的试航，就打一仗吧！」阿科特一边喊，一边从座椅上纵身跃起。巨船那庞大的质量拽住了他，他缓缓向它飘落。

他飞进敞开的舱口，其他人跟在后面，太空服还没有脱。阿科特在控制位上操纵横门，门在他们身后关上。此刻空气储备尚未释放，舱内还没有空气。

接着，空气的嘶嘶声和渐渐回暖的热气在船内弥漫开来。热力从巨大的加热器中渗透过来，水箱里冻住的水随之消融。几分钟之内，这个庞然大物里里外外的空气与温度都恢复了正常。动力舱里也充了空气，虽然预计不会有人进去，因为只需控制室有人值守；在这里凭观测眼便可看到全船每个部分，连同它四周的一切。

===Original===
The eyes of the new ship were set in recesses of the tremendously strong cosmium wall, and over them, protecting them, was an infinitely thin, but infinitely strong wall of artificial matter, permanently maintained. It was opaque to all forms of radiation known from the longest Hertzian to the shortest cosmics, save for the very narrow band of visible light. Whether this protection would stop the Thessian beam that was so deadly to lux and relux was not, of course, known. But Arcot hoped it would, and, if that beam was radiant energy, or material particles, it would.

“We’ll destroy our station here now, and leave the *Ancient Mariner* where it is. Of course we are a long way out of the orbit this planetoid followed, due to the effect of the time apparatus, but we can note where it is, and we’ll be able to find it when we want it,” said Arcot, seated at the great control board now. There were no buttons now, or visible controls; all was mental.

A tiny sphere of artificial matter formed, and shot toward the control board of the time machine outside. It depressed the main switch, and space about them shifted, twisted, and returned to normal. The time apparatus was off for the first time in six weeks.

“Can’t fuse that, and we can’t crush it. It’s made of cosmium, and trying to crush it against the rock would just drive it into it. We’ll see what we can do though,” muttered Arcot. A plane of artificial matter formed just beneath it, and sheared it from its bed on the planetoid, cutting through the heavy cosmium anchors. The framework lifted, and the apparatus with it. A series of planes, a gigantic honeycomb formed, and the apparatus was cut across again and again, till only small fragments were left of it. Then these were rolled into a ball, and crushed by a sphere of artificial matter beyond all repair. The enemy would never learn their secret.

===Chinese===
新船的观测眼嵌在极其坚固的科斯米姆舱壁的凹龛里，上面罩着一层无限之薄却又无限之强的人工物质护壁，恒久维持不灭。除了可见光那极窄的一段，从最长的赫兹电波到最短的宇宙射线，一切已知辐射都无法穿透它。这层防护能否挡住塞特人那种对卢克斯和雷卢克斯都致命的光束，当然无人知晓。但阿科特希望它能——倘若那光束是辐射能，或是实物粒子，它就挡得住。

「咱们现在就毁掉在这里的工场，老水手号留在原处。当然，由于时间装置的作用，咱们已经远远偏离了这颗小行星原本的轨道，不过记下它的位置，需要时总能找到它。」阿科特说。此刻他已在大控制台前就座。如今再没有按钮，再没有任何可见的操控——一切都凭心智。

一颗小小的物质之球凭空凝成，疾射向外面那台时间装置的控制台，按下主开关。他们四周的空间一阵挪移、扭转，随即恢复如常。六个星期以来，时间装置头一回关闭了。

「熔化不掉，也压不碎。它是科斯米姆造的，想借岩石来压它，只会把它压进岩石里去。不过总会有办法。」阿科特喃喃道。一张人工物质平面恰在装置下方成形，切断沉重的科斯米姆锚栓，把它从行星碎块上的基座里整个切了下来。构架连同装置一起被抬起。一张张平面组成巨大的蜂巢格栅，将装置横一刀竖一刀地切了个遍，最后只剩一些小块碎片。这些碎片又被滚作一团，由一颗人工物质之球碾了个粉碎，再也无法复原。敌人休想探知他们的秘密。

===Original===
A huge cylinder of artificial matter cut a great gouge from the plane that was left where the apparatus had been, and a clamp of the same material picked up the *Ancient Mariner*, deposited it there, then covered it with rubble and broken rock. A cosmic flashed on the rock for an instant, and it was glowing, incandescent lava. The *Ancient Mariner* was buried under a hundred feet of rapidly solidifying rock, but rock which could be fused away from its infusible walls when the time came.

“We’re ready to go now—get to work with the radio, Morey, when we get to Earth.”

The gravity seemed normal here as they walked about, no accelerations affected them as the ship darted forward, for all its inconceivably great mass, like an arrow, then flashed forward under time control. The sun was far distant now, for six weeks they had been traveling with the section of Eros under time control. But with their tremendous time control plant, and the space control, they reached the solar system in very little time.

It seemed impossible to them that that battle could still be waging, but it was. The ships of Earth and Venus, battling now as a last, hopeless stand, over Chicago, were attempting to stop the press of a great Thessian fleet. Thin, long Negrian, or Sirian ships had joined them in the hour of Earth time that the men had been working. Still, despite the reinforcements, they were falling back.

===Chinese===
一根巨大的人工物质圆柱，在装置原址留下的平面上剜出一道大坑；一只同材质的巨钳夹起老水手号，放进坑里，再用碎石乱岩掩埋。一道宇宙射线在岩石上一闪而过，岩石顿时化作炽热白亮的熔岩。老水手号被埋进一百英尺厚、正在迅速凝固的岩石之下——不过待到需要之时，这些岩石尽可以从它那不可熔蚀的船壳上熔开。

「咱们准备好出发了——莫里，到了地球就马上用无线电忙起来。」

他们在船内四处走动，只觉重力一如平常；飞船纵有那不可思议的巨大质量，却如离弦之箭般疾冲向前，不受任何加速度的影响，随即又在时间控制之下向前闪逝。太阳已远远退在身后，六个星期以来，他们一直载着那块爱神星残骸，在时间控制下兼程赶路。但凭着他们那强大的时间控制机组和空间控制装置，他们没过多久便抵达了太阳系。

在他们想来，那场战斗竟还在进行，实在难以置信——但它确实还在打着。地球与金星的战舰正在芝加哥上空作最后的绝望阻击，试图挡住塞特庞大舰队的进逼。在他们劳作的这地球时间的一个钟头里，细长的涅格里安战舰——亦即天狼星人的战舰——也加入了他们的行列。然而纵有援兵，他们仍在节节败退。

## Endnotes / 尾注

===Original===
1. *Islands of Space*. ↩︎
2. *The Black Star Passes*. ↩︎

===Chinese===
1. 《空间之岛》。↩︎
2. 《黑星掠过》。↩︎

## XVI / 重返家园

===Original===
They were getting very near their own time, Arcot felt. Indeed, they must already exist on Earth. "One thing that puzzles me," he commented, "is what would happen if we were to go down now, and see ourselves."

"Either we can't or we don't want to do it," pointed out Morey, "because we didn't."

"I think the answer is that nothing can exist two times at the same time-rate," said Arcot. "As long as we were in a different time-rate we could exist at two times. When we tried to exist simultaneously, we could not, and we were forced to slip through time to a time wherein we either did not exist or wherein we had not yet been. Since we were nearer the time when we last existed in normal time, than we were to the time of our birth, we went to the time we left. I suspect that we will find we have just left Earth. Shall we investigate?"

===Chinese===
阿科特感到，他们已经十分接近自己原有的时代了。说真的，此刻的地球上必定已经存在着他们自己。“有一件事一直让我犯嘀咕，”他说道，“要是我们现在就降下去，迎面撞见了我们自己，那会发生什么？”

“要么是我们做不到，要么是我们根本不愿那么做，”莫里指出，“因为事实上，我们没有那么做。”

“我想答案在于：任何东西都无法以同一时间速率（time-rate）存在于两个时刻，”阿科特说，“只要我们处于不同的时间速率，便能同时存在于两个时间。可一旦试图同时存在，便绝无可能，于是我们被迫在时间中滑移，滑进一个我们或是并不存在、或是尚未出世的时代。比起我们出生的时刻，我们距离上次存在于正常时间的那一时刻更近，所以我们回到了临行时的那个时间。我猜想，我们会发现自己才刚离开地球不久。要不要去查证一番？”

===Original===
"Absolutely, Arcot, and here's hoping we didn't overshoot the mark by much." As Morey intimated, had they gone much beyond the time they left Earth, they might find conditions very serious, indeed. But now they went at once toward Earth on the time control. As they neared, they looked anxiously for signs of the invasion. Arcot spotted the only evident signs, however; two large spheres, tiny points in appearance on the telectroscope screen, were circling Earth, one at about 1,000 miles, moving from east to west, the other about 1,200 miles moving from north to south.

"It seems the enemy have retreated to space to do their fighting. I wonder how long we were away."

As they swept down at a speed greater than light, they were invisible till Arcot slowed down near the atmosphere. Instantly half a dozen fast ships darted toward them, but the ship was very evidently unlike the Thessian ships, and no attack was made. First the occupants would have an opportunity to prove their friendliness.

===Chinese===
“当然要去，阿科特，但愿我们没有把目标冲过了头。”正如莫里所暗示的，倘若他们在时间上越过离开地球的时刻太远，眼下的局面恐怕就着实严重了。此刻，他们立即通过时间控制朝地球进发。临近时，他们焦急地搜寻着入侵的迹象。然而，唯一显见的迹象还是由阿科特发现的：两颗硕大的球体——在电视望远镜（telectroscope）的屏幕上只是两个微小的光点——正环绕地球运行，一颗在约 1,000 英里的高度上自东向西，另一颗约 1,200 英里，自北向南。

“看样子，敌人是撤到太空里去交战了。不知道我们离开了多久。”

他们以超光速俯冲而下，直到阿科特在大气层附近减速之前，始终无人能窥见他们的踪影。转眼间，六艘快船便朝他们疾射而来，但这艘飞船显然与塞特人（Thessian）的船舰毫无相似之处，对方并未发动攻击——总得先让船上的人有机会证明自己心怀善意。

===Original===
"Terrestrians Arcot, Morey and Wade reporting back from exploration in space, with two friends. All have been on Earth with us previously," said Arcot into the radio vision apparatus.

"Very well, Dr. Arcot. You are going to New York or Vermont?" asked the Patrol commander.

"Vermont."

"Yes, Sir. I'll see that you aren't stopped again."

And, thanks to the message thus sent ahead, they were not, and in less than half an hour they landed once more in Vermont, on the field from which they had started.

===Chinese===
“地球人（Terrestrian）阿科特、莫里与韦德，自太空探索归来，随行还有两位朋友。所有人都曾随我们到过地球。”阿科特对着无线电电视装置（radio vision apparatus）说道。

“很好，阿科特博士。您是去纽约，还是去佛蒙特？”巡逻队长问道。

“佛蒙特。”

“是，长官。我会关照下去，不会再有人拦您。”

多亏这通报信的电文先行送到，一路上果然再无人拦阻；不到半小时，他们便再度降落在佛蒙特，落在他们当初出发的那片场地上。

===Original===
The group of scientists who had been here on their last call had gone, which seemed natural enough to them, who had been working for three months in the interval of their trip, but to Dr. Arcot senior, as he saw them, it was a misfortune.

"Now I never will get straight all you'll have ready, and I didn't expect you back till next week. The men have all gone back to their laboratories, since that permits of better work on the part of each, but we can call them here in half an hour. I'm sure they'll want to come. What did you learn, Son, or haven't you done any calculating on your data as yet?"

"We learned plenty, and I feel quite sure that a hint of what we have would bring all those learning-hounds around us pretty quickly, Dad," laughed Arcot junior, "and believe it or not, we've been calculating on this stuff for three months since we left yesterday!"

"What!"

===Chinese===
上次他们来访时在此工作的那批科学家早已散去。对这三位旅行者来说——在旅程的间隙里，他们已经干了三个月的活——这本是再自然不过的事；可在老阿科特博士眼里，见此情景却不免暗呼不巧。

“这下我可永远没法把你们备下的东西一一弄清了。我原以为你们要到下周才回来呢。人都回各自的实验室去了，那样各人手头的工作能做得更好，不过半小时之内就能把他们叫到这里来。我相信他们一定愿意来。你们此行学到了什么，孩子？还是说，那些数据你们还没来得及推算？”

“我们学到的东西可不少。老爸，我敢肯定，只要透出一点口风，那些闻风而来的‘学问猎犬’很快就会把我们团团围住，”小阿科特大笑着说，“信不信由你——我们昨天才动身，可这批东西，我们已经足足算了三个月！”

“什么！”

===Original===
"Yes, it's true! We were on our time field, and turned on the space control—and a Thessian ship picked that moment to run into us. We cut the ship in half as neatly as you please, but it threw us eighty thousand years into the past. We have been coasting through time on retarded rate while Earth caught up with itself, so to speak. In the meantime—three months in a day!

"But don't call those men. Let them come to the appointment, while we do some work, and we have plenty of work to do, I assure you. We have a list of things to order from the standard supply houses, and I think you better get them for us, Dad." Arcot's manner became serious now. "We haven't gotten our Government Expense Research Cards yet, and you have. Order the stuff, and get it out here, while we get ready for it. Honestly, I believe that a few ships such as this apparatus will permit, will be enough in themselves to do the job. It really is a pity that the other men didn't have the opportunity we had for crowding much work into little time!

"But then, I wouldn't want to take that road to concentration again myself!

"Have the enemy amused you in my absence? Come on, let's sit down in the house instead of standing here in the sun."

===Chinese===
“千真万确！我们当时正在时间场（time-field）里，刚打开空间控制装置（space control）——偏偏就在那个当口，一艘塞特人的飞船撞了上来。我们把它干净利落地剖成了两半，可这一下却把我们抛到了八万年前的过去。此后我们便以滞缓的时间速率在时间之流中一路滑行，不妨说是等着地球赶上它自己的进度。这期间——一天顶三个月！

“不过先别把人叫来。就让他们到约定时再来吧，这工夫我们还有些活要干，而且我向你保证，活儿还多得很。我们有一份清单，要从标准器材行订一批货，老爸，我看还是由您去办妥当。”阿科特的神情此刻严肃起来。“我们的政府公费研究卡（Government Expense Research Card）还没批下来，您手里有。请您把材料订好送到这里来，我们这边同步着手准备。说真的，我相信只要有几艘这种装置所能造出的飞船，单凭它们本身就足以把这件事办成。真可惜，其他人没能像我们这样，把这么多工作塞进这么短的时间里！

“不过话说回来，要我再来一回那种赶工的门道，我可真不愿意！

“我不在的这阵子，敌人可曾给你们添了些乐子？来吧，进屋里坐下说，别站在太阳底下晒着。”

===Original===
They started toward the house, as Arcot senior explained what had happened in the short time they had been away.

"There is a friend of yours here, whom you haven't seen in some time, Son. He came with some allies."

As they entered the house, they could hear the boards creak under some heavy weight that moved across the floor, soundlessly and light of motion in itself. A shadow fell across the hall floor, and in the doorway a tremendously powerfully-built figure stood.

He seemed to overflow the doorway, nearly six and a half feet tall, and fully as wide as the door. His rugged, bronzed face was smiling pleasantly, and his deep-set eyes seemed to flash; a living force flowed from them.

===Chinese===
他们朝屋子走去，老阿科特一路讲起他们离开这短短时间里发生的事。

“孩子，这儿有你的一位朋友，你们有阵子没见了。他还是带着几位盟友一道来的。”

进屋时，他们听见地板在一团沉重之物的碾压下吱嘎作响——那庞然之物移过地板，本身却悄然无声、步履轻捷。一道影子横投在厅堂的地板上，门口立着一个极其魁梧的身影。

他几乎把整个门框都占满了，身高近六英尺半，体宽几乎与门洞相当。他那张粗犷的古铜色面孔带着愉快的笑容，深陷的双眼仿佛闪着光，一股生命的活力正从眼中奔涌而出。

===Original===
"Torlos! By the Nine Planets! Torlos of Nansal! Say, I didn't expect you here, and I will not put my hand in that meatgrinder of yours," grinned Arcot happily, as Torlos stretched forth a friendly, but quite too powerful hand.

Torlos of Nansal, that planet Arcot had discovered on his first voyage across space, far in another Island of Space, another Island Universe, was not constructed as are human beings of Earth, nor of Venus, Talso, or Ortol, but most nearly resembled, save in size, the Thessians. Their framework, instead of being stone, as is ours, was iron, their bones were pure metallic iron, far stronger than bone. On these far stronger bones were great muscles of an entirely different sort, a muscle that used heat of the body as its fuel, a muscle that was utterly tireless, and unbelievably powerful. Not a chemical engine, but a molecular motion engine, it had no chemical fatigue-products that would tire it, and needed only the constant heat supply the body sucked from the air to work indefinitely. Unlimited by waste-carrying considerations, the strength was enormous.

It was one of the commercial space freighters plying between Nansal, Sator, Earth and Venus that had brought the news of this war to him, Torlos explained, and he, as the new Trade Coordinator and Fourth of the Four who now ruled Nansal, had suggested that they go to the aid of the man who had so aided them in their great war with Sator. It was Arcot's gift of the secret of the molecular ray and the molecular ship that had enabled them to overcome their enemy of centuries, and force upon them an unwelcome peace.

Now, with a fleet of fifty interstellar, or better, intergalactic battleships, Nansal was coming to Earth's aid.

===Chinese===
“托洛斯（Torlos）！凭九大行星起誓！南萨尔（Nansal）的托洛斯！哎呀，真没想到会在这儿见着你。我可不要把手塞进你那台绞肉机里。”托洛斯正伸出一只友善却威力过人的大手，阿科特笑逐颜开地说道。

南萨尔的托洛斯——南萨尔是阿科特首次横越太空航行时发现的行星，远在另一个空间之岛（Islands of Space）、另一个岛宇宙之中——他的身体构造与地球人不同，与金星人（Venerian）、塔尔索（Talso）人或奥托尔（Ortol）人也不同；除去体型，反倒与塞特人最为相近。他们的骨架不像我们的是石质，而是铁质——骨骼是纯金属铁，远比骨质坚固。在这远为坚固的骨架上，生着一种截然不同的强健肌肉：这种肌肉以体温为燃料，不知疲倦，力大得难以置信。那不是化学引擎，而是分子运动引擎（molecular motion engine），体内没有会使它疲乏的化学疲劳产物，只需不断从空气中吸取热量供给身体，便能永续工作。由于无须分神运送废物，它的力量极为惊人。

托洛斯解释说，是一艘往返于南萨尔、萨托（Sator）、地球与金星之间的商业太空货船，把这场战争的消息带给了他；而他，作为新任贸易协调官（Trade Coordinator）、如今统治南萨尔的四人团之第四人，提议前来援助那位在他们与萨托的大战中鼎力相助过他们的人。正是阿科特慷慨相赠的分子射线（molecular ray）与分子飞船（molecular ship）之秘，使他们得以战胜绵延数百年的宿敌，迫使对方接受了一段并不情愿的和平。

如今，南萨尔正带着一支五十艘恒星际——不，更确切地说，星系际——战舰组成的舰队，赶来援助地球。

===Original===
The battleships were now on patrol with all of Earth's and Venus' fleet. But the Nansalian ships were all equipped with the enormously rapid space distortion system of travel, of course, and were a shock troop in the patrol. The Terrestrian and Venerian patrols were not so equipped in full.

"And Arcot, from what I have learned from your father, it seems that I can be of real assistance," finished Torlos.

"But now, I think, I should know what the enemy has done. I see they built some forts."

===Chinese===
这些战舰如今正与地球和金星的全部舰队一道执行巡逻任务。不过，南萨尔人（Nansalian）的舰船全都装备了无比迅捷的空间畸变航行系统，自然是巡逻编队中的一支突击力量；地球与金星的巡逻舰队尚未全面装备这类系统。

“还有，阿科特，从你父亲那里听闻的情形来看，我似乎真能帮上大忙。”托洛斯最后说道。

“不过现在，我想我该听听敌人干了些什么。看得出来，他们修筑了一些要塞。”

===Original===
"Yes," replied Arcot senior, "they did. They decided that the system used on the forts of North and South poles was too effective. They moved to space, and cut off slices of Luna, pulled it over on their molecular rays, and used some of the most magnificent apparatus you ever dreamed of. I have just started working on the mathematics of it.

"We sent out a fleet to do some investigating, but they attacked, and stopped work in the meantime. Whatever the ray is that can destroy matter at a distance, they are afraid that we could find its secret too easily, and block it, for they don't think it is a weapon, and it is evidently slow in action."

"Then it isn't what I thought it was," muttered Arcot.

"What did you think it was?" asked his father.

"Er—tell you later. Go on with the account."

===Chinese===
“是的，”老阿科特答道，“他们确实修了。他们认定南北两极要塞上用的那套体系太过有效，于是转移到了太空里，从月球上切下一片片岩块，用分子射线拖拽过来，装上了你做梦也想不到的精良设备。我最近刚开始推演其中的数学。

“我们派出一支舰队前去侦察，可他们随即来攻，调查工作也就此中断。至于那种能在远处摧毁物质的射线究竟是什么，他们是生怕我们太容易窥破其中奥秘、设法将其封死；因为他们并不把那东西看作武器，而且它的作用显然相当迟缓。”

“这么说，它并不是我以为的那种东西。”阿科特低声咕哝。

“你以为它是什么？”父亲问。
