“呃——待会儿再告诉您。接着讲吧。”

===Original===
"Well, to continue. We have not been idle. Following your suggestion, we built up a large ray screen apparatus, in fact, several of them, and carried them in ships to different parts of the world. Also some of the planets, lest they start dropping worlds on us. They are already in operation, sending their defensive waves against the Heaviside layer. Radio is poor, over any distance, and we can't call Venus from inside the layer now. However, we tested the protection, and it works—far more efficiently than we calculated, due to the amazing conductivity of the layer.

"If they intend to attack in that way, I suspect that it will be soon, for they are ready now, as we discovered. An attack on their fort was met with a ray screen from the fort.

"They fight with a wild viciousness now. They won't let a ship get near them. They destroy everything on sight. They seem tremendously afraid of that apparatus of yours. Too bad we had no more."

"We will have—if you will let me get to work."

===Chinese===
“好吧，接着说。我们也没有闲着。遵照你的建议，我们造起了大型射线屏障（ray screen）装置——实际上造了好几套——用船运往世界各地，有几颗行星上也安放了，以防他们朝我们砸下整个星球。这些装置已经在运转，正把防御波射向海维赛德层（Heaviside layer）。如今无线电在任何距离上信号都很糟糕，从层内已经无法呼叫金星。不过我们测试过这层防护，它确实管用——由于海维赛德层惊人的导电性，其效率远远超出我们的计算。

“如果他们打算从这条路子进攻，我料想时日不会太远，因为我们已经发现，他们如今准备就绪。曾有一次对他们要塞的进攻，便被要塞射出的射线屏障挡了回去。

“如今他们打起仗来狂暴而凶残。他们不许任何飞船靠近，一见来物便加摧毁。他们似乎极怕你那套装置。可惜我们手头没有更多的了。”

“我们会有的——只要您让我开工。”

===Original===
They went to the ship, and entered it. Arcot senior did not follow, but the others waited, while the ship left Earth once more, and floated in space. Immediately they went into the time-field.

They worked steadily, sleeping when necessary, and the giant strength of Torlos was frequently as great an asset as his indefatigable work. He was learning rapidly, and was able to do a great deal of the work without direction. He was not a scientist, and the thing was new to him, but his position as one of the best of the secret intelligence force of Nansal had proven his brains, and he did his share.

The others, scientists all, found the operations difficult, for work had been allotted to each according to his utmost capabilities.

It was still nearly a week of their time before the apparatus was completed to the extent possible, less than a minute of normal time passing.

Finally the unassembled, but completed apparatus, was carried to the laboratory of the cottage, and word was sent to all the men of Earth that Arcot was going to give a demonstration of the apparatus he hoped would save them. The scientists from all over Earth and Venus were interested, and those of Earth came, for there was no time for the men of Venus to arrive to inspect the results.

===Chinese===
他们走向飞船，进舱而去。老阿科特没有跟来，其余人留在舱内，飞船再度离开地球，悬停在太空之中。他们随即进入了时间场。

他们马不停蹄地工作，倦极才眠；托洛斯巨人的神力，时常同他那不知疲倦的劳作一样成为一大助力。他学得极快，许多活儿无须指点便能独立完成。他并非科学家，这些事对他也是全新的，但他身为南萨尔密谍部队中最出色的成员之一，头脑早已得到证明，也尽了自己的一份力。

其余几人虽全是科学家，却觉得这些操作艰难吃力，因为每人的工作量都是按各自能力的极限来分配的。

等到装置尽其可能地宣告完工，他们在场时间里已过了将近一周，而正常时间却只流逝了不到一分钟。

最后，这套尚未组装却已全部完工的装置被搬进了小屋的实验室，同时向全地球的人们传出一个消息：阿科特将公开演示一种他希望足以拯救众人的装置。全地球和金星的科学家无不关注；地球的科学家们赶来赴会，金星的人则已来不及赶到现场查验成果。

## X / 改进与计算

===Original===

“It is still incredible. But you have done it. It is certainly successful!” said the Talsonian scientist with conviction.
Arcot shook his head. “Far from it—we have not realized a thousandth part of the tremendous possibilities of this invention. We must work and calculate and then invent.
“Think of the possibilities as a shield—naturally if we can make the matter we should be able to control its properties in any way we like. We should be able to make it opaque, transparent, or any color.” Arcot was speaking to Morey now. “Do you remember, when we were caught in that cosmic ray field in space when we first left this universe, that I said that I had an idea for energy so vast that it would be impossible to describe its awful power?1 I mentioned that I would attempt to liberate it if ever there was need? The need exists. I want to find that secret.”
Stel Felso Theu was looking out through the window at a group of men excitedly beckoning. He called the attention of the others to them, and himself went out. Arcot and Wade joined him in a moment.

===Chinese===

“这仍然令人难以置信。可你们毕竟做成了。这无疑是成功了！”那位塔尔索人（Talsonian）科学家语气笃定地说。
阿科特（Arcot）摇了摇头。“差得远呢——这项发明的巨大可能性，我们连千分之一都还没有实现。我们必须钻研、计算，然后再去发明。
“想想把它用作护盾的前景——既然能造出这种物质，我们自然也能随心所欲地支配它的种种性质。我们可以叫它不透明、透明，或者任意一种颜色。”阿科特此时是对莫里（Morey）说的。“还记得吗，当初我们头一回离开这个宇宙、被太空中那片宇宙射线（cosmic ray）场困住的时候，我曾说，我想到了一种能量，其威力之可畏，根本无法形容？¹我还说过，一旦有需要，我就试着把它释放出来。如今，需要来了。我要找出那个秘密。”
斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤（Stel Felso Theu）正望着窗外一群兴奋招手的人。他唤起众人注意，自己先走了出去。阿科特和韦德（Wade）随即跟了上去。

===Original===

“They tell me that Fellsheh, well to the poleward of here has used four of its eight shots. They are still being attacked,” explained the Talsonian gravely.
“Well, get in,” snapped Arcot as he ran back to the ship. Stel Felso hastily followed, and the *Ancient Mariner* shot into the air, and darted away, poleward, to the Talsonian’s directions. The ground fled behind them at a speed that made the scientist grip the handrail with a tenseness that showed his nervousness.
As they approached, a tremendous concussion and a great gout of light in the sky informed them of the early demise of several Thessians. But a real fleet was clustered about the city. Arcot approached low, and was able to get quite close before detection. His ray screen was up and Morey had charged the artificial matter apparatus, small as it was, for operation. He created a ball of substance outside the *Ancient Mariner*, and thrust it toward the nearest Thessian, just as a molecular hit the *Ancient Mariner*’s ray screen.
The artificial matter instantly exploded with terrific violence, slightly denting the tremendously strong lux metal walls. The pressure of the light was so great that the inner relux walls were dented inward. The ground below was suddenly, instantaneously fused.

===Chinese===

“他们告诉我，偏极方向离此地很远的费尔谢（Fellsheh），八发防御射击已经用掉了四发。他们还在挨打。”那位塔尔索人神色凝重地解释道。
“好，快上船。”阿科特一边喝令，一边奔回飞船。斯特尔·费尔索急忙跟上；老水手号（Ancient Mariner）冲天而起，朝着塔尔索人所指的方向，向极地疾驰而去。大地在身后飞退，速度快得让那位科学家紧紧攥住扶手，紧张之情显露无遗。
还在途中，天空中一声惊天动地的爆震和一大团迸射的光，便告诉他们：已有几名塞特人（Thessian）提前毙命。但一支真正的舰队正围城集结。阿科特压低高度潜近，直到相当近了才被察觉。他的射线护盾（ray screen）已经升起，莫里也早已给那台人工物质（artificial matter）装置——尽管小得很——充能待发。就在一发分子射线击中老水手号的射线护盾之际，他在船外造出一团物质，直朝最近的塞特战舰掷去。
人工物质顷刻间猛烈炸开，威力之大，把无比坚固的卢克斯（lux）金属壁都震得微微凹陷。光的压力如此之巨，连内层的雷卢克斯（relux）壁都被压得向内瘪去。下方的地面刹那间熔成一片。

===Original===

“Lord—they won’t pass a ray screen, obviously,” Morey muttered, picking himself from where he had fallen.
“Hey—easy there. You blinked off the ray screen, and our relux is seriously weakened,” called Arcot, a note of worry in his voice.
“No artificial matter with the ray screen up. I’ll use the magnet,” called Morey.
He quickly shut off the apparatus, and went to the huge magnet control. The power room was crowded, and now that the battle was raging in truth, with three ships attacking simultaneously, even the enormous power capacity of the ship’s generators was not sufficient, and the storage coils had been thrown into the operation. Morey looked at the instruments a moment. They were all up to capacity, save the ammeter from the coils. That wasn’t registering yet. Suddenly it flicked, and the other instrument dropped to zero. They were in artificial space.

===Chinese===

“天哪——很显然，人工物质是穿不过射线护盾的。”莫里从摔倒的地方爬起身，低声咕哝。
“喂——当心点儿。你把射线护盾闪灭了，我们的雷卢克斯壁已经严重受损。”阿科特喊道，话音里带着一丝忧虑。
“护盾升着，人工物质就用不上。我改用磁力装置。”莫里喊道。
他迅速关掉那台装置，走向巨型磁铁的操纵台。动力舱里早已满满当当；既然战斗已真刀真枪地打响，三艘飞船同时发起攻击，就连飞船发电机那惊人的功率也难以为继，储能线圈（storage coils）已被投入运行。莫里端详了片刻仪表。所有读数都已顶到满负荷，唯有线圈那一路的电流表还没有示数。突然，它猛地一跳，另一台仪表随之落到零点。他们已身处人造空间（artificial space）。

===Original===

“Come here, will you, Morey,” called Arcot. In a moment Morey joined his much worried friend.
“That artificial matter control won’t work through ray screens. The Thessians never had to protect against moleculars here, and didn’t have them up—hence the destruction wrought. We can’t take our screen down, and we can’t use our most deadly weapon with it up. If we had a big outfit, we might throw a screen around the whole ship, and sail right in. But we haven’t.
“We can’t stand ten seconds against that fleet. I’m going to find their base, and make them yell for help.” Arcot snapped a tiny switch one notch further for the barest instant, then snapped it back. They were several millions miles from the planet. “Quicker,” he explained, “to simply follow those ships back home—go back in time.”
With the telectroscope, he took views at various distances, thus quickly tracing them back to their base at the pole of the planet. Instantly Arcot shot down, reaching the pole in less than a second, by careful maneuvering of the space device.

===Chinese===

“到这儿来一下，行吗，莫里。”阿科特喊道。莫里旋即来到他那位忧心忡忡的朋友身旁。
“人工物质这招穿不透射线护盾。塞特人在这一带从无须防备分子射线，所以压根没开护盾——这才遭了那样的破坏。我们的护盾不能撤；护盾开着，我们最致命的武器又用不上。要是有一套大装置，我们本可以给整艘船罩上一层护壳，径直杀进去。可我们没有。
“凭我们这点家底，在那支舰队面前撑不过十秒。我要去找他们的老巢，让他们嗷嗷喊救命。”阿科特把一个小开关向前扳过一挡，只在一瞬之间，随即又扳了回来。他们已距行星数百万英里。“这样更快，”他解释道，“只要顺着那些飞船回老家的路走就是——把时间往回拨。”
他用电视望远镜（telectroscope）在各个距离上取景，很快便回溯到了他们的基地——位于行星的极点。阿科特当即俯冲而下，凭着对空间装置的精细操控，不到一秒便抵达极地。

===Original===

A gigantic dome of polished relux rose from rocky, icy plains. The thing was nearly half a mile high, a mighty rounded roof that covered an area almost three-quarters of a mile in diameter. Titanic—that was the only word that described it. About it there was the peculiar shimmer of a molecular ray screen.
Morey darted to the power room and set his apparatus into operation. He created a ball of matter outside the ship and hurled it instantly at the fort. It exploded with a terrific concussion as it hit the wall of the ray screen. Almost instantly a second one followed. The concussion was terrifically violent, the ground about was fused, and the ray screen was opened for a moment. Arcot threw all his moleculars on the screen, as Morey sent bomb after bomb at it. The coils supplied the energy, cracked the rock beneath. Each energy release disrupted the ray-screen for a moment, and the concentrated fury of the molecular beams poured through the opened screen, and struck the relux behind. It glowed opalescent now in a spot twenty feet across. But the relux was tremendously thick. Thirty bombs Morey hurled, while they held their position without difficulty, pouring their bombs and rays at the fort.
Arcot threw the ship into space, moved, and reappeared suddenly nearly three hundred yards further on. A snap of the eyes, and he saw that the fleet was approaching now. He went again into space, and retreated. Discretion was the better part of valor. But his plan had worked.

===Chinese===

一座抛光雷卢克斯铸成的巨型穹顶，拔起于岩石嶙峋的冰原之上。这东西几乎有半英里高，浑圆的巨顶覆盖着直径近四分之三英里的一片地面。巨大无比——唯有这个词能形容它。穹顶四周，荡漾着一层分子射线护盾特有的微光。
莫里飞奔进动力舱，把他的装置开动起来。他在船外造出一团物质，立时朝要塞掷去。物质撞上射线护盾的壁障，随着一声惊天动地的爆震炸开。几乎同时，第二团跟着而至。爆震猛烈至极，四周的地面尽数熔化，射线护盾也洞开了一瞬。莫里一发接一发地把“炸弹”砸向护盾，阿科特则把全部分子射线倾泻在护盾上。能量由储能线圈供给，把底下的岩石都震裂了。每一次能量释放都会让射线护盾中断一瞬，分子射线束聚成一股狂澜，从洞开的护盾里灌进去，打在后面的雷卢克斯上。那里已有一块二十英尺宽的地方泛起乳白光晕。可雷卢克斯厚得惊人。莫里一连掷出三十枚炸弹，飞船则不费吹灰之力地稳住阵脚，把炸弹和射线不停泼向要塞。
阿科特把飞船没入空间，挪移一程，又在将近三百码开外倏然现身。他目光一扫，只见舰队已朝这边逼近。他再度遁入空间，退了出去。谨慎总比逞勇强。而他的计划已然奏效。

===Original===

He waited half an hour, and returned. From a distance the telectroscope told him that one lone ship was patrolling outside the fort. He moved toward it, creeping up behind the icy mountains. His magnetic beam reached out. The ship lurched and fell. The magnetic beam reached out toward the fort, from which a molecular ray had flashed already, tearing up the icy waste which had concealed him. The ray-screen stopped it, while again Morey turned the magnetic beam on—this time against the fort. The ray remained on! Arcot retreated hastily.
“They found the secret, all right. No use, Morey, come on up,” called the pilot. “They evidently put magnetic shielding around the apparatus. That means the magnetic beam is no good to us any more. They will certainly warn every other base, and have them install similar protection.”
“Why didn’t you try the magnetic ray on our first attack?” asked Zezdon Afthen.
“If it had worked, their sending apparatus would have been destroyed, and no message could have been sent to call their attackers off Fellsheh. By forcing them to recall their fleet I got results I couldn’t get by attacking the fleet,” Arcot said.

===Chinese===

他等了半个小时，又折返回来。电视望远镜从远处告诉他，要塞之外只有一艘孤船在巡逻。他朝那船挪近，从冰山背后悄悄爬行接近。他的磁场束（magnetic beam）探了出去。那艘船猛地一歪，坠了下去。磁场束继而探向要塞——要塞那边早已闪出一道分子射线，把他藏身的冰原撕了个稀烂。射线护盾挡下了这道射线；与此同时，莫里再度开动磁场束——这回对准的是要塞本身。可对方的射线始终没有中断！阿科特慌忙后撤。
“他们的秘诀算是找到了。没用了，莫里，快上来吧。”驾驶座上的阿科特喊道。“他们显然给那些装置加了磁屏蔽。这就是说，磁场束对我们再也派不上用场。他们必定会警告其余每一处基地，让它们装上同样的防护。”
“第一次进攻时，你怎么不试试磁场束？”泽兹登·阿夫森（Zezdon Afthen）问。
“要是那一招成了，他们的发报装置就会毁掉，就再没有讯息能叫围攻费尔谢的敌舰撤围了。逼他们自己把舰队召回去，我得到的效果，是直接攻打舰队所得不到的。”阿科特说。

===Original===

“I think there is little more I can do here, Stel Felso Theu. I will take you to Shesto, and there make final arrangements till my return, with apparatus capable of overthrowing your enemies. If you wish to accompany me—you may.” He glanced around at the others of his party. “And our next move will be to return to Earth with what we have. Then we will investigate the Sirian planets, and learn anything they may have of interest, thence—to the real outer space, the utter void of intergalactic space, and an attempt to learn the secret of that enormous power.”
They returned to Shesto, and there Arcot arranged that the only generator they could spare, the one already in their possession, might be used till other terrestrian ships could bring more. They left for Earth. Hour after hour they fled through the void, till at last old Sol was growing swiftly ahead of them, and finally Earth itself was large on the screens. They changed to a straight molecular drive, and dropped to the Vermont field from which they had taken off.
During the long voyage, Morey and Arcot had both spent much of the time working on the time-distortion field, which would give them a tremendous control over time, either speeding or slowing their time rate enormously. At last, this finished, they had worked on the artificial matter theory, to the point where they could control the shape of the matter perfectly, though as yet they could not control its exact nature. The possibility of such control was, however, definitely proven by the results the machines had given them. Arcot had been more immediately interested in the control of form. He could control the nature as to opacity or transparency to all vibrations that normal matter is opaque or transparent to. Light would pass, or not as he chose, but cosmics he could not stop nor would radio or moleculars be stopped by any present shield he could make.
They had signaled, as soon as they slowed outside the atmosphere, and when they settled to the field, Arcot’s father and a number of very important scientists had already arrived.

===Chinese===

“我想，我在这里能做的已经不多了，斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤。我送你去谢斯托（Shesto），在那里把最后的安排定妥，等我带着足以推翻你们敌人的装置回来。你们若愿随我同行——可以。”他环视同行的众伙伴。“我们的下一步，是带着现有的收获返回地球。然后去考察天狼星的诸行星，看看有没有值得留意的东西；再往后——便是真正的外层空间，星系际空间的浩瀚虚空，去设法揭开那种巨能的秘密。”
他们回到了谢斯托。阿科特在那里安排妥当：他们唯一匀得出来的那台发电机——也就是先前已经交付他们的那一台——可先行使用，等其余地球人（terrestrian）的飞船再运来更多。随后他们启程返回地球。一小时又一小时，他们在虚空中疾飞，直到最后，那颗古老的太阳在前方迅速胀大，终于，地球本身也在视屏上显出庞大的轮廓。他们改为分子推进装置直航，降落到当初起飞的佛蒙特机场。
漫长的航程中，莫里和阿科特都把大量时间花在时间畸变场上；有了它，他们对时间将获得惊人的掌控，能极大地加快或减慢自身的时间速率（time rate）。这项完成之后，他们又转攻人工物质理论，已进展到能把物质的形状控制得分毫不差，只是尚不能精确控制它的本性。不过，这种控制的可能性，已由那些机器交出的结果确凿地证明。阿科特更直接关心的，是形态的控制。凡寻常物质对之不透明或透明的各类振动，他都能相应控制人工物质的不透明或透明。光可以随他的意愿通过或不通过；但宇宙射线他挡不住，无线电波和分子射线，也不是他眼下造得出的任何护盾所能拦截。
一出大气层减速，他们就发出了信号；待到降落在机场上，阿科特的父亲和好几位举足轻重的科学家已经赶到了。

===Original===

Arcot senior greeted his son very warmly, but he was tremendously worried, as his son soon saw.
“What’s happened, Dad—won’t they believe your statements?”
“They doubted when I went to Luna for a session with the Interplanetary Council, but before they could say much, they had plenty of proof of my statements,” the older man answered. “News came that a fleet of Planetary Guard ships had been wiped out by a fleet of ships from outer space. They were huge things—nearly half a mile in length. The Guard ships went up to them—fifty of them—and tried to signal for a conference. The white ship was instantly wiped out—we don’t know how. They didn’t have ray screens, but that wasn’t it. Whatever it was—slightly luminous ray in space—it simply released the energy of the lux metal and relux of the ship. Being composed of light energy simply bound by photonic attraction, it let go with terrible energy. They can do it almost instantly from a distance. The other Guards at once let loose with all their moleculars and cosmics. The enemy shunted off the moleculars, and wiped out the Guard almost instantly.
“Of course, I could explain the screen, but not the detonation ray. I am inclined to believe from other casualties that the destruction, though reported as an instantaneous explosion, was not that. Other ships have been destroyed, and they seemed to catch fire, and burn, but with terrific speed, more like gun powder than coal. It seems to start a spreading decomposition, the ship lasts perhaps ten minutes. If it went instantly, the shock of such a tremendous energy release would disrupt the planet.
“At any rate, the great fleet separated, twelve went to the North Pole of Earth, twelve to the south, and similarly twelve to each pole of Venus. Then one of them turned, and went back to wherever it had come from, to report. Just turned and vanished. Similarly one from Venus turned and vanished. That leaves twelve at each of the four poles, for, as I said, there were an even fifty.

===Chinese===

老阿科特热切地迎接儿子，可他忧心如焚，这一点儿子很快就看了出来。
“出什么事了，爸——他们不相信你的陈述吗？”
“我去月球出席行星际理事会（Interplanetary Council）的会议时，他们还将信将疑；可还没等他们多说，我的话就有了充足的证明，”老者答道。“消息传来：一支行星警卫队（Planetary Guard）的舰队，被一支来自外层空间的飞船舰队全数消灭。那些东西庞大得很——将近半英里长。五十艘警卫队战舰迎上去，试图打信号要求会商。那艘白色飞船顷刻间便被抹掉了——我们不知道是怎么做到的。警卫队的飞船没有射线护盾，可问题不在这儿。不论那是什么——太空中一道微微发光的射线——它径直把飞船的卢克斯金属和雷卢克斯中所含的能量释放了出来。那些舰体本就是单纯靠光子引力束缚在一起的光能，一旦松脱，便以可怖的能量倾泻而出。而且他们几乎能从远处瞬间做到这一点。其余的警卫队战舰立刻把所有分子射线和宇宙射线一齐倾泻过去。敌人把分子射线尽数引开，转瞬便把整支警卫队消灭殆尽。
“护盾的原理我自然解释得了，可那种引爆射线我解释不了。从其他几起损失来看，我倾向于认为，那种毁灭虽然被报道成瞬时爆炸，实际却并非如此。还有别的飞船被毁：它们像是起了火，燃烧起来，但速度奇快，更像火药，而不像煤。那似乎是一种不断蔓延的分解，一艘船大约还能撑上十分钟。若是瞬间炸尽，这般巨量的能量骤然释放，其震波足以把整颗行星都震散。
“不管怎样，那支大舰队分开了：十二艘奔地球北极，十二艘奔南极；金星的两极也照此各去了十二艘。随后其中一艘掉头，回它来的地方报信去了——就那么一转身，没了踪影。金星那边同样有一艘掉头消失。这样，四个极点各剩十二艘，因为我说过，来的是整整五十艘。

===Original===

“They all followed the same tactics on landing, so I’ll simply tell what happened in Attica. In the North they had to pick one of the islands a bit to the south of the pole. They melted about a hundred square miles of ice to find one.
“The ships arranged themselves in a circle around the place, and literally hundreds of men poured out of each and fell to work. In a short time, they had set up a number of machines, the parts coming from the ships. These machines at once set to work, and they built up a relux wall. That wall was at least six feet thick; the floor was lined with thick relux as well as the roof, which is simply a continuation of the wall in a perfect dome. They had so many machines working on it, that within twenty-four hours they had it finished.
“We attacked twice, once in practically our entire force, with some ray-shield machines. The result was disastrous. The second attack was made with ray shielded machines only, and little damage was done to either side, though the enemy were somewhat impeded by masses of ice hurled into their position. Their relux disintegration ray was conspicuous by its absence.
“Yesterday—and it seems a lot longer than that, son—they started it again. They’d been unloading it from the ship evidently. We had had ray-shielded machines out, but they simply melted. They went down, and Earth retreated. They’re in their fortress now. We don’t know how to fight them. Now, for God’s sake, tell us you have learned of some weapon, son!”
The older man’s face was lined. His iron gray head showed his fatigue due to hours of concentration on his work.

===Chinese===

“它们着陆后的做法千篇一律，我就只讲讲阿提卡（Attica）发生的事吧。在北方，它们只得在诸岛之中挑了一座略偏北极以南的——为寻这座岛，它们熔掉了大约一百平方英里的冰。
“那些飞船围着这块地方排成一圈，每艘船里当真涌出好几百人，随即动起工来。不多时，他们便架起了许多机器——零件都来自船上。这些机器立刻开工，筑起一圈雷卢克斯墙。那墙至少六英尺厚；地面也衬着厚厚的雷卢克斯，顶盖同样是雷卢克斯，不过是墙体顺势收拢、合抱成的完美穹顶。他们投入施工的机器如此之多，不到二十四小时便大功告成。
“我们进攻过两次。头一次几乎倾巢而出，还带上了若干装了射线护盾的机器，结果一败涂地。第二次只出动带射线护盾的机器，双方损失都不大——不过大块大块的冰被掷进敌方阵地，多少给他们添了些麻烦。至于他们那分解雷卢克斯的射线，倒是该出场时偏偏始终不见。
“就在昨天——儿子，我却觉得比一百年还长——他们又动手了。看样子，那种东西他们一直在从船上往下卸。我们也派过带射线护盾的机器出去，可它们径直熔化了。它们坠毁了，地球一方随之撤退。如今他们缩在要塞里，我们不知道该怎么打。看在老天份上，儿子，快告诉我们，你总学到了什么武器吧！”
老者满面沟壑。铁灰色的头发，显露出长时专注操劳留下的疲惫。

===Original===

“Some,” replied Arcot briefly. He glanced around. Other men had arrived, men whom he met in his work. But there were Venerians here, too, in their protective suits, insulated against the cold of Earth, and against its atmosphere.
“First, though, gentlemen, allow me to introduce Stel Felso Theu of the planet Talso, one of our allies in this struggle, and Zezdon Afthen and Fentes of Ortol, one of our other allies.
“As to progress, I can say only that it is in a more or less rudimentary stage. We have the basis for great progress, a weapon of inestimable value—but it is only the basis. It must be worked out. I am leaving with you today the completed calculations and equations of the time field, the system used by the Thessian invaders in propelling their ships at a speed greater than that of light. Also, the uncompleted calculations in regard to another matter, a weapon which our ally, Talso, has given us, in exchange for the aid we gave in allowing them the use of one of our generators. Unfortunately the ship could not spare more than the single generator. I strongly advise rushing a number of generators to Talso in intergalactic freighters. They badly need power—power of respectable dimensions.
“I have stopped on Earth only temporarily, and I want to leave as soon as possible. I intend, however, to attempt an attack on the Arctic base of the Thessians, in strong hopes that they have not armored against one weapon that the *Ancient Mariner* carries—though I sadly fear that old Earth herself has played us false here. I hope to use the magnetic beam, but Earth’s polar magnetism may have forced them to armor, and they may have sufficiently heavy material to block the effects.”

===Chinese===

“有一点。”阿科特答得干脆。他环顾四周。又有些人到了，都是他工作中结识的。这里也有金星人（Venerian），穿着防护服，既隔绝地球的严寒，也隔绝地球的大气。
“不过，先请诸位容我介绍：这位是塔尔索（Talso）星来的斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤，塔尔索是我们在这场战争中的盟友之一；这两位是奥托尔（Ortol）来的泽兹登·阿夫森和泽兹登·芬特斯（Zezdon Fentes），奥托尔是我们的另一个盟友。
“至于进展，我只能说到目前还处在多少有点初创的阶段。我们已经掌握了大步推进的基础，一件价值无可估量的武器——但这仅仅是基础，还有待完善。今天我留给各位的，是时间场（time field）的完整计算和方程——也就是塞特入侵者用以把飞船推进到超光速的那套系统。另外还有一份未完成的计算，事关另一件东西：那是一件武器，我们的盟友塔尔索把它送给了我们，以回报我们曾给予的援助——让他们使用了一台我们的发电机。遗憾的是，飞船至多只能匀出这一台发电机。我强烈建议，用星系际货船把一批发电机火速送往塔尔索。他们急需功率——像模像样的大功率。
“我在地球只是暂作停留，越早离开越好。不过，我打算试着攻打塞特人的北极基地，满怀希望地盼着，他们还没有针对老水手号携带的某种武器设防——尽管我很痛心地担心，在这一点上，古老的地球自己坏了我们的好事。我想动用的是磁场束，可地球自身的极地磁性，也许早已迫使他们装上防护，而且他们手中的材料或许足够沉重，足以挡住磁场束的效应。”

===Original===

Morey already had a ground crew servicing the ship. He gave designs to machinists on hand to make special control panels for the large artificial matter machines. Arcot and Wade got some badly needed equipment.
In six hours, Arcot had announced himself ready, and a squadron of Planetary Guard ships were ready to accompany the refitted *Ancient Mariner*.
They approached the pole cautiously, and were rewarded by the hiss and roar of ice melting into water which burst into steam under a ray. It was coming from an outpost of the camp, a tiny dome under a great mass of ice. But the dome was of relux. A molecular reached down from a Guard ship—and the Guard ship crumbled suddenly as dozens of moleculars from the points hit it.
“They know how to fight this kind of a war. That’s their biggest advantage,” muttered Arcot. Wade merely swore.

===Chinese===

莫里早已安排了一组地勤人员检修飞船。他把设计图交给在场的机械师，请他们为大型人工物质机器赶制专用控制面板。阿科特和韦德则置办了一些急需的装备。
六小时后，阿科特宣布一切就绪；一个中队的行星警卫队飞船也整装待发，将伴随翻修一新的老水手号出征。
他们小心翼翼地逼近极点，迎接他们的是冰在射线之下熔成水、水又迸作蒸汽的嘶嘶声与轰鸣。声音来自营地的一处前哨：一大块冰层下的一座小穹顶。而那穹顶是雷卢克斯造的。一艘警卫队战舰放下一道分子射线——霎时，几十道分子射线从那些发射点齐齐命中，战舰顿时崩解。
“他们懂得怎么打这种仗。这正是他们最大的本钱。”阿科特低声咕哝。韦德只是骂了一句。

===Original===

“Ray screens, no moleculars!” snapped Arcot into the transmitter. He was not their leader, but they saw his wisdom, and the squadron commander repeated the advice as an order. In the meantime, another ship had fallen. The dome had its screen up, allowing the multitudes of hidden stations outside to fight for it.
“Hmm—something to remember when terrestrians have to retire to forts. They will, too, before this war is over. That way the main fort doesn’t have to lower its ray screen to fight,” commented Arcot. He was watching intensely as a tiny ship swung away from one of the larger machines, and a tremendously powerful molecular started biting at the fort’s ray screen. The ship seemed nothing but a flying ray projector, which was what it was.
As they had hoped, the deadly new ray stabbed out from somewhere on the side of the fort. It was not within the fort.
“Which means,” pointed out Morey, “that they can’t make stuff to stand that. Probably the projector would be vulnerable.”
But a barrage of heat rays which immediately followed had no apparent effect. The little radio-controlled molecular beam projector lay on the rock under the melted ice, blazing incandescent with the rapidly released energy of the relux.

===Chinese===

“开射线护盾！别放分子射线！”阿科特对着发报机厉声喊道。他并非他们的指挥官，可众人见识了他的高明，中队长便把这条建议当作命令传了下去。与此同时，又有一艘船栽了下去。穹顶护盾高悬，让外面成群隐蔽的火力点替它出战。
“唔——这一招值得记住，往后地球人不得不退守要塞时用得着。这场战争打完之前，准有那一天。这样，主堡就无须为了开火而降下自己的射线护盾了。”阿科特评点道。他正凝神注视着：一艘小船从一台较大的机器旁荡开，一道功率惊人的分子射线开始啃咬要塞的射线护盾。那小船看上去不外乎一具长了翅膀的射线投射器——事实上也正是。
不出所望，那道致命的新射线从要塞侧面某处猛然刺出。发射源并不在要塞之内。
“这说明，”莫里指出，“他们造不出扛得住那种射线的材料。投射器本身多半经不起打击。”
可紧随其后的一阵热射线（heat ray）弹幕，却看不出什么效果。那台小小的无线电遥控分子射线投射器躺在熔冰之下的岩石上，雷卢克斯急速释放的能量把它烧得白炽耀眼。

===Original===

“Now to try the real test we came here for,” Morey clambered back to the power room, and turned on the controls of the magnetic beam. The ship was aligned, and then he threw the last switch. The great mass of the machine jerked violently, and plunged forward as the beam attracted the magnetic core of the Earth.
Morey could not see it, but almost instantly the shimmer of the molecular screen on the fort died out. The deadly ray sprang out from the Thessian projector—and went dead. Frantically the Thessians tried weapon after weapon, and found them dead almost as soon as they were turned on—which was the natural result in the terrific magnetic field.
And these men had iron bones, their very bones were attracted by the beam; they plunged upward toward the ship as the beam touched them, but, accustomed to the enormous gravitation accelerations of an enormous world, most of them were not killed.

===Chinese===

“现在，来试试我们此行真正要考的那道题吧。”莫里爬回动力舱，合上磁场束的操纵装置。船身对准方位，随后他扳下了最后一个开关。庞大的机器猛地一震，随即向前猛冲——磁场束吸住了地球的磁性核心。
莫里看不见那边的情形，但几乎就在一瞬之间，要塞上分子护盾的微光熄灭了。致命射线从塞特人的投射器里蹿出——随即哑火。塞特人疯狂地一件接一件试用武器，却发现几乎每一件刚一开启就失了效——在这样骇人的磁场里，这是必然的结果。
而这些人长着铁骨头，连他们的骨骼也被磁场束吸引；磁场束一触到他们，他们便朝飞船的方向直冲上来。不过，他们本就习惯于巨大世界的强大引力加速度，大多数人并没有送命。

===Original===

“Ah—!” exclaimed Arcot. He picked up the transmitter and spoke again to the Squadron Commander. “Squadron Commander Tharnton, what relux thickness does your ship carry?”
“Inch and a quarter,” replied the surprised voice of the commander.
“Any of the other ships carry heavier?”
“Yes, the special solar investigator carries five inches. What shall we do?”
“Tell him to lower his screen, and let loose at once on all operating forts. His relux will stand for the time needed to shut them down for their own screens, unless some genius decides to fight it out. As soon as the other ships can lower their screens, tell them to do so, and tell them to join in. I’ll be able to help then. My relux has been burned, and I’m afraid to lower the screen. It’s mighty thin already.”

===Chinese===

“啊——！”阿科特惊呼一声。他抓起发报机，重新呼叫中队长。“桑顿（Tharnton）中队长，你们飞船的雷卢克斯有多厚？”
“一又四分之一英寸。”中队长惊讶的声音答道。
“其他船上有更厚的吗？”
“有。那艘专用的太阳系调查舰（solar investigator）装甲厚达五英寸。我们该怎么办？”
“叫他降下护盾，立刻向所有还在运转的要塞开火。要塞得先关掉自己的护盾才打得着人，这中间的工夫，他那层雷卢克斯顶得住——除非哪位天才决意死拼到底。其余飞船一能降盾，就叫他们降下，加入战斗。到那时我也能出手相助。我的雷卢克斯已经烧蚀，我不敢降盾——本来就已经薄得厉害了。”

===Original===

The squadron commander was smiling joyously as he relayed the advice as a command.
Almost at once a single ship, blunt, an almost perfect cylinder, lowered its screen. In an instant the opalescence of the transformation showed on it, but its dozen ray projectors were at work. Fort after fort glowed opalescent, then flashed into protective ionization of screening. Quickly other ships lowered their screens, and joined in. In a moment more, the forts had been forced to raise their screens for protection.
A disc of artificial matter ten feet across suddenly appeared beside the *Ancient Mariner*. It advanced with terrific speed, struck the great dome of the fort, and the dome caved, bent in, bent still more—but would not puncture. The disc retreated, became a sharp cone, and drove in again. This time the point smashed through the relux, and made a small hole. The cone seemed to change gradually, melting into a cylinder of twenty foot diameter, and the hole simply expanded. It continued to expand as the cylinder became a huge disc, a hundred feet across, set in the wall.
Suddenly it simply dissolved. There was a terrific roar, and a mighty column of white rushed out of the gaping hole. Figures of Thessians caught by the terrific current came rocketing out. The inside was at last visible. The terrific pressure was hurling the outside line of ships about like thistledown. The *Ancient Mariner* reeled back under the tremendous blast of expanding gas. The snow that fell to the boiling water below was not water, in toto; some was carbon dioxide—and some oxygen chilled in the expansion of the gas. It was snowing within the dome. The falling forms of Thessians were robbed of the life-giving air pressure to which they were accustomed. But all this was visible for but an instant.

===Chinese===

中队长喜滋滋地把这条建议当作命令传达下去。
几乎立刻，一艘船降下了护盾——船身钝钝的，近乎一个完美的圆柱体。转瞬间，船体上已显出蚀变泛起的乳白光晕，可它那十二座射线投射器仍在开火。一座接一座要塞泛起乳光，随即闪现出防护性的电离屏蔽。其他飞船也迅速降下护盾，加入战团。又过了片刻，各座要塞便被迫升起护盾自保。
一块十英尺宽的人工物质圆盘，蓦地出现在老水手号近旁。它以骇人的速度推进，一头撞上要塞那巨大的穹顶。穹顶塌瘪、内凹、越凹越深——却终究不曾洞穿。圆盘退了回来，化作一枚锐利的锥体，再度突进。这一回，锥尖轰穿了雷卢克斯，凿出一个小洞。锥体似乎渐渐变了模样，熔铸成一根直径二十英尺的圆柱，小洞便随之扩开。圆柱继而化作一面嵌进墙体的巨盘，宽达百英尺，洞口仍在继续扩张。
它蓦然消散了。一声骇人的轰吼，一股巨大的白色气柱从豁开的洞口夺腔而出。被狂流卷住的塞特人身影，一个个像出膛的火箭般直飞出来。要塞内部终于显露出来。那骇人的气压，把外围一圈飞船抛掷得如蓟絮般乱舞。老水手号在急骤膨胀的气体的狂暴冲击下连连摇晃后退。落向下方沸水的雪，并不全是水；有些是二氧化碳，还有些是气体膨胀时受冻凝结的氧。穹顶之内，正落着雪。塞特人的身影纷纷坠下——他们素来赖以维生的气压，已从他们身边被剥夺殆尽。然而这一切，仅仅显露了一瞬。

===Original===

Then a small, thin sheet of artificial matter formed beside the fort, and advanced on the dome. Like a knife cutting open an orange, it simply went around the dome’s edge, the great dome lifted like the lid of a teapot under the enormous gas pressure remaining—then dropped under its own weight.
The artificial matter was again a huge disc. It settled over the exact center of the dome—and went down. The dome caved in. It was crushed under a load utterly inestimable. Then the great disc, like some monstrous tamper, tamped the entire works of the Thessians into the bedrock of the island. Every ship, every miniature fort, every man was caught under it—and annihilated.
The disc dissolved. A terrific barrage of heat beams played over the island, and the rock melted, flowed over the ruins, and left only the spumes of steam from the Arctic ice rising from a red-hot: mass of rock, contained a boiling pool.
The Battle of the Arctic was done.

===Chinese===

随后，一小片薄薄的人工物质在要塞近旁凝成，朝穹顶推进。它像一把切开橙子的刀，径直沿着穹顶的边棱划了一整圈；残存的巨大气压随即将大穹顶像茶壶盖一样顶了起来——跟着，穹顶又在自己重量的拖拽下塌落下去。
人工物质再度凝成一面巨盘，稳稳罩在穹顶的正中央——随之下压。穹顶塌陷了。在一份全然无从估量的重负之下，它被碾得粉碎。随后，那面巨盘宛如一柄骇人的夯锤，把塞特人的全套工事齐齐夯进小岛的基岩。每一艘飞船，每一座小堡垒，每一个人，都被压在底下——灰飞烟灭。
巨盘消散了。一阵可怕的热射线弹幕在岛上往复犁过，岩石熔化了，熔浆漫过废墟，最后只剩北冰洋的冰化成的团团蒸汽，从炽热的岩体上袅袅升起——那岩体环抱着一片沸滚的湖。
北极之战，就此告终。

## XIV Intergalactic Space / XIV / 星系际空间

===Original===
“Well, Sirius has retreated a bit,” observed Arcot. The star was indeed several trillions of miles away. Evidently they had not been motionless as they had thought, but the interference of the Thessian ship had thrown their machine off.

“Shall we go back, or go on?” asked Morey.

“The ship works. Why return?” asked Wade. “I vote we go on.”

“Seconded,” added Arcot.

“If they who know most of the ship vote for a continuance of the journey, then assuredly we who know so little can only abide by their judgment. Let us continue,” said Zezdon Afthen gravely.

===Chinese===
“嗯，天狼星已经退后了一段。”阿科特（Arcot）说。这颗恒星确实已在数万亿英里之外。显然，他们并不像自己以为的那样始终停在原地，而是那艘塞特人（Thessian）飞船的干扰，把他们机器的运转带偏了。

“我们是回去，还是继续前进？”莫里（Morey）问。

“船好好的，干嘛回去？”韦德（Wade）说。“我投继续前进一票。”

“附议。”阿科特补上一句。

“最了解这艘船的人都主张继续此行，那么所知甚少的我们，唯有遵从他们的判断。我们继续前进吧。”泽兹登·阿夫森（Zezdon Afthen）庄重地说。

===Original===
Space was suddenly black about them. Sirius was gone, all the jewels of the heavens were gone in the black of swift flight. Ten seconds later Arcot lowered the space-control. Black behind them the night of space was pricked by points of light, the infinite multitude of the stars. Before them lay—nothing. The utter emptiness of space between the galaxies.

“Thlek Styrs! What happened?” asked Morey in amazement, his pet Venerian phrase rolling out in his astonishment.

“Tried an experiment, and it was overly successful,” replied Arcot, a worried look on his face. “I tried combining the Thessian high speed *time* distortion with our high *speed* space distortion—both on low power. ‘There ain’t no sich animals,’ as the old agriculturist remarked of the giraffe. God knows what speed we hit, but it was plenty. We must be ten thousand light years beyond the galaxy.”

===Chinese===
四周的空间骤然漆黑一片。天狼星不见了，满天珍宝般的星辰，尽数没入疾速飞驰的黑暗。十秒钟后，阿科特调低了空间控制装置（space-control）。身后，太空的黑夜中缀起点点光亮，那是无量数的星辰。而前方——空无一物。唯有星系与星系之间那片绝对空虚的空间。

“Thlek Styrs！这是怎么回事？”莫里惊愕地问，情急之下，他那句挂在嘴边的金星式（Venerian）口头禅脱口而出。

“做了个实验，结果成功得过了头。”阿科特答道，脸上一副忧色。“我试着把塞特人那种高速*时间*畸变，同我们自己的高*速*空间畸变合在一起——两边都只开了低功率。正如那位老农人看见长颈鹿时说的：‘压根儿没这种动物。’天知道我们冲到了什么速度，反正相当可观。我们此刻必定已在银河系之外一万光年了。”

===Original===
“That’s a fine way to start the trip. You have the old star maps to get back however, have you not?” asked Wade.

“Yes, the maps we made on our first trip out this way are in the cabinet. Look ’em up, will you, and see how far we have to go before we reach the cosmic fields?”

Arcot was busy with his instruments, making a more accurate determination of their distance from the “edge” of the galaxy. He adopted the figure of twelve thousand five hundred light years as the probable best result. Wade was back in a moment with the information that the fields lay about sixteen thousand light years out. Arcot went on, at a rate that would reach the fields in two hours.

===Chinese===
“这趟旅程的开场可真精彩。不过你手里还有旧星图可以找路回去，是不是？”韦德问。

“有，我们头一回沿这条路出来时绘的星图就在柜子里。帮我翻翻，看看还得走多远才能到宇宙射线场（cosmic ray fields）？”

阿科特忙着操作仪器，更精确地测定他们与银河系“边缘”的距离，最后取一万二千五百光年作为最可信的结果。韦德很快便查了回来：那片射线场大约在一万六千光年之外。阿科特继续前进，航速恰好能在两小时后抵达。

===Original===
Several hours more were spent in measurements, till at last Arcot announced himself satisfied.

“Good enough—back we go.” Again in the control room, he threw on the drive, and shot through the twenty-seven thousand light years of cosmic ray fields, and then more leisurely returned to the galaxy. The star maps were strangely off. They could follow them, but only with difficulty as the general configuration of the constellations that were their guides were visibly altered to the naked eye.

“Morey,” said Arcot softly, looking at the constellation at which they were then aiming, and at the map before him, “there is something very, very rotten. The Universe either ‘ain’t what it used to be’ or we have traveled in more than space.”

“I know it, and I agree with you. Obviously, from the degree of alteration off the constellations, we are off by about 100,000 years. Question: how come? Question: what are we going to do about it?”

===Chinese===
之后又用了几个小时进行测量，直到阿科特终于宣布满意为止。

“行了——回去吧。”他再次回到控制室，接通驱动，一举射穿那两万七千光年的宇宙射线场，随后从容了一些，朝银河系返航。星图却错得离奇。他们能照着走，却走得相当吃力，因为那些充当向导的星座，整体轮廓已然肉眼可辨地变了样。

“莫里，”阿科特轻声说，目光在他们此刻对准的星座与面前的星图之间来回移动，“事情非常、非常不对劲。要么这宇宙‘不复旧观’，要么我们不只是在空间里旅行。”

“我知道，我也同意你的看法。显而易见，从星座偏移的程度判断，我们错后了大约 100,000 年。问题一：怎么会这样？问题二：我们打算怎么办？”

===Original===
“Answer one: remembering what we observed in re Sirius, I suspect that the interference of that Thessian ship, with its time-field opposing our space-field did things to our time-frame. We were probably thrown off then.

“As to the second question, we have to determine number one first. Then we can plan our actions.”

With Wade’s help, and by coming to rest near several of the stars, then observing their actual motions, they were able to determine their time-status. The estimate they made finally was of the order of eighty thousand years in the past! The Thessian ship had thrown them that much out of their time.

===Chinese===
“回答一：回想我们在天狼星一事上的观察，我怀疑是那艘塞特飞船的干扰作祟——它的时间场（time-field）顶撞了我们的空间场，动了我们的时间坐标。多半就是在那时被甩偏的。

“至于第二个问题，得先把第一个问题弄个水落石出，才谈得上安排行动。”

在韦德的帮助下，他们停靠到几颗恒星近旁，观察其真实运动，终于得以确定自己所处的时间状态。最后的估算结果是：他们回到了大约八万年之前！那艘塞特飞船把他们从原本的时间里甩出了这么远。

===Original===
“This isn’t all to the bad,” said Morey with a sigh. “We at least have all the time we could possibly use to determine the things we want for this fight. We might even do a lot of exploring for the archeologists of Earth and Venus and Ortol and Talso. As to getting back—that’s a question.”

“Which is,” added Arcot, “easy to answer now, thank the good Lord. All we have to do is wait for our time to catch up with us. If we just wait eighty thousand years, eight hundred centuries, we will be in our own time.”

“Oh, I think waiting so long would be boring,” said Wade sarcastically. “What do you suggest we do in the intervening eighty millenniums? Play cards?”

“Oh, cards or chess. Something like that,” grinned Arcot. “Play cards, calculate our fields—and turn on the time rate control.”

“Oh—I take it back. You win! Take all! I forgot all about that,” Wade smiled at his friend. “That will save a little waiting, won’t it.”

===Chinese===
“这也不全是坏事。”莫里叹了口气。“至少我们有的是时间，足以把这一仗要用的东西统统研究出来。甚至还能替地球、金星、奥托尔（Ortol）和塔尔索（Talso）的考古学家们好好探一探险。至于回不回得去——那倒是另一个问题。”

“这个问题嘛，”阿科特接道，“如今倒容易回答，感谢上苍。我们只需等时间追上我们自己。只要等上八万年——八百个世纪——我们就回到自己的时代了。”

“哦，我看干等这么久可够无聊的。”韦德挖苦道。“这中间的八万年，你打算让我们怎么打发？打牌？”

“哦，打牌或者下棋，诸如此类。”阿科特咧嘴一笑。“打打牌，算算我们的场——再把时间速率控制（time rate control）打开。”

“哦——我收回。你赢了！通吃！我把这茬忘得一干二净。”韦德朝朋友笑道。“这倒能省下一点干等，是不是。”

===Original===
“The exploring of our worlds would without doubt be of infinite benefit to science, but I wonder if it would not be of more direct benefit if we were to get back to our own time, alive and well. Accidents always happen, and for all our weapons, we might easily meet some animal which would put an abrupt and tragic finish to our explorations. Is it not so?” asked Stel Felso Theu.

“Your point is good, Stel Felso Theu. I agree with you. We will do no more exploring than is necessary, or safe.”

“We might just as well travel slowly on the time retarder, and work on the way. I think the thing to do is to go back to Earth, or better, the solar system, and follow the sun in its path.”

===Chinese===
“考察我们的诸世界，对科学无疑有莫大裨益。可我在想，倘若我们能活着、健全地回到自己的时代，岂不是更直接的裨益？意外总是难免，凭我们这些武器，也难保不会遇上什么野兽，让我们的考察戛然而止，落得悲剧收场。难道不是吗？”斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤（Stel Felso Theu）问道。

“你说得在理，斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤。我同意。考察只以必要与安全为限，不再多走一步。”

“我们不妨开着时间延缓器（time retarder）慢慢赶路，途中照常工作。我看眼下该做的，是回地球去——或者更好，回太阳系——循着太阳的轨迹行进。”

===Original===
They returned, and the desolation that the sun in its journey passes through is nothing to the utter, oppressive desolation of empty space between the stars, for it has its family of planets—and it has no conscious thought.

The Sun was far from the point that it had occupied when the travelers had left it, billions on billions of miles further on its journey around the gravitational center of our galactic universe, and in the eighty millenniums that they must wait, it would go far.

They did not go to the planets now, for, as Arcot said in reply to Stel Felso Theu’s suggestion that they determine more accurately their position in time, life had not developed to an extent that would enable them to determine the year according to our calendar.

===Chinese===
他们踏上归途。太阳一路行经的那点荒凉，比起恒星与恒星之间那片绝对空寂、压得人心头发沉的荒凉，实在不值一提——太阳自有它的行星家族相伴，而且它自己并无知觉。

太阳早已远离旅行者们离开时它所在的位置，在环绕我们银河宇宙引力中心的旅程上，又前行了亿亿万万英里；而在他们必须等待的这八万年里，它还要走得很远很远。

这一趟他们没有往诸行星去。斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤曾提议更精确地测定他们所处的时间位置；如阿科特所答，此间的生命尚未繁盛到那种程度，无从据以按我们的历法断定年份。

===Original===
So for thirty thousand years they hung motionless as the sun moved on, and the little spots of light, that were worlds, hurled about it in a mad race. Even Pluto, in its three-hundred-yearlong track seemed madly gyrating beneath them; Mercury was a line of light, as it swirled about the swiftly moving sun.

But that thirty thousand years was thirty days to the men of the ship. Their time rate immensely retarded, they worked on their calculations. At the end of that month Arcot had, with the help of Morey and Wade, worked out the last of the formulas of artificial matter, and the machines had turned out the last graphical function of the last branch of research that they could discover. It was a time of labor for them, and they worked almost constantly, stopping occasionally for a game of some sort to relax the nervous tension.

At the end of that month they decided that they would go to Earth.

===Chinese===
于是整整三万年，他们悬停不动，看太阳前行，看那些作为世界的微小光点疯狂竞逐，绕它飞旋。就连轨道一周绵延三百年的冥王星，在他们脚下也像在疯狂打转；水星则化作一线流光，绕着疾驰的太阳盘旋。

但这三万年，对船上的人不过是三十天。他们的时间速率被极大地减缓，便埋头推进计算。到这一个月的尽头，阿科特已在莫里和韦德的协助下推出了人工物质（artificial matter）的最后一批公式，机器也交出了他们所能发现的最后一个研究分支的最后一张图解函数。对他们而言，这是一个劳作的时节，他们几乎不停地工作，只是偶尔停下来玩上一局什么，松弛一下紧绷的神经。

那一个月结束时，他们决定去地球。

===Original===
They speeded their time rate now, and flashed toward Earth at enormous speed that brought them within the atmosphere in minutes. They had landed in the valley of the Nile. Arcot had suggested this as a means of determining the advancement of life of man. Man had evidently established some of his earliest civilizations in this valley where water and sun for his food plants were assured.

“Look—there *are* men here!” exclaimed Wade. Indeed, below them were villages, of crude huts made of timber and stone and mud. Rubble work walls, for they needed little shelter here, and the people were but savages.

“Shall we land?” asked Arcot, his voice a bit unsteady with suppressed excitement.

===Chinese===
此刻他们加快了时间速率，以骇人的速度朝地球飞去，几分钟便进入了大气层。他们降落在尼罗河流域。这是阿科特提议的落脚之处，以此判断人类生命进程的推进程度。人类最早的若干文明，显然正是发祥于这条流域——这里水源与日照无虞，足以滋养人们的食用作物。

“看——这儿*真的*有人！”韦德喊道。果然，他们下方是些村落，尽是木、石、泥巴搭成的粗糙棚屋。墙是毛石垒砌的——这里的人用不着多少遮蔽，居民也不过是些野蛮人。

“我们降落吗？”阿科特问，压抑着的激动让他的声音微微发颤。

===Original===
“Of course!” replied Morey without turning from his station at the window. Below them now, less than half a mile down on the patchwork of the Nile valley, men were standing, staring up, collecting in little groups, gesticulating toward the strange thing that had materialized in the air above them.

“Does everyone agree that we land?” asked Arcot.

There were no dissenting voices, and the ship sank gently toward a road below and to the left. A little knot of watchers broke, and they fled in terror as the great machine approached, crying out to their friends, casting affrighted glances at the huge, shining monster behind them.

===Chinese===
“当然！”莫里答道，人没有离开窗前的岗位。此刻在他们下方，不足半英里处，尼罗河谷斑驳的大地上，人们正站着仰望，三五成群地聚拢，朝着那个凭空出现在头顶的怪东西指指点点。

“大家都同意降落吗？”阿科特问。

没有人反对。飞船朝左下方的一条道路缓缓沉落。一小簇围观的人霎时散开，在那庞然大物逼近时惊惶奔逃，一边朝同伴呼喊，一边回望身后那闪闪发光的巨怪。

===Original===
Without a jar the mighty weight of the ship touched the soil of its native planet, touched it fifty millenniums before it was made, five hundred centuries before it left!

Arcot’s brow furrowed. “There is one thing puzzles me—I can’t see how we can come back. Don’t you see, Morey, we have disturbed the lives of those people. We have affected history. This must be written into the history that exists.

“This seems to banish the idea of free thought. We have changed history, yet history is that which is already done!

===Chinese===
毫无震颤，飞船那千钧之重触上了这颗母星的土地——比这艘船被造出来还早五万年，比它启程远航早了五百个世纪！

阿科特眉头紧锁。“有件事我想不通——我看不出我们怎么回得去。你不明白吗，莫里，我们搅扰了那些人的生活，我们影响了历史。这件事必定要写进那部已然存在的史册。

“这似乎把自由意志的念头也一笔勾销了。我们改变了历史，可历史乃是已然发生之事！

===Original===
“Had I never been born, had—but I *was* already—I existed fifty-eighty thousand years before I was born!”

“Let’s go out and think about that later. We’ll go to a psych hospital, if we don’t stop thinking about problems of space and time for a little while. We need some kind of relaxation.”

“I suggest that we take our weapons with us. These men may have weapons of chemical nature, such as poisons injected into the flesh on small sticks hurled either by a spring device or by pneumatic pressure of the lungs,” said Stel Felso Theu as he rose from his seat unstrapping himself.

===Chinese===
“假如我从未出生，假如——可我早已存在——我早在自己出生之前五万乃至八万年就已存在了！”

“先出去走走，这问题回头再想。咱们要是不把空间和时间的问题搁下片刻，早晚得进精神病院。我们需要松快松快。”

“我建议把武器带上。这些人或许持有化学性质的武器——比如装在小杆上的毒剂，刺入血肉，或用弹簧装置掷出，或借肺部的气压吹出。”斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤说着，解开束带，从座位上起身。

===Original===
“Arrows and blowguns we call ’em. But it’s a good idea, Stel Felso, and I think we will,” replied Arcot. “Let’s not all go out at once, and the first group to go out goes out on foot, so they won’t be scared off by our flying around.”

Arcot, Wade, Zezdon Afthen, and Stel Felso Theu went out. The natives had retreated to a respectful distance, and were now standing about, looking on, chattering to themselves. They were edging nearer.

“Growing bold,” grinned Wade.

===Chinese===
“我们管它们叫箭和吹箭筒。不过这主意不错，斯特尔·费尔索，我看就照办。”阿科特答道。“咱们别一口气全出去，头一批步行出去，免得我们飞来飞去把他们吓跑了。”

阿科特、韦德、泽兹登·阿夫森和斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤走了出去。土人已退到一段不敢造次的距离之外，这会儿正四下站着张望，叽叽咕咕地互相招呼，一点点往前蹭。

“胆子渐渐大了。”韦德咧嘴笑道。

===Original===
“It is the characteristic of intelligent races manifesting itself—curiosity,” pointed out Stel Felso Theu.

“Are these the type of men still living in this valley, or who will be living there in fifty thousand years?” asked Zezdon Afthen.

“I’d say they weren’t Egyptians as we know them, but typical Neolithic men. It seems they have brains fully as large as some of the men I see on the streets of New York. I wonder if they have the ability to learn as much as the average man of—say about 1950?”

===Chinese===
“这是智慧种族的特征在显形——好奇心。”斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤指出。

“这些就是仍旧生活在这条河谷里的人种吗？或者说，五万年后住在那里的会是他们吗？”泽兹登·阿夫森问。

“要我说，他们还不是我们所熟知的埃及人，而是典型的新石器人（Neolithic men）。看样子，他们的脑子完全不比我在纽约街头见到的某些人小。我倒好奇，他们的学习能力赶得上普通人——比方说 1950 年前后的普通人——吗？”

===Original===
The Neolithic men were warming up. There was an orator among them, and his grunts, growls, snorts and gestures were evidently affecting them. They had sent the women back (by the simple and direct process of sweeping them up in one arm and heaving them in the general direction of home). The men were brandishing polished stone knives and axes, various instruments of war and peace. One favorite seemed to be a large club.

“Let’s forestall trouble,” suggested Arcot. He drew his ray pistol, and turned it on the ground directly in front of them, and about halfway between them and the Neoliths. A streak of the soil about two feet wide flashed into intense radiation under the impact of millions on millions of horsepower of radiant energy. Further, it was fused to a depth of twenty feet or more, and intensely hot still deeper. The Neoliths took a single look at it, then turned, and raced for home.

“Didn’t like our looks. Let’s go back.”

===Chinese===
那些新石器人渐渐躁动起来。他们中间有个演说家，又是哼哼又是低吼又是喷鼻息，还连比带划，显然正鼓动着众人。妇女已被打发回去（办法简单而直接：一条胳膊夹起来，大致朝家的方向一扔）。男人们挥舞着磨光的石刀石斧，各色战争与和平的器物，其中最受青睐的似乎是一根大棒。

“咱们先下手，免得生事。”阿科特提议。他拔出射线手枪（ray pistol），对准他们正前方的地面，大约在他们与那些新石器人的正中间。一条约莫两英尺宽的土带，在千百万马力的辐射能量冲击下，骤然迸出炽烈的辐射光芒；而且熔融深达二十英尺有余，更深处的泥土仍然灼热逼人。新石器人只看了一眼，便转身撒腿往家跑。

“他们不喜欢我们的长相。回去吧。”

===Original===
They wandered about the world, investigating various peoples, and proved to their own satisfaction that there was no Atlantis, not at this time at any rate. But they were interested in seeing that the polar caps extended much farther toward the equator; they had not retreated at that time to the extent that they had by the opening of history.

They secured some fresh game, an innovation in their larder, and a welcome one. Then the entire ship was swept out with fresh, clean air, their water tanks filled with water from the cold streams of the melting glaciers. The air apparatus was given a new stock to work over.

Their supplies in a large measure restored, thousands of aerial photographic maps made, they returned once more to space to wait.

===Chinese===
他们漫游世界，考察各个族裔，并令自己信服地证实：并无亚特兰蒂斯（Atlantis）——至少这个时代没有。倒是有一件事让他们兴致盎然：极冠朝赤道延伸得远得多；在那个年代，它们尚未退缩到历史开篇时的范围。

他们弄到了一些新鲜猎物，给储藏室添了桩新鲜事，而且大受欢迎。随后，整艘船都换进了清新洁净的空气，水箱也灌满了冰川消融溪流里的凉水。空气装置领到了新的一批原料可供处理。

物资大体补充完毕，数千幅航摄地图也测绘停当，他们便再一次回到太空，继续等待。

===Original===
Their time was taken up for the most part by actual work on the enormous mass of calculation necessary. It is inconceivable to the layman what tremendous labor is involved in the development of a single mathematical hypothesis, and a concrete illustration of it was the long time, with tremendously advanced calculating machines, that was required in their present work.

They had worked out the problem of the time-field, but there they had been aided by the actual apparatus, and the possibilities of making direct tests on machines already set up. The problem of artificial matter, at length fully solved, was a different matter. This had required within a few days of a month (by their clocks; close to thirty thousand years of Earth’s time), for they had really been forced to develop it all from the beginning. In the small improvements Arcot had instituted in Stel Felso Theu’s device, he had really merely followed the particular branch that Stel Felso Theu had stumbled upon. Hence it was impossible to determine with any great variety, the type of matter created. Now, however, Arcot could make any known kind of matter, and many unknown kinds.

But now came the greatest problem of all. They were ready to start work on the data they had collected in space.

===Chinese===
他们的时间绝大部分都花在了必不可少的浩繁计算的实际工作上。单单一个数学假说的推演要耗费何等惊人的劳动，外行根本无从想象；一个具体的例证便是：即便有先进绝伦的计算机器，他们眼下的工作仍需旷日持久。

时间场的问题他们已经解决，但那一路有现成装置助阵，还能在业已装好的机器上直接做试验。人工物质的问题虽然终于彻底解决，却是另一回事：它花了将近一个月（按他们的钟，约合地球时间三万年），因为他们实在不得不从头把整套东西建立起来。阿科特先前对斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤那台装置所做的小改进，其实不过是沿着斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤偶然撞见的那条分支走而已，因此所造物质的类型无从大幅多样化。而如今，阿科特已能造出任何已知的物质，乃至许多未知的物质。

然而如今，最大的难题来了。他们已准备就绪，要着手处理在太空中收集的数据。

===Original===
“What,” asked Zezdon Afthen, as he watched the three terrestrians begin their work, “is the nature of the thing you are attempting to harness?”

“In a word, energy,” replied Arcot, pausing.

“We are attempting to harness energy in its primeval form, in the form of a space-field. Remember, mass is a measure of energy. Two centuries ago a scientist of our world proposed the idea that energy could be measured by mass, and proceeded to prove that the relationship was the now firmly intrenched formula E=Mc2.

===Chinese===
“你们想要驾驭的，”泽兹登·阿夫森望着三个地球人（terrestrian）开始工作，问道，“究竟是何性质的东西？”

“一言以蔽之——能量。”阿科特顿了一顿，答道。

“我们试图驾驭的，是能量的原初形态——空间场的形态。记住，质量是能量的量度。两百年前，我们世界的一位科学家提出，能量可以用质量来量度，并进而证明了两者的关系，正是如今早已根深蒂固的公式 E=Mc2。

===Original===
“The sun is giving off energy. It is giving off mass, then, in the form of light photons. The field of the sun’s gravity must be constantly decreasing as its mass decreases. It is a collapsing field. It is true, the sun’s gravitational field does decrease, by a minute amount, despite the fact that our sun loses a thousand million tons of matter every four minutes. The percentage change is minute, but the energy released is—immeasurable.

“But, I am going to invent a new power unit, Afthen. I will call it the ‘sol,’ the power of a sun. One sol is the rating of our sun. And I will measure the energy I use in terms of sun-powers, not horsepower. That may tell you of its magnitude!”

“But,” Zezdon Afthen asked, “while you men of Earth work on this problem, what is there for us? We have no problems, save the problem of the fate of our world, still fifty thousand years of your time in the future. It is terrible to wait, wait, wait and think of what may be happening in that other time. Is there nothing we can do to help? I know our hopeless ignorance of your science. Stel Felso Theu can scarcely understand the thoughts you use, and I can scarcely understand his explanations! I cannot help you there, with your calculations, but is there nothing I can do?”

===Chinese===
“太阳在释放能量，那么也就是在以光子的形式释放质量。太阳的引力场必然随其质量之减而不断减弱。这是一个坍缩中的场。的确，太阳的引力场在减小，虽然幅度极微——要知道我们的太阳每四分钟就要损失十亿吨物质。变化的百分比微不足道，可释放出的能量——无可计量。

“不过，阿夫森，我打算发明一种新的功率单位。我把它叫作‘索尔’（sol），一颗太阳的功率。一索尔，就是我们太阳的额定功率。往后我计量所用能量，将不以马力计，而以太阳功率（sun-power）计。这该能让你掂出它的量级了！”

“可是，”泽兹登·阿夫森问，“你们地球人攻关的时候，我们做什么呢？我们无事可做，唯有悬心于我们世界的命运——它还在你们时间的五万年之后。等待，等待，无尽地等待，还要想象另一重时间里或许正发生着什么，这太折磨人了。我们就没有能出力的地方吗？我知道，我们对你们的科学一窍不通，无可救药。斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤几乎听不懂你们的思路，我又几乎听不懂他的解释！你们的计算我帮不上忙，可当真没有我能做的事吗？”

===Original===
“There is, Ortolian, decidedly. We badly need your help, and as Stel Felso Theu cannot aid us here as much as he can by working with you, I will ask him to do so. I want your knowledge of psycho-mechanical devices to help us. Will you make a machine controlled by mental impulses? I want to see such a system and know how it is done that I may control machines by such a system.”

“Gladly. It will take time, for I am not the expert worker that you are, and I must make many pieces of apparatus, but I will do what I can,” exclaimed Zezdon Afthen eagerly.

So, while Arcot and his group continued their work of determining the constants of the space-energy field, the others were working on the mental control apparatus.

===Chinese===
“有，奥托尔人（Ortolian），当然有。我们极需你帮忙。斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤与你协作，比留在这里更能助我们一臂之力，我会请他这么做。我要借重你在心智机械装置（psycho-mechanical devices）方面的学识。你能不能造一台由精神脉冲（mental impulses）操控的机器？我想见识这样一套系统，弄清其中的门道，好让我也能用这样的系统操控机器。”

“乐意之至。这得费些时日，我的手艺不及你们娴熟，许多器材都得现做，但我必尽全力。”泽兹登·阿夫森热切地说。

于是，阿科特一行继续测定空间能量场的各个常数，其余人则埋头研制心智控制装置（mental control apparatus）。

## I / 入侵者

===Original===
Russ Evans, Pilot 3497, Rocket Squad Patrol 34, unsnapped his seat belt, and with a slight push floated “up” into the air inside the weightless ship. He stretched himself, and yawned broadly.

“Red, how soon do we eat?” he called.

“Shut up, you’ll wake the others,” replied a low voice from the rear of the swift little patrol ship. “See anything?”

“Several million stars,” replied Evans in a lower voice. “And—” His tone became suddenly severe. “Assistant Murphy, remember your manners when addressing your superior officer. I’ve a mind to report you.”

A flaming head of hair topping a grinning face poked around the edge of the door. “Lower your wavelength, lower your wavelength! You may think you’re a sun, but you’re just a planetoid. But what I’d like to know, Chief Pilot Russ Evans, is why they locate a ship in a forlorn, out of the way place like this—three-quarters of a billion miles, out of planetary plane. No ships ever come out here, no pirates, not a chance to help a wrecked ship. All we can do is sit here and watch the other fellows do the work.”

===Chinese===
火箭巡逻队（Rocket Squad）第 34 巡逻分队的 3497 号飞行员拉斯·埃文斯（Russ Evans）解开座椅安全带，轻轻一推，便飘“起”在无重船舱的空中。他伸了伸懒腰，打了个大大的哈欠。

“红毛（Red），咱们什么时候吃饭？”他喊道。

“住嘴，你会吵醒别人的。”这艘迅捷的小巡逻船后部传来一个压低的嗓音，“看见什么了吗？”

“几百万颗星星。”埃文斯把声音压得更低，“还有——”他的语气陡然一肃，“墨菲（Murphy）助理，跟上司讲话别忘了规矩。我真想打份报告治治你。”

一颗顶着火红头发的脑袋从门边探进来，底下是张咧嘴嬉笑的脸。“降降你的波长，降降你的波长！你自以为是颗恒星，其实不过是颗小行星。不过我真正想知道的是，首席飞行员拉斯·埃文斯，上头干吗把一艘船摆在这么个鸟不生蛋的偏僻角落——七亿五千万英里开外，还偏出了行星轨道平面。从来没有船到这儿来，没有海盗，连救助失事船只的机会都没有。咱们能干的，就是坐在这儿看别人卖力干活。”

===Original===
“Which is exactly why we’re here. Watch—and tell the other ships where to go, and when. Is that chow ready?” asked Russ looking at a small clock giving New York time.

“Uh—think she’ll be on time? Come on an’ eat.”

Evans took one more look at the telectroscope screen, then snapped it off. A tiny, molecular towing unit in his hand, he pointed toward the door to the combined galley and lunch room, and glided in the wake of Murphy.

“How much fuel left?” he asked, as he glided into the dizzily spinning room. A cylindrical room, spinning at high speed, causing an artificial “weight” for the foods and materials in it, made eating of food a less difficult task. Expertly, he maneuvered himself to the guide rail near the center of the room, and caught the spiral. Braking himself into motion, he soon glided down its length, and landed on his feet. He bent and flexed his muscles, waiting for the now-busied assistant to get to the floor and reply.

“They gave us two pounds extra. Lord only knows why. Must expect us to clean up on some fleet. That makes four pound rolls left, untouched, and two thirds of the original pound. We’ve been here fifteen days, and have six more to go. The main driving power rolls have about the same amount left, and three pound rolls in each reserve bin,” replied Red, holding a curiously moving coffee pot that strove to adjust itself to rapidly changing air velocities as it neared the center of the room.

===Chinese===
“我们守在这儿，为的正是这个。监视——然后通知其他船该上哪儿去、什么时候去。饭好了吗？”拉斯问，一面瞅了瞅那只显示纽约（New York）时间的小钟。

“呃——你觉得她能准时来吗？得了，吃饭去。”

埃文斯又朝电视望远镜（telectroscope）的屏幕望了一眼，随即啪的一声关掉。他手里捏着一个小小的分子拖曳装置，指向通往厨房兼餐室的舱门，跟在墨菲身后滑了过去。

“还剩多少燃料？”他一面滑进那间转得人头晕目眩的舱室，一面问道。那是个高速旋转的圆柱形舱室，靠旋转为舱内的食物和用品造出人工“重量”，使进食不再那么费事。他娴熟地调整身形，够到舱室中段附近的导轨，一把抓住那条螺旋轨。刹住来势、顺势一送，他很快便沿轨滑到底，双脚落了地。他弯腰屈伸了几下肌肉，等着已经忙活起来的助手落到舱底再作回答。

“他们多给了咱们两磅。天知道为什么。八成是指望咱们收拾掉整整一支舰队呢。这么一来，还剩四卷一磅装的燃料卷原封未动，另有原先那一磅的三分之二。咱们在这儿待了十五天，还得再待六天。主驱动力的燃料卷剩下的量也差不多，每个备用箱里还有三磅装。”红毛答道，手里端着一只动得怪模怪样的咖啡壶——它正努力调整自己，好适应逼近舱心时气流的急速变化。

===Original===
“Sounds like a fleet’s power stock. Martian lead or the terrestrial isotope?” asked Evans, tasting warily a peculiar dish before him. “Say, this is energy food. I thought we didn’t get any more till Saturday.” The change from the energy-less, flavored pastes that made up the principal bulk of a space-pilot’s diet, to prevent overeating, when no energy was used in walking in the weightless ship, was indeed a welcome change.

“Uh-huh. I got hungry. Any objections?” grinned the Irishman.

“None!” replied Evans fervently, pitching in with a will.

Seated at the controls once more, he snapped the little switch that caused the screen to glow with flashing, swirling colors as the telectroscope apparatus came to life. A thousand tiny points of flame appeared scattered on a black field with a suddenness that made them seem to snap suddenly into being. Points, tiny dimensionless points of light, save one, a tiny disc of blue-white flame, old Sol from a distance of close to one billion miles, and under slight reverse magnification. The skillful hands at the controls were turning adjustments now, and that disc of flame seemed to leap toward him with a hundred light-speeds, growing to a disc as large as a dime in an instant, while the myriad points of the stars seemed to scatter like frightened chickens, fleeing from the growing sun, out of the screen. Other points, heretofore invisible, appeared, grew, and rushed away.

The sun shifted from the center of the screen, and a smaller reddish-green disc came into view—a planet, its atmosphere coloring the light that left it toward the red. It rushed nearer, grew larger. Earth spread as it took the center of the screen. A world, a portion of a world, a continent, a fragment of a continent as the magnification increased, boundlessly it seemed.

===Chinese===
“听着倒像一支舰队的动力储备。火星铅，还是地球的同位素？”埃文斯问着，小心翼翼地尝了一口面前那道古怪的菜。“嘿，这是能量食品。我还以为要到星期六才另有供应呢。”太空飞行员在无重的船舱里行走不耗体力，为防吃得过多，伙食向来以不含能量的调味糊为主，如今换成能量食品，着实是个令人欢迎的变化。

“不错。我饿了。有意见吗？”这爱尔兰人咧嘴一笑。

“没有！”埃文斯答得斩钉截铁，兴致勃勃地大快朵颐起来。

他重新在操纵台前坐下，啪地扳动那个小开关，电视望远镜装置应声苏醒，屏幕上顿时闪动起旋舞流转的色光。上千个细小的火焰光点撒在黑色天幕上，来得如此突兀，仿佛砰然一声凭空显现。光点，一颗颗小到没有尺寸的光点——唯有一个例外：一小片蓝白色火焰的圆盘，那是从将近十亿英里外望见的太阳，正处在轻微的反向放大之中。操纵台上那双灵巧的手此刻转动着调节旋钮，那片火焰圆盘仿佛以百倍光速向他扑来，转瞬便胀成一枚十美分硬币大小的圆盘；而满天星斗的无数光点像受惊的鸡群四散奔逃，逃离那轮越来越大的太阳，纷纷退出屏幕。还有些此前看不见的光点，冒了出来，胀大，又疾掠而去。

太阳移出屏幕中心，一个略小的、泛着红绿色的圆盘进入视野——那是一颗行星，它的大气把离开它的光染向红色。它扑面而来，越来越大。地球（Earth）占据了屏幕中心，在眼前铺展开来。一个世界，一个世界的一隅，一块大陆，一块大陆的碎片——随着放大倍数不断攀升，仿佛无边无际。

===Original===
Finally, New York spread across the screen; New York seen from the air, with a strange lack of perspective. The buildings did not seem all to slant toward some point, but to stand vertical, for, from a distance of a billion miles, the vision lines were practically parallel. Titanic shafts of glowing color in the early summer sun appeared; the hot rays from the sun, now only 82,500,000 miles away, shimmering on the colored metal walls.

The new Airlines Building, a mile and a half high, supported at various points by actual spaceship driving units, was a riot of shifting, rainbow hues. A new trick in construction had been used here, and Evans smiled at it. Arcot, inventor of the ship that carried him, had suggested it to Fuller, designer of that ship, and of that building. The colored berylium metal of the wall had been ruled with 20,000 lines to the inch, mere scratches, but nevertheless a diffraction grating. The result was amazingly beautiful. The sunlight, split up to its rainbow colors, was reflected in millions of shifting tints.

In the air, supported by tiny packs strapped to their backs, thousands of people were moving, floating where they wished, in any direction, at any elevation. There were none of the helicopters of even five years ago, now. A molecular power suit was far more convenient, cost nothing to operate, and but $50 to buy. Perfectly safe, requiring no skill, everyone owned them. To the watcher in space, they were mere moving, snaky lines of barely distinguishable dots that shivered and seemed to writhe in the refractions of the air. Passing over them, seeming to pass almost through them in this strange perspectiveless view, were the shadowy forms of giant space liners, titanic streamlined hulls. They were streamlined for no good reason, save that they looked faster and more graceful than the more efficient spherical freighters, just as passenger liners of two centuries earlier, with their steam engines, had carried four funnels and used two. A space liner spent so minute a portion of its journey in the atmosphere that it was really inefficient to streamline them.

“Won’t be long!” muttered Russ, grinning cheerily at the familiar, sunlit city. His eyes darted to the chronometer beside him. The view seemed to be taken from a ship that was suddenly scudding across the heavens like a frightened thing, as it ran across from Manhattan Island, followed the Hudson for a short way, then cut across into New Jersey, swinging over the great woodland area of Kittatiny Park, resting finally on the New Jersey suburb of New York nestled in the Kittatinies, Blairtown. Low apartment buildings, ten or twelve stories high, nestled in the waving green of trees in the old roadways. When ground traffic ceased, the streets had been torn up, and parkways substituted.

Quickly the view singled out a single apartment, and the great smooth roof was enlarged on the screen to the absolute maximum clarity, till further magnification simply resulted in worse stratospheric distortion. On the broad roof were white strips of some material, making a huge V followed by two I’s. Russ watched, his hand on the control steadying the view under the Earth’s complicated orbital motion, and rotation, further corrections for the ship’s orbital motion making the job one requiring great skill. The view held the center with amazing clarity. Something seemed to be happening to the last of the I’s. It crumpled suddenly, rolled in on itself and disappeared.

===Chinese===
最后，纽约铺满了屏幕；这是从空中望见的纽约，带着一种奇异的无透视感。楼宇似乎并不向某一点倾斜收拢，而是根根垂直耸立，因为从十亿英里外望去，视线几乎完全平行。初夏的阳光下，一根根巍然的巨型彩色光柱显现出来；此刻太阳只在 82,500,000 英里之外，灼热的光焰在彩色金属墙上闪闪烁烁。

新建的航空大厦（Airlines Building）高达一英里半，好几处由如假包换的飞船推进装置支撑着，通体是变幻不休的彩虹色调。这里用上了建筑上的一个新花样，埃文斯见了不禁莞尔。那是阿科特（Arcot）——他所乘这艘飞船的发明者——向富勒（Fuller）出的主意；后者既是那艘船的设计师，也是这座大厦的设计师。墙上有色铍金属表面刻着每英寸 20,000 条的细纹，不过是道道划痕罢了，却俨然构成一面衍射光栅。其效果美得惊人：阳光被分解成彩虹诸色，反射出流转不定的万千色晕。

半空中，成千上万的人背着绑在背后的小小行囊来来往往，随心所欲地飘向任何方向、任何高度。就连五年前那种直升机，如今也绝迹了。一套分子动力服（molecular power suit）要方便得多，运转分文不费，买来不过 50 美元；绝对安全，不需要任何技巧，于是人手一套。在太空中的观察者眼里，他们只是一条条蠕动的、蛇形的点线，勉强可辨的小点在空气折射中颤动，仿佛在扭曲翻腾。从他们头顶掠过的，是一艘艘巨型太空班轮朦胧的身影——庞大无比的流线型船体，在这种怪异的无透视景象里，仿佛几乎要从人群中间穿行而过。它们的流线型其实毫无必要，只不过比效率更高的球形货船看上去更快、更优雅罢了；正如两个世纪前那些烧蒸汽机的客轮，明明竖着四根烟囱，却只用其中两根。太空班轮的航程中只有极小一段在大气层内，把它们造成流线型实在是件不划算的事。

“用不了多久啦！”拉斯低声咕哝，望着这座阳光遍照的熟悉城市，欢快地咧嘴一笑。他的目光扫向身旁的天文钟。屏幕上的景象仿佛取自一艘骤然飞掠天际的飞船，像什么受惊的小兽一样疾驰：它从曼哈顿岛（Manhattan Island）横掠而过，沿哈德逊河（the Hudson）行了一小段，随即斜插进新泽西（New Jersey），荡过基塔提尼公园（Kittatiny Park）那片广袤的林区，最后停在偎依在基塔提尼山（the Kittatinies）间的纽约郊区——布莱尔敦（Blairtown）。十层十二层高的低矮公寓楼，掩映在旧日街道两旁摇曳的绿树之间。地面交通废止之后，街道被掘开，改成了林荫园路。

画面迅速锁定其中一栋公寓，那宽阔平滑的屋顶在屏幕上被放大到绝对清晰的最大限度——再放大就只剩更糟的平流层畸变了。宽阔的屋顶上贴着某种白色材料制成的条带，拼成一个巨大的 V，后面跟着两个 I。拉斯注视着，一只手按在控制器上，在地球复杂的轨道运动与自转之下稳住画面；还要为飞船自身的轨道运动作进一步校正，这活儿极见功力。画面牢牢居中，清晰得惊人。后面那个 I 似乎出了事——它猛然皱缩，向内一卷，消失了。

===Original===
“She’s there, and on time,” grinned Russ happily.

He tried more magnification. Could he—

He was tired, terribly, suddenly tired. He took his hands from the viewplate controls, relaxed, and dropped off to sleep.

“What made me so tired—wonder—*God*!” He straightened with a jerk, and his hands flew to the controls. The view on the machine suddenly retreated, flew back with a velocity inconceivable. Earth dropped away from the ship with an apparent velocity a thousand times that of light; it was a tiny ball, a pinpoint, gone, the sun—a minute disc—gone—then the apparatus was flashing views into focus from the other side of the ship. The assistant did not reply. Evans’ hands were growing ineffably heavy, his whole body yearned for sleep. Slowly, clumsily he pawed for a little stud. Somehow his hand found it, and the ship reeled suddenly, little jerks, as the code message was flung out in a beam of such tremendous power that the sheer radiation pressure made it noticeable. Earth would be notified. The system would be warned. But light, slow crawling thing, would take hours to cross the gulf of space, and radio travels no faster.

===Chinese===
“她在那儿呢，一分钟也不差。”拉斯乐呵呵地咧嘴而笑。

他试着再放大些。能不能看得更清——

他忽然倦了，倦得厉害，来得猝不及防。他的双手从观测屏控制器上滑落，浑身一松，便坠入了睡眠。

“我怎么忽然这么累——怪了——*上帝*！”他猛地一挺身，双手飞扑向操纵台。机器上的画面骤然倒退，以一种不可思议的速度飞掠而回。地球以表面上千倍于光速的速度离船远去；它缩成一个小球，一个针尖，没了；太阳——一个小小的圆盘——也没了；接着，装置开始从飞船另一侧闪电般调来景象聚焦成形。助手没有应声。埃文斯的双手正变得说不出的沉重，全身上下都渴求着睡眠。他迟缓而笨拙地摸索一枚小小的按钮。不知怎的，手终于碰到了它，飞船猛地一阵踉跄，接着是一连串轻微的抖动——电码讯号正从一束功率大得惊人的波束中掷出，单是那辐射压就已明显可感。地球会得到通知，太阳系会得到警报。可是光，这缓慢爬行的东西，得花上好几个钟头才能跨过这道太空鸿沟，而电波也快不了分毫。

===Original===
Half conscious, fighting for his faculties with all his will, the pilot turned to the screen. A ship! A strange, glistening thing streamlined to the *n*th degree, every spare corner rounded till the resistance was at the irreducible minimum. But, in the great pilotport of the stranger, the patrol pilot saw faces, and gasped in surprise as he saw them! Terrible faces, blotched, contorted. Patches of white skin, patches of brown, patches of black, blotched and twisted across the faces. Long, lean faces, great wide flat foreheads above, skulls strangely squared, more boxlike than man’s rounded skull. The ears were large, pointed tips at the top. Their hair was a silky mane that extended low over the forehead, and ran back, spreading above the ears, and down the neck.

Then, as that emotion of surprise and astonishment weakened his will momentarily, oblivion came, with what seemed a fleeting instant of memories. His life seemed to flash before his mind in serried rank, a file of events, his childhood, his life, his marriage, his wife, an image of smiling comfort, then the years, images of great and near great men, his knowledge of history, pictures of great war of 2074, pictures of the attackers of the Black Star—then calm oblivion, quiet blankness.

The long, silent ship that had hovered near him turned, and pointed toward the pinhead of matter that glowed brilliantly in the flaming jewel box of the heavens. It was gone in an instant, rushing toward Sun and Earth at a speed that outraced the flying radio message, leaving the ship of the Guard Patrol behind, and leaving the Pilot as he leaves our story.

===Chinese===
飞行员半昏半醒，以全部意志与浑沉的倦意争夺着神志，转向屏幕。一艘飞船！一个奇异的、亮闪闪的东西，流线型到了 *n* 次方的极致，每一处多余的棱角都被磨圆，直到阻力降到无法再降的最低限。然而，就在那陌生来客巨大的驾驶窗里，这名巡逻飞行员看见了脸——他一见之下，惊得倒抽一口冷气！可怕的脸，斑驳狰狞。一块块白皮，一块块棕斑，一块块黑斑，斑驳扭曲地爬满一张张面孔。瘦长的脸，上方是宽阔扁平的额头，头颅方得出奇，比人类浑圆的头骨更像一只方匣。耳朵很大，顶端尖尖。他们的头发是丝绸般的鬃毛，低低压在额上，向后铺展，漫过耳廓，一直垂到颈后。

接着，就在这份惊愕与震骇令他的意志松懈了一瞬的当口，昏迷袭来，携着仿佛记忆的倏忽一闪。他的一生似乎在脑海中列队闪过，一长串往事：他的童年，他的成长，他的婚礼，他的妻子，一张含笑的、令人心安的面容；然后是这些年月，伟人与准伟人的身影，他脑海中的历史，2074 年那场大战的画面，进袭黑星（Black Star）者的画面——然后是平静的昏迷，寂静的空白。

那艘在他近旁悬停已久的修长的沉默飞船转过身，直指苍穹这只烈焰熊熊的珠宝匣中那一点灿然生辉、针尖大小的微尘。顷刻之间，它已无影无踪，以一种赶超那道疾飞的无线电讯的速度直扑太阳与地球，把警卫巡逻队（Guard Patrol）的飞船抛在身后，也把这位飞行员抛在了身后——正如他从此离开了我们的故事。

## XXVI / 人——创造者与毁灭者

===Original===
"What we must find," said Arcot, between contented puffs, for he had slept well, and his breakfast had been good, "is some weapon which will attack them, but won't attack us. The question is, what is it? And I think, I think—I know." His eyes were dreamy, his thoughts so cryptically abbreviated that not even Morey could follow them.

"Fine—what is it?" asked Morey after vainly striving to deduce some sense from the formulas that were chasing through Arcot's thoughts. Here and there he recognized them: Einstein's energy formula, Planck's quantum formulas, Nitsu Thansi's electron interference formulas, Stebkowfski's proton interference, Williamson's electric field, and his own formulas appeared, and others so abbreviated he could not recognize them.

"Do you remember what Dad said about the way the Thessians made the giant forts out in space—hauled matter from the moon and transformed it to lux and relux. Remember, I said then I thought it might be a ray—but found it wasn't what I thought? I want to to use the ray I was thinking of. The only question in my mind is—what is going to happen to us when I use it?"

===Chinese===
“我们必须找到的，”阿科特（Arcot）在心满意足的吞云吐雾之间说道，他昨夜睡得香甜，早餐又极其可口，“是一种能攻击他们、却不会伤到我们的武器。问题在于，那是什么？而我想，我想——我知道了。”他目光如梦，思绪压缩得隐晦至极，连莫里（Morey）也跟不上。

“好啊——是什么？”莫里问道。他方才竭力想从追逐过阿科特思绪的那些公式里推出点意思来，却是徒劳。那些公式他东一处西一处还能认得出：爱因斯坦的能量公式、普朗克的量子公式、尼苏·坦西（Nitsu Thansi）的电子干涉公式、斯特布考夫斯基（Stebkowfski）的质子干涉公式、威廉逊（Williamson）的电场公式，还有他自己的公式，其间也夹杂着一些简略得无从辨认的公式。

“你还记得爸爸讲过塞特人（Thessian）是怎么在外层空间造起那些巨型要塞的吗——从月球拖来物质，再把它们变成卢克斯（lux）和雷卢克斯（relux）。记得我当时说过，我猜那靠的是一种射线——后来却发现跟我所想的不一样？我如今要用的，正是我那时想到的那种射线。我心里唯一的问题是——一旦用它，我们自己会落个什么下场？”

===Original===
"What's the ray?"

"Why is it, Morey, that an electron falls through the different quantum energy levels, falls successively lower and lower till it reaches its 'lowest energy level,' and can radiate no more. Why can't it fill another step, and reach the proton? Why has it no more quanta to release? We know that electrons tend to fall always to lower energy level orbits. Why do they stop?"

"And," said Morey, his own eyes dreamily bright now, "what would happen if it did? If it fell all the way?"

"I cannot follow your thoughts, Earthmen, beyond a glimpse of an explosion. And it seems it is Thett that is exploding, and that Thett is exploding itself. Can you explain?" asked Stel Felso Theu.

===Chinese===
“什么射线？”

“莫里，你说这是为什么：一个电子沿着一个个量子能级跌落下去，一级接一级，越落越低，直到抵达它的‘最低能级’（lowest energy level），便再不能辐射分毫。为什么它不能再往下填一级，直落到质子上？为什么它再没有量子可以释放了？我们知道，电子总是倾向于跌向更低的能级轨道。它们为什么偏偏停住了？”

“还有，”莫里说，此刻他自己的眼里也闪着梦一般的光亮，“倘若它当真跌下去了，会怎样？倘若它一跌到底呢？”

“二位地球人的思路，我跟不上，只瞥见一场爆炸的影子。而且看那情形，爆炸的似乎是塞特星（Thett），而且是塞特星把自己炸掉。你们能解释解释吗？”斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤（Stel Felso Theu）问道。

===Original===
"Perhaps—you know that electrons in their planetary orbits, so called, tend to fall away to orbits of lower energy, till they reach the lowest energy orbit, and remain fixed till more energy comes and is absorbed, driving them out again. Now we want to know why they don't fall lower, fall all the way? As a matter of fact, thanks to some work I did last year with disintegrating lead, we do know. And thanks to the absolute stability of artificial matter, we can handle such a condition.

"The thing we are interested in is this: Artificial matter has no tendency to radiate, its electrons have no tendency to fall into the proton, for the matter is created, and remains as it was created. But natural matter does have a tendency to let the electron fall into the proton. A force, the 'lowest energy wall,' over which no electron can jump, caused by the enormous space distorting of the proton's mass and electrical attraction, prevents it. What we want to do is to remove that force, iron it out. Requires inconceivable power to do so in a mass the size of Thett—but then—!

"And here's what will happen: Our wall of protonic material won't be affected by it in the least, because it has no tendency to collapse, as has normal matter, but Thett, beyond the wall, *has* that tendency, and the ray will release the energy of every planetary electron on Thett, and every planetary electron will take with it the energy of one proton. And it will take about one one-hundred-millionth of a second. Thett will disappear in one instantaneous flash of radiation, radiation in the high cosmics!

===Chinese===
“或许能解释——你们知道，电子在它们那所谓的‘行星轨道’上，倾向于跌向能量更低的轨道，直到抵达能量最低的轨道，便固定下来，直到有更多的能量传来、被它吸收，再把它重新赶出去。如今我们想知道的是：它们为什么不再往下落、一落到底？其实呢，多亏我去年做过的几项铅蜕变实验，我们确实知道答案。而多亏人工物质（artificial matter）的绝对稳定，这样一种局面我们也驾驭得住。

“我们关心的是这一点：人工物质没有辐射的倾向，它的电子没有跌入质子的倾向，因为那物质是被创造出来的，便一直保持被创造时的模样。天然物质却当真有一种倾向，要让电子跌进质子里去。有一股力阻止它这么做——那便是‘最低能墙’（lowest energy wall），任何电子都跳不过去的高墙；它由质子的质量与电性引力所造成的巨大空间畸变而生。我们要做的，就是把那股力消除掉，把它熨平。要在塞特星这样规模的质量上做到这一点，需要难以想象的功率——可是——！

“而接下来会发生的是：我们的质子性物质（protonic material）之墙丝毫不受影响，因为它没有寻常物质那种塌陷的倾向；可墙那一头的塞特星*有*那种倾向。射线会把塞特星上每一颗行星电子的能量都释放出来，而每一颗行星电子还会顺带带走一颗质子的能量。这整个过程只消大约一亿分之一秒。塞特星将在一次瞬发的辐射闪光中消失——极高能宇宙射线频段的辐射！

===Original===
"Here's the trouble: Thett represents a mass as great as our sun. And our sun can throw off energy at the present rate of one sol for a period of some ten million million years, three and a half million tons of matter a second for ten million years. If all of that went up in *one one-hundred-millionth of a second*, how many sols?" asked Morey.

"Too many, is all I can say. Even this ship couldn't maintain its walls of energy against that!" declared Stel Felso Theu, awed by the thought.

"But that same power would be backing this ship, and helping it to support its wall. We would operate from—half a million miles."

===Chinese===
“麻烦就在这里：塞特星代表着一个跟我们的太阳一般大的质量。我们的太阳以如今每秒一个索尔（sol）的速率倾泻能量，能倾泻上十万亿年之久——相当于每秒三百五十万吨物质，足足烧上一千万年。要是这一切在*一亿分之一秒*里统统放出来，那是多少个索尔？”莫里问道。

“多得我没法说。我只知道，就连这艘船也撑不住它的能量之墙去抵挡那个！”斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤大声说道，这个念头令他心生敬畏。

“可托住这艘船、帮它撑住那道墙的，恰恰也是同一股力量。我们就从——五十万英里之外动手。”

===Original===
"We will. If we are destroyed—so is Thett, and all the worlds of Thett. Let that flood of energy get loose, and everything within a dozen light years will be destroyed. We will have to warn the Venonians, that their people on nearby worlds may escape in the time before the energy reaches them," said Arcot slowly.

The *Thought* started toward one of the nearer suns, and as it went, Arcot and Morey were busy with the calculators. They finished their work, and started back from that world, having given their message of warning, with the artificial matter constructors. When they reached Thett, less than a quarter of an hour of Thessian time had passed. But, before they reached Thett, Arcot's viewplates were blinded for an instant as a terrific flood of energy struck the artificial matter protectors, and caused them to flame into defense. Thett's satellite was sending its message of instantaneous destruction. That terrific ray had reached it, touched it, and left it a shattered, glowing ball of hydrogen.

"There won't be even that left when we get through with Thett!" said Arcot grimly. The apparatus was finished, and once more they were over the now fiery-red lava sea that had been mountains. The fort was still in action. Arcot had cut a sheet of sheer energy now, and as the triple-ray struck it, he knew what would happen. It did. The triple-ray shunted off at an angle of forty-five degrees in the energy field, and spread instantly to a diffused beam of blackness. Arcot's molecular reached out. The lava was instantly black, and mountains of ice were forming over the struggling defenses of the fort. The molecular screen was working.

===Chinese===
“我们会的。就算我们被毁——塞特星照样被毁，塞特星的全部世界也一样。那股能量的洪水一旦脱缰，方圆十几光年之内的一切都将毁灭。我们得先警告维诺恩人（Venonian），好让他们近旁各世界上的人民，赶在能量抵达之前逃出去，”阿科特缓缓说道。

*思想号*（Thought）朝较近的一颗恒星驶去，一路之上，阿科特和莫里忙着操作计算装置。活计做完，警告的讯息也已送到，他们便带着人工物质建构装置，从那个世界启程返航。抵达塞特星时，塞特人时间才过去不到一刻钟。可就在到达塞特星之前，一股骇人的能量洪流猛然击中人工物质防护层，逼得它们燃起防御之焰，阿科特的遥视屏刹那间一片盲黑。塞特星的卫星，正在播送它瞬间毁灭的讯息——那道可怕的射线已经够到了它，触到了它，只留下一颗炸碎的、兀自炽燃的氢质球体。

“等我们跟塞特星了结的时候，连这点残骸都不会剩下！”阿科特狠声说道。装置已经备好，他们再度飞临那片火红的熔岩之海——那里原本是崇山峻岭。要塞仍在开火。这一回，阿科特切出了一整面纯粹能量之幕；三重射线（triple ray）撞上来时，他知道会发生什么。果然如此：三重射线在能量场中偏折开去，成四十五度角，顷刻散成一束漫射的黑流。阿科特的分子射线伸了出去。熔岩霎时转黑，一座座冰山，正在要塞负隅顽抗的防御上方凝结成形。分子射线屏障正在起作用。

===Original===
"I'd like to know how they make tubes that'll stand that, Morey," said Arcot, pointing to an instrument that read .01 millisols. "They have tubes now, that would have wiped us out in minutes, seconds before this."

The triple-ray snapped off. They were realigning it to hit the ship now, correcting for the shield. Arcot threw out his protonic shield, and retreated to half a million miles, as he had said.

"Here goes." But before even his thoughts could send Theft to radiation, the entire side of the planet blazed suddenly incandescent. Thett was learning what had happened when their ray had wounded the *Thought*.

===Chinese===
“真想知道，他们那种受得住这股劲的功率管是怎么造出来的，莫里，”阿科特指着一台读数为 0.01 毫索尔（millisol）的仪表说道，“他们如今这些管子，这等威力，换在先前，几分钟、几秒钟就能把我们消灭干净。”

三重射线戛然而止。他们正在重新校调射线，要直接命中飞船，并针对护盾作出修正。阿科特张开他的质子护盾（protonic shield），照他先前所说的，退到了五十万英里之外。

“这就放了。”然而甚至还没等他的思想把塞特星送去化为辐射，行星的整整一面已骤然烧成白炽。塞特星这是在亲身体会：当初他们的射线伤了*思想号*，招来的是什么下场。

===Original===
And then, in the barest instant of time, there was no Thett. There was an instant of intolerable radiation, then momentary blackness, and then the stars were shining where Thett had been. Thett was utterly gone.

But Arcot did not see this. About him there was a tremendous roar, titanic generator-converters that had not so much as hummed under the impact of Thett's greatest weapons, whined and shuddered now. The two enormous generators, the blackness of the protonic shield, and the great artificial matter generator, throwing an inner shield impervious to the cosmics Thett gave off as it vanished, both were whining. And the six smaller machines, which Arcot had succeeded in interconnecting with the protonic generator, were whining too. Space was weirdly distorted, glowing gray about them, the great generators struggling to maintain the various walls of protecting power against the surge of energy as Thett, a world of matter, disintegrated.

But the very energy that fought to destroy those walls was absorbed in defending it, and by that much the attacking energy was lessened. Still, it seemed hours, days that the battle of forces continued.

Then it was over, and the skies were clear once more as Arcot lowered the protonic screen silently. The white sky of Thett was gone, and only the black starriness of space remained.

===Chinese===
而后，在最短促不过的一瞬之间，塞特星不复存在。一阵令人无法忍受的辐射闪过，继而是片刻的黑暗，随后，星辰正在塞特星曾在之处闪耀。塞特星彻底消失了。

但这一切阿科特无暇看见。他四周是一片惊天动地的轰鸣——在塞特星最强武器的轰击下连哼都不曾哼一声的那些巨型发电机变流器（generator-converter），此刻竟呜咽震颤起来。两台庞大的发电机、漆黑如墨的质子护盾，还有那台抛出内层护罩、足以挡住塞特星湮灭时迸发的一切宇宙射线的大型人工物质发电机，全都在呜鸣。而阿科特设法与质子发电机互联起来的那六台较小的机器，也在呜鸣。空间诡异地扭曲着，四周泛起灰光——巨大的发电机正拼死撑住一层层防护力之墙，抵挡塞特星这颗物质世界解体时迸涌而来的能量狂潮。

然而，那股竭力要摧毁这些墙的能量，恰恰被防御自身吸收了去，进攻的能量也因之削弱了相应的一份。尽管如此，这场力量对力量的鏖战，持续得仿佛有几个钟头、几天之久。

然后一切终了。阿科特默默降下质子护盾，天穹重归澄澈。塞特星那白色的天空不见了，只剩下空间里黑色的星辰。

===Original===
"*It's gone!*" gasped Torlos. He had been expecting it—still, the disappearance of a world—

"We will have to do no more. No ships had time to escape, and the risk we run is too great," said Morey slowly. "The escaping energy from that world will destroy the others of this system as completely, and it will probably cause the sun itself to blow up—perhaps to form new planets, and so the process repeats itself. But Venone knows better now, and their criminals will not populate more worlds.

"And we can go—home. To our little dust specks."

"But they're wonderfully welcome dust specks, and utterly important to us, Earthman," reminded Zezdon Afthen.

"Let us go then," said Arcot.

===Chinese===
“*没有了！*”托洛斯（Torlos）喘息道。他原是料到了的——然而，一个世界的消失——

“我们不必再动手了。没有任何飞船来得及逃脱，而我们再去冒险，代价也太大，”莫里缓缓说道，“从那个世界逸出的能量，会把本星系其余的世界毁灭得同样彻底，多半还会叫那颗恒星本身炸开——或许就此炸出新的行星来，于是这个循环再度重演。只是维诺恩（Venone）如今看明白了，他们的罪犯也再不能去充实更多的世界。

“我们也可以走了——回家。回到我们那些小小的尘埃微粒上去。”

“可对地球人来说，那些尘埃微粒却是无比可亲的，而且是至关重要之物，”泽兹登·阿夫森（Zezdon Afthen）提醒道。

“那么，走吧，”阿科特说。

---

===Original===
