The tubes of the power bank were, however, a puzzle to them. They were made of relux, so that it was impossible to see the interior of the tube. To open one was to destroy it, but calculations made from readings of their instruments showed that they were more efficient, and could readily carry nearly half again the load that the best terrestrian tubes could sustain. This meant the enemy could send heavier rays and heavier ray screens.

But finally they returned to the *Ancient Mariner*, and as the Ortolian ship whined its way out to space, the *Ancient Mariner* started, rising faster and faster through the atmosphere till it was in the night of space. Then the molecular power was shut off. The ship suddenly seemed to writhe, space was black and starless about them, then sparkling weirdly distorted stars, all before them. They were moving already. Almost before the Ortolians fully realized what was happening, a dozen stars had swung past the ship, driving on now at better than five light years in every second. At this speed, approximately fourteen hours would be needed to reach Ortol.

===Chinese===
这时，一名身材颀长、面目和善的犬族之人（canine people）立在舱门口，白色的灯光从他周身涌进夜色，在着陆场上拖出一道怪影——纵然整片场地都浸在泛光灯的光海里。

泽兹登·阿夫森迎上前去，与那位显然指挥着这条船的犬族之人急速交谈。全体一行人随之登船，他们实验室里最精良的仪器也一并搬了进去。

一连几个小时，阿科特、莫里和韦德埋头研究船上的装置：测量、计算，追索那些复杂得令人咋舌的电、磁与纯力路接线。他们无意找出确切的建造方法，只想弄清其中涉及的原理，好自行推算，复现敌人的成果。这样等造出自己的，他们对这类机器的了解会透彻得多。

至于真正的推进机组，他们没太费神——那是一套分子推进装置（molecular drive），配的铅燃料燃烧器与他们自己船上的同一类型。然而能库（power bank）里的功率管却叫他们犯了难。管子由雷卢克斯制成，看不见内部；打开一根，便毁掉一根。但根据仪表读数推算的结果表明，这些管子效率更高，且能轻松承受几乎比地球人最好的功率管还高出一半的负荷。这意味着敌人能发射更强的射线，也能张开更厚的射线屏障。

最后，他们回到*老水手号*。奥托尔人的船啸鸣着冲入太空的同时，*老水手号*启动了，在大气层里越升越快，直入太空的暗夜。随后分子动力切断。飞船蓦地仿佛扭动了一瞬，四周的空间漆黑无星，接着，一片闪烁的、怪异扭曲的星光在船首前浮现。他们已经在航程之中。奥托尔人还没完全回过神来，已有十几颗星从船侧掠过——船此刻正以每秒五光年有余的速度疾驰。照这个速度，抵达奥托尔（Ortol）大约需要十四个小时。

===Original===
“Now, Arcot, perhaps you will explain to me the secret of this ship,” said Zezdon Afthen at last, turning from the great lux pilot’s window, to Arcot seated in the pilot’s chair. “I know that only the broadest principles will be intelligible to me, for I could not understand that ship we captured, after almost four months of study. Yet it crept through space compared with this ship. Certainly no ship could outdistance this in a race!”

“As a matter of fact—watch!” Arcot pushed a little metal button along a slide to the extreme end. Again the ship seemed to writhe. Space was no longer black, but faintly gray, and beside them, on either side, floated two exact replicas of their ship! Zezdon Afthen stared. But in another moment, both were gone, and space was black, yet in but a few moments a grayness was showing, and light was appearing from all about, growing gradually in intensity. For three seconds Arcot continued thus, then he pulled the metal button down the slide, and flicked over another that he had pulled to cause the second change. The stars were again before them, their colors changed beyond all recognition at that speed. But the orientation of the stars behind them had been familiar. Now an entirely different set of constellation showed.

“I merely opened the ship out to her maximum speed for a moment. I was able to see any large star 2000 light years in our path, and there were none. Small stars do not bother us as I will explain. When I put on full power of the main power coils, I drove the ship up to a speed of 30 light years a second. When I turned in the full power of the auxiliary coils as well I doubled the power, and the speed was multiplied by eight. The result was that in the four seconds of racing, we made approximately 1000 light years!”

Zezdon Afthen gasped. “Two hundred and forty light years *per second*!” He paused in bewilderment. “Suppose we had struck a small sun, a dark star, even a meteor at that speed? What would have been the result?”

Arcot smiled. “The chances are excellent that we plowed through more than one meteor, more than one dark star, and more than one small sun.

“But this is the secret: the ship attains the speed only by going out of space. *Nothing in space can attain the speed of light, save radiation.* Nothing in normal space. But, we alter space, make space along patterns we choose, and so distort it that the natural speed of radiation is enormously greater. In fact, we so change space that nothing can go *slower* than a speed we fix.

===Chinese===
“那么，阿科特，现在总该给我讲讲这艘船的秘密了吧，”泽兹登·阿夫森终于开口，从那扇巨大的卢克斯驾驶窗前转过身来，望向坐在驾驶椅上的阿科特。“我知道，至多是最粗浅的原理我才能听懂——我们俘虏的那艘船，我研究了将近四个月也没弄明白。可比起这艘船来，它简直是在空间里爬行。论赛跑，肯定没有哪条船能胜过它！”

“事实上——看着！”阿科特把一枚小小的金属按钮沿滑槽推到尽头。飞船又像扭动了一下。空间不再漆黑，而是泛起浅灰；两侧，赫然悬浮着两艘与他们分毫不差的复制品！泽兹登·阿夫森看直了眼。但转眼之间，两艘都消失了，空间重归漆黑；又过片刻，灰蒙蒙的微光渐渐显出，光从四面八方透进来，越来越亮。如此持续了三秒钟，阿科特把金属按钮拉回滑槽下端，又拨回了另一个开关——先前正是扳下它造成了第二次变化。群星重新出现在眼前，颜色在这个速度下已变得无从辨认。方才他们身后那片星野还是熟悉的方位，此刻展现的已是全然不同的另一组星座。

“我不过是让船把最大速度开出来跑了一瞬。沿我们航路 2000 光年以内的任何一颗大恒星都逃不过我的眼睛，而那里一颗也没有。小恒星碍不着我们，理由我随后解释。主功率线圈全力投入时，船被推到每秒 30 光年。再把辅助线圈的功率尽数并入，功率加倍，速度便提到八倍。结果就是：全速疾驰的这四秒钟里，我们大约走出了 1000 光年！”

泽兹登·阿夫森倒抽一口冷气。“每秒 240 光年！”他惶然怔了半晌。“假如我们在那个速度下撞上一颗小太阳、一颗暗星，甚至一颗陨石呢？会是什么下场？”

阿科特微微一笑。“十有八九，我们这一路已经犁穿过不止一颗陨石、不止一颗暗星，也不止一颗小太阳了。

“而秘密就在这里：船唯有逸出空间，才能达到那个速度。*空间中没有任何东西能达到光速，辐射除外。*正常空间里的任何东西都不行。可我们能改造空间，按照自己选定的图样去造空间，把它畸变得使辐射的自然速度大得惊人。事实上，我们把空间改到了这种地步：没有什么能比我们设定的速度更*慢*。

===Original===
“Morey—show Afthen the coils, and explain it all to him. I’ve got to stay here.”

Morey rose, and diving through the weightless ship, went down to the power room, Zezdon Afthen following. Here, giant pots five feet high were in close packed rows. The “pots” contained specially designed coils storing tremendous energy, the energy of four tons of disintegrated lead, in the only form that energy may be stored, as a strain, or distortion in space. These charged coils distorted only the space within themselves, making a closed field entirely within themselves. But in the exact gravitational center of the quarter of a million ton ship was a single high coil of different design that distorted space around it as well as the space within it. This, as Morey explained, was the control that altered the constants of space to suit. The coils were charged, and the energy stored. Their energy could be pumped into the big coil, and then, when the ship slowed to normal space, could be pumped back to them. The pumping energy, as well as any further energy needed for recharging the coils could be supplied by three huge power generators.

“These energy-producers,” Morey explained, “work on a principle known for hundreds of years on Earth. Lead, when reduced to a temperature approaching absolute zero as closely as, for instance, liquid helium, has *no* electrical resistance. In other words, no matter how great a current is sent through it, there is no resistance, and no heat is produced to raise the temperature. What we do is to send a powerful current through a lead wire. The wire has a current density so huge that the atoms are destroyed, and the protons and electrons coalesce into pure radiant energy. Relux, under the influence of a magnetic field, converts this directly into electrical potential. Electricity we can convert to the spatial strain in the power coils, and thus the ship is driven.” Morey pointed out the huge molecular power cylinder overhead, where the main power drive was located in the inertial center of the ship, or as near as the great space coil would permit.

The smaller power units for vertical lift, and for steering, were in the side walls, hidden under heavy walls of relux.

“The projectors for throwing molecular and heat rays are on the outside of course. Both of these projectors are protected. The walls of the ship are made of an outer wall of heavy lux metal, a vacuum between, and an inner wall of heavy relux. The lux is stronger than relux, and is therefore used for an outer shell. The inner shell of relux will reflect any dangerous rays and serve to hold the heat in the ship, since a perfect reflector is a perfect non-radiator. The vacuum wall is to protect the occupants of the ship against any undue heat. If we should get within the atmosphere of a sun, it would be disastrous if the physical conduction of heat were permitted, for though the relux will turn out any radiated heat, it is a conductor of heat, and we would roast almost instantly. These artificial metals are both absolutely infusible and nonvolatile. The ship has actually been in the limb of a star tremendously hotter than your sun or mine.

“Now you see why it is we need not fear a collision with a small sun, meteor or suchlike. Since we are in our own, artificial space, we are alone, and there is nothing in space to run into. But, if we enter a huge sun, the terrific gravitational field of the mass of matter would be enough to pull the energy of our coil away from us. That actually happened the time we made our first intergalactic exploration. But it is almost impossible to fall into a large star—they are too brilliant. We won’t be worrying about it,” grinned Morey.

===Chinese===
“莫里——带阿夫森去看看线圈，把一切讲给他听。我得留在这儿。”

莫里起身，穿过无重力的船身一路俯游而下，来到动力室，泽兹登·阿夫森紧随其后。这里，五英尺高的巨型储能罐排得密密实实。这些“罐子”里装着特制的线圈，储存着惊人的能量——四吨铅蜕变所释放的能量，而且是以能量唯一可被储存的形式：空间的应变，亦即畸变。这些充了能的线圈只畸变自身内部的空间，形成一个完全闭合于自身的场。而在二十五万吨船体的精确引力中心，立着一只设计迥异的高线圈，它连四周的空间也一并畸变。据莫里解释，这正是按需改写空间常数的控制件。线圈充能之后，能量便储存其中：既可以泵入那只大线圈，待船减速、回归正常空间时，又能泵回各线圈。泵送所耗的能量，连同线圈再充电所需的任何补充能量，都可由三台巨型发电机供给。

“这些产能装置，”莫里解释道，“依据的是地球上几百年前便已知晓的一条原理。铅被冷却到接近绝对零度——比如液氦所能达到的程度——电阻便是*零*。换句话说，无论多大的电流通过，都不受阻力，也不生热升温。我们要做的，是让一股强电流通过铅线。铅线的电流密度大到原子被摧毁殆尽，质子与电子聚合成纯辐射能。雷卢克斯在磁场作用下，把这种辐射能直接转换为电位差。电再转成功率线圈中的空间应变，船就这样被驱动了。”莫里指了指头顶那具巨大的分子动力圆筒——主推进装置就设在飞船的惯性中心，或者说，设在那只巨大的空间线圈容许的最接近之处。

垂直升降与转向用的小型动力机组设在侧壁里，深藏在厚重的雷卢克斯壁层之下。

“发射分子射线（molecular ray）和热射线（heat ray）的投射器当然装在船体外部，两种投射器都有防护。船壁的构造是：厚重的卢克斯金属外层，中间隔一层真空，内里是厚重的雷卢克斯层。卢克斯比雷卢克斯更强韧，故用作外壳。雷卢克斯内壳能反射一切危险射线，又能把热量锁在船内——完美的反射体，同时就是完美的不辐射体。真空夹层则保护船员不受任何过量的热。倘若我们闯入某颗太阳的大气层，热量若能靠物理传导涌进来，便是灭顶之灾：雷卢克斯虽把辐射热尽数拒之门外，本身却是热的导体，我们顷刻间就会被烤熟。这两种人造金属绝对不熔、绝不挥发。这艘船确确实实进过一颗恒星的边缘——那颗星比你们的太阳或我们的太阳都要炽热得多。

“现在你该明白，我们为何不怕与小太阳、陨石之类相撞了。既然航行在自己营造的人造空间里，那里便只有我们，空间中没有东西可供相撞。可要是我们闯进一颗巨大的太阳，那团物质的强大引力场就足以把我们线圈里的能量夺走。我们第一次跨星系探险时，这种事就真真切切出过一回。不过，要坠入一颗大恒星几乎不可能——它们太亮了。这个不必担心。”莫里咧嘴一笑。

===Original===
“But how did the ship we captured operate?” asked Zezdon Afthen.

“It was a very ingenious system, very closely related to ours, really.

“We distort space and change the velocity characteristics; in other words, we distort the rate of motion through distance characteristics of normal space. The Thessian ships work on the principle of distorting the rate of progress through time instead of through space.

“*Velocity* is really ‘units of travel through space per unit of travel through time.’ Now if we make the time unit twice as great, and the units traveled through space are not changed, the *velocity* is twice as great. That is, if we are moving five light years per second, make the second twice as long and we are moving ten light years per double-second. Make it ten thousand times as long, and we are traveling fifty thousand light years per ten-thousand-seconds. This is the principle—but there is a drawback. We might increase the velocity by slowing time passage, that is, if it takes me a year for one heartbeat, two years to raise my arm thus, and six months to turn, my head, if all my body processes are slowed down in this way, I will be able to live a tremendous length of time, and though it takes me two hundred years to go from one star to another, so low is my time rate that the two hundred years will seem but a few minutes. I can then make a trip to a distant star—one five light years distant, let us say, in three minutes to me. I then will say, looking at my chronometer (which has been similarly slowed) ‘I have gone five light years in three minutes, or five thirds light years per minute. I have exceeded the speed of light.’

“But people back on Earth would say, he has taken two hundred years to go five light years, therefore he has gone at a speed one fortieth of that of light, which would be true—for their time rate.

“But suppose I can also speed up time. That is, I can live a year in a minute or two. Then everyone else will be exceedingly slow. The ideal thing would be to combine these two effects, arranging that space about your ship will have a very rapid time rate, ten thousand times that of normal space. Then the speed of radiation through that space will be 1,860,000,000 miles per second, and a speed of 1,000,000,000 miles per second would be possible, but still you, too, will be affected, so that though the people back home will say you are going far faster than light, you will say ‘No, I am going only 100,000 miles per second.’

===Chinese===
“那我们俘虏的那艘船，是靠什么飞的呢？”泽兹登·阿夫森问。

“那是一套非常精巧的系统，说到底，与我们的原理一脉相承。

“我们畸变空间，改变速度特性；换句话说，我们畸变的是正常空间里那种穿越距离的运动速率特性。塞特人（Thessian）的船，依据的却是另一个原理：畸变的是穿越时间的行进速率，而不是穿越空间的。

“*速度*，说穿了就是‘单位时间里穿越空间的单位数’。若把时间单位拉长一倍，而穿越的空间单位不变，*速度*便大了一倍。也就是说，假如我们正以每秒五光年行进，把秒拉长一倍，我们就成了每双倍秒十光年。把它拉长一万倍，我们便是每万倍秒五万光年。原理如此——但有个缺陷。我们也可以靠拖慢时间的流逝来‘提高’速度：比方说，我心跳一次要一年，像这样抬一下手臂要两年，转一下头要半年；倘若我全身的机能都这样放慢，我便能活得极久极久。纵然从一颗恒星到另一颗恒星要花上两百年，可我的时间速率低到那两百年于我不过须臾几分钟。这样我便能去一颗遥远的恒星走一遭——比方说，五光年外的那颗——于我自己只需三分钟。然后我望着我的计时器（它也被同样放慢了）说：‘我在三分钟里走了五光年，合每分钟三分之五光年。我超过光速了。’

“可地球上的人会说：他花了两百年走完五光年，所以他只有光速的四十分之一。就他们的时间速率而言，这话没错。

“可再假设我还能把时间加快——也就是说，我把一年活进一两分钟里。那么其余一切都会显得极其迟缓。最理想的，是把这两种效应合而为一：让你飞船四周的空间具有极快的时间速率，是正常空间的一万倍。于是，辐射穿过那片空间的速度就是每秒 1,860,000,000 英里，每秒 1,000,000,000 英里的航速也成为可能；可你自己同样被那种时间速率裹挟，所以尽管家乡的人说你远远超过了光速，你却会说：‘不，我的速度只有每秒 100,000 英里。’

===Original===
“But now imagine that your ship and surrounding space for one mile is at a time rate 10,000 times normal, and you, in a space of one hundred feet within your ship, are affected by a time rate 1/10,000 that, or normal, due to a second, reversing field. The two fields will not fight, or be mutually antagonistic; they will merely compound their effects. Result: you will agree that you are exceeding the speed of light!

“Do you understand? That is the principle on which your ship operated. There were two time-fields, overlapping time-fields. Remember the terrible speed with which your ship landed, and yet there was no appreciable jar according to the men? The answer of course was, that their time rate had been speeded enough, due to the fact that one field had been completely shut off, the other had not.

“That is the principle. The system is so complex, naturally, that we have not yet learned the actual method of working the process. We must do a great deal of mathematical and physical research.

“Wish we had it done—we could use it now,” mused the terrestrian.

“We have some other weapons, none as important, of course, as the molecular ray and the heat ray. Or none that have been. But, if the enemy have ray shields, then perhaps these others also will be important. There are molecular motion guns, metal tubes, with molecular director apparatus at one end. A metal shell is pulling the power turned on, and the shell leaps out at a speed of about ten miles per second—since it has been super-heated—and is very accurately aimed, as there is no terrific shock of recoil to be taken up by the gun.

“But a more effective weapon, if these men are as I expect them to be, will be a peculiarly effective magnetic field concentrator device, which will project a magnetic field as a beam for a mile or more. How useful it will be—I don’t know. We don’t know what the enemy will turn against *us*!”

===Chinese===
“现在再设想：你的飞船连同周身一英里范围的空间，处于一万倍于正常的时间速率之下；而在你船内一百英尺的空间里，由于第二个反向场的作用，你受的时间速率是它的万分之一——也就是正常速率。两个场不会相互抗衡、彼此为敌，只会把各自的效应叠加起来。结果就是：连你自己也会承认，你超过了光速！

“明白了吗？你们那艘船，依据的正是这个原理：两个时间场（time-field），两个彼此交叠的时间场。还记得你们那艘船降落时的速度有多吓人，可据船员们说，却没有感到明显的震撞吗？答案当然是：他们的时间速率已被加快到足够的程度——一个场被彻底切断，另一个却没有。

“原理便是如此。这套系统自然极其复杂，我们尚未掌握实际运用它的方法。这还需要大量的数学与物理学研究。

“要是已经研究成了就好——现在正用得上呢。”这位地球人沉吟道。

“我们还有另外几种武器。当然，论要紧，没有一样比得上分子射线和热射线——至少迄今如此。可倘若敌人有射线护盾，那么其余这些或许也要紧起来。有分子运动炮（molecular motion gun）：金属炮管，一端装有分子导向装置。装入一枚金属弹丸，功率一通，弹丸便跃身而出，速度约每秒 10 英里——因为它已被过热——而且瞄准极为精准，因为炮身无须承受巨大的后坐冲击。

“不过，倘若这些人真如我所料，那么更见成效的武器，将是一种效能奇佳的磁场聚束装置（magnetic field concentrator）——它能把磁场像光束一样投射出一英里开外。它究竟有多大用处——我不知道。我们可不知道敌人会拿什么来对付*我们*！”

## IX / 无坚不摧与岿然不动

===Original===

The generator Arcot had brought was one of the two spare generators used for laboratory work. He took it now into the substation, and directed the Talsonian students and the scientist in the task of connecting it into the lines; though they knew where it belonged, he knew *how* it belonged.

Then the terrestrian turned on the power, and gradually increased it until the power authorities were afraid of breakdowns. The accumulators were charged in the city, and the power was being shipped to other cities whose accumulators were not completely charged.

But, after giving simple operating instructions to the students, Arcot and Morey went with Stel Felso Theu to his laboratory.

===Chinese===

阿科特（Arcot）带来的那台发电机，是实验室用的两台备用发电机之一。他把它搬进变电站，指挥塔尔索人（Talsonian）的学生和那位科学家把它接进线路；他们虽然知道它该接到哪里，他却知道它该*怎样*接法。

随后，这位地球人（terrestrian）合闸送电，功率一点点加大，直加得电力主管部门生怕线路吃不消。城里的蓄能器纷纷充上了电，电力还源源输往其他蓄能器尚未充满的城市。

不过，阿科特给学生们交代了几条简单的操作规程之后，便与莫里（Morey）一道，随斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤（Stel Felso Theu）前往他的实验室。

===Original===

“Here,” Stel Felso Theu explained, “is the original apparatus. All these other machines you see are but replicas of this. How it works, why it works, even what it does, I am not sure of. Perhaps you will understand it. The thing is fully charged now, for it is, in part, one of the defenses of the city. Examine it now, and then I will show its power.”

Arcot looked it over in silence, following the great silver leads with keen interest. Finally he straightened, and returned to the Talsonian. In a moment Morey joined them.

The Talsonian then threw a switch, and an intense ionization appeared within the tube, then a minute spot of light was visible within the sphere of light. The minute spot of radiance is the real secret of the weapon. The ball of fire around it is merely wasted energy.

===Chinese===

“就是这台，”斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤解释道，“是最初的那台装置。您看到的其他机器，统统不过是它的复制品。它怎样运作、为何能运作，乃至它究竟在干什么，我都说不准。也许你们能弄个明白。这台机器现在已经蓄满了，因为它在某种程度上，也算这座城池的一重防御。请二位先检查一番，回头我让它显显威风。”

阿科特一言不发地把装置细细打量，兴致极浓地顺着那几根粗大的银色引线一路看去。末了，他直起身来，走回塔尔索人身旁。不多时，莫里也到了他们跟前。

随后，塔尔索人合上一只开关，管内顿时现出强烈的电离；接着，光球之中显出一个细微的亮点。这一点点微光，才是这件武器真正的奥秘。它四周那团火球，不过是白白耗散的能量罢了。

===Original===

“Now I will bring it out of the tube.” There were three dials on the control panel from which he worked, and now he adjusted one of these. The ball of fire moved steadily toward the glass wall of the tube, and with a crash the glass exploded inward. It had been highly evacuated. Instantly the tiny ball of fire about the point of light expanded to a large globe.

“It is now in the outer air. We make the—thing, in an evacuated glass tube, but as they are cheap, it is not an expensive procedure. The ball will last in its present condition for approximately three hours. Feel the exceedingly intense heat? It is radiating away its vast energy.

“Now here is the point of greatest interest.” Again the Talsonian fell to work on his dials, watching the ball of fire. It seemed far more brilliant in the air now. It moved, and headed toward a great slab of steel off to one side of the laboratory. It shifted about until it was directly over the center of the great slab. The slab rested on a scale of some sort, and as the ball of fire touched it, the scale showed a sudden increase in load. The ball sank into the slab of steel, and the scale showed a steady, enormous load. Evidently the little ball was pressing its way through as though it were a solid body. In a moment it was through the steel slab, and out on the other side.

===Chinese===

“现在，我把它从管子里引出来。”他操作用的控制板上装着三只刻度盘，这时他调好了其中一只。火球平稳地移向玻璃管管壁，咔啦一声，玻璃向内炸裂——管中本是高度真空。刹那间，光点四周那小小的火球，胀成了一只大球。

“它如今已在外头的空气里了。那个——东西，我们是在抽成真空的玻璃管里造出来的；玻璃管价钱便宜，这道手续花费不大。火球在眼下这种状态，大约能维持三个钟头。觉出这灼热逼人了么？它正在把体内庞大的能量辐射出去。

“如今到了最有趣的地方。”塔尔索人又埋头调起刻度盘，双眼盯住火球。此刻它在空气里显得亮得多。它动了起来，径直朝实验室一侧那块巨大的钢板而去。它挪移了好一阵，直到恰恰停在大钢板中央的正上方。钢板搁在某种秤上；火球一触到钢板，秤上显出的载荷便陡然增加。火球沉入钢板，秤上的读数稳定不移，却又大得惊人。显然，那小小的火球正像一件实体般硬挤过去。不一会儿，它便穿透钢板，从另一面钻了出来。

===Original===

“It will pass through any body with equal ease. It seems to answer only these controls, and these it answers perfectly, and without difficulty.

“One other thing we can do with it. I can increase its rate of energy discharge.”

The Talsonian turned a fourth dial, well off to one side, and the brilliance of the spot increased enormously. The heat was unbearable. Almost at once he shut it off.

===Chinese===

“随便什么物体，它都能同样轻松地穿透。它似乎只听这几只刻度盘的调遣，而且俯首听命，完美无碍，毫不作难。

“我们还能拿它办一件事。我可以加大它释放能量的速率。”

塔尔索人拧动了远远偏在一旁的第四只刻度盘，那光点的亮度登时暴增。热得叫人受不住。他几乎立刻便关了闸。

===Original===

“That is the principle we use in making it a weapon. Watch the actual operation.”

The ball of fire shot toward an open window, out the window, and vanished in the sky above. The Talsonian stopped the rotation of the dials. “It is motionless now, but scarcely visible. I will now release all the energy.” He twirled the fourth dial, and instantly there was a flash of light, and a moment later a terrific concussion.

“It is gone.” He left the controls, and went over to his apparatus. He set a heavy silver bladed switch, and placed a new tube in the apparatus. A second switch arced a bit as he drove it home. “Your generator is recharging the accumulators.”

===Chinese===

“我们把它做成武器，用的便是这个道理。请看实际操作。”

火球朝一扇敞开的窗疾射而去，穿窗而出，消失在高天之上。塔尔索人停住刻度盘的旋转。“它此刻已静止不动，只是几乎看不见。现在，我要把全部能量一口气放出来。”他猛拧第四只刻度盘，霎时间白光一闪，片刻之后，一阵惊天动地的震波滚滚袭来。

“它没了。”他离开操纵台，走到装置跟前，扳上一把沉重的银刀闸，又往装置里换上一根新管子。另一只开关推到位置时，还噼啪迸了几点电弧。“你们的发电机正在给蓄能器重新充电。”

===Original===

Stel Felso Theu took the backplate of the control cabinet off, and the terrestrians looked at the control with interest.

“Got it, Morey?” asked Arcot after a time.

“Think so. Want to try making it up? We can do so out of spare junk about the ship, I think. We won’t need the tube if what I believe of it is true.”

===Chinese===

斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤卸下控制柜的后板，几位地球人饶有兴味地端详起那套控制机构。

过了一阵，阿科特问道：“弄明白了没有，莫里？”

“想来是明白了。想不想照它试制一台？我琢磨着，拿船上散落的零碎备件就能凑出来。倘若我对它的判断不错，连管子都用不着。”

===Original===

Arcot turned to the Talsonian. “We wish you to accompany us to the ship. We have apparatus there which we wish to set up.”

Back to the ship they went. There Arcot, Morey and Wade worked rapidly.

It was about three-quarters of an hour later when Arcot and his friends called the others to the laboratory. They had a maze of apparatus on the power bench, and the shining relux conductors ran all over the ship apparently. One huge bar ran into the power room itself, and plugged into the huge power-coil power supply.

===Chinese===

阿科特转向塔尔索人：“我们想请您随我们回船一趟。船上有几件装置，我们打算架设起来。”

一行人回到船上。阿科特、莫里和韦德（Wade）在船上飞快地干了起来。

约莫三刻钟之后，阿科特和朋友们才招呼众人到实验室来。功率台上摆着迷宫般的一堆装置，闪闪发亮的雷卢克斯（relux）导线，看样子已爬满全船。一根巨大的导棒径直通进动力舱，插进了功率线圈那套庞大的供电装置里。

===Original===

They were still working at it, but looked up as the others entered. “Guess it will work,” said Arcot with a grin.

There were four dials, and three huge switches. Arcot set all four dials, and threw one of the switches. Then he started slowly turning the fourth dial. In the center of the room a dim, shining mist a foot in diameter began to appear. It condensed, solidified without shrinking, a solid ball of matter a foot in diameter. It seemed black, but was a perfectly reflective surface—and luminous!

“Then—then you had already known of this thing? Then why did you not tell me when I tried to show it?” demanded the Talsonian.

===Chinese===

两人犹自忙着调试，见众人进来，方才抬头。“料想能成。”阿科特咧嘴一笑。

台上装着四只刻度盘、三只巨大的开关。阿科特把四只刻度盘一一调定，合上一只开关，随后缓缓旋动第四只刻度盘。房间中央，一团直径一英尺、朦胧发亮的雾霭开始显现。它凝聚、固化，却并未收缩，成了一颗直径一英尺的实体物质球。它看似乌黑，表面却是完美无缺的反射镜面——而且还在发光！

“那——那么这东西你们早就知道了？既然如此，我方才演示给你们看的时候，为何不说？”塔尔索人质问道。

===Original===

Arcot was sending the globe, now perfectly non-luminous, about the room. It flattened out suddenly, and was a disc. He tossed a small weight on it, and it remained fixed, but began to radiate slightly. Arcot readjusted his dials, and it ceased radiating, held perfectly motionless. The sphere returned, and the weight dropped to the floor. Arcot maneuvered it about for a moment more. Then he placed his friends behind a screen of relux, and increased the radiation of the globe tremendously. The heat became intense, and he stopped the radiation.

“No, Stel Felso Theu, we do not have this on our world,” Arcot said.

“You do not have it! You look at my apparatus fifteen minutes, and then work for an hour—and you have apparatus far more effective than ours, which required years of development!” exclaimed the Talsonian.

===Chinese===

阿科特正驱着那颗球——此刻已全然不发光——在屋里四处游走。它霍然摊平，成了一张圆盘。他往盘上抛去一枚小重物，圆盘稳稳定在原处，却开始微微放热。阿科特重新调定刻度盘，辐射随之而止，圆盘悬定原处，纹丝不动。待球体复原，那重物便坠落在地板上。阿科特又操控了它片刻。随后，他让朋友们站到一面雷卢克斯屏风之后，把那颗球的辐射陡然调大。热浪登时灼人，他便止住了辐射。

“不，斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤，我们的世界上并没有这种东西。”阿科特道。

“你们没有这种东西！你们看了我的装置不过一刻钟，再忙上一个钟头——便弄出了远比我们这件耗费多年方才发展出来的东西更有效的装置！”塔尔索人失声惊呼。

===Original===

“Ah, but it was not wholly new to me. This ship is driven by curving space into peculiar coordinates. Even so, we didn’t do such a hot job, did we, Morey?”

“No, we should have—”

“What—it was not a good job?” interrupted the Talsonian. “You succeeded in creating it in air—in making it stop radiating, in making a ball a foot in diameter, made it change to a disc, made it carry a load—what do you want?”

===Chinese===

“唉，可这东西于我也并非全然陌生。这艘船，便是靠把空间弯折成特殊坐标来驱动的。话虽如此，我们这活儿干得也并不怎么漂亮，是不是，莫里？”

“是啊，我们本该——”

“什么——这还不算漂亮活儿？”塔尔索人截断了话头，“你们成功地在空气里把它造了出来——叫它停止辐射，造出一颗直径一英尺的球，叫它变作圆盘，又叫它负载重物——你们还想要什么？”

===Original===

“We want the full possibilities, the only thing that can save us in this war,” Morey said.

“What you learned how to do was the reverse of the process we learned. How you did it is a wonder—but you did. Very well—matter is energy—does your physics know that?” asked Arcot.

“It does; matter contains vast energy,” replied the Talsonian.

===Chinese===

“我们要的是它的全部潜力——这场战争里，唯有它能救我们。”莫里道。

“你们学会的，是我们所习那一过程的逆过程。你们究竟是怎么做到的，实在不可思议——可你们做到了。很好——物质即能量——你们的物理学可懂得这一点？”阿科特问。

“懂得；物质蕴含着巨大的能量。”塔尔索人答道。

===Original===

“Matter has mass, and energy because of that! Mass *is* energy. Energy in any known form is a field of force in space. So matter is ordinarily a combination of magnetic, electrostatic and gravitational fields. Your apparatus combined the three, and put them together. The result was—matter!

“You created matter. We can destroy it but we cannot create it.

“What we ordinarily call matter is just a marker, a sign that there are those energy-fields. Each bit is surrounded by a gravitational field. The bit is just the marker of that gravitational field.

===Chinese===

“物质具有质量，也正因此才有能量！质量*就*是能量。任何已知形式的能量，皆是空间中的一片力场。故而寻常的物质，本就是磁场、静电场与引力场三者的合成。你们的装置把这三者拼合起来。其结果便是——物质！

“你们造出了物质。我们能毁灭物质，却不能创造它。

“我们平常所称的物质，不过是一个标记，标示着那里存在着那些能量场。每一粒物质都被一个引力场所环抱。这粒物质，只是那引力场的标记罢了。

===Original===

“But that seems to be wrong. This artificial matter of yours seems also a sort of knot, for you make all three fields, combine them, and have the matter, but not, very apparently, like normal matter. Normal matter also holds the fields that make it. The artificial matter is surrounded by the right fields, but it is evidently not able to hold the fields, as normal matter does. That was why your matter continually disintegrated to ordinary energy. The energy was not bound properly.

“But the reason why it would blow up so was obvious. It did not take much to destroy the slight hold that the artificial matter had on its field, and then it instantly proceeded to release all its energy at once. And as you poured millions of horsepower into it all day to fill it, it naturally raised merry hell when it let loose.”

Arcot was speaking eagerly, excitedly.

===Chinese===

“但这说法似乎又不尽然。你们这人工物质，似乎也是一种‘结’：你们把三种场一并造出，使之合流，便有了物质——只是显而易见，它与寻常物质并不相同。寻常物质同样束缚着构成本身的那些场。人工物质四周固然环绕着恰切的场，却显然不能像寻常物质那样把场束缚住。这便是你们的物质不断崩解、化为寻常能量的缘故。那些能量没有被束缚妥当。

“至于它何以炸得那般凶，缘由也是明摆着的：摧垮人工物质对其场所持的那点微弱束缚，费不了多大事，随之它便顷刻间把全部能量一举倾泻而出。而你们又成天价把几百万马力的功率灌进去把它填满，它一撒起手来，自然要闹个天翻地覆。”

阿科特说得急切而亢奋。

===Original===

“But here is the great fact, the important thing: It is artificially created in a given place. It is made, and exists at the point determined by these three coordinated dials. It is not natural, and can exist only where it is made and nowhere else—obvious, but important. It cannot exist save at the point designated. Then, if that point moves along a line, the artificial matter must follow that moving point and be always at that point. Suppose now that a slab of steel is on that line. The point moves to it—through it. To exist, that artificial matter *must* follow it through the steel—if not, it is destroyed. Then the steel is attempting to destroy the artificial matter. If the matter has sufficient energy, it will force the steel out of the way, and penetrate. The same is true of any other matter, lux metal or relux—it will penetrate. To continue in existence it must. And it has great energy, and will expend every erg of that energy of existence to continue existence.

“It is, as long as its energy holds out, absolutely irresistible!

“But similarly, if it is at a given point, it must stay there, and will expend every erg staying there. It is then immovable! It is either irresistible in motion, or immovable in static condition. It is the irresistible and the immovable!

“What happens if the irresistible meets the immovable? It can only fight with its energy of existence, and the more energetic prevails.”

===Chinese===

“但此处有一条至理，也是至要之事：它是在某一特定地点由人工造出的。它一经造出，便存在于这三只联动刻度盘共同规定的那个点上。它不是天然之物，只能在它生成之处存在，舍此无他——此理显而易见，却至关重要。它非在指定之点存在不可。那么，倘若此点循一条线移动，这人工物质就必须追随那个移动之点，无时无刻不正处于该点。而今且设一块钢板横在那条线上。此点向钢板移去——径直穿入。为了存在，这人工物质便*必须*随点穿透钢板——否则，它便遭毁灭。如此说来，便是钢板在企图毁灭这人工物质。倘若这物质握有充足的能量，它便会把钢板逼开，一穿而过。其余任何物质亦复如是，卢克斯金属（lux）也罢，雷卢克斯也罢——它皆能穿透。为求继续存在，它势在必行。而它握有巨大的能量，为延续存在，它必会把这份存在之能尽数倾出，一尔格（erg）也在所不惜。

“只要能量不竭，它便绝对无坚不摧！

“然而反过来说，倘若它处在某个定点之上，它就必须留守彼处，且会把每一尔格能量尽数花在固守其位上。此时它便岿然不动！它非动则无坚不摧，即静则岿然不动。它便是那无坚不摧者，亦是那岿然不动者！

“若教无坚不摧者撞上岿然不动者，又当如何？二者唯有以各自的存在之能相拼，能量更盛者胜。”

## XXI / 思想号之力

===Original===
But it seemed, or must have seemed to any infinite being capable of watching it as it moved now, that the *Thought* was a mad thought. With the time control opened to the limit, and a touch of the space control, it fled across the Universe at a velocity such as no other thing was capable of.

One star—it flashed to a disc, loomed enormous—overpowering—then suddenly they were flashing *through* it! The enormous coils fed their current into the space-coils and the time field, and the ship seemed to twist and writhe in distorted space as the gravitational field of a giant star, and a giant ship’s space field fought for a fraction of time so short as to be utterly below measurement. Then the ship was gone—and behind it a star, the center of which had suddenly been hurled into another space forever, as the counteracting, gravitational field of the outer layers was removed for a moment, and only its own enormous density affected space, writhed and collapsed upon itself, to explode into a mighty sea of flames. Planets it formed, we know, by a process such as can happen when only this man-made accident happens.

But the ship fled on, its great coils partly discharged, but still far more charged than need be.

It was minutes to Talso where it had been hours with the *Ancient Mariner*, but now they traveled with the speed of *Thought*!

===Chinese===
然而，眼看着思想号（Thought）此刻的疾驰，任何一个有能力注视它的无穷存在，想必都会觉得：这思想号，是一个疯狂的思想。时间控制开到极限，空间控制轻轻一带，它便以世间万物无可企及的速度横掠过宇宙。

一颗恒星——闪眼间胀成一轮圆盘，庞大无比——压顶而至——突然，他们竟已闪身穿了过去！巨大的线圈把电流灌入空间线圈与时间场，当巨星的引力场与巨舰的空间场相互角力——那一瞬短得根本无从测量——飞船仿佛在扭曲的空间里扭动、翻腾。随即飞船已无影无踪——在它身后，一颗恒星的中心骤然被永远抛入了另一个空间：外层那股反向的引力场消隐了一瞬，空间里便只剩下它自身那巨大的密度在起作用，于是它痛苦地翻卷，向自身坍缩，爆成了一片烈焰的汪洋。我们知道，它就这样造出了行星——这样的过程，唯有在这类人为灾变中才会发生。

飞船继续飞驰，巨大的线圈已放掉一部分电，但所余的能量仍远超所需。

这一程去塔尔索（Talso），搁在老水手号（Ancient Mariner）时代要飞上几个小时，如今却只需几分钟——他们是以思想的速度在疾驰！

===Original===
Talso too was the scene of a battle, and more of a battle than Ortol had been, for here where more powerful defensive forces had been active, the Thessians had been more vengeful. All their remaining ships seemed concentrated here. And the great molecular screen that terrestrian engineers had flung up here had already fallen. Great holes had opened in it, as two great forts, and a thousand ships, some mighty battleships of the intergalactic spaces, some little scout cruisers, had turned their rays on the struggling defensive machines. It had held for hours, thanks to the tremendous tubes that Talso had in their power-distribution stations, but in the end had fallen, but not before many of their largest cities had been similarly defended, and the people of the others had scattered broadcast.

True, wherever they might be, a diffused molecular would find them and destroy all life save under the few screens, but if the Thessians once diffused their rays, without entering the atmosphere, the broken screen would once more be able to hold.

No fleet had kept the Thessian forces out of this atmosphere, but dozens of more adequately powered artificial matter bomb stations had taught Thett respect for Talso. But Talso’s own ray screen had stopped their bombs. They could only send their bombs as high as the screen. They did not have Arcot’s tremendous control power to maintain the matter without difficulty even beyond a screen.

===Chinese===
塔尔索同样成了战场，而且比奥托尔（Ortol）那一战更惨烈，因为这里的防御力量更为强大，塞特人（Thessian）的报复也就更为凶狠。他们残存的战舰似乎全部集中到了这里。地球人（terrestrian）工程师在塔尔索上空张起的那面巨大的分子射线屏障，此时已经陷落。两座巨型堡垒和一千艘战舰——有的是星系际空间的巨型战列舰，有的是小小的侦察巡洋舰——把射线一齐压在苦苦支撑的防御机器上，屏障上被撕开了巨大的破口。多亏塔尔索各功率配电站里那些功率惊人的功率管，屏障苦撑了几个小时，但终究还是陷落了——不过在陷落之前，许多最大的城市已经得到同样的庇护，其余城市的人们也已疏散四方。

的确，无论人们逃到哪里，一道扩散开的分子射线都会找上他们，把寥寥几面屏障之外的一切生命毁灭殆尽；但只要塞特人不进入大气层便扩散射线，那面残破的屏障就还能再度支撑。

没有哪支舰队能把塞特人的兵力挡在这层大气之外，但数十座功率更为充足的物质炸弹台站，已经教会塞特人敬畏塔尔索。而挡住他们炸弹的，是塔尔索自己的射线屏障。他们的炸弹至多只能送到屏障为止。他们没有阿科特（Arcot）那种惊人的操控之力，能把物质毫不费力地维持到屏障之外。

===Original===
At last the screen had fallen, and the Thessian ships, a hole once made, were able to move, and kept that hole always under them, though if it once were closed, they would again have the struggle to open it.

Exploding matter bombs had twice caused such spatial strains and ionized conditions as to come near closing it, but finally the Thessian fleet had arranged a ring of ships about the hole, and opened a cylinder of rays that reached down to the planet.

Like some gigantic plow the rays tore up mountains, oceans, glaciers and land. Tremendous chasms opened in straight lines as it plowed along. Unprotected cities flashed into fountains of rock and soil and steel that leaped upwards as the rays touched, and were gone. Protected cities, their screens blazing briefly under the enormous ray concentrations as the ships moved on, unheeding, stood safe on islands of safety amidst the destruction. Here in the lower air, where ions would be so plentiful, Thett did not try to break down the screens, for the air would aid the defenders.

===Chinese===
屏障终于陷落，塞特人的战舰一旦撕开一个破口便得以自由行动，并且始终让那个破口保持在下方——而破口一旦合拢，他们就得重新苦战一场才能再把它撕开。

爆炸的物质炸弹曾两度激起那样强烈的空间应变与电离状态，险些把破口合拢，但塞特舰队最终在破口四周排成一个环，向下开启了直抵行星的一道射线圆柱。

射线如一张巨犁，所过之处，山脉、海洋、冰川与大地尽数翻起。巨犁前行，笔直的裂谷豁然而开。没有屏障的城市，射线一触之下，便迸成岩石、泥土与钢铁喷涌而起的泉柱，转瞬消逝。有屏障的城市则安然无恙，如同毁灭之海中的安全孤岛——战舰旁若无人地继续推进，巨量汇聚的射线压在盾上，也只令那些屏障短暂地炽亮几分。在这低空之中，离子无处不在，塞特人并不试图击破那些屏障，因为空气会帮防御者的忙。

===Original===
Finally, as Thett’s forces had planned, they came to one of the ionized layer ray-screen stations that was still projecting its cone of protective screening to the layer above. Every available ray was turned on that station, and, designed as it was for protecting part of a world, the station was itself protected, but slowly, slowly as its already heated tubes weakened their electronic emission, the disc of ions retreated more and more toward the station, as, like some splashing stream, the Thessian rays played upon it forcing it back. A rapidly accelerating retreat, faster and faster, as the disc changed from the dull red of normal defense to the higher and bluer quanta of failing, less complete defense, the disc of interference retreated.

Then, with a flash of light, and a roar as the soil below spouted up, the station was gone. It had failed.

Instantly the ring of ships expanded as the great screen was weakened by the withdrawal of this support. Wider was the path of destruction now as the forces moved on.

But high, high in the sky, far out of sight of the naked eye, was a tiny spot that was in reality a giant ship. It was flashing forward, and in moments it was visible. Then, as another deserted city vanished, it was above the Thessian fleet.

===Chinese===
最后，正如塞特兵力所计划的那样，他们逼到了一座电离层射线屏障台站跟前——这座台站仍在向上方的电离层投射着它的防护锥。所有能用的射线一齐压向那座台站。台站本为守护一颗行星的一部分而设计，自身也有防护，但它那早已发热的功率管电子发射日渐疲弱，那片离子之盘便在塞特人射线的冲刷之下——如激流拍打——步步向台站退却。干扰之盘从正常防御时的暗红，渐渐变为防御失效、残缺不全时更高更蓝的量子之色，退势也随之急剧加快。

随后，一道光闪，下方泥土轰然喷起，台站灰飞烟灭。它失守了。

这一支撑一撤，大屏障随之减弱，环阵战舰当即向外扩张。兵力继续推进，毁灭之路如今更宽了。

但在高天之上，远在肉眼视界之外，有一个小小的光点，实则是一艘巨舰。它向前疾闪，片刻之后便已可见。当又一座被弃的城市湮灭时，它已悬在塞特舰队的上空。

===Original===
Their rays were directed downward through a hole that was even larger. A second station had gone with that city. But, as by magic, the hole closed up, and chopped their rays off with a decisiveness that startled them. The interference was so sharp now that not even the dullest of reds showed where their beams touched. The close interference was giving off only radio! In amazement they looked for this new station of such enormous power that their combined rays did not noticeably affect it. A world had been fighting their rays unsuccessfully. What single station could do this, if the many stations of the world could not? There was but one they knew of, and they turned now to search for the ship they knew must be there.

“No horrors this time; just clean, burning energy,” muttered Arcot.

It was clean, and it was burning. In an instant one of the forts was a mass of opalescence that shifted so swiftly it was purest white, then rocketed away, lifeless, and no longer relux.

===Chinese===
他们的射线正穿过一个更大的破口向下倾泻——第二座台站已随那座城一同毁灭。然而那破口竟如魔法般合拢，以令他们骇然的决定性一刀斩断了他们的射线。如今干扰之强，竟连最黯淡的红色都照不出射线触及之处。紧密的干扰只发出无线电波！他们惊愕地搜寻起这座新台站——它功率如此巨大，他们的合力射线竟奈何它不得。一整个世界都未能挡住他们的射线；若全世界的众多台站都做不到，一座台站又怎能如此？他们所能想到的只有一个答案，于是他们转过身来，搜寻那艘他们确信必在此处的战舰。

“这一次不用什么恐怖意象，只要干净、燃烧的能量。”阿科特低声道。

干净，而且燃烧。转眼之间，一座堡垒化作一团乳色虹光，虹光疾变，纯白之极，随即火箭般飞散而去——生机断绝，也不再是雷卢克斯（Relux）。

===Original===
The other fort had its screen up, though its power, designed to withstand the attack of a fleet of enormous intergalactic, matter-driven, fighting ships lasted but an instant under the driving power of half a million million suns, concentrated in one enormous ray of energy. The sheer energy of the ray itself, molecular ray though it was, heated the material it struck to blinding incandescence even as it hurled it at a velocity close to that of light into outer space. With little sparkling flashes battleships of the void after giant cruisers flashed into lux, and vanished under the ray.

A tremendous combined ray of magnetism and cosmic ray energy replaced the molecular, and the ships exploded into a dust as fine as the primeval gas from which came all matter.

Sweeping energy, so enormous that the defenses of the ships did not even operate against it, shattered ship after ship, till the few that remained turned, and, faster than the pursuing energies could race through space, faster than light, headed for their base.

===Chinese===
另一座堡垒撑起了屏障，但它的功率本是设计来抵挡一支由巨型星系际物质动力战舰组成的舰队的，在五千亿颗太阳的功率汇成一道巨能射线倾压而下时，却只撑得一瞬。那射线本身——尽管它只是分子射线——纯粹的能量便足以把它所击中的物质加热到炫目的白炽，同时以近于光速的速度将它抛入外层空间。太空中的战列舰继巨型巡洋舰之后，闪着细碎的火花化作卢克斯（Lux），在射线之下消失无踪。

一道由磁场与宇宙射线能量合成的巨射线取代了分子射线，战舰们轰然爆散，化作与孕育万物的原始气体一般细腻的尘埃。

那横扫的能量大得连战舰的防御装置都来不及对它启动，战舰接二连三地被击碎，直到残存的寥寥数艘掉头便逃——跑得比追击的能量掠过太空还快，快过光速，向他们的基地遁去。

===Original===
“That was fair fight; energy against energy,” said Arcot delightedly, for his new toy, which made playthings of suns and fed on the cosmic energy of a universe, was behaving nicely, “and as I said, Stel Felso Theu, at the beginning of this war, the greater Power wins, always. And in our island here, I have five hundred thousand million separate power plants, each generating at the rate of decillions of ergs a second, backing this ship.

“Your world will be safe now, and we will head for our last embattled ally, Sirius.” The titanic ship turned, and disappeared from the view of the madly rejoicing billions of Talso below, as it sped, far faster than light, across a universe to relieve another sorely tried civilization.

Knowing their cause was lost, hopeless in the knowledge that nothing known to them could battle that enormous force concentrated in one ship, the *Thought*, the Thessians had but one aim now, to do all the damage in their power before leaving.

===Chinese===
“这才叫公平一战——能量对能量，”阿科特欣然说道，他那拿太阳作玩物、以一整个宇宙的宇宙能量为食的新玩具，眼下正表现良好，“而且我早就说过，斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤（Stel Felso Theu），开战之初我就说过：更大的功率永远是赢家。在我们这片空间之岛上，撑着这艘船的，是五千亿座彼此独立的电站，每座都以每秒数十亿亿亿亿尔格的速率发电。”

“如今你们的世界安全了，我们要去我们最后一个战火中的盟友那里——天狼星（Sirius）。”巨舰转过身去，从下方亿万狂欢的塔尔索人的视野中消失，以远超光速的速度横越宇宙，赶去救援另一个备受煎熬的文明。

塞特人自知大势已去，深知他们所知的任何力量都无法与集中在思想号这一艘船上的巨能相抗，如今只剩一个目标：在撤离之前，把能造的破坏统统造出来。

===Original===
Already their tremendous, unarmed and unarmored transports were departing with their hundreds of thousands from that base system for the far-off Island of Space from which they had come. Their battlefleets were engaged in destroying all the cities of the allies, and those other helpless races of our system that they could. Those other inhabited worlds, many of which were completely wiped out because Arcot had no knowledge of them, were relieved only when the general call for retreat to protect the mother planet was sent out.

But Sirius was looming enormous before them. And its planets, heavily defended now by the combined Sirian, Terrestrial and Venerian fleets and great ray screens as well as a few matter-bomb stations, were suffering losses none the less. For the old Sixth of Negra, the Third here, had fallen. Slipping in on the night side of the planet, all power off, and so sending forth no warning impulses till it actually fell through the ray screen, a small fleet of scouts had entered. Falling still under simple gravity, they had been missed by the rays till they had fallen to so small a distance, that no humans or men of our allied systems could have stopped, but only their enormous iron boned strength permitted them to resist the acceleration they used to avert collision with the planet. Then scattering swiftly, they had blasted the great protective screen stations by attacking on the sides, where the ray screen projectors were not mounted. Designed to protect above, they had no side armor, and the Sixth was opened to attack.

Two and one-half billion people lost their lives painlessly and instantaneously as tremendous diffused moleculars played on the revolving planet.

Arcot arrived soon after this catastrophe. The Thessians left almost immediately, after the loss of three hundred or more ships. One hundred and fifty wrecks were found. The rest were so blasted by the forces which attacked them, that no traces could be found, and no count made.

===Chinese===
他们那些庞大而全无武装、全无装甲的运输船，已经载着数十万同胞离开那处基地星系，向着他们来时的遥远空间之岛（Island of Space）返航。他们的战斗舰队则忙着摧毁盟友们的所有城市，以及我们这个星系里那些他们够得着的、毫无还手之力的种族。其余那些有居民的世界——其中许多因阿科特毫不知情而被赶尽杀绝——直到撤退回防母星的号令发出，才得以解脱。

而天狼星已在前方显出它的巨大。它的行星此刻由天狼星人（Sirian）、地球人与金星人（Venerian）的联合舰队、巨大的射线屏障和若干物质炸弹台站重重设防，却仍在蒙受损失。因为那颗往日涅格拉（Negra）的第六行星——如今的天狼星第三行星——已经陷落。一支小小的侦察舰队切入行星的夜面，全舰熄火，因而直到果真坠穿射线屏障为止，都不曾发出任何预警脉冲。他们依旧凭单纯的重力下坠，射线始终捕捉不到他们，直到他们坠得那样近——近到换作我们盟友星系的任何人类都已无从阻截——也唯有他们那身铁骨钢筋的怪力，才抵受得住为免撞上行星而骤然施加的减速度。随后他们迅速散开，专挑侧面——射线屏障投射器没有架设的方位——发起攻击，炸毁了各座巨大的护盾台站。那些台站设计来防卫上方，侧面全无装甲，这颗第六行星就此门户洞开。

当巨量的扩散分子射线扫过这颗自转的行星时，二十五亿居民顷刻间毫无痛苦地丧生。

这场浩劫过后不久，阿科特便赶到了。塞特人折损了三百多艘战船，随即几乎立刻撤离。人们找到了一百五十艘残骸；其余的则被攻击它们的能量炸得那样彻底，连踪迹都无从寻找，更遑论清点。

===Original===
But as those ships fled back to their base, Arcot, with the wonderfully delicate mental control of his ship, was able to watch them, and follow them; for, invisible under normal conditions, by twisting space in the same manner that they did he was able to see them flee, and follow.

Light year after light year they raced toward the distant base. They reached it in two hours, and Arcot saw them from a distance sink to the various worlds. There were twelve gigantic worlds, each far larger than Jupiter of Sol, and larger than Stwall of Talso’s sun, Renl.

“I think,” said Arcot as he stopped the ship at a third of a light year, “that we had best destroy those planets. We may kill many men, and innocent noncombatants, but they have killed many of our races, and it is necessary. There are, no doubt, other worlds of this Universe here that we do not know of that have felt the vengeance of Thett, and if we can cause such trouble to them by destroying these worlds, and putting the fear of our attacking their mother world into them, they will call off those other fleets. I could have been invisible to Thett’s ships as we followed them here, and for the greater part of the way I was, for I was sufficiently out of their time-rate, so that they were visible only by the short ultraviolet, which would have put in their infrared, and, no photoelectric cell will work on quanta of such low energy. When at last I was sure of the sun for which they were heading, I let them see us, and they know we are aware of their base, and that we can follow them.

===Chinese===
而当那些战船逃回基地时，阿科特凭着他对本舰那精妙入微的心智操控，得以监视并跟踪他们；那些船在正常条件下是不可见的，他便以与它们相同的方式扭曲空间，从而看见了它们的逃遁，并一路跟随。

一光年接一光年，他们朝那遥远的基地疾驰。两个小时后他们抵达了基地，阿科特从远处望见他们降落到一个个世界上。那里有十二颗巨行星，每一颗都远比我们太阳的木星更大，也比绕塔尔索的恒星伦尔（Renl）运行的斯托尔（Stwall）更大。

“我认为，”阿科特在三分之一光年处停船时说道，“我们最好毁掉那些行星。我们或许会杀死许多人，许多无辜的非战斗人员，但他们已经杀了我们那么多族类，这是必要的。毫无疑问，这片宇宙里还有别的、我们不知道的世界遭受过塞特人的报复；如果我们毁掉这些行星，让他们尝到这样的苦头，让他们害怕我们也会攻打他们的母星，他们就会把那些别的舰队召回去。我们一路跟踪他们到这里，我本可以叫塞特人的战舰看不见我，而且绝大部分路程上我也确实没有现身，因为我把时间速率错开得足够远，他们只能借短紫外线才看得到我们，而这一错置，也把我们放进了他们的红外区——任何光电管，对能量低到那种程度的光量子都无能为力。等我终于确定他们要去的是哪一颗太阳，我便让他们看见了我们的踪影。于是他们知道，我们已发觉他们的基地，也知道我们能把他们一路跟到底。

===Original===
“I will destroy one of these worlds, and follow a fleet as it starts for their home nebula. Gradually, as they run, I will fade into invisibility, and they will not know that I have dropped back here to complete the work, but will think I am still following. Probably they will run to some other nebula in an effort to throw me off, but they will most certainly send back a ship to call the fleets here to the defense of Thett.

“I think that is the best plan. Do you agree?”

“Arcot,” asked Morey slowly, “if this race attempts to settle another Universe, what would that indicate of their own?”

“Hmmm—that it was either populated by their own race or that another race held the parts they did not, and that the other race was stronger,” replied Arcot. “The thought idea in their minds has always been a single world, single solar system as their home, however.”

“And single solar systems cannot originate in this Space,” replied Morey, referring to the fact that in the primeval gas from which all matter in this Universe and all others came, no condensation of mass less than thousands of millions of times that of a sun could form and continue.

===Chinese===
“我要毁掉这些世界中的一颗，然后，当有舰队动身前往他们的母星云时，我便跟上去。在他们奔逃的途中，我再渐渐隐没身形，他们不会知道我又折回这里来完成其余的毁灭，只会以为我仍在后面追赶。多半他们会逃往别的什么星云，想借以甩脱我，但他们必定会派船回去，把各路舰队召回来保卫塞特星。

“我看这是最好的计划。你同意吗？”

“阿科特，”莫里（Morey）缓缓问道，“如果这个种族打算去另一个宇宙定居，那说明他们自己的宇宙怎么样了？”

“唔——那说明它要么已住满了他们同族，要么是另一个种族占据了他们占不了的其余部分，而那个种族更为强大，”阿科特答道，“不过，他们心底的那个念头始终是：以单独一颗行星、单独一个恒星级系统为家园。”

“而单独的恒星级系统在这个空间里是产生不了的，”莫里答道。他指的是这样一个事实：在孕育出这个宇宙和所有其他宇宙之全部物质的原始气体中，质量不足太阳数十亿倍的凝聚体根本无法形成并存续。

===Original===
“We can only investigate—and hope that they do not inhabit the whole system, for I am determined that, unpleasant as the idea may be, there is one race that we cannot afford to have visiting us, and it is going to be permanently restrained in one way or another. I will first have a conference with their leaders and if they will not be peaceful—the *Thought* can destroy or make a Universe! But I think that a second race holds part of that Universe, for several times we have read in their minds the thought of the ‘Mighty Warless Ones of Venone.’ ”

“And how do you plan to destroy so large a planet as these are?” asked Morey, indicating the telectroscope screen.

“Watch and see!” said Arcot.

They shot suddenly toward the distant sun, and as it expanded, planets came into view. Moving ever slower on the time control, Arcot drove the ship toward a gigantic planet at a distance of approximately 300,000,000 miles from its primary, the sun of this system.

===Chinese===
“我们只能去查证——但愿他们没有占据整个宇宙。因为我已打定主意：这个念头再令人不快也罢，有一个种族，我们是万万容不得他们来串门的，必须以这种或那种方式把他们永久管束起来。我要先同他们的首领谈一谈，倘若他们不肯和平——思想号能毁灭一个宇宙，也能创造一个宇宙！不过我想，那个宇宙里还有第二个种族占据着一部分，因为已有好几次，我们从他们心中读到了‘维诺恩的无战伟者’（the Mighty Warless Ones of Venone）这一念头。”

“那么你打算怎样毁掉这么大的行星呢？”莫里指着电视望远镜（telectroscope）的视屏问道。

“看着就是！”阿科特说。

他们突然向那颗遥远的太阳射去，随着它不断胀大，一行行星球进入视野。阿科特把时间控制越调越慢，驾船朝一颗巨行星飞去——它距其主星、也就是这个星系的太阳，约有 300,000,000 英里。

===Original===
Arcot fell into step with the planet as it moved about in its orbit, and watched the speed indicator carefully.

“What’s the orbital speed, Morey?” asked Arcot.

“About twelve and a half miles per second,” replied the somewhat mystified Morey.

“Excellent, my dear Watson,” replied Arcot. “And now does my dear friend know the average molecular velocity of ordinary air?”

“Why, about one-third of a mile a second, average.”

===Chinese===
阿科特让船跟上这颗行星沿轨道运行的速度，密切注视着速度指示器。

“轨道速度多少，莫里？”阿科特问。

“大约每秒十二英里半。”莫里有点摸不着头脑地答道。

“妙极了，我亲爱的华生，”阿科特答道，“那么我亲爱的朋友可知道，普通空气的平均分子速度是多少？”

“呃，平均大约每秒三分之一英里。”

===Original===
“And if that planet as a whole should stop moving, and the individual molecules be given the entire energy, what would their average velocity be? And what temperature would that represent?” asked Arcot.

“Good—Why, they would have to have the same kinetic energy as individuals as they now have as a whole, and that would be an average molecular velocity in random motion of 12.5 miles a second—giving about—about—about—twelve thousand degrees centigrade!” exclaimed Morey in surprise. “That would put it in the far blue-white region!”

“Perfect. Now watch.” Arcot donned the headpiece he had removed, and once more took charge. He was very far from the planet, as distances go, and they could not see his ship. But he wanted to be seen. So he moved closer, and hung off to the sunward side of the planet, then moved to the night side, but stayed in the light. In seconds, a battlefleet was out attempting to destroy him.

Surrounding the ship with a wall of artificial matter, lest they annoy him, he set to work.

===Chinese===
“那么，倘若这颗行星整体停止运动，而把全部的能量都交给一个个分子，它们的平均速度会是多少？这又相当于多高的温度？”阿科特问。

“好——呃，那样的话，各个分子单独具有的动能，就必须等于它们如今作为一个整体所具有的动能，也就是说，无序运动的平均分子速度是每秒 12.5 英里——那就相当于大约——大约——大约——一万两千摄氏度！”莫里惊呼起来，“那可就把它推进深蓝白光区了！”

“很好。现在看吧。”阿科特戴回他先前摘下的头环（headpiece），重新接掌了控制。就距离而论，他离行星还很远，他们看不见他的船。但他要的就是被看见。于是他驶近了些，先悬在行星的向阳面外侧，又移到夜面，却仍留在光照之中。几秒钟内，便有一支战斗舰队冲出来，试图消灭他。

他用一道人工物质（artificial matter）之墙把船围住，免得他们来烦扰，随后动手干活。

===Original===
Directly in the orbit of the planet, a faint mistiness appeared, and rapidly solidified to a titanic cup, directly in the path of the planet.

Arcot was pouring energy into the making of that matter at such a rate that space was twisted now about them. The meter before them, which had not registered previously, was registering now, and had moved over to three. Three sols—and was still climbing. It stopped when ten were reached. Ten times the energy of our sun was pouring into that condensation, and it solidified quickly.

The Thessians had seen the danger now. It was less than ten minutes away from their planet, and now great numbers of ships of all sorts started up from the planet, swarming out like rats from a sinking vessel.

Majestically the great world moved on in its orbit toward the thin wall of infinite strength and infinite toughness. Already Thessian battleships were tearing at that wall with rays of all types, and the wall sputtered back little gouts of light, and remained. The meters on the *Thought* were no longer registering. The wall was built, and now Arcot had all the giant power of the ship holding it there. Any attempt to move it or destroy it, and all the energy of the Universe would rush to its defense!

===Chinese===
就在这颗行星的轨道正中，一团淡淡的雾霭显现出来，迅速凝成一只硕大无朋的杯体，不偏不倚，正堵在行星的必经之路上。

阿科特往那团物质的凝成之中灌注能量的速率，已高得让他们四周的空间都扭曲起来。他们面前那只此前一直毫无读数的仪表，此刻动了，爬到了三——三个索尔（sol）——而且还在上涨，到十才停住。十倍于我们太阳的能量正灌入那团凝聚物，它很快便凝固成形。

塞特人此时已看出危险所在。那东西距他们的行星已不到十分钟的路程，于是大批各式战舰从行星上升起，如沉船上逃窜的鼠群般蜂拥而出。

那颗巨世界威严地循着轨道前行，撞向那道薄薄的、强韧无穷的墙。塞特人的战列舰早已用各式射线撕扯那道墙，墙上只溅回点点光屑，纹丝不动。思想号上的仪表不再有读数。墙已筑成，如今阿科特以全船的巨力把它钉在那里。谁胆想移动它、毁掉它，整个宇宙的能量都会涌来护它！

===Original===
The atmosphere of the planet reached the wall. Instantly, as the pressure of that enormous mass of air touched it, the wall fought, and burst into a blaze of energy. It was fighting now, and the meter that measured sun-powers ran steadily, swiftly up the scale. But the men were not watching the meter; they were watching the awesome sight of Man stopping a world in its course! Turning a world from its path!

But the meter climbed suddenly, and the world was suddenly a tremendous blaze of light. The solid rock had struck the giant cup, 110,000 miles in diameter. It was silent, as a world pitted its enormous kinetic energy against the combined forces of a universe. Soundless—and as hopeless. Its strength was nothing, its energy pitted unnoticed against the energy of five hundred thousand million suns—as vain as those futile attempts of the Thessian battleships on the invulnerable walls of the *Thought*.

What use is there to attempt description of that scene as 2,500,000,000,000,000,000,000,000 tons of rock and metal and matter crashed against a wall of energy, immovable and inconceivable. The planet crumpled, and split wide. A thousand pieces, and suddenly there was a further mistiness about it, and the whole enormous mass, seeming but a toy, as it was from this distance in space, and as it was in this ship, was enclosed in that same, immovable, unalterable wall of energy.

===Chinese===
行星的大气触到了墙。刹那间，那大团空气的压力一沾上它，墙便奋起相抗，迸成一团炽亮的能量之焰。它在搏斗了，那只测量太阳功率的仪表沿着刻度平稳而迅疾地攀升。但船上的人没有盯着仪表；他们盯着的是那令人屏息的奇景——人类生生止住了一颗世界的进程！把它从既定的路径上扭转过去！

可仪表猛地一窜，那颗世界骤然化作一团炫目的光焰。坚实的岩体撞上了那只直径 110,000 英里的巨杯。一切悄然无声——一颗世界正以它庞大的动能，对抗一整个宇宙的合力。无声，也同样无望。它的力量微不足道，它的能量在无人察觉中撞向五千亿颗太阳的能量——正如塞特战列舰对思想号不坏之墙的那些徒劳冲击，同样枉然。

那样的场面——2,500,000,000,000,000,000,000,000 吨的岩石、金属与物质撞在一堵不可移动、超乎想象的能量之墙上——纵然费尽笔墨，又何足描摹。行星皱缩、崩裂，碎作千块；而刹那间，它四周又泛起一层新的雾霭，整个庞大的质量——在这个距离的空间里看去只是一个玩具，在这艘船中也确乎只是个玩具——被同一道不可撼动、不可更移的能量之墙整个罩住了。

===Original===
The ship was as quiet and noiseless, as without indication of strain as when it hummed its way through empty space. But the planet crumpled and twirled, and great seas of energy flashed about it.

The world, seeming tiny, was dashed helpless against a wall that stopped it, but the wall flared into equal and opposite energy, so that matter was raised not to the twelve thousand Morey had estimated but nearer twenty-four thousand degrees. It was over in less than half an hour, and a broken, misshapen mass of blue incandescence floated in space. It would fall now, toward the sun, and it would, because it was motionless and the sun moved, take an eccentric orbit about that sun. Eventually, perhaps, it would wipe out the four inferior planets, or perhaps it would be broken as it came within the Roches limit of that sun. But the planet was now a miniature sun, and not so very small, at that.

And from every planet of the system was pouring an assorted stream of ships, great and small, and they all set panic-stricken across the void in the same direction. They had seen the power of the *Thought*, and did not contest any longer its right to this system.

===Chinese===
船依旧安静无声，依旧不见半点吃力的迹象，与它在虚空中嗡嗡巡航时无异。而那颗行星皱缩、翻滚，能量的浩海在它四周明灭闪动。

那颗看起来小小的世界，被一头掷向止住它去路的墙上，无力还手；而墙则燃起等量而反向的能量，于是物质并未止于莫里估算的一万两千度，而是被烧到了将近两万四千度。前后不到半小时便告终结，一团破碎扭曲的蓝炽物体漂浮在太空中。它现在要坠向太阳了；而且由于它已静止不动、太阳却仍在运行，它将绕那颗太阳走出一条偏心的轨道。最终，它或许会扫灭那四颗内行星，又或许在进入那颗太阳的洛希极限（Roche limit）之内时被扯得粉碎。但无论如何，这颗行星如今已是一颗小太阳——而且个头还不算小。

星系里每一颗行星上都涌出形形色色的飞船，大的小的，全都惊慌失措地朝同一个方向没命横越虚空而去。它们已见识了思想号的威力，也不再否认思想号对这个星系的支配之权。

## VI / 第二手棋

===Original===
“What happened to him, though?” asked Wade, bewildered. “I haven’t yet figured it out. He went down in a heap, and he didn’t have any power. Of course, if he had his power he could have pulled out again. He could just melt and burn all the excess rock off, and he would be all set. But his rays all went dead. And why the explosion?”

“The magnetic beam is the answer. In our boat we have everything magnetically shielded, because of the enormous magnetic flux set up by the current flowing from the storage coils to the main coil. But—with so many wires heavily charged with current, what would have happened if they had not been shielded?

“If a current cuts across a magnetic field, a side thrust is developed. What do you suppose happened when the terrific magnetic field of the beam and the currents in the wires of their power-board were mutually opposed?”

“Lord, it must have ripped away everything in the ship. It’d tear loose even the lighting wires!” gasped Wade in amazement.

“But if all the power of the ship was destroyed in this way, how was it that one of their rays was operating as they fell?” asked Zezdon Afthen.

“Each ray is a power plant in itself,” explained Arcot, “and so it was able to function. I do not know the cause of the explosion, though it might well have been that they had light-bombs such as the Kaxorians of Venus have,” he added, thoughtfully.

===Chinese===
“可是，他到底出了什么事？”韦德（Wade）大惑不解地问，“我一直没想明白。他是一头栽下去的，摔作一团，当时一点功率也没有。当然啦，功率若还在，他满可以重新拔出来——只消把多余的那些岩石熔掉烧尽，便一切照旧。可他的射线全数失灵了。还有，为什么会爆炸？”

“答案就是磁场束（magnetic beam）。我们的船里，一切都加了磁屏蔽，因为电流从储能线圈流向主线圈时，会激起极大的磁通量。可是——那么多导线都载着强电流，倘若当初不加屏蔽，会出什么事？

“电流若横截磁场，便会产生一个侧向推力。你想想，当磁场束那可怕至极的磁场，与他们配电板导线里的电流彼此对抗时，会是什么光景？”

“天哪，那非得把船里的东西扯个精光不可。连照明线路都会给撕扯得脱落下来！”韦德惊得倒抽一口气。

“可倘若全船的能量都这样毁掉了，他们坠落的时候，怎么还有一道射线在运转？”泽兹登·阿夫森（Zezdon Afthen）问。

“每一道射线本身就是一座电站，”阿科特（Arcot）解释道，“所以它还能运转。至于爆炸的缘由，我不清楚——不过很可能是因为他们也带着光炸弹（light-bomb），就像金星（Venus）的卡索里安人（Kaxorian）所用的那种。”他若有所思地补充道。

===Original===
They landed, at Zezdon’s advice, in the city that their arrival had been able to save. This was Ortol’s largest city, and their industrial capital. Here, too, was the University at which Afthen taught.

They landed, and Arcot, Morey and Wade, with the aid of Zezdon Afthen and Zezdon Fentes worked steadily for two of their days of fifty hours each, teaching men how to make and use the molecular ships, and the rays and screens, heat beams, and relux. But Arcot promised that when he returned he would have some weapon that would bring them certain and easy salvation. In the meantime other terrestrians would follow him.

They left the morning of their third day on the planet. A huge crowd had come to cheer them on their way as they left, but it was the “silent cheer” of Ortol, a telepathic well-wishing.

===Chinese===
他们依照泽兹登的建议，降落在了那座因他们及时赶到而得以保全的城市。这是奥托尔（Ortol）最大的城市，也是这颗行星的工业首都。阿夫森执教的大学也设在这里。

着陆之后，阿科特、莫里（Morey）和韦德，加上泽兹登·阿夫森与泽兹登·芬特斯（Zezdon Fentes）从旁协助，一鼓作气苦干了整整两个奥托尔日——每一天长达五十小时——教人们如何制造并使用分子飞船（molecular ship）、种种射线与屏障、热射线，还有雷卢克斯。但阿科特许诺，等他回来时，必带上某种武器，让他们稳稳当当、轻轻松松地得到拯救。与此同时，其他地球人（terrestrian）也将接踵而至。

在这颗行星上的第三个清晨，他们启程了。大群民众赶来为他们的离去欢送壮行，但那是奥托尔式的“无声欢呼”——一场心灵感应的美好祝愿。

===Original===
“Now,” said Arcot as their ship left the planet behind, “we will have to make the next move. It certainly looks as though that next move would be to the still-unknown race that lives on world 3769–37, 478, 326, 894–6. Evidently we will have to have some weapon they haven’t, and I think that I know what it will be. Thanks to our trip out to the Islands of Space.”

“Shall we go?”

“I think it would be wise,” agreed Morey.

“And I,” said Wade. The Ortolians agreed, and so, with the aid of the photographic copies of the Thessian charts that Arcot had made, they started for world 3769–37, 478, 326, 894–6.

“It will take approximately twenty-two hours, and as we have been putting off our sleep with drugs, I think that we had better catch up. Wade, I wish you’d take the ship again, while Morey and I do a little concentrated sleeping. We have by no means finished that calculation, and I’d very much like to. We’ll relieve you in five hours.”

===Chinese===
“现在，”阿科特说，此时飞船已把那颗行星抛在身后，“我们该走下一步棋了。看情形，这一步多半要走到那支至今还全然未知的种族面前——住在 3769–37, 478, 326, 894–6 号世界上的那一支。显然，我们必须握有一样他们没有的武器，而我想我已经知道那会是什么了。这还得谢谢我们先前那趟空间之岛（Islands of Space）之行。”

“那么，我们动身吧？”

“我看这是明智之举。”莫里（Morey）赞同道。

“我也这么看。”韦德说。奥托尔人（Ortolian）也都同意。于是，凭着阿科特翻拍的塞特人（Thessian）星图照相副本，他们朝 3769–37, 478, 326, 894–6 号世界进发了。

“这趟航程约需二十二小时。我们一直拿药物压着困意，我看最好把欠下的觉补回来。韦德，还得请你再掌一阵船，我和莫里去集中补睡一觉。那道演算我们还远没有算完，我很想把它算完。五个小时后我们来换你。”

===Original===
Wade took the ship, and following the course Arcot laid out, they sped through the void at the greatest safe speed. Wade had only to watch the view-screen carefully, and if a star showed as growing rapidly, it was proof that they were near, and nearing rapidly. If large, a touch of a switch, and they dodged to one side, if small, they were suddenly plunged into an instant of unbelievable radiation as they swept through it, in a different space, yet linked to it by radiation, not light, that were permitted in.

Zezdon Afthen had elected to stay with him, which gave him an opportunity he had been waiting for. “If it’s none of my business, just say so,” he began. “But that first city we saw the Thessians destroy—it was Zezdon Fentes’ home, wasn’t it? Did he have a family?”

The words seemed blunt as he said them, but there was no way out, once he had started. And Zezdon Afthen took the question with complete calm.

“Fentes had both wives and children,” he said quietly. “His loss was great.”

===Chinese===
韦德接过飞船，循着阿科特标定的航线，以最大的安全速度疾穿虚空。他只须仔细盯着视屏（view-screen）：若哪颗星显得在迅速胀大，便证明他们已临近，而且正急速逼近。星若硕大，手指一按开关，船便闪向一旁；星若细小，那么当他们扫过它的一瞬，便会骤然没入一股难以置信的辐射之中——他们航行在另一个空间里，那空间与正常空间仍靠辐射相连——被容许透入的，是辐射，而非光。

泽兹登·阿夫森自愿留下陪他，这正好给了他一个等待已久的机会。“这话若不归我问，你直说无妨。”他开口道，“可我们看见塞特人毁掉的头一座城——那是泽兹登·芬特斯的家乡，对吧？他有家眷吗？”

这些话出口时显得唐突生硬，可话既开了头，便没了退路。而泽兹登·阿夫森以十足的平静接下了这个问题。

“芬特斯有妻有子。”他平静地说，“他失去的很多。”

===Original===
Wade concentrated on the screen for a moment, trying to absorb the shock. Then, fearing Zezdon Afthen might misinterpret his silence, he plunged on. “I’m sorry,” he said. “I didn’t realize you were polygamous—most people on Earth aren’t, but some groups are. It’s probably a good way to improve the race. But … Blast it, what bothers me is that Zezdon Fentes seemed to recover from the blow so quickly! From a canine race, I’d expect more affection, more loyalty, more. …”

He stopped in dismay. But Zezdon Afthen remained unperturbed. “More unconcealed emotion?” he asked. “No. Affection and loyalty we have—they *are* characteristic of our race. But affection and loyalty should not be uselessly applied. To *forget* dead wives and children—that would be insulting to their memory. But to mourn them with senseless loss of health and balance would also be insulting—not only to their memory, but to the entire race.

“No, we have a better way. Fentes, my very good friend, has not forgotten, no more than you have forgotten the death of your mother, whom you loved. But you no longer mourn her death with a fear and horror of that natural thing, the Eternal Sleep. Time has softened the pain.

“If we can do the same in five minutes instead of five years, is it not better? That is why Fentes has *forgotten*.”

===Chinese===
韦德盯着屏幕出了一会儿神，努力消化这番震动。随后，他怕泽兹登·阿夫森误解自己的沉默，索性往下说。“我很遗憾。”他说，“我不知道你们实行多妻——地球上大多数人都不这样，不过有些族群是的。这没准倒是个改良种族的好法子。可是……见鬼，叫我别扭的是，泽兹登·芬特斯从这场打击里恢复得也太快了！从犬族出身的种族里，我原指望看到更深的依恋，更多的忠诚，更多的……”

他讪讪地住了口。但泽兹登·阿夫森依旧泰然自若。“你是说，更多外露的情感？”他问，“不。深情与忠诚，我们都有——它们*正是*我们这个种族的特质。但深情与忠诚，不该被无谓地挥洒。*忘记*死去的妻儿——那是对亡者记忆的侮辱。可若以无谓的憔悴失衡去哀悼他们，同样是侮辱——不仅侮辱亡者的记忆，也侮辱整个种族。

“不，我们有一个更好的办法。芬特斯，我极好的朋友，并没有忘记，正如你并没有忘记你深爱的母亲的死。但你已不再怀着对那件自然之事——永恒长眠（Eternal Sleep）——的恐惧与惊怖来哀悼她的死。时间把痛楚磨软了。

“倘若我们能在五分钟里做到这件事，而不必用上五年，岂不更好？这正是芬特斯*忘记*了的缘故。”

===Original===
“Then you have aged his memory of that event?” asked Wade in surprise.

“That is one way of stating it,” replied Zezdon Afthen seriously.

Wade was silent for a while, absorbing this. But he could not contain his curiosity completely. Well, to hell with it, he decided. Conventional manners and tact don’t have much meaning between two different races. “Are you—married?” he asked.

“Only three times,” Zezdon Afthen told him blandly. “And to forestall your next question—no, our system does not create problems. At least, not those you’re thinking of. I know my wives have never had the jealous quarrels I see in your mind pictures.”

“It isn’t safe thinking things around you,” laughed Wade. “Just the same, all of this has made me even more interested in the ‘Ancient Masters’ you keep mentioning. Who were they?”

===Chinese===
“这么说，你们是把他对那件事的记忆变老了？”韦德惊讶地问。

“可以这么说。”泽兹登·阿夫森正色答道。

韦德沉默了片刻，咀嚼着这番道理。可他的好奇心终究按捺不全。唉，管他呢，他打定了主意。在两个不同的种族之间，俗套的礼数与乖巧的分寸本就没有多大意义。“你——结过婚吗？”他问。

“只结过三回。”泽兹登·阿夫森平淡地告诉他，“再抢先堵上你下一个问题——不，我们的制度不会造成麻烦。至少，不是你正想着的那几种。你心象（mind picture）里的那些争风吃醋的口角，我知道我的妻子们是从来没有过的。”

“在你旁边想事情可不大安全。”韦德笑道。“话虽如此，这一切倒叫我对你们口口声声提到的‘远古宗师（Ancient Masters）’愈发感兴趣了。他们究竟是什么人？”

===Original===
“The Ancient Ones,” began Zezdon Afthen slowly, “were men such as you are. They descended from a primeval omnivorous mammal very closely related to your race. Evidently the tendency of evolution on any planet is approximately the same with given conditions.

“The race existed as a distinct branch for approximately 1,500,000 of your years before any noticeable culture was developed. Then it existed for a total of 1,525,000 years before extinction. With culture and learning they developed such marvelous means of killing themselves that in twenty-five thousand years they succeeded perfectly. Ten thousand years of barbaric culture—I need not relate it to you, five thousand years of the medieval culture, then five thousand years of developed science culture.

“They learned to fly through space and nearly populated three worlds; two were fully populated, one was still under colonization when the great war broke out. An interplanetary war is not a long drawn out struggle. The science of any people so far advanced as to have interplanetary lines is too far developed to permit any long duration of war. Selto declared war, and made the first move. They attacked and destroyed the largest city of Ortol of that time. Ortolian ships drove them off, and in turn attacked Selto’s largest city. Twenty million intelligences, twenty million lives, each with its aims, its hopes, its loves and its strivings—gone in four days.

===Chinese===
“远古先民（Ancient Ones），”泽兹登·阿夫森缓缓开了口，“原本是同你们一样的人。他们源自一种原始的杂食性哺乳动物，与你们的种族血缘极近。看来，只要条件相当，任何一颗行星上演化的趋向都大体相同。

“在发展出任何值得一提的文化之前，这个种族作为一个独立的分支，已存在了约 1,500,000 个你们的年头；此后又绵延了共 1,525,000 年，方才灭绝。伴随着文化与学识，他们发明了种种奇妙无比的自相残杀之法，以致只用了两万五千年，便大功告成。一万年的野蛮文化——其中情状不必向你细述；然后是五千年的中世纪文化，再是五千年的科学昌明文化。

“他们学会了在太空中飞行，几乎住满了三个世界；大战爆发之时，两个已经人烟充盈，第三个还在开拓之中。行星际战争绝不是一场旷日持久的消耗。凡是进步到拥有行星际航线的人民，其科学都发达得容不下任何旷日持久之战。塞尔托（Selto）宣了战，先出了手。他们进攻并摧毁了当时奥托尔最大的城市。奥托尔人的战舰把他们逐退，转而攻打塞尔托最大的城市。两千万个智慧，两千万条性命，每一条都有自己的目标、希望、热爱与奋斗——四天之内，同归寂灭。

===Original===
“The war continued to get more and more hateful, till it became evident that neither side would be pacified till the other was totally subjugated. So each laid his plans, and laid them to wipe out the entire world of the other.

“Ortol developed a ray of light that made things not happen,” explained Zezdon Afthen, his confused thoughts clearly indicating his own uncertainty.

“ ‘A ray of light that made things not happen,’ ” repeated Wade curiously. “A ray, which prevented things, which caused processes to stop—*The Negrian Death Ray*!” he exclaimed as he suddenly recognized, in this crude and garbled description of its powers, the Negrian ray of anti-catalysis, a ray which tended to stop the processes of life’s chemistry and bring instant, painless death.

“Ah, you know it, too?” asked the Ortolian eagerly. “Then you will understand what happened. The ray was turned first on Selto, and as the whirling planet spun under it, every square foot of it was wiped clean of every living thing, from gigantic Welsthan to microscopic Ascoptel, and every man, woman and child was killed, painlessly, but instantly.

===Chinese===
“战事愈演愈烈，愈变愈狠，直到双方都看得分明：不把对方彻底征服，谁也不会罢休。于是各自定下计划——定下的都是把对方整个世界从地图上抹去的计划。

“奥托尔人研制出一种能让事情不发生的光线。”泽兹登·阿夫森解释道；他纷乱的思绪明白无误地显出，他自己也拿不准。

“‘一种能让事情不发生的光线。’”韦德好奇地重复道，“一种能遏止事态、能让种种过程停下来的射线——*内格里安死射线*（Negrian Death Ray）！”他失声喊了出来。他忽然认了出来：在这段粗糙讹乱的能力描述里，藏着的正是内格里安的反催化射线——一种能遏止生命化学进程、带来瞬息无痛之死的射线。

“啊，你们也知道它？”这位奥托尔人热切地问，“那你就能明白后来发生的事了。那射线首先转向了塞尔托。旋转的行星在射线底下转动，每一平方英尺都被扫荡得干干净净，不留一丝活物——从巨硕的韦尔斯桑（Welsthan）到微渺的阿斯科特尔（Ascoptel）；每一个男人、女人和孩子都被杀死——无痛，却在刹那之间。

===Original===
“Then Thenten spun under it, and all were killed, but many who had fled the planets were still safe—many?—a few thousand.

“The day that Thenten spun under that ray, men of Ortol began to complain of disease—men by the thousands, hundreds of thousands. Every man, every woman, every child was afflicted in some way. The diseases did not seem all the same. Some seemingly died of a disease of the lungs, some went insane, some were paralyzed, and lay helplessly inactive. But most of them were afflicted, for it was exceedingly virulent, and the normal serums were helpless. Before any quantity of new serum was made, all but a slender remnant had died, either of starvation through paralysis, none being left to care for them, or from the disease itself, while thousands who had gone mad were painlessly killed.

“The Seltonians came to Ortol, and the remaining Ortolians, with their aid, tried to rebuild the civilization. But what a sorry thing! The cities were gigantic, stinking, plague-ridden morgues. And the plague broke among those few remaining people. The Ortolians had done everything in their power with the serums—but too late. The Seltonians had been protected with it on landing—but even that was not enough. Again the wild fires of that loathsome disease broke out.

===Chinese===
“随后，塞恩滕（Thenten）也在射线底下转了过去，尽数死亡。可当初逃出诸行星的那些人依然无恙——很多吗？——不过几千人而已。

“就在塞恩滕在那射线底下转过去的那一天，奥托尔人开始病倒——成千上万，几十万之众。每一个男人、每一个女人、每一个孩子，都以这样那样的方式染了病。病症似乎并不尽同：有的看似死于肺疾，有的疯了，有的瘫痪下来，无助地躺着动弹不得。但绝大多数人都染疫不轻，因为那病毒性奇烈，寻常血清束手无策。还没等制出成批的新血清，除了一线孑遗，众人皆已死去——有的因瘫痪无人照料而活活饿死，有的死于疫病本身；还有成千上万疯了的人，被无痛地处死了。

“塞尔托人（Seltonian）来到了奥托尔，残存的奥托尔人在他们的援助下，试图重建文明。可那是何等凄惨的一件事！一座座城市成了巨大、恶臭、疫气弥漫的停尸房。而疫病又在那寥寥无几的幸存者中间爆发了。奥托尔人已经拿血清竭尽了一切人力——但太迟了。塞尔托人登陆时曾以血清防护——可连那也不够。那可憎疫病的野火，再度熊熊燃起。

===Original===
“Since first those men had developed from their hairy forebears, they had found their eternal friends were the dogs, and to them they turned in their last extremity, breeding them for intelligence, hairlessness, and resemblance to themselves. The Deathless ones alone remained after three generations of my people, but with the aid of certain rays, the rays capable of penetrating lead for a short distance, and most other substances for considerable distances.” X-rays, thought Wade. “Great changes had been wrought. Already they had developed startling intelligence, and were able to understand the scheme of their Masters. Their feet and hands were being modified rapidly, and their vocal apparatus was changing. Their jaws shortened, their chins developed, the nose retreated.

“Generation after generation the process went on, while the Deathless Ancient Ones worked with their helpers, for soon my race was a real helping organization.

“But it was done. The successful arousing of true love-emotion followed, and the unhappy days were gone. Quickly development followed. In five thousand years the new race had outstripped the Ancient Masters, and they passed, voluntarily, willingly joining in oblivion the millions who had died before.

“Since then our own race has risen, it has been but a short thousand years, a thousand years of work, and hope, and continuous improvement for us, continual accomplishment on which we can look, and a living hope to which we could look with raised heads, and smiling faces.

“Then our hope died, as this menace came. Do you see what you and your world was meant to us, Man of Earth?” Zezdon Afthen raised his dark eyes to the terrestrian with a look in their depths that made Wade involuntarily resolve that Thet and all Thessians should be promptly consigned to that limbo of forgotten things where they belonged.

===Chinese===
“自从那些人初从多毛的祖先演化而来，他们便发现，狗是他们永恒的朋友。到了最后的绝境，他们便转向了狗，培育它们的智力，让它们褪尽毛发，长得越来越像自己。我的族人传过三代之后，唯余不死者（the Deathless ones）尚存于世。他们借助某些射线——那种射线能穿透铅一小段距离，又能穿透大多数其他物质相当一段距离——”X 射线，韦德心中暗道。“剧变就此铸成。它们那时已发展出惊人的智力，能够领会宗主们的谋划。它们的脚和手被迅速改造，发音器官也在变化。颌骨变短，下巴隆起，鼻子向后退缩。

“一代又一代，这一进程延续不断，不死的远古先民与他们的帮手并肩劳作——因为不久之后，我的种族便已是一支真正顶用的帮手队伍。

“然而事竟成。接着，真正的爱之情感被成功唤醒，不幸的岁月就此终结。发展随之突飞猛进。不出五千年，新的种族便已后来居上，超越了远古宗师；而宗师们自愿谢世，甘愿与先前死去的数百万同胞一同没入遗忘。

“自那以后，我们自己的种族兴起至今，不过短短一千年。这一千年，于我们有劳作，有希望，有绵绵不绝的精进；有足以回首的、接连不断的成就；更有一种活生生的希望，让我们得以昂起头来，面带笑容地望向它。

“可随后，当这场威胁降临，我们的希望死了。你可明白，你和你的世界，于我们意味着什么，地球人？”泽兹登·阿夫森抬起一双黑眸，望向这位地球人；那双眼睛深处有一种神情，逼得韦德不由自主地暗下决心：定要把塞特（Thet）和所有塞特人即刻扫进那座被遗忘之物的湮没之所——那才是他们该待的地方。

## XXIV / 塞特星的备战

===Original===
Streaking through the void toward Thett was again a tiny scout ship. It carried but a single man, and with all the power of the machine he was darting toward distant Thett, at a speed insanely reckless, but he knew that he must maintain such a speed if his mission were to be successful.

Again a tiny ship entered Thett’s far-flung atmosphere, and slowed to less than a light speed, and sent its signal call ahead. In moments the patrol ship, less than three hundred miles away, had reached it, and together they streaked through the dense air in a screaming dive toward Shatnsoma, the capital city. It was directly beneath, and it was not long before they had reached the great palace grounds, and settled on the upper roof. Then the scout leaped out of his tiny craft, and dove for the door. Flashing his credentials, he dove down, and into the first shielded room. Here precious seconds were wasted while a check was made of the credentials the man carried, then he was sent through to the Council Room. And he, too, stood on that exact spot where the other scout, but a few weeks before, had stood—and vanished. Waiting, it seemed, were four councilors and the new Sthanto, Thalt.

“What news, Scout?” asked the Sthanto.

===Chinese===
又一艘小小的侦察舰划破虚空，朝塞特星（Thett）疾驰而去。舰上只载着一个人，他把机器的全部功率都压了上去，向着遥远的塞特星飞掠，速度快得近乎疯狂；但他知道，此行要成，就必须保住这样的速度。

又一艘小船闯入塞特星辽远绵延的大气层，减速到光速以下，向前方发出信号呼叫。顷刻之间，不到三百英里外的巡逻舰便已赶到，两舰一同以尖啸的俯冲穿过浓密大气，直扑首都沙特索马（Shatnsoma）。都城就正在下方，不多时他们便飞抵宏伟的宫苑上空，降落在殿顶上层。侦察员随即纵身跃出小小的座舰，一头扑向大门。他一晃证件，径直俯冲而下，钻进第一间屏蔽室。在这里，核验他所持证件白白耗去了几秒宝贵的光阴，随后他才获准通行，进入评议厅。而他，也同样站上了数周前另一名侦察员站过的那一点——旋即消失。等在那里的，似乎是四位评议员，以及新任斯坦托（Sthanto）塔尔特（Thalt）。

“有何军情，侦察员？”斯坦托问道。

===Original===
“They have arrived in the Universe to Venone, and gone to the planet Venone. They were on the planet when I left. None of our scouts were able to approach the place, as there were innumerable Venonian watchers who would have recognized our deeper skin-color, and destroyed us. Two scouts were rayed, though the Galactians did not see this. Finally we captured two Venonians who had seen it, and attempted to force the information we needed from them. A young man and his chosen mate.

“The man would tell nothing, and we were hurried. So we turned to the girl. These accursed Venonians are courageous for all their pacifism. We were hurried, and yet it was long before we forced her to tell what we needed to know so vitally. She had been one of the notetakers for the Venonian government. We got most of their conversation, but she died of burns before she finished.

“The Galactians know nothing of the twin-ray beyond its action, and that it is an electromagnetic phenomenon, though they have been able to distort it by using a sheet of pure energy. But their walls are impregnable to it, and their power of creating matter from the pure energy of space, as we saw from a distance, would enable them to easily defeat it, were it not that the twin-ray passes through matter without harming it. Any ray which will destroy matter of the natural electrical types, will be stopped.

===Chinese===
“他们已经抵达维诺恩宇宙（Venone），去了维诺恩星。我离开时，他们还在那颗行星上。我们的侦察员谁也无法靠近那地方，那里有数不清的维诺恩人（Venonian）守望者，一眼就能认出我们更深的肤色，把我们毁掉。有两名侦察员遭了射线，银河人（Galactian）并没有瞧见。最后我们抓到了两个目击此事的维诺恩人，想从他们口中逼出我们急需的情报。是一个年轻男子，和他选定的伴侣。

“那男子什么都不肯讲，而我们时间紧迫。于是我们转向那姑娘。这些可诅咒的维诺恩人，尽管力行无战之道，却勇毅得很。我们催得急，可还是费了许久，才逼她说出我们性命攸关、亟需知道的东西。她原是维诺恩政府的记录员之一。他们的谈话内容，我们截获了大半，可她没能讲完，便死于烧伤。

“对于双子射线（twin-ray），银河人除了它的效应、除了知道它是一种电磁现象之外，一无所知；不过他们已经能凭一层纯能量使它发生畸变。但他们的壁障在它面前固若金汤，而他们那手从空间纯粹能量中创造物质的本领——我们曾远远领教——本可以叫他们轻而易举地破掉这道射线，只因双子射线穿透物质而不伤物质，才未能奏效。凡能摧毁天然电性物质的射线，都会被挡下。

===Original===
“The girl was damnably clever, for she gave us only the things we already knew, and but few new facts; knowing that she would inevitably die soon, she talked—but it was empty talk. The one thing of import we have learned is that they burn no fuel, use no fuel of any sort but in some inconceivable manner get their energy from the radiations of the suns of space. This could not be great—but we know she told the truth, and we know their power is great. She told the truth, for we could determine when she lied, by mental action, of course.

“But more we could not learn. The man died without telling anything, merely cursing. He knew nothing anyway, as we already had determined,” concluded the scout.

Silently the Sthanto sat in thought for some moments. Then he raised his head, and looked at the scout once more.

===Chinese===
“那姑娘狡猾得可恨，她只肯交代我们早已知道的事，新东西少得可怜；她自知不久人世，便开了口——说的却尽是空话。我们唯一探明的要紧事情是：他们不烧燃料，不用任何燃料，而是以某种不可思议的方式，从空间诸太阳的辐射中汲取能量。这种来源本不该有多大能量——可我们知道她说的是实话，我们也知道他们的力量强大。她说的确是实话——她哪一句在撒谎，我们都能判明，当然是凭心智探查。

“可再要多的，我们就问不出来了。那男子至死一字不吐，只是咒骂。反正他也一无所知——这一点我们先前便已查明。”侦察员收尾道。

斯坦托默默坐着，出神了片刻。随后他抬起头来，再一次望向那名侦察员。

===Original===
“You have done well. You secured some information of import, which was more than we had dared hope for. But you managed things poorly. The woman should not have died so soon. We can only guess.

“The radiation of the suns of space—hmmm—” Sthanto Thalt’s brow wrinkled in thought. “The radiation of the *suns* of space. Were his power derived from the sun near which he is operating, he would not have said *suns*. It was more than one?”

“It was, oh Sthanto,” replied the scout positively.

===Chinese===
“你办得很好。你弄到了一些要紧的情报，这已超出我们敢指望的分量。只是你行事失当。那女人不该死得那么早。如今我们只能凭空揣测了。

“空间诸太阳的辐射——唔——”斯坦托塔尔特沉思中皱起了眉头。“空间诸太阳的辐射。倘若他的力量得自他活动其旁的那一颗太阳，他说的就不会是‘诸太阳’。是不止一颗？”

“确是如此，斯坦托大人。”侦察员满口肯定地答道。

===Original===
“His power is unreasonable. I doubt that he gave the true explanation. It may well have been that he did not trust the Venonians. I would not, for all their warless ways. But surely the suns of space give very little power at any given point at random. Else space would not be cold.

“But go, Scout, and you will be assigned a position in the fleet. The Colonial fleet, the remains of it, have arrived, and the colonists been removed. They failed. We will use their ships. You will be assigned.” The scout left, and was indeed assigned to a ship of the colonists. The incoming colonial transports had been met at the outposts of the system, and rayed out of existence at once—failures, and bringing danger at their heels. Besides—there was no room for them on Thett without Thessians being crowded uncomfortably.

As their battleships arrived they were conducted to one of the satellites, and each man was “fumigated,” lest he bring disease to the mother planet. Men entered, men apparently emerged. But they were different men.

===Chinese===
“他的力量不合情理。我怀疑他没有吐露真正的解释。多半是他信不过维诺恩人。换了我也信不过，任凭他们多么与世无争。可是，空间中的太阳在任一随意之点所能给出的功率必定微乎其微，不然空间就不会是冷的了。

“去吧，侦察员，舰队里会派给你一个职位。殖民舰队——它的残部——已经抵达，殖民者也都处置掉了。他们失败了。他们的船，我们留下自用。会给你分派职位的。”侦察员退了出去，果然被派到殖民者的一艘船上。那些陆续赶到的殖民运输船，都在星系外围的哨线被截住，当场遭射线化为乌有——失败者，身后还跟着危险。何况——塞特星上也没有空处安置他们，除非叫塞特人（Thessian）挤得苦不堪言。

他们的战舰一艘艘抵达，被引往一颗卫星，每个人都得受一番“熏蒸消毒”，免得把疾病带给母星。进去的是人，出来的似乎也是人。可他们已是另一批人。

===Original===
“It seems,” said the Sthanto softly, after the scout had left, “that we will have little difficulty, for they are, we know, vulnerable to the triple ray. And if we can but once destroy their driving units they will be helpless on our world. I doubt that wild tale of their using no fuel. Even if that be true they will be helpless with their power apparatus destroyed, and—if we miss the first time, we can seek it out, or drive them off!

“All of which is dependent on the fact that they attack at a point where we have a triple ray station to meet them. There are but three of these, actually, but I have had dummy stations, apparently identical with our other real stations, set up in many places.

“This gibberish we hear of creating matter—it is impossible, and surely unsuitable as a weapon. Their misty wall—that may be a force plane, but I know of no such possibility.
