The artificial substance though—why should anyone make it? It but consumes energy, and once made is no more dangerous than ordinary matter, save that there is the possibility of creating it in dangerous position. Remember, we have heard already of the mental suggestions planes—mere force planes—plus a wonderfully developed power of suggestion. They do most of their damage by mental impression. Remember, we have heard already of the mental suggestions of horrible things that drove one fleet of the weak-minded colonists mad.

===Chinese===
侦察员走后，斯坦托轻声说道：“看来我们不会有多大麻烦，因为我们知道，他们对三重射线（triple ray）毫无招架之力。只要我们能哪怕一次毁掉他们的驱动装置，他们在我们的世界上便束手无策。他们不用燃料那个荒诞说法，我不信。就算是真的，他们的功率装置一旦被毁，也照样无能为力；而——就算头一回失手，我们也能把它搜寻出来，再不然就把他们赶走！

“而这一切，都系于一点：他们恰会在我们设有三重射线站迎击的地点发动进攻。这种射线站其实只有三座，但我已命人在多处立起假站，外观与其余真站一般无二。

“我们听来的那些创造物质的呓语——绝无可能，而且多半不宜用作武器。他们那道雾墙——或许是一种力场平面（force plane），可我不知道天地间竟有此物。至于人工物质——谁会去造它？它只会白白耗费能量，一旦造成，也并不比寻常物质更危险，只除非径直把它造在危险的位置上。记住，我们已经听说过他们的精神暗示力场——不过是力场平面——再添上一副发达得出奇的精神暗示本领。他们的杀伤大半是凭精神印象造成的。记住，我们已经听说过，他们以精神暗示出的恐怖之物，曾把一支心智孱弱的殖民舰队逼疯。

===Original===
“And that, I think, we will use to protect ourselves. If we can, with the apparatus which you, my son, have developed, cause them to believe that all the other forts are equally dangerous, and that this one on Thett is the best point of attack—It will be easy. Can you do it?”

“I can, Oh Sthanto, if but a sufficient number of powerful minds may be brought to aid me,” replied the youngest of the four councilmen.

“And you, Ranstud, are the stations ready?” asked the ruler.

“We are ready.”

===Chinese===
“而这一手，我想，正可用来保全我们自己。倘若我们能凭你——我的孩子——所研制的那台装置，让他们相信其余各要塞同样危险，而塞特星上这一座才是最佳的进攻点——那就易如反掌。你做得到吗？”

“做得到，斯坦托大人，只要能为我调集足够多的强大心智。”四位评议员中最年轻的一位答道。

“那么你，兰斯图德（Ranstud），各站可都备好了？”统治者问道。

“我们准备好了。”

## XXII / 塞特星

===Original===

Through the utter void of intergalactic space sped a tiny shell, a wee mite of a ship. Scarcely twenty feet long, it was one single power plant. The man who sat alone in it, as it tore through the void at the maximum speed that even its tiny mass was capable of, when every last twist possible had been given to the distorted time fields, watched a far, far galaxy ahead that seemed unchanging.

Hours, days sped by, and he did not move from his position in the ship. But the ship had crossed the great gulf, and was speeding through the galaxy now. He was near the end. At a reckless speed, he sat motionless before the controls, save for slight movements of supple fingers that directed the ship at a mad pace about some gigantic sun and its family of planets. Suns flashed, grew to discs, and were left behind in the briefest instant.

The ship slowed, the terrific pace it had been holding fell, and dull whine of overworked generators fell to a contented hum. A star was looming, expanding before it. The great sun glowed the characteristic red of a giant as the ship slowed to less than a light-speed, and turned toward a gigantic planet that circled the red sun. The planet was very close to 50,000 miles in diameter, and it revolved at a distance of four and one half billions of miles from the surface of its sun, which made the distance to the center of the titanic primary four billion, eight hundred million miles, in round figures, for the sun’s diameter was close to six hundred and fifty million miles! Greater even than Antares, whose diameter is close to four hundred million miles, was this star of another universe, and even from the billions of miles of distance that its planet revolved, the disc was enormous, a titanic disc of dull red flame. But so low was its surface temperature, that even that enormous disc did not overheat the giant planet.

===Chinese===

星系际空间的死寂虚空里，一具小小的壳体疾掠而过——一艘微如芥子的小船。它长不足二十英尺，整艘船便是一台单独的动力机。扭曲的时间场已被拧到了所能拧出的最后一丝极限，船正以这点微小质量所能榨出的最高速度撕裂虚空；独坐船中的那人，望着前方遥远又遥远的星系，它仿佛纹丝不动。

数小时、数日飞掠而过，他始终不曾离开船中的位置。船已渡过那道巨大的鸿沟，此刻正疾驰在星系之间。终点近了。他以一种不顾一切的速率纹丝不动地坐在操控台前，唯有柔韧的手指轻微移动，引着船发疯般绕过一颗颗巨大的恒星和它们的行星家族。恒星倏然闪至，胀成圆盘，又在最短的一瞬被抛在身后。

船减速了，一直死死维持的可怖速率降了下来，超负荷的发电机那沉闷的啸声落成餍足的嗡鸣。一颗恒星在前方隐现、胀大。船减速到低于光速，掉头飞向那颗环绕红色巨日的巨大行星，大太阳燃着巨星特有的红色光辉。这颗行星直径接近 50,000 英里，沿距其太阳表面四十五亿英里的轨道运转——也就是说，到那颗庞然主星中心的距离，取整数计约四十八亿英里，因为太阳本身的直径就接近六亿五千万英里！这颗异宇宙的恒星，比直径近四亿英里的心宿二还要巨大；即便从行星运转所在的数十亿英里之外望去，它的圆盘也大得骇人——一轮暗红色火焰的巨盘。只是它表面温度极低，纵然圆盘如此庞大，也不至于把这颗巨型行星烤得过热。

===Original===

The planet’s atmosphere stretched out tens of thousands of miles into space, and under the enormous gravitational acceleration of the tremendous mass of that planet, it was near the surface a blanket dense as water. There was no temperature change upon it, though its night was one hundred hours long, and its day the same. The centrifugal force of the rapid rotation of this enormous body had flattened it when still liquid till it seemed now more of the shape of a pumpkin than of an orange. It was really a double planet, for its satellite was a world of one hundred thousand miles diameter, yet smaller in comparison to its giant primary than is Luna in comparison to Earth. It revolved at a distance of five million miles from its primary’s center, and it, too, was swarming with its people.

But the racing ship sped directly toward the great planet, and shrieked its way down through the atmosphere, till its outer shell was radiating far in the violet.

Straight it flew to where a gigantic city sprawled in the heaped, somber masonry, but in some order yet, for on closer inspection the appearance of interlaced circles came over the edge of the giant cities. Ray screens were circular and the city was protected by dozens of stations.

===Chinese===

这颗行星的大气向太空延展出数万英里，在那份惊人质量的巨大引力加速度作用下，贴近表面处已浓稠如水。行星上没有温度变化，尽管它的黑夜长达一百小时，白昼亦然。这个庞然大物急速自转的离心力，早在它尚呈液态之时便把它压扁了，如今它的形状与其说像橙子，不如说更像南瓜。它实则是一对双行星：它的卫星是一颗直径十万英里的世界，只是与巨大主星相比，其比例尚不及月球与地球之比。卫星沿距主星中心五百万英里的轨道运转，上面同样挤满了它的人民。

而那艘疾驰的小船直朝巨行星冲去，尖啸着扎入大气一路下坠，直到外壳辐射的光波远远越入紫区。

它笔直飞向一座巨城。暗色的石构堆叠蔓延，却自有章法：细看之下，巨城边缘显出环环相扣的圆形轮廓。射线护盾皆是圆形，而整座城市由数十座台站守护。

===Original===

The scout was going well under the speed of light now, and a message, imperative and commanding, sped ahead of him. Half a dozen patrol boats flashed up, and fell in beside him, and with him raced to a gigantic building that reared its somber head from the center of the city.

Under a white sky they proceeded to it, and landed on its roof. From the little machine the single man came out. Using the webbed hands and feet that had led the Allied scientists to think them an aquatic race, he swam upward, and through the water-dense atmosphere of the planet toward the door.

Trees overtopped the building, for it had but four stories, above ground, though it was the tallest in the city. The trees, like seaweed, floated most of their enormous weight in the dense air, but the buildings under the gravitational acceleration, which was more than one hundred times Earth’s gravity, could not be built very high ere they crumple under their own weight. Though one of these men weighed approximately two hundred pounds on Earth, for all their short stature, on this planet their weight was more than ten tons! Only the enormously dense atmosphere permitted them to move.

And such an atmosphere! At a temperature of almost exactly 360 degrees centigrade, there was no liquid water on the planet, naturally. At that temperature water cannot be a liquid, no matter what the pressure, and it was a gas. In their own bodies there was liquid water, but only because they lived on heat, their muscles absorbed their energy for work from the heat of the air. They carried in their own muscles refrigeration, and, with that aid, were able to keep liquid water for their life processes. With death, the water evaporated. Almost the entire atmosphere was made up of oxygen, with but a trace of nitrogen, and some amount of carbon dioxide.

===Chinese===

此时侦察舰的速度已远低于光速，一道不容违抗、充满威严的讯息先行飞至。六艘巡逻艇闪出，落在他身侧，随他一同飞向城中心那座暗色巨厦——它正从城市中央昂起暗沉的头颅。

一行人在白色的天空下飞抵巨厦，降落在楼顶。小船里走出唯一的一个人。他摆动手足上的蹼——正是这双蹼，曾让盟方科学家误以为他们是一支水生种族——向上泅游而去，穿过这颗行星水一般浓稠的大气，朝那扇门游去。

树木高过了楼顶，因为这幢建筑地上仅有四层，虽然它已是全城最高。树木如同海藻，庞大的身躯大半悬浮在稠密的空气里；而建筑物在超过地球重力一百多倍的引力加速度之下，根本建不高，否则不等成形便会被自身的重量压垮。这里的人虽然身材矮小，在地球上一个约重二百磅，在这颗行星上体重却超过十吨！全亏这极端稠密的大气，他们才得以行动。

而这样的大气！温度几乎恰在 360 摄氏度，行星上自然没有液态水。在这样的温度下，无论压力多大，水都不可能是液体，只能以气态存在。他们体内固然有液态水，那全因为他们以热为生——肌肉从空气的热量中汲取做功的能量。他们的肌肉里自带制冷机制，借这份助力，方能在生命进程中保住液态水。死亡一至，水便蒸发殆尽。大气几乎全由氧构成，只含极微量的氮，外加一些二氧化碳。

===Original===

Here their enormous strength was not needed, as Arcot had supposed, to move their own bodies, but to enable them to perform the ordinary tasks of life. The mere act of lifting a thing weighing perhaps ten pounds on Earth, here required a lifting force of more than half a ton! No wonder enormous strength had been developed! Such things as a man might carry with him, perhaps a ray pistol, would weigh half a ton; his money would weigh near to a hundred pounds!

But—there were no guns on this world. A man could throw a stone perhaps a short distance, but when a gravitational acceleration of more than a half a mile per second acted on it, and it was hurled through an atmosphere dense as water—what chance was there for a long range?

But these little men of enormous strength did not know other schemes of existence, save in the abstract, and as things of comical peculiarity. To them life on a planet like Earth was as life to a terrestrian on a planetoid such as Ceres, Juno or Eros would have seemed. Even on Thettsost, the satellite planet of Thett, life was strange, and they used lux roofs over their cities, though their weight there was four tons!

===Chinese===

在这里，他们那惊人的力气并不像阿科特原先设想的那样用来移动自己的身体，而是用来完成日常生活中最普通的营生。举起一件在地球约重十磅的东西，在这里需要半吨多的举力！难怪会练出这般惊人的膂力！一个人随身携带之物——比如一把射线手枪——在这里要重上半吨；他的钱币也得重近百磅！

可是——这个世界上没有枪炮。人固然能把一块石头掷出短短一程，但当每秒半英里以上的引力加速度作用在石块上，而它又被掷进浓稠如水的大气——还谈得上什么远程？

然而这些身量矮小、膂力惊人的生灵，对别的生存形态一无所知——除了抽象概念，便只当那是滑稽古怪的奇谈。在他们看来，地球那种行星上的生涯，就好比地球人落到谷神星、婚神星或爱神星那类小行星上的生涯一般匪夷所思。就连在塞特索斯特（Thettsost）——塞特星的卫星行星——生活也够奇特：尽管他们在那里体重足有四吨，城市上空却罩着卢克斯屋顶！

===Original===

As the scout swam through the dense atmosphere of his world toward the entrance way to the building, guards stopped him, and examined his credentials. Then he was led through long halls, and down a shaft ten stories below the planet’s surface, to where a great table occupied a part of a low ceilinged, wide room. This room was shielded, interference screens of all known kinds lined the hollow walls, no rays could reach through it to the men within. The guard changed, and new men examined the scout’s credentials, and he was led still deeper into the bowels of the planet. Once more the guard changed, and he entered a room guarded not by single shields but by triple, and walled with six foot relux, and ceiled with the same strong material. But here, under the enormous gravity, even its great strength required aid in the form of pillars.

A giant of his race sat before a low table. The table ran half the length of the room, and beside it sat four other men. But there were places for more than two dozen.

“A scout from the colony? What news?” demanded the leader. His voice was a growl, deep and throaty.

===Chinese===

侦察员泅过本世界稠密的大气，朝大厦入口通道游去，卫兵拦住他，查验他的证件。随后他被引过长长的厅廊，顺一条竖井降下行星地表以下十层深处，来到一间低顶而宽敞的房间，一张巨桌占去屋内一隅。这间房设有屏蔽，各种已知的干扰屏蔽层衬满空心的夹墙，任何射线都无法穿透它、抵达房内之人。卫兵换了一班，新一班人再度查验侦察员的证件，他又被引着深入行星的内脏。卫兵再换一班，他走进一个房间，守护它的不再是单层护盾，而是三重护盾，四壁是六英尺厚的雷卢克斯，顶棚用的也是同样坚固的材料。但在这种巨大引力之下，纵是它那样的强度，也得靠柱子撑持方才立得住。

他种族中的一个巨人坐在一张矮桌之前。桌子沿屋摆开了半间房长，桌旁坐着另外四人。但屋里留出的座位，足够二十多人。

“殖民星来的侦察员？有什么消息？”为首者喝问。他的嗓音低沉喑哑，如兽咆哮。

===Original===

“Oh mighty Sthanto, I bring news of resistance. We waited too long, in our explorations, and those men of World 3769–8482730–3 have learned too much. We were wrong. They had found the secret of exceeding the speed of light, and can travel through space fully as rapidly as we can, and now, since by some means we cannot fathom, they have learned to combine both our own system and theirs, they have one enormous engine of destruction that travels across their huge universe in less time than it takes us to travel across a planetary system.

“Our cause is lost, which is by far the least of our troubles. Thett is in danger. We cannot hope to combat that ship.”

“Thalt—what means have we. Can we not better them?” demanded Sthanto of his chief scientist.

“Great Sthanto, we know that such a substance can be made when pressure can be brought to bear on cosmic rays under the influence of field 24–7649–321, but that field cannot be produced, because no sufficient concentration of energy is available. Energy cannot be released rapidly enough to replace the losses when the field is developing. The fact that they have that material indicates their possession of an unguessed and terrific energy source. I would have said that there was no energy greater than the energy of matter, but we know the properties of this material and that the triple ray which has at last been perfected, can be produced providing your order for all energy sources is given, will release its energy at a speed comparable to the rate of energy relux in a twin ray, but that the release takes place only in the path of the ray.”

===Chinese===

“尊贵的斯坦托（Sthanto），我带来了抵抗的消息。我们探测得太久，等得太久，3769–8482730–3 号星上那些人已经知晓得太多了。我们错了。他们早已找到超越光速的奥秘，穿行太空的速度已与我们不相上下；而如今，他们又以某种我们无从揣度的手段，学会把我们自己的体系与他们自己的体系合而为一，于是他们握有了一台庞大的毁灭机器，横越他们那浩瀚的宇宙所需的时间，比我们横越一个行星系还短。

“我们的大业已经输定，而这还是所有麻烦里最微不足道的一桩。塞特星危在旦夕。我们没有希望与那艘船抗衡。”

“塔尔特（Thalt）——我们还有什么手段？难道就没有法子胜过他们吗？”斯坦托向他的首席科学家厉声问道。

“伟大的斯坦托，我们知道，只要能在 24–7649–321 号场的作用下对宇宙射线施以压力，便能造出那样的物质；可那个场建立不起来，因为没有足够的能量浓度可供调用。场在成形之时，能量释放的速度不够快，补不上耗散。他们既然握有那种材料，便表明他们掌握着一种无从想象的可怖能源。我原会说，天下没有比物质之能更大的能量；但我们了解这种材料的种种性质，也知道如今终于完善的三重射线（triple ray），只要您一声令下、调集所有能源，便可造出——它释放能量的速率，堪比双子射线（twin ray）中能量反射的速率，只是这种释放只发生在射线途经的路径之上。”

===Original===

“What more, Scout?” asked Sthanto smoothly.

“The ship first appeared in connection with our general attack on world 3769–8482730–3. The attack was near success, their screens were already failing. They have devised a new and very ionized layer as a conductor. It was exceedingly difficult to break, and since their sun had been similarly screened, we could not throw masses of that matter upon them.

“In another sthan of time, we would have destroyed their world. Then the ship appeared. It has molecular rays, magnetic beams and cosmic rays, and a fourth weapon we know nothing of. It has molecular screens, we suspect, but has not had occasion to use them.

“Our heaviest molecular screens flash under their molecular rays. Ordinary screens fall instantly without momentary defense. The ray power is incalculable.

===Chinese===

“还有呢，侦察员？”斯坦托不动声色地问。

“那艘船初次现身，是在我们大举进攻 3769–8482730–3 号星之时。那次进攻眼看就要得手，他们的护盾已然告急。他们设计出一个电离度极高的新型导电层，极难击破；又因他们的太阳也被同样屏蔽起来，我们无法把那种物质的大团质量掷到他们头上。

“再过一个斯坦（sthan）的时间，我们就能毁掉他们的世界。就在这时，那艘船出现了。它装有分子射线、磁场束和宇宙射线，还有第四种我们一无所知的武器。我们怀疑它也装有分子射线屏障，只是一直没机会用上。

“我们最厚重的分子射线屏障，在他们的分子射线之下也只闪得几闪；寻常护盾转瞬即溃，连一瞬的抵御都谈不上。那射线的功率无法估量。

===Original===

“Their magnetic beams are used in conjunction with cosmics. The action of the two causes the relux to induce current, and due to reaction of currents on the magnetic field—”

“And the resistance due to the relux, the relux is first heated to incandescence and then the ship opens out as the air pressure bends the magnetically softened relux?” finished Thalt.

“No, the effect is even more terrific. It explodes into powder,” replied the scout.

“And what happens to worlds that the magnetic ray touches?” inquired the scientist.

===Chinese===

“他们的磁场束与宇宙射线配合使用。两者一齐作用，使雷卢克斯感应出电流，而电流又对磁场产生反作用——”

“于是雷卢克斯的电阻随之生热——雷卢克斯先被灼至白炽，接着磁场使它软化，气压一弯折，船体便绽裂开来？”塔尔特接口替他说完。

“不，效果还要可怖得多。它会炸成齑粉。”侦察员答道。

“那么被这种磁场射线扫中的世界，又会怎样？”科学家问道。

===Original===

“A corner of it touched the world we fought over, and the world shook,” replied the colonist.

“And the last weapon?” asked Sthanto, his voice soft now.

“It seems a ghost. It is a mistiness that comes into existence like a cloud, and what it touches is crushed, what it rams is shattered. It surrounds the great ship, and machines crashing into it at a speed of more than six times that of light are completely destroyed, without in the slightest injuring the shield.

“Then—what caused my departure from the colony—it showed once more its unutterable power. The mistiness formed in the path of our colonial world, number 3769–1–5, and the planet swept against that wall of mistiness, and was shattered, and turned in less than five sthan to a ball of blue-white fire. The wall stopped the planet in its motion. We could not fight that machine, and we left the worlds. The others are coming,” finished the scout.

===Chinese===

“它的一个犄角扫过我们交战的那颗世界，那颗世界都震颤了。”殖民星来客答道。

“那么最后那种武器呢？”斯坦托问道，声音此刻变得轻柔。

“它像个幽灵。那是一片迷雾般的虚影，如云霓般凭空而生：它触到什么，什么便被压碎；它撞上什么，什么便被撞得粉碎。它环护着那艘巨船，机器以六倍以上光速撞进雾里，会被彻底摧毁，而那层雾障却分毫无损。

“还有——正是它逼我离开殖民星——它再度展示了那种无可言喻的威力。那片迷雾在我们殖民星——3769–1–5 号星——的行进路径上凭空成形，整颗行星迎头撞上那堵雾墙，登时粉碎，不到五个斯坦便化作一团蓝白色的火焰。那堵雾墙生生止住了行星的运行。我们斗不过那台机器，只得弃了那些世界而去。其余的人正在赶来。”侦察员说完了。

===Original===

The ruler turned his slightly smiling face to the commander of his armies, who sat beside him.

“Give orders,” he said softly, almost gently, “that a triple ray station be set up under the direction of Thalt, and further notice that all power be made instantly available to it. Add that the colonists are returning defeated, and bringing danger at their heels. The triple ray will destroy each ship as it enters the system.” His hand under the table pushed an invisible protuberance, and from the perfectly conducting relux floor to the equally perfectly conducting ceiling, and between four pillars grouped around the spot where the scout stood, terrific arcs suddenly came into being. They lasted for the thousandth part of a second, and when they suddenly died away, as swiftly as they had come, there was not even ash where the scout had been.

“Have you any suggestions, Thalt?” he asked of the scientist, his voice as soft as before.

“I quite agree with your conduct so far, but the future conduct you had planned is quite unsatisfactory,” replied the scientist. The ruler sat motionless in his great seat, staring fixedly at the scientist. “I think it is time I take your place, therefore.” The place where the ruler had been was suddenly seen as through a dark cloud, then the cloud was gone, and with it the king, only his relux chair, and the bits of lux or relux that had been about his garments remained.

===Chinese===

统治者把他那微微含笑的脸转向坐在身旁的军队统帅。

“传令，”他轻声说道，语调近乎温和，“在塔尔特的主持下建起一座三重射线站；再传令所有能量立时拨归该站调用。另加一条：殖民军正败退而归，身后紧随着灾祸。三重射线会在每一艘船进入本星系时将其摧毁。”他的手在桌下按下一个隐形的凸起，刹那间，从导电尽善的雷卢克斯地板到同样导电尽善的天花板，在环绕侦察员所立之处聚拢的四根立柱之间，可怖的电弧骤然亮起。电弧持续了千分之一秒，待它倏然熄灭——来得有多快，去得便有多快——侦察员立过的地方，连灰烬都没剩下。

“你有什么建议吗，塔尔特？”他问科学家，声音仍像先前一样轻柔。

“您迄今为止的举措，我十分赞同；但您为将来所拟的举措，却实在难以令人满意。”科学家答道。统治者一动不动地坐在宽大的座椅里，直勾勾地盯着科学家。“所以我想，如今该由我来接替您的位置了。”统治者方才所在之处，忽然像隔着一层黑云似的暗了下来，随即黑云散去，国王也随之消失，只留下他那把雷卢克斯座椅，还有他衣物上镶嵌的些许卢克斯与雷卢克斯饰片。

===Original===

“He was a fool,” said the scientist softly, as he rose, “to plan on removing his scientist. Are there any who object to my succession?”

“No one objects,” said Faslar, the ex-king’s Prime Minister and councilor.

“Then I think, Phantal, Commander of planetary forces, that you had best see Ranstud, my assistant, and follow out the plan outlined by my predecessor. And you Tastal, Commander of Fleets, had best bring your fleets near the planets for protection. Go.”

“May I suggest, mighty Thalt,” said Faslar after the others had left, “that my knowledge will be exceedingly useful to you. You have two commanders, neither of whom loves you, and neither of whom is highly capable. The family of Thadstil would be glad to learn who removed that honored gentleman, and the family of Datstir would gladly support him who brought the remover of their head to them.

===Chinese===

“他是个蠢货，”科学家轻声说着，站起身来，“居然打算除掉自己的科学家。有谁反对我继任吗？”

“无人反对。”先王的宰相兼谋臣法斯拉尔（Faslar）说道。

“那么，凡塔尔（Phantal），行星部队司令，你最好去见我的副手兰斯图德（Ranstud），把我前任勾勒出的计划付诸实行。还有你，塔斯塔尔（Tastal），舰队司令，你最好把舰队调近诸星，以资守卫。去吧。”

“容我进言，尊贵的塔尔特，”旁人离去之后，法斯拉尔说道，“我的见识会对您大有用处。您麾下有两位司令，既不爱戴您，也称不上干练。塞德斯蒂尔（Thadstil）一族会很乐意知道，究竟是哪位除掉了他们那位尊贵的老爷；达茨蒂尔（Datstir）一族也会乐于拥戴那个把害死他们家主的凶手送到他们眼前的人。

===Original===

“This would remove two unwelcome menaces, and open places for such as Ranstud and your son Warrtil.

“And,” he said hastily as he saw a slight shift in Thalt’s eyes, “I might say further that the bereaved ones of Parthel would find great interest in certain of my papers, which are only protected by my personal constant watchfulness.”

“Ah, so? And what of Kelston Faln, Faslar?” smiled the new Sthanta.

Thalt’s hand relaxed and they started a conversation and discussion on means of defense.

===Chinese===

“这样一来，便能除去两个讨厌的威胁，还能为兰斯图德这样的人才、以及您的儿子沃尔蒂尔（Warrtil）腾出位子。

“还有，”他瞥见塔尔特的眼神微微一动，赶忙又道，“帕塞尔（Parthel）的遗属若见到我手中某些文书，必定大感兴趣——而那些文书，如今全凭我个人时刻的看护，方得保全。”

“哦，是吗？那么凯尔斯顿·法尔恩（Kelston Faln）又如何呢，法斯拉尔？”新任斯坦托微笑道。

塔尔特的手松了下来，两人随即开始交谈，就防御之策商议起来。

## VIII / 不可战胜，抑或不可驾驭？

===Original===

Fifteen minutes after they started, they came to Shesto. They were forced to land, and explain, for their relux ship was decidedly not the popular Talsonian idea of a lifesaver.
Shesto was defended by two of the machines, and each machine had been equipped with two fully charged accumulators. Their four possible shots were hoped to be sufficient protection, and, so far, had been. The city had been attacked twice, according to Tho Stan Drel, the Talsonian: once by a single ship which had been instantly destroyed, and once by a fleet of six ships. The interval had permitted time to recharge the discharged accumulator, and the fleet had been badly treated. Of the six ships, four had been brought down in rapid succession, and the remaining two ships had fled.
When the first city had been wiped out, with a loss of life well in the hundreds of thousands, the other cities had, to limit of their abilities, set up the protective apparatus. Apparently the Thessians were holding off for the present.
“In a way,” said Morey seriously, “it was distinctly fortunate that we were attacked almost at once. Their instantaneous system of destruction would have worked for the one shot needed to send the *Ancient Mariner* to eternal blazes.” He laughed, but it was a slightly nervous laugh.

===Chinese===

出发十五分钟后，他们便到了谢斯托（Shesto）。他们不得不降落，并作出解释，因为他们那艘雷卢克斯（relux）飞船，实在全然不合塔尔索人（Talsonian）心目中救星的模样。
守卫谢斯托的是两台那种机器，每台都配备了两具满蓄的蓄能器。四发可能的射击，人们指望足以护住城市——而迄今，确实如此。据塔尔索人索·斯坦·德雷尔（Tho Stan Drel）说，这座城市遭受过两次进袭：一次来的是单艘飞船，当即被毁；另一次则是一支六艘飞船的舰队。两场进袭之间的间隙，让放空了的蓄能器得以重新蓄满，那支舰队被打得够呛：六艘之中，四艘接二连三被击落，余下两艘仓皇而逃。
第一座城市被抹平时，丧生者远逾数十万之众；自那以后，其余各城都竭尽所能架设了这种防御装置。看样子，塞特人（Thessian）暂时按兵不动了。
“从某种意义上讲，”莫里（Morey）神色凝重地说，“我们几乎一到就挨了打，实在是万幸。他们那套瞬间毁灭系统只需一发，便足以把老水手号（Ancient Mariner）送进永恒的火海。”他笑了一声，却笑得有些发紧。

===Original===

The terrestrial ship landed in a great grassy court, and out of respect for the parklike smoothness of the turf, Arcot left the ship on its power units, suspended a bit above the surface. Then he, Morey and the Talsonian left the ship. Zezdon Afthen was left with the ship and with Wade in charge, for if some difficulties were encountered, Wade would be able to help them with the ship, and Zezdon Afthen with the tremendous power of his thought locating apparatus, was busy seeking out the Thessian stronghold.
A party of men of Talso met the terrestrians outside the ship.
“Welcome, Men of another world, and to you go our thanks for the destruction of one of our enemies.” The clear thoughts of the spokesman evinced his ability to concentrate.
“And to your world must go our thanks for saving of our lives, and more important, our ship,” replied Arcot. “For the ship represents a thing of enormous value to this entire star-system.”

===Chinese===

地球人的飞船降落在一片宽阔的绿草庭园里。出于对草坪那公园般平整的敬意，阿科特（Arcot）让飞船开着自身的动力装置，悬停在离地表一点点的高处。随后，他、莫里和那位塔尔索人离船而出。泽兹登·阿夫森（Zezdon Afthen）与韦德（Wade）一同留守飞船：万一遇到困难，韦德能从飞船上接应他们；而泽兹登·阿夫森则凭着思维定位装置（thought locating apparatus）的惊人威力，忙着搜寻塞特人的老巢。
一队塔尔索人在船外迎住了地球来客。
“欢迎，异世界的人们。你们替我们毁掉了一个敌人，谢谢你们。”发言者的思维清晰明澈，足见其凝神专注的功力。
“而贵世界救了我们的性命——更要紧的是，救了我们的船——我们也同样感激不尽，”阿科特答道，“因为这艘船，对整个星系而言都是无价之宝。”

===Original===

“I see﻿—understand﻿—your﻿—thoughts that you wish to learn more of this weapon we use. You understand that it is a question among us as to whether it is undefeatable, uncontrollable or just un-understandable. We have had fair success with it. It is not a weapon, was not developed as such; it was an experiment in the line of electric-waves. How it works, what it is, what happens﻿—we do not know.
“But men who can create so marvelous a ship as this of yours, capable of destroying a ship of the Thessians with their own weapons must certainly be able to understand any machine we may make﻿—and you have power?” he finished eagerly.
“Practically infinite power. I will throw into any power line you suggest, all the direct current you wish.” Arcot’s thoughts were pure reflection, but the Talsonian brightened at once.
“I feared it might be alternating﻿—but we can handle direct current. All our transmission is done at high voltage direct current. What potential do you generate? Will we have to install changers?”

===Chinese===

“我看清了——也懂得了——你的——想法：你想更多地了解我们所用的这件武器。你该清楚，在我们中间，它究竟是不可战胜、不可驾驭，还是干脆不可理解，至今仍无定论。凭它御敌，我们颇有斩获。但它并不是武器，当初也不是作为武器研制的；它本是电波方面的一项实验。它如何运作、它是什么、会产生什么——我们一概不知。”
“可你们造得出这般神奇的飞船，还能用塞特人自己的武器击毁他们的飞船，这样的人，必定能弄懂我们造出的任何机器——再说，你们有电力？”他急切地收了尾。
“电力近乎无穷。你指定哪条输电线，你们要多少直流电，我就灌进去多少。”阿科特的思维只是平静的映照，那位塔尔索人却顿时一亮。
“我还担心会是交流——直流我们倒能对付。我们的输电全用高压直流。你们能发多高的电压？用不用我们加装变流器（changer）？”

===Original===

“We generate DC at any voltage up to fifty million, any power up to that needed to lift ten trillion men through their own height in this time a second.” The power represented approximately twenty trillion horsepower.
The Talsonian’s face went blank with amazement as he looked at the ship. “In that tiny thing you generate such power?” he asked in amazement.
“In that tiny ship we generate more than one million times that power,” Arcot said.
“Our power troubles are over,” declared the military man emphatically.

===Chinese===

“我们发直流电，电压要多高有多高，最高可到五千万伏；功率嘛，最高能达到这样的程度：在一秒钟里，把十万亿人各自举过他们自身的身高。”这个功率约合二十万亿马力。
那塔尔索人望着飞船，脸上惊愕得一片空白。“那么个小玩意儿，你们竟发得出这样的功率？”他惊问。
“就在那么小的飞船里，我们发出的功率是它的百万倍还多。”阿科特说。
“我们的能源难题解决了。”那位军人斩钉截铁地宣布。

===Original===

“Our troubles are not over,” replied a civilian who had joined the party, with equal emphasis. “As a matter of fact, they are worse than ever. More tantalizing. What he says means that we have a tremendous power source, but it is in one spot. How are you going to transmit the power? We can’t possibly move any power anywhere near that amount. We couldn’t touch it to our lines without having them all go up in one instantaneous blaze of glory.
“We cannot drain such a lake of power through our tiny power pipes of silver.”
“This man is Stel Felso Theu,” said Tho Stan Drel. “The greatest of our scientists, the man who has invented this weapon which alone seems to offer us hope. And I am afraid he is right. See, there is the University. For the power requirements of their laboratories, a heavy power line has been installed, and it was hoped that you could carry leads into it.” His face showed evident despair greater than ever.
“We can always feed some power into the lines. Let us see just what hope there is. I think that it would be wiser to investigate the power lines at once,” suggested Morey.

===Chinese===

“难题并没有解决。”一位此时也加入进来的人物——一个平民——同样斩钉截铁地回敬，“事实上，难题比以往更棘手，也更磨人。他这话的意思是：我们有了一座了不起的电源，可它定死在一个点上。功率怎么输出去？这么大的量，我们根本运不动。哪怕往我们的线路上碰上一碰，它们也会顷刻间烧成一片辉煌的烈焰，一根不剩。”
“这样一片功率之湖，凭我们那几根纤细的银质输电细管，是引不动的。”
“这位是斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤（Stel Felso Theu），”索·斯坦·德雷尔介绍道，“我们最了不起的科学家，发明了那件似乎唯有它能给我们希望的武器。恐怕，他说得不错。瞧，那边就是大学。为满足其实验室的用电需求，专门架了一条重型输电线，原先就指望你们能把引线接进去。”他脸上的绝望，比先前更明显了。
“往线路上灌些功率总归是办得到的。先去看看究竟有多大指望吧。我认为，马上查验输电线才是上策。”莫里提议。

===Original===

Ten minutes later, with but a single officer now accompanying them, Tho Stan Drel, the terrestrial scientist, and the Talsonian scientist were inspecting the power installation.
They had entered a large stone building, into which led numerous very heavy silver wires. The insulators were silicate glass. Their height suggested a voltage of well over one hundred thousand, and such heavy cables suggested a very heavy amperage, so that a tremendous load was expected.
Within the building were a series of gigantic glass tubes, their walls fully three inches thick, and even so, braced with heavy platinum rods. Inside the tubes were tremendous elements such as the tiny tubes of their machine carried. Great cables led into them, and now their heating coils were glowing a somberly deep red.
Along the walls were the switchboards, dozens of them, all sizes, all types of instruments, strange to the eyes of the terrestrians, and in practically all the light-beam indicator system was used, no metallic pointers, but tiny mirrors directing a very fine line of brilliant light acted as a needle. The system thus had practically no inertia.

===Chinese===

十分钟后，此时随行的只剩一名军官，索·斯坦·德雷尔、地球来的科学家，连同那位塔尔索科学家，已在查验供电设施。
他们进了一座巨大的石砌建筑，许许多多极粗的银线通进其中。绝缘子是硅酸盐玻璃的，其高度表明电压远在十万伏之上；线缆如此粗重，又表明电流极大——预期的负载想必极为可观。
建筑内竖着一排巨型玻璃管，管壁足足三英寸厚，即便如此，还用粗大的铂杆加固着。管内装着巨大的电极，与他们机器上那些小管里的电极同类。粗大的缆线通入管中，此刻管内的加热线圈正泛着深沉的暗红。
沿墙排开的是配电盘，足足数十面，大小齐备；各式仪表令地球人大开眼界，而且几乎全都采用光束指示系统：不见金属指针，而是由小镜子导引一缕极细的亮光充当指针——整套系统因此几乎没有惯性。

===Original===

“Are these the changers?” asked Arcot gazing at the gigantic tubes.
“They are; each tube will handle up to a hundred thousand volts,” said Stel Felso Theu.
“But I fear, Stel Felso Theu, that these tubes will carry power only one way; that is, it would be impossible for power to be pumped from here into the power house, though the process can be reversed,” pointed out Arcot. “Radio tubes work only one way, which is why they can act as rectifiers. The same was true of these tubes. They could carry power one way only.”
“True, of tubes in general,” replied the Talsonian, “and I see by that that you know the entire theory of our tubes, which is rather abstruse.”
“We use them on the ship, in special form,” interrupted Arcot.

===Chinese===

“这些就是变流器吗？”阿科特凝视着那些巨管问道。
“正是；每根管子能承受十万伏。”斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤说。
“不过，斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤，恐怕这些管子只能单程输送功率；也就是说，要把功率从这里泵进电厂，是办不到的——尽管这个过程可以反转过来。”阿科特指出，“无线电真空管就只朝一个方向导通，正因如此，它们才能用作整流器。这些管子也是一样，只能朝一个方向送功率。”
“就一般的管子而言，确实如此，”那位塔尔索人答道，“由此可见，我们这些管子的整套理论，你已经尽数了然——那理论可是相当深奥呢。”
“我们的船上就用着它们，是特制的形式。”阿科特插言。

===Original===

“Then I will only say that the college here has a very complete electric power plant of its own. On special occasions, the power generated here is needed by the city, and so we arranged the tubes with switches which could reverse the flow. At present they are operating to pour power into the city.
“If your ship can generate such tremendous power, I suspect that it would be wiser to eliminate the tubes from the circuit, for they put certain restrictions on the line. The main power plant in the city has tube banks capable of handling anything the line would. I suggest that your voltage be set at the maximum that the line will carry without breakdown, and the amperage can be made as high as possible without heat loss.”
“Good enough. The line to the city power will stand what pressure?”
“It is good for the maximum of these tubes,” replied the Talsonian.
“Then get into communication with the city plant and tell them to prepare for every work-unit they can carry. I’ll get the generator.” Arcot turned, and flew on his power suit to the ship.

===Chinese===

“那我只需说明一点：这里的学院自备了一座相当完备的电厂。遇上特殊情形，城里也需要这里发的电，所以我们给管子配了开关，能让流向反转。眼下，它们正往城里灌电。”
“既然贵船能发这样惊人的功率，我想，更明智的做法是把管子从线路里剔除——它们对线路总有几分掣肘。城里的主电厂装有成组的管子，输电线承受得住的，它们都承受得住。我建议：电压定在线路不致击穿所能承受的最高值；电流则在不产生热损耗的前提下，能开多高便开多高。”
“好。通往城电厂的线路，耐得住多大电压？”
“这些管子的最高耐压，它都承受得住。”塔尔索人答道。
“那就与城电厂取得联络，叫他们做好准备：线路能输送多少功单位（work-unit），就预备接收多少。我去取发电机。”阿科特一转身，驾着分子动力服飞回船去。

===Original===

In a few moments he was back, a molecular pistol in one hand, and suspended in front of him on nothing but a ray of ionized air, to all appearances, a cylindrical apparatus, with a small cubical base.
The cylinder was about four feet long, and the cubical box about eighteen inches on a side.
“What is that, and what supports it?” asked the Talsonian scientists in surprise.
“The thing is supported by a ray which directs the molecules of a small bar in the top clamp, driving it up,” explained Morey, “and that is the generator.”
“That! Why it is hardly as big as a man!” exclaimed the Talsonian.

===Chinese===

不多时他便回来了，一手握着一把分子手枪（molecular pistol），身前悬着一台装置：看样子是个圆柱体，底下连着一只小小的立方箱体——托着它的，只有一束电离空气。
圆柱约长四英尺，立方箱体边长约十八英寸。
“那是什么？又是什么把它托住的？”塔尔索科学家们诧异地问。
“托住它的是一束射线：那射线驱动顶部夹钳里一根小棒的分子，使之齐齐向上——装置便被顶了起来，”莫里解释道，“而那台，就是发电机。”
“那个！嘿，它还不及一个人大哩！”塔尔索人惊呼。

===Original===

“Nevertheless, it can generate a billion horsepower. But you couldn’t get the power away if you did generate it.” He turned toward Arcot, and called to him.
“Arcot﻿—set it down and let her rip on about half a million horsepower for a second or so. Air arc. Won’t hurt it﻿—she’s made of lux and relux.”
Arcot grinned, and set it on the ground. “Make an awful hole in the ground.”
“Oh﻿—go ahead. It will satisfy this fellow, I think,” replied Morey.

===Chinese===

“话虽如此，它能发出十亿马力。可就算发了出来，这份功率你们也取不走。”他转身朝阿科特喊道。
“阿科特——把它放下来，让它撒开了跑，五十万马力上下，跑个一两秒。打空气电弧。伤不着它——它是卢克斯（lux）和雷卢克斯造的。”
阿科特咧嘴一笑，把它放到地上。“这可要在地上烧出个可怕的窟窿了。”
“哦——放手干吧。我想，这足以叫这位老兄心服口服。”莫里答道。

===Original===

Arcot pulled a very thin lux metal cord from his pocket, and attached one end of a long loop to one tiny switch, and the other to a second. Then he adjusted three small dials. The wire in hand, he retreated to a distance of nearly two hundred feet, while Morey warned the Talsonians back. Arcot pulled one end of his cord.
Instantly a terrific roar nearly deafened the men, a solid sheet of blinding flame reached in a flaming cone into the air for nearly fifty feet. The screeching roar continued for a moment, then the heat was so intense that Arcot could stand no more, and pulled the cord. The flame died instantly, though a slight ionization clung briefly. In a moment it had cooled to white, and was cooling slowly through orange﻿—red deep﻿—red﻿—
The grass for thirty feet about was gone, the soil for ten feet about was molten, boiling. The machine itself was in a little crater, half sunk in boiling rock. The Talsonians stared in amazement. Then a sort of sigh escaped them and they started forward. Arcot raised his molecular pistol, a blue green ray reached out, and the rock suddenly was black. It settled swiftly down, and a slight depression was the only evidence of the terrific action.
Arcot walked over the now cool rock, cooled by the action of the molecular ray. In driving the molecules downward, the work was done by the heat of these molecules. The machine was frozen in the solid lava.

===Chinese===

阿科特从口袋里抽出一根极细的卢克斯金属线，把线绕成的长环一端系在一只小开关上，另一端系在第二只上。接着他调好三只小刻度盘，把线攥在手里，退到将近两百英尺之外；莫里则招呼塔尔索人后退。阿科特猛地拽动线头。
霎时，一声骇人的轰吼几乎震聋了众人；一整片耀得人睁不开眼的烈焰结成燃烧的锥体，直刺空中将近五十英尺。尖啸的轰鸣又持续了片刻，热浪便已灼人至极，阿科特再也受不住了，拽动线绳。火焰霎时熄灭，只余一缕淡淡的电离短暂萦绕。转眼间，它已冷得只余白热，继而缓缓由橙转深红——红——
周围三十英尺内的青草化为乌有，十英尺内的土壤熔化、翻沸。机器本身落在一个小坑里，半截埋进滚沸的岩石。塔尔索人们看得呆了。继而，他们中间透出一声近乎叹息的低呼，便要抢步上前。阿科特举起分子手枪，一束蓝绿色的射线探出，岩石霎时变黑，迅速凝沉下去；方才那番惊天动地的威力，只剩一处浅浅的凹坑可为见证。
阿科特踏过业已冷却的岩石——正是分子射线的作用使它冷却：分子被驱向下行的过程中，所做的功恰由这些分子自身的热量支付。机器被封冻在凝固的熔岩里。

===Original===

“Brilliant idea, Morey,” said Arcot disgustedly. “It’ll be a nice job breaking it loose.”
Morey stuck the lux metal bar in the top clamp, walked off some distance, and snapped on the power. The rock immediately about the machine was molten again. A touch of the molecular pistol to the lux metal bar, and the machine jumped free of the molten rock.
Morey shut off the power. The machine was perfectly clean, and extremely hot.
“And your ship is made of that stuff!” exclaimed the Talsonian scientist. “What will destroy it?”

===Chinese===

“真是高招啊，莫里，”阿科特没好气地说，“要把它弄脱身，可真是桩美差。”
莫里把那根卢克斯金属棒插回顶部夹钳，走出一段距离，啪地合上功率。机器四周的岩石再度熔化。分子手枪朝那根金属棒轻轻一触，机器便从熔岩里一跃而出。
莫里切断功率。机器干干净净，却烫得惊人。
“你们那艘船，竟然就是用这种材料造的！”塔尔索科学家惊呼，“那还有什么能毁得了它？”

===Original===

“Your weapon will, apparently.”
“But do you believe that we have power enough?” asked Morey with a smile.
“No﻿—it’s entirely too much. Can you tone that condensed lightning bolt down to a workable level?”

===Chinese===

“看样子，你们的武器能。”
“不过——你们该相信我们的功率够用了吧？”莫里含笑问道。
“不——是大得过了头。那道凝缩起来的闪电，你们能把它调到好用的水平吗？”

## XXIII / 维诺恩

===Original===
Up from Earth, out of its clear blue sky, and into the glare and dark of space and near a sun the ship soared. They had been holding it motionless over New York, and now as it rose, hundreds of tiny craft, and a few large excursion ships followed it until it was out of Earth’s atmosphere. Then—it was gone. Gone across space, racing toward that far Universe at a speed no other thing could equal. In minutes the great disc of the Universe had taken form behind them, as they took their route photographs to find their way back to Earth after the battle, if still they could come.

Then into the stillness of the Intergalactic spaces.

“This will be our first opportunity to test the full speed of this ship. We have never tried its velocity, and we should measure it now. Take a sight on the diameter of the Island, as seen from here, Morey. Then we will travel ten seconds, and look again.”

===Chinese===
飞船从地球腾空而起，冲出澄澈的蓝天，驶入明暗交错的太空，又从一颗恒星近旁掠过。他们原本让它悬停在纽约上空一动不动；此刻它冉冉上升，成百上千艘小型飞行器，还有几艘大型游览船一路相随，直到它出了地球大气层。接着——它不见了。消失于太空深处，以世间万物无可匹敌的速度，朝那个遥远的宇宙疾驰而去。几分钟内，自己这个宇宙的巨大圆盘已在他们身后成形；他们拍下沿途的航线照片，好在大战之后——倘若他们还能回来——循原路找到地球。

随后，便是一路星系际空间的死寂。

“这将是我们头一回有机会检验这艘船的极速。这船的速度我们还从未试过，现在正该测一测。莫里，从这里测一下这座岛的视直径。然后我们飞行十秒，再看一遍。”

===Original===
Half a million light years from the center of the Island now, the great disc spread out over the vast space behind them, apparently the size of a dinner plate at about thirty inches distance, it was more than two hundred and fifty thousand light years across. Checking carefully, Morey read their distance as just shy of five hundred thousand light years.

“Hold on—here we go,” called Arcot. Space was suddenly black, and beside them ran the twin ghost ships that follow always when space is closed to the smallest compass, for light leaving, goes around a space whose radius is measured in miles, instead of light centuries and returns. There was no sound, no slightest vibration, only Torlos’ iron bones felt a slight shock as the inconceivable currents flowed into the gigantic space distortion coil from the storage fields, their shielded magnetic flux leaking by in some slight degree.

For ten seconds that seemed minutes Arcot held the ship on the course under the maximum combined powers of space distortion and time field distortion. Then he released both simultaneously.

===Chinese===
此刻他们距岛的中央已有五十万光年，巨大的圆盘在身后浩瀚的太空里铺展开来，看上去就像三十英寸开外的一只餐盘，实际直径却超过二十五万光年。莫里仔细核对，读出他们的距离是略少于五十万光年。

“抓稳——开始了，”阿科特喊道。太空骤然变得漆黑，船侧掠过一对孪生的幽影船（ghost ships）——每当空间被蜷缩到最小尺度，它们总会如影随形，因为此时离去的光，会绕过一个半径以英里而非光世纪计的空间，折返而回。没有声音，没有丝毫震颤，只有托洛斯的铁骨感到一阵轻微的震荡——难以想象的强大电流正从储能场涌入巨大的空间畸变线圈，线圈屏蔽的磁通仍有微量的泄漏。

这十秒钟漫长得如同几分钟，阿科特以空间畸变与时间场畸变的双重最大功率，把飞船牢牢按在航向上。随后，他同时放开了两者。

===Original===
The velvet black of space was about them as before, but now the disc of the Nebula was tiny behind them! So tiny was it, that these men, who knew its magnitude, gasped in sudden wonder. None of them had been able to conceive of such a velocity as this ship had shown! In seconds, Morey announced a moment later, they had traveled *one million, one hundred thousand light years*! Their velocity was six hundred and sixty quadrillion miles per second!

“Then it will take us only a little over one thousand seconds to travel the hundred and fifty million light years, at 110,000 light years per second—that’s about the radius of our galaxy, isn’t it!” exclaimed Wade.

They started on now, and one thousand and ten seconds, or a little more than eighteen minutes later, they stopped again. So far behind them now as to be almost lost in the far scattered universes, lay their own Island, and carefully they photographed the Universe that now lay less than twenty million light years ahead. Still, it was further, even after crossing this enormous gulf, than are many of those nebulae we see from Earth, many of which lie within that distance. They must proceed cautiously now, for they did not know the exact distance to the Nebula. Carefully, running forward in jumps of five million light years, forty-five second drives, they worked nearer.

===Chinese===
天鹅绒般的黑色太空一如既往地包裹着他们，可此刻身后那银河的圆盘已经小得可怜！小得让这些深知它体量的人猝然惊得倒吸一口气。谁也不曾设想这艘船竟跑得出这样的速度！莫里稍后宣布：仅仅几秒钟，他们就已飞出一百一十万光年！他们的速度是每秒六十六亿亿英里！

“那么，以每秒 110,000 光年的速度，飞完一亿五千万光年只需一千秒出头——这个数差不多就是我们银河系的半径了，对不对！”韦德喊道。

他们继续上路；一千零一十秒——十八分钟稍多——之后，他们再次停下。自家的这座岛已被远远抛在身后，几乎湮没在四面遥布的诸宇宙之间；他们仔细拍下了前方那座宇宙的照片，此刻距它已不足两千万光年。尽管已跨过这道浩瀚鸿沟，它仍比我们从地球上望见的许多星云更远——那些星云有不少就散布在这样的距离之内。如今必须谨慎前行，因为他们并不知道到那片星云的准确距离。他们小心翼翼，以四十五秒推进、五百万光年一跃的步子，步步逼近。

===Original===
Then finally they entered the Island, and drove toward the denser center.

“Good Lord, Arcot, look at those suns!” exclaimed Morey in amazement. For the first time they were seeing the suns of this system at a range that permitted observation, and Arcot had stopped to observe. The first one they had chosen had been a blue-white giant of enormous mass, nearly one hundred and fifty times as heavy as our own sun, and all the enormous surface was radiating power into space at a rate of nearly thirty thousand horsepower per square inch! No planets circled it, however, in its journey through space.

“I’ve been noticing the number of giants here. Look around.”

===Chinese===
终于，他们驶入了那座岛，朝恒星稠密的核心进发。

“天哪，阿科特，快看那些太阳！”莫里惊呼。这是他们头一回来到容许观测的距离上看清这个宇宙的诸太阳，阿科特当即停船观测。他们选中的头一颗是一颗蓝白巨星，质量惊人，近乎我们太阳的一百五十倍，那巨大的表面正以每平方英寸近三万马力的速率向太空倾泻功率！然而它漫游太空，却没有一颗行星相伴环绕。

“我早就注意到这里的巨星未免太多。你四下看看。”

===Original===
The *Thought* moved on, on to other suns. They must find one that was inhabited.

They stopped at last near a great orange giant, and examined it. It had indeed planets, and as Arcot watched, he saw in the telectroscope a line of gigantic freighters rise from the world, and whisk off to nothingness as they exceeded the speed of light! Instantly he started the *Thought* searching in time fields for the freighters. He found them, and followed them as they raced across the void. He knew he was visible to them, and as he suspected, they soon stopped, slowing down and signaling to him.

“Morey—take the *Thought*. I’m going to visit them in the *Banderlog* as I think we shall name the tender,” called Arcot, stripping off the headset, and leaving the control seat. The other fleet of ships was now less than a hundred thousand miles away, clearly visible in the telectroscope. They were still signaling, and Arcot had set an automatic signaling device flashing an enormously powerful searchlight toward them in a succession of dots and dashes, an obvious signal, though also, obviously unintelligible to those others.

===Chinese===
思想号继续前行，驶向其他的太阳。他们必须找到一颗有人居住的。

他们终于在一颗硕大的橙色巨星近旁停下，加以察看。它确实拥有行星，而且就在阿科特注视之际，他从电视望远镜里看见一列巨型货船自那颗行星腾起，待到超过光速，便倏然隐没于无形！他立刻驾驶思想号在时间场中搜寻那些货船。找到了，他便尾随着它们横越虚空疾驰。他知道自己已被对方看见，而正如所料，那些船很快停了下来，一边减速，一边向他发出信号。

“莫里——接管思想号。我坐班德洛格号（Banderlog）过去拜访——我看就用这个名字给交通艇命名，”阿科特喊道，一面摘下头环，离开了操控座椅。那支船队此刻已近在十万英里之内，在电视望远镜里清晰可辨。对方仍在发信号；阿科特则启动了一台自动信号装置，用一盏功率大得惊人的探照灯朝他们打出一连串点划——信号再明白不过，只是对方显然无从索解。

===Original===
“Is it safe, Arcot?” asked Torlos anxiously. To approach those enormous ships in the relatively tiny *Banderlog* seemed unwise.

“Far safer than they’ll believe. Remember, only the *Thought* could stand up against such weapons as even the *Banderlog* carries, run as they are by cosmic energy,” replied Arcot, diving down toward the little tender.

In a moment it was out through the lock, and sped away from them like a bullet, reaching the distant stranger fleet in less than ten seconds.

===Chinese===
“这样安全吗，阿科特？”托洛斯忧心忡忡地问。乘着相对小得可怜的班德洛格号去接近那些巨型飞船，似乎并不明智。

“比他们敢信的还要安全。记住，就连班德洛格号携带的那种武器——以宇宙之力驱动的武器——也只有思想号才抵挡得住，”阿科特答道，向那艘小交通艇俯冲而去。

转眼之间，小艇便穿出气闸舱，像一颗子弹般飞离，不出十秒便抵达了远方的陌生船队。

===Original===
“They are communicating by thought!” announced Zezdon Afthen presently. “But I cannot understand them, for the impulses are too weak to be intelligently received.”

For nearly an hour the *Banderlog* hung beside the fleet, then it turned about, and raced once more to the *Thought*. Inside the lock, and a moment later Arcot appeared again on the threshold of the door. He looked immensely relieved.

“Well, I have some good news,” he said and smiled, sitting down. “Follow that bunch, Morey, and I’ll tell you about it. Set it and she’ll hold nicely. We have a long way to go, and those are slow freighters, accompanied by one Cruiser.

===Chinese===
“他们在用思维交流！”泽兹登·阿夫森不久便宣告，“可我读不懂他们，那些脉冲太过微弱，接收不出其中的含义。”

将近一个钟头，班德洛格号一直悬停在船队近旁，随后掉转船头，再一次朝思想号疾驰而回。进了气闸舱，片刻之后，阿科特重新出现在舱门口，神情如释重负。

“好了，我带来一点好消息，”他说着坐下，面带微笑。“莫里，跟上那批船，我边飞边讲。设定好航向，她会咬得很稳。我们要走的路还长，那些是慢速货船，由一艘巡洋舰随行。

===Original===
“Those men,” he began, “are men of Venone. You remember Thett’s records said something of the Mighty Warless Ones of Venone? Those are they. They inhabit most of this universe, leaving the Thessians but four planets of a minor sun, way off in one corner. It seems the Thessians are their undesirable exiles, those who have, from generation to generation, been either forced to go there, or who wanted to go there.

“They did not like the easier and more effective method of disposing of undesirables, the instantaneous death chamber they now use. Thett was their prison world. No one ever returned and his family could go with him if they desired, but if they did not, they were carefully watched for outcroppings of undesirable traits—murder, crime of any sort, any habitual tendency to injustice.

“About six hundred years ago of our time, Thett revolted. There were scientists there, and their scientists had discovered a thing that they had been seeking for generations—the Twin-ray. I don’t know what it is, and the Venonians don’t either. It is the ray that destroys relux and lux, however, and can be carried only on a machine the size of their forts, due to some limitations. Just what those limitations are the Venonians don’t know. Other than that ray they had no new weapons.

===Chinese===
“那些人，”他开口道，“是维诺恩星（Venone）的人。还记得塞特星的档案里提过维诺恩的无战伟者（Mighty Warless Ones）吗？就是他们。他们栖居在这个宇宙的大部分地方，只给塞特人留下一个不起眼的小恒星的四颗行星，远在天边一角。看来，塞特人正是他们流放的劣民——世世代代，不是被迫迁去，便是自愿前往。

“维诺恩人不喜欢用更省事也更有效的法子处置不良分子——也就是他们如今使用的那种瞬间死刑室（instantaneous death chamber）。塞特星曾是他们的监狱之星。流放者没有一个回来过；家人若愿意，可以随行；若不愿，便会受到严密监视，看他们身上可否有不良品性冒头——凶杀、任何罪行、任何惯常的不义倾向。

“以我们的时间计，约六百年前，塞特星反了。那里也有科学家，而他们的科学家发现了世世代代苦求不得的一样东西——双子射线（Twin-ray）。我不知道它是什么，维诺恩人也不知道。只知道那是能摧毁雷卢克斯和卢克斯的射线，而且由于某些限制，只能装载在要塞那么大的机器上。那些限制究竟为何，维诺恩人同样无从知晓。除了那道射线，他们没有任何新武器。

===Original===
“But it was enough. Their guard ships which had circled the worlds of the prison system, Antseck, were suddenly destroyed, so suddenly that Venone received no word of it till a consignment ship, bringing prisoners, discovered their absence. The consignment ship returned without landing. Thett was now independent. But they were bound to their system, for although they had the molecular ships, they had never been permitted to have time apparatus, nor to see it, nor was anyone who knew its principles ever consigned there. The result was that they were as isolated as ever.

“This was for two centuries. Two centuries later it was worked out by one of their scientists, and the Warless Ones had a War of defense. Their small fleet of cruisers, designed for rescue work and for clearing space lanes of wrecks and asteroids, was destroyed instantly, their world was protected only by the ray screen, which the Thessians did not have, and by the fact that they could build more cruisers. In less than a year Thett was defeated, and beaten back to her world, though Venone could not overcome Thett, now, for around their planets they had so many forts projecting the deadly rays, that no ship could approach.

“Then Thett learned how to make the screen, and came again. Venone had planetoid stations, that projected molecular rays of an intensity I wonder at, with their system of projecting. It seems these people have force-power feeds that operate through space, by which an entire solar system can tie in for power, and they fed these stations in that way. Lord only knows what tubes they had, but the Thessians couldn’t get the power to fight.

===Chinese===
“但这已经足够。环绕监狱星系安塞克诸世界巡逻的守卫飞船被骤然摧毁，事起仓促，维诺恩竟一无所知，直到一艘押送船（consignment ship）押着囚犯到来，才发现守卫船杳无踪影。押送船没有降落，径直返航。塞特星就此独立。但他们仍被困在自己的星系里，因为尽管他们有分子飞船，却从未获准拥有时间装置，也不许见识它，凡通晓其原理的人也从不曾被流放到那里。结果便是，他们依旧与世隔绝。

“这样的局面持续了两个世纪。两百年后，他们的一位科学家把时间装置造了出来，于是无战伟者（Warless Ones）不得不打了一场自卫之战。维诺恩那支为救援和清理航道残骸与小行星而设计的小小巡洋舰队顷刻覆灭，他们的世界仅靠射线屏障护佑——那是塞特人所没有的——此外便只剩下他们还能再造巡洋舰这一条。不到一年，塞特星便告战败，被逐回自己的世界；只是维诺恩至此也无法再降服塞特星，因为他们在诸行星周围修筑了太多投射死光的要塞，任何飞船都无法接近。

“后来塞特星学会了制造屏障，卷土重来。维诺恩设有一批小行星台站（planetoid stations），以其独到的投射系统，射出强度令我叹为观止的分子射线。看来这些人类拥有贯通太空的力能馈线（force-power feeds），可以让整个恒星系并网供电，那些台站便是由此馈能的。天知道他们配的是什么功率管，反正塞特人拼不出能与之抗衡的功率。

===Original===
“They’ve been let alone since then, they did not know why. I told them what their dear friends had been doing in that time, and the Venonians were immensely surprised, and very evidently sorry. They begged my pardon for letting loose such a menace, quite sincerely feeling that it was their fault. They offered any help they could give, and I told them that a chart of this system would be of the greatest use. They are going now to Venone, and we are to go with them, and see what they have to offer. Also, they want a demonstration of this ‘remarkable ship that can defeat whole fleets of Thessians, and destroy or make planets at will,’ ” concluded Arcot.

“I do not in the least blame them for wanting to see this ship in operation, Arcot, but they are, very evidently, a much older race than yours,” said Torlos, his thoughts coming clear and sharp, as those of a man who has thought over what he says carefully. “Are you not running danger that their minds may be more powerful than yours, that this story they have told you is but a ruse to get this ship on their world where thousand, millions can concentrate their will against you and capture the ship by mind where they cannot capture it by force?”

“That,” agreed Arcot, “is where ‘the rub’ comes in as an ancient poet of Earth put it. I don’t know and I did not have a chance to see. Wherefore I am about to do some work. Let me have the controls, Morey, will you?”

===Chinese===
“打那以后，他们再未受到侵扰，自己也不明所以。我把他们那些亲爱的邻居在这段时间里的所作所为告诉了他们，维诺恩人大为震动，而且显而易见深感歉疚。他们为放出了这样一个祸患而向我致歉，并且发自内心地认为那是他们的过错。他们表示愿尽其所能施以援手，我则告诉他们，眼下最有用的，莫过于一份这个宇宙的星图。此刻他们正返回维诺恩星，我们随行同去，看看他们能拿出些什么。另外，他们还想亲眼见识见识这艘‘能击败整支塞特舰队、随心所欲毁灭或制造行星的非凡飞船’。”阿科特收尾道。

“阿科特，他们想看看这艘船的实际本领，这我丝毫不能责怪。但显而易见，他们是比你们古老得多的种族，”托洛斯说道。他的思绪传递得清晰而锐利，像一个对言辞字斟句酌之人的心声。“你难道不担心，他们的心智也许强过你们？他们讲的这番故事，会不会只是个圈套——诱使这艘船飞临他们的世界，让成千上万、乃至亿万之众把意志汇聚起来对付你，在武力夺不到船的地方，用心智把船夺走？”

“这一点，”阿科特表示赞同，“正是地球一位古代诗人所说的‘症结’所在。我不知道答案，也没有机会验证。所以我现在得做点工作了。莫里，把操控交给我，好吗？”

===Original===
Arcot made a new ship. It was made entirely, perforce, of cosmium, lux and relux, for those were the only forms of matter he could create in space permanently from energy. It was equipped with gravity drive, and time distortion speed apparatus, and his far better trained mind finished this smaller ship with his titanic tools in less than the two days that it took them to reach Venone. In the meantime, the Venonian cruiser had drawn close, and watched in amazement as the ship was fashioned from the energy of space, became a thing of glistening matter, materializing from the absolute void of space, and forming under titanic tools such as the commander could not visualize.

Now, this move was partly the reason for this construction, for while the Venonian was busy, absorbed in watching the miraculous construction, his mind was not shielded, and it was open for observation of two such wonderfully trained minds as those of Zezdon Afthen and Zezdon Inthel. With their instruments and wonderfully developed mind-science, aided at times by Morey’s less skillful, but more powerful mind of his older race, and powerful too, both because of long concentration and training, and because of his individual inheritance, they examined the minds of many of the officers of the ship without their awareness.

As a final test, Arcot, having finished the ship, suggested that the Venonian officer and one of the men of his ship have a trial of mental powers.

===Chinese===
阿科特造了一艘新船。它只得完全用科斯米姆、卢克斯和雷卢克斯造成，因为唯有这几种物质形态，他才能以能量在太空中永久地创造出来。新船装备了引力驱动装置和时间畸变加速装置；他那受训远为精深的心智，凭着那些巨神般的工具，在他们赶抵维诺恩星所需的两天时间之内，就造好了这艘较小的船。与此同时，那艘维诺恩巡洋舰已渐渐逼近，惊愕地注视着这艘船如何由太空的能量塑就，化作一件流光溢彩的实体——从太空的绝对虚空中物质化而出，在那些指挥官根本无法想象的伟力工具之下渐次成形。

而这一建造，部分正是出于这层用意：那维诺恩人全神贯注于观看这场奇迹般的建造，心神无暇设防，便向泽兹登·阿夫森与泽兹登·因特尔两颗训练精妙的心智敞开了。两人凭借仪器与高度发达的心智科学，还不时有莫里从旁襄助——莫里的心智技巧稍逊，力量却更强：那属于他更古老的种族，其强大既源于长年的凝神与训练，也源于他个人的禀赋。他们就这样在众军官毫无觉察之下，探查了船上许多军官的心智。

船造好之后，作为最后一项检验，阿科特提议：由那位维诺恩军官和他船上的一名船员，来一场心智力量的较量。

===Original===
Zezdon Afthen tried first, and between the two ships, racing along side by side at a speed unthinkable, the two men struggled with those forces of will.

Quickly Zezdon Afthen told Arcot what he had learned.

The sun of Venone was close, now, and Arcot prepared to use as he intended the little space machine he had made. Morey took it, and went away from the *Thought* flying on its time field. The ship had been stocked with lead fuel for its matter-burning generators from the supply that had been brought on the *Thought* for emergencies, and the air had come from the *Thought*’s great tanks. Morey was going to Venone ahead of the *Thought* to scout—“to see many of the important men of Venone and find out from them what I can of the relationship between Venone and Thett.”

===Chinese===
泽兹登·阿夫森率先出手。两艘船以难以想象的速度并肩疾驰，那两个维诺恩人则在意志之力中较起了劲。

泽兹登·阿夫森很快便把探得的结果告诉了阿科特。

维诺恩星的太阳已然在望，阿科特准备按原先的打算动用他造的那艘小飞船。莫里驾着它，凭借自身的时间场驶离思想号。小船为物质焚烧发电机储备了铅燃料，取自思想号为应急而携带的补给，空气则取自思想号的大储气罐。莫里要赶在思想号之前先行去维诺恩星侦查——“去见许多维诺恩星的重要人物，从他们口中尽量探明维诺恩与塞特星之间的关系。”

===Original===
Hours later Morey returned with a favorable report. He had seen many of the important men of Venone, and conversed with them mentally from the safety of his ship, where the specially installed gravity apparatus had protected him and the ship against the enormous gravity of this gigantic world. He did not describe Venone; he wanted them to see it as he had first seen it.

So the little ship, which had served its purpose now, was destroyed, nearly a light year from Venone, and left a crushed wreck when two plates of artificial matter had closed upon it, destroying the apparatus, lest some unwelcome finder use it. There was little about it, the gravity apparatus alone perhaps, that might have been of use to Thett, and Thett already had the ray—but why take needless risk?

Then once more they were racing toward Venone. Soon the giant star of which it was a planet loomed enormous. Then, at Morey’s direction, they swung, and before them loomed a planet. Large as Thett, near a half million miles in diameter, its mass was very closely equal to that of our sun. Yet it was but the burned-out sweepings of the outermost photospheric layers of this giant sun, and the radioactive atoms that made a sun active were not here; it was a cold planet. But its density was far, far higher than that of our sun, for our sun is but slightly denser than ordinary sea water. This world was dense as copper, for with the deeper sweepings of the tidal strains that had formed it, more of the heavier atoms had gone into its making, and its core was denser than that of Earth.

===Chinese===
几个小时后，莫里带着令人放心的报告归来。他见到了许多维诺恩星的重要人物，在船的安全庇护下与他们以思维交谈——船上特装的引力装置护住了他和飞船，抵御这颗巨行星惊人的引力。他没有描述维诺恩星的模样；他要让大家像他初见时那样亲眼去看。

于是，这艘已完成使命的小船，在距维诺恩星近一光年处被销毁：两块人工物质板材合拢，将它压成一团残骸，装置尽毁，以免哪个不受欢迎的发现者加以利用。船上的东西，塞特人多半都用不上，或许唯有那套引力装置除外；而塞特星连那道射线都已经有了——可又何必去冒无谓的风险呢？

于是他们再一次朝维诺恩疾驰。不久，维诺恩星所环绕的那颗巨星赫然庞大。接着，依莫里的指引，他们一转，一颗行星出现在眼前。它有塞特星那么大，直径近五十万英里，质量几乎与我们太阳的相等。然而它不过是这颗巨星最外侧光球层（photospheric layers）燃尽后的残余扫集物，那些使恒星保持炽烈的放射性原子并不在此；这是一颗冰冷的行星。但它的密度远远高于我们的太阳——须知太阳的密度只比寻常海水略高。这颗星球致密如铜，因为造就它的潮汐应力扫集的是更深处的内容物，更多的重原子参与了它的构成，它的核心密度更超过了地球。

===Original===
About it swept two gigantic satellite Worlds, each larger than Jupiter, but satellites of a satellite here! And Venone itself was inhabited by countless millions, yet their low, green tile and metal cities were invisible in the aspect of rolling lands with tiny hillocks, dwarfed by gigantic bulbous trees that floated their enormous weight in the water-dense atmosphere.

Here, too, there were no seas, for the temperature was above the critical temperature of water, and only in the self-cooling bodies of these men and in the trees which similarly cooled themselves, could there be liquid.

The sun of the world was another of the giant red stars, close to three hundred and fifty times the mass of our sun. It was circled by but three giant planets. Its enormous disc was almost invisible from the surface of the world as the *Thought* sank slowly through fifteen thousand miles of air, due to the screening effect on light passing through so much air. Earth could have rested on this planet and not extended beyond its atmosphere! Had Earth been situated at this planet’s center, the Moon could have revolved about it, and would not have been beyond the planet’s surface!

===Chinese===
环绕它运行着两个巨大的卫星世界，每一个都比木星还大——而在这里，它们竟只是一颗行星的卫星！维诺恩星上栖居着亿万生民，可他们低矮的绿色砖瓦与金属城市，在起伏大地与点点小丘的景致中竟看不见；大地上那些巨大的球茎树木，在稠密如水的大气中托浮着自身惊人的重量，把一切都衬得渺小。

这里也没有海洋，因为温度高于水的临界温度，唯有在这些生民自行降温的躯体里，以及在同样能使自己降温的树木体内，才可能有液体存在。

这个世界的太阳是又一颗红色巨星，质量接近我们太阳的三百五十倍，环绕它的仅有三颗巨行星。当思想号缓缓沉入一万五千英里厚的大气时，从行星表面望去，那巨大的日面几乎不可见——光线穿过如此厚重的大气，受到了层层遮蔽。地球若搁在这颗行星上，也越不出它的大气层！倘若把地球放在这颗行星的中心，月球尽可以绕它运转，还不会越出这颗行星的表面！

===Original===
In silent wonder the terrestrians watched the titanic world as they sank, and their friends looked on amazed, comprehending even less of the significance of what they saw. Already within the titanic gravitational field, they could see that indescribable effects were being produced on them, and on the ship. Arcot alone could know the enormous gravitation, and his accelerometer told him now that he was subject to a gravitational acceleration of three thousand four hundred and eighty-seven feet per second, or almost exactly one hundred and nine times Earth’s pull.

“The *Thought* weighs one billion, two hundred and six million, five hundred thousand tons, with tender, on Earth. Here it weighs approximately one hundred and twenty-one billion tons,” said Arcot softly.

“Can you set it down? It may crush under this load if the gravity drive isn’t supporting it,” asked Torlos anxiously.

===Chinese===
地球人默然惊叹，凝望着这巨人般的世界随着他们的沉落徐徐展现；他们的朋友们愕然旁观，对眼前景象的意义更是茫然。他们已身处那巨大的引力场之中，看得出种种难以名状的效应正作用于他们的身体，也作用于船。唯有阿科特能够确知这惊人的引力——他的加速度计（accelerometer）此刻告诉他，他正承受每秒三千四百八十七英尺的重力加速度，几乎恰好是地球引力的一百零九倍。

“思想号连同交通艇，在地球上重十二亿零六百五十万吨。在这里，它的重量约为一千二百一十亿吨，”阿科特轻声说道。

“你能把它放下去吗？要是引力驱动没把它托住，这么大的载荷会把船压垮的，”托洛斯忧心地问。

===Original===
“Eight inches cosmium, and everything else supported by cosmium. I made this thing to stand any conceivable strain. Watch—if the planet’s surface will take the load,” replied Arcot.

They were still sinking, and now a number of small marvelously streamlined ships were clustered around the slowly settling giant. In a few moments more people, hundreds, thousands of men were flying through the air up to the ship.

A cruiser had appeared, and was very evidently intent on leading them somewhere, and Arcot followed it as it streaked through the dense air. “No wonder they streamline,” he muttered as he saw the enormous force it took to drive the gigantic ship through this air. The air pressure outside their ship now was so great, that the sheer crushing effect of the air pressure alone was enormous. The pressure was well over nine tons to the square inch, on the surface of that enormous ship!

===Chinese===
“八英寸厚的科斯米姆，其余一切都由科斯米姆承托。我造这艘船，就是要它经得住任何可以想象的应变。看着——就看这颗行星的地表受不受得住这份载荷，”阿科特答道。

他们仍在下沉。此刻，几艘流线型精妙绝伦的小船聚拢在这缓缓降落的巨人四周。又过片刻，更多的人——成百上千——破空而来，朝大船飞近。

一艘巡洋舰出现了，显然有意在前引路，阿科特便跟着它，看它如流星般疾掠过稠密的大气。“难怪他们把船造得这么流线，”他喃喃道——他看得出，巨船要在这空气中穿行，得耗费何等惊人的推力。此刻船外的气压大得惊人，单是气压本身的挤压效应便已非同小可。在那艘巨船的表面，每平方英寸的气压足足超过九吨！

===Original===
They landed approximately fifty miles from a large city which was the capital. The land seemed absolutely level, and the horizon faded off in distance in an atmosphere absolutely clear. There was no dust in the air at their height of nearly three hundred feet, for dust was too heavy on this world. There were no clouds. The mountains of this enormous world were not large, could not be large, for their sheer weight would tear them down, but what mountains there were were jagged, tortured rock, exceedingly sharp in outline.

“No rain—no temperature change to break them down,” said Wade looking at them. “The zone of fracture can’t be deep here.”

“What, Wade, is the zone of fracture?” asked Torles.

===Chinese===
他们在距一座大城约五十英里处降落，那是维诺恩星的都城。大地看上去平得没有一丝起伏，大气澄澈至极，地平线在远方淡淡隐去。在近三百英尺的高度上，空气中不见尘埃——在这个世界，尘埃太重，浮不起来。天上没有云。这个巨大世界上的山脉并不高大——也不可能高大，单是自身的重量就会将它们扯垮；仅有的那些山，尽是嶙峋扭曲的岩石，轮廓锋锐至极。

“没有雨水——没有温度变化去剥蚀它们，”韦德望着那些山说。“这里的断裂带（zone of fracture）不会深。”

“韦德，什么是断裂带？”托洛斯问。

===Original===
“Rock has weight. Any substance, no matter how brittle, will flow if sufficient pressure is brought to bear from all sides. A thing which can flow will not break or fracture. You can’t imagine the pressure to which the rock three hundred feet down is subject to. There is the enormous mass of atmosphere, the tremendous mass of rock above, and all forced down by this gravitation. By the time you get down half a mile, the rock is under such an inconceivably great pressure that it will flow like mud. The rock there cannot break; it merely flows under pressure. Above, the rock can break, instead of flowing. That is the zone of fracture. On Earth the zone of fracture is ten miles deep. Here it must be of the order of only five hundred feet! And the planetary blocks that made a planet’s surface float on the zone of flowage—they determine the zone of fracture.”

The gigantic ship had been sinking, and now, suddenly it gave a very unexpected demonstration of Wade’s words. It had landed, and Arcot shut off the power. There was a roaring, and the giant ship trembled, rocked, and rolled along a bit. Instantly Arcot drove it into the air.

“Whoa—can’t do it. The ship will stand it, and won’t bend under the load—but the planet won’t. We caused a Venone-quake. One of those planetary blocks Wade was talking about slipped under the added strain.”

===Chinese===
“岩石有重量。任何物质，无论多么脆，只要四面八方受到足够的压力，都会流动。能流动的东西就不会断裂。岩石在三百英尺之下承受的压力，你根本无从想象：有巨量的大气，有上方骇人的岩层，而这一切都被这引力死死压下。下到半英里深处，岩石所受的压力大得难以想象，会像泥浆一样流动。那里的岩石不会断裂，只会在压力下流动；而在那之上，岩石可以断裂，不再流动。这就是断裂带。在地球，断裂带有十英里深；在这里，恐怕只有五百英尺上下！而行星的地表，正是靠一块块行星板块（planetary blocks）漂浮在流变带（zone of flowage）上——正是它们决定了断裂带之所在。”

巨船一直在缓缓下沉，此刻，它忽然对韦德的话做了一次出人意料的实证。船一落地，阿科特便关掉了功率。一阵轰鸣声中，巨船震颤、摇晃，还向前滚移了一小段。阿科特立时将它升入空中。

“哦嗬——不能这么干。船经得住，不会在载荷下弯折——可这行星受不了。我们引发了一场维诺恩地震（Venone-quake）。韦德说的那些行星板块，有一块在新增载荷下打滑了。”

===Original===
Quickly Wade explained that all the planetary blocks were floating, truly floating, and in equilibrium just as a boat must be. The added load had been sufficiently great, so that, with an already extant overload on this particular planetary block, this ‘boat’ had sunk a bit further into the flowage zone, till it was once more at rest and balanced.

“They wish us to come out that they may see us, strangers and friends from another Island,” interrupted Zezdon Afthen.

“Tell them they’d have to scrape us up off the ground, if we attempted it. We come from a world where we weigh about as much as a pebble here,” said Wade, grinning at the thought of terrestrians trying to walk on this world.

===Chinese===
韦德连忙解释：所有行星板块都是漂浮的，如假包换的漂浮，而且必然像船一样保持平衡。新增的载荷相当可观，这一块行星板块本就带着超出平衡的负载，再一加码，这只“船”便在流变带中又下沉了一截，直到重新静止、恢复平衡为止。

“他们请我们出去，好见一见我们——来自另一座岛的异客与朋友，”泽兹登·阿夫森插言道。

“告诉他们，我们若真出去，得让他们拿铲子把我们从地上刮起来。在我们来的那个世界，我们的体重搁在这儿不过相当于一粒石子，”韦德笑道，一想到地球人试着在这个世界上走路的样子，就忍不住咧嘴。

===Original===
“Don’t—tell them we’ll be right out,” said Arcot sharply. “All of us.”

Morey and the others all stared at Arcot in amazement. It was utterly impossible!

But Zezdon Afthen did as Arcot had asked. Almost immediately, another Morey stepped out of the airlock wearing what was obviously a pressure suit. Behind him came another Wade, Torlos, Stel Felso Theu, and indeed all the members of their party save Arcot himself! The Galactians stared in wonder—then comprehended and laughed together. Arcot had sent artificial matter images of them all!

===Chinese===
“别这么说——告诉他们，我们马上就出去，”阿科特斩钉截铁地说。“全体都出去。”

莫里和其余人都愕然瞪着阿科特。这根本不可能！

但泽兹登·阿夫森仍照阿科特所请传了话。几乎立刻，一个一模一样的莫里穿着一身显然是加压服（pressure suit）的行头，从气闸舱里走了出来。他身后跟着又一个韦德、托洛斯、斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤——实际上，他们一行人除了阿科特本人，全员到齐！这群银河人（Galactians）先是惊愕瞪视——随即恍然领会，齐声大笑。原来阿科特放出去的，是用人工物质仿造的他们每个人的拟像！

===Original===
Their images stepped out, and the Venonian crowd which had collected, stared in wonder at the giants, looming twice their height above them.

“You see not us, but images of us. We cannot withstand your gravity nor your air pressure, save in the protection of our ship. But these images are true images of us.”

For some time then they communicated, and finally Arcot agreed to give a demonstration of their power. At the suggestion of the cruiser commander who had seen the construction of a spaceship from the emptiness of space, Arcot rapidly constructed a small, very simple, molecular drive machine of pure cosmium, making it entirely from energy. It required but minutes, and the Venonians stared in wonder as Arcot’s unbelievable tools created the machine before their eyes. The completed ship Arcot gave to an official of the city who had appeared. The Venonian looked at the thing skeptically, and half expecting it to vanish like the tools that made it, gingerly entered the port. Powered as it was by lead burning cosmic ray generators, the lead alone having been made by transmutation of natural matter, it was powerful, and speedy. The official entered it, and finding it still existing, tried it out. Much to his amazement it flew, and operated perfectly.

===Chinese===
拟像们走了出去，聚拢而来的维诺恩人群惊愕地仰望这些巨人——他们比维诺恩人足足高出一倍。

“你们看到的不是我们本人，而是我们的拟像。除了藏身船中，我们经不起你们的引力，也经不起你们的气压。但这些拟像与我们形貌分毫不差。”

此后他们交流了许久，最后，阿科特答应展示一下他们的力量。应那位曾目睹飞船自太空虚空中建成的巡洋舰指挥官之请，阿科特迅速造出一台小型而极简的分子推进机器，通体纯科斯米姆，完全以能量铸成。前后不过几分钟，维诺恩人便瞪大眼睛，惊叹地望着阿科特那难以置信的工具在众人眼前造出这台机器。造好的小船，阿科特赠给了届时现身的一位城中官员。那位维诺恩人满腹狐疑地打量着它，半以为它会像造它的那些工具一样凭空消失，小心翼翼地从舱口钻了进去。它由燃铅的宇宙射线发电机供能——连那铅本身也是由天然物质嬗变而成——功率强劲，速度飞快。那官员进了船，见它依然存在，便试航起来。令他大吃一惊的是，它当真飞了起来，而且一切运转完美。

===Original===
Nearly ten hours Arcot and his friends stayed at Venone, and before they left, the Venonians, for all their vast differences of structure, had proven themselves true, kindly honest men, and a race that our Alliance has since found every reason to respect and honor. Our commerce with them, though carried on under difficulties, is none the less a bond of genuine friendship.

===Chinese===
阿科特和朋友们在维诺恩星停留了近十个小时。临别之际，维诺恩人尽管躯体构造与他们相去悬远，却已证明自己真诚、仁厚而正直，是一个我们联盟自那以后有充分理由敬重与推许的种族。我们与他们的商贸往来虽在重重困难中进行，却始终是一条真切的友谊纽带。

## XXV / 星系存亡一线

===Original===
The *Thought* arose from Venone after long hours, and at Arcot’s suggestion, they assumed an orbit about the world, at a distance of two million miles, and all on board slept, save Torlos, the tireless molecular motion machine of flesh and iron. He acted as guard, and as he had slept but four days before, he explained there was really no reason for him to sleep as yet.

But the terrestrians would feel the greatest strain of the coming encounter, especially Arcot and Morey, for Morey was to help by repairing any damage done, by working from the control board of the *Banderlog*. The little tender had sufficient power to take care of any damage that Thett might inflict, they felt sure.

For they had not learned of the triple ray.

===Chinese===
思想号在漫长的数小时之后自维诺恩星腾空而起。依阿科特的建议，众人驶入环绕该星的轨道，相距两百万英里；船上所有人都睡去了，唯有托洛斯例外——他是一台血肉与钢铁铸就、永不知疲倦的分子运动机器，担任守卫。他四天前才睡过，便解释说自己实在还毫无睡觉的理由。

然而即将到来的这场交锋，压力最大的当属地球人，尤其是阿科特与莫里——莫里要在班德洛格号的操控台前值守，随时修复所受的一切损伤。他们坚信，这只小交通艇的功率，足以应付塞特星可能造成的任何破坏。

因为，他们还不知道三重射线的存在。

===Original===
It was hours later that, rested and refreshed, they started for Thett. Following the great space-chart that they had been given by the Venonians, a series of blocks of clear lux metal, with tiny points of slowly disintegrating lux, such as had been used to illuminate the letters of the *Thought*’s name representing suns, the colors and relative intensity being shown. Then there was a more manageable guide in the form of photographs, marked for route by constellations formations as well, which would be their actual guide.

At the maximum speed of the time apparatus, for thus they could better follow the constellations, the *Thought* plunged along in the wake of the tiny scout ship that had already landed on Thett. And, hours later, they saw the giant red sun of Antseck, the star of Thett and its system.

“We’re about there,” said Arcot, a peculiar tenseness showing in his thoughts. “Shall we barge right in, or wait and investigate?”

===Chinese===
数小时之后，众人歇息已足、精神一振，遂启程奔赴塞特星。他们循着维诺恩人所赠的大型空间星图（space-chart）航行——那是一套透明卢克斯金属方块，上面以缓缓衰变的卢克斯微点标出诸恒星——这种微点当初便曾用来照亮思想号舰名的字样——颜色与相对亮度一一呈现。此外另有一份更便于使用的向导：若干照片，同样以星座图形标出航线，这才是他们实际依循的指引。

时间装置开到全速——如此更便于追循星座——思想号沿着那艘已先行降落于塞特星的微型侦察艇的尾迹疾进。数小时后，安塞克恒星那轮巨大的红色太阳映入眼帘——那正是塞特星及其行星系所属的恒星。

“快到了，”阿科特说，思绪中透出一种异样的紧绷，“是直接闯进去，还是先等一等、侦察一番？”

===Original===
“We’ll have to chance it. Where is their main fort here?”

“From the direction, I should say it was to the left and ahead of our position,” replied Zezdon Afthen.

The ship moved ahead, while about it the tremendous Thessian battlefleet buzzed like flies, thousands of ships now, and more coming with each second.

===Chinese===
“只能碰碰运气了。他们在这里的主要塞在哪儿？”

“从航向判断，我看是在我们左前方，”泽兹登·阿夫森答道。

思想号向前挺进，四周庞大的塞特战斗舰队像苍蝇一般嗡嗡环绕——如今已有数千艘，且每一秒都有更多赶来。

===Original===
In a few moments the titanic ship had crossed a great plain, and came to a region of bare, rocky hills several hundred feet high. Set in those hills, surrounded by them, was a huge sphere, resting on the ground. As though by magic the Thessian fleet cleared away from the *Thought*. The last one had not left, when Arcot shot a terrific cosmic ray toward the sphere. It was relux, and he knew it, but he knew what would happen when that cosmic ray hit it. The solometer flickered and steadied at three as that inconceivable ray flashed out.

Instantly there was a terrific explosion. The soil exploded into hydrogen atoms, and expanded under heat that lashed it to more than a million degrees in the tiniest fraction of a second. The terrific recoil of the ray-pressure was taken by all space, for it was generated in space itself, but the direct pressure struck the planet, and that titanic planet reeled! A tremendous fissure opened, and the section that had been struck by the ray smashed its way suddenly far into the planet, and a geyser of fluid rock rolled over it, twenty miles deep in that world. The relux sphere had been struck by the ray, and had turned it, with the result that it was pushed doubly hard. The enormously thick relux strained and dented, then shot down as a whole, into the incandescent rock.

For miles the vaporized rock was boiling off. Then the fort sent out a ray, and that ray blasted the rock that had flowed over it as Arcot’s titanic ray snapped out. In moments the fort was at the surface again—and a molecular hit it. The molecular did not have the energy the cosmic had carried, but it was a single concentrated beam of destruction ten feet across. It struck the fort—and the fort recoiled under its energy. The marvelous new tubes that ran its ray screen flashed instantly to a temperature inconceivable, and, so long as the elements embedded in the infusible relux remained the metals they were, those tubes could not fail. But they were being lashed by the energy of half a sun. The tubes failed. The elements heated to that enormous temperature when elements cannot exist—and broke to other elements that did not resist. The relux flashed into blinding iridescence—

===Chinese===
片刻之间，巨舰已越过一片辽阔平原，来到一处光秃的岩石丘陵地带，山丘高数百英尺。群丘环抱之中，端端正正坐落着一个巨大的球体，搁停在地面上。塞特舰队仿佛中了魔法一般，纷纷从思想号近旁散开。最后一艘尚未离尽，阿科特便朝那球体射出一道狂猛的宇宙射线。他明知那是雷卢克斯，也明知这道宇宙射线命中后会发生什么。那道不可思议的射线闪出之际，索尔计（solometer）的指针颤了几颤，稳稳停在“三”上。

爆炸随之而起，声势骇人。泥土轰然爆解为氢原子，在热力下急骤膨胀——那份热在极短的一瞬便把泥土鞭策到一百万度以上。射线压力的可怕反冲由整个空间承受，因为它本就生成于空间自身，而直接的压力却砸在这颗行星上，砸得这颗巨大行星摇撼震颤！一道巨大的裂罅豁然张开，被射线击中的那一区块轰然砸开去路，骤然陷入行星深处，一股熔流间歇泉随之轰然卷下，将那一片吞没——这个世界的熔质岩层深达二十英里。雷卢克斯球体挨了射线，又把射线反射出去，结果便是被加倍用力地推挤。极厚的雷卢克斯绷裂凹陷，随即整体坠入炽烈的白热岩浆之中。

方圆数英里内，汽化的岩石沸沸扬扬地蒸腾四散。随后要塞射出一道射线——恰在阿科特的巨射线敛去之际——把覆盖其上的岩流轰开。转瞬之间，要塞重又露出地表——一道分子射线随即命中。分子射线虽没有宇宙射线那般的能量，却是一束直径十英尺、凝聚不散的毁灭之流。它击中要塞——要塞在这股能量下震退。驱动其射线屏障的那些神奇新功率管，温度霎时窜升到不可思议的地步；只要镶在不可熔雷卢克斯里的元件仍是金属，那些功率管便不会失效。可它们正被半个太阳的能量鞭挞着。功率管终于顶不住了。元件被加热到元素无法存在的骇人高温——崩解为别种再难抵御的元素。雷卢克斯倏然迸作一片耀目流光——

===Original===
And from the fort came a beam of pure silvery light. It struck the *Thought* just behind the bow, for the operator was aiming for the point where he knew the control room and pilot must be. But Arcot had designed the ship for mental control, which the enemy operator could not guess. The beam was a flat beam, perhaps an inch thick, but it fanned out to fifty feet width. And where it touched the *Thought*, there was a terrific explosion, and inconceivably violent energy lashed out as the cosmium instantaneously liberated its energy.

A hundred feet of the nose was torn off the ship, and the enormously dense air of Thett rushed in. But that beam had cut through the very edge of one of the ray projectors, or better, one of the ray feed apparatus. And the ray feed released it without control; it released all the energy it could suck in from space about it, as one single beam of cosmic energy, somewhat lower than the regular cosmics, and it flashed out in a beam as solid matter.

There was air about the ship, and the air instantly exploded into atoms of a different sort, threw off their electrons, and were raised to the temperature at which no atom can exist, and became protons and electrons. But so rapidly was that coil sucking energy from space that space tended to close in about it, and in enormous spurts the energy flooded out. It was directed almost straight up, and but one ship was caught in its beam. It was made of relux, but the relux was powdered under the inconceivable blow that countless quintillions of cosmic ray photons struck it. That ray was in fact, a solid mass of cosmium moving with the velocity of light. And it was headed for that satellite of Thett, which it would reach in a few hours time.

===Chinese===
而要塞射出了一道纯银色的光束。它击中思想号船首正后方的位置——操作者瞄准的正是他认定的控制室与驾驶者所在之处。可阿科特设计这艘船时用的是心智控制，这是敌方操作者无从猜想的。那是一道扁平光束，厚不过一英寸上下，扇展开来却有五十英尺宽。光束触及思想号的地方，立时爆出一场大爆炸——科斯米姆瞬间释放出自身能量，狂暴得超乎想象。

舰首一百英尺被齐齐撕裂，塞特星那极其浓稠的大气灌涌而入。但那道光束恰恰切中了射线投射器之一的边缘——更确切地说，是一套射线馈能装置（ray feed）的边缘。射线馈能装置顿失控驭，就此放能：它把从周围空间吸吮而来的能量悉数放出，凝成一道宇宙能量的单一射线，略低于寻常宇宙射线，如固体一般实实地迸射而出。

船体四周尚有空气，那些空气霎时爆解为异种原子，甩脱电子，被抬升到任何原子都无法存在的温度，化为质子与电子。可那只线圈从空间汲取能量实在太快，空间竟有向它收拢合围之势，能量便一阵阵狂涌而出，洪流滚滚。射线几乎笔直向上，仅有一艘船被罩进光束。那船是雷卢克斯所造，却在无数亿亿计的宇宙射线光子无可想象的轰击下化为齑粉。那道射线实际上便是一团以光速行进的实体科斯米姆。而它的去向，直指塞特星的一颗卫星——数小时后便会抵达。

===Original===
The *Thought*, due to the spatial strains of the wounded coil, was constantly rushing away to an almost infinite distance, as the ship approached that other space toward which the coil tended with its load, and rushing back, as the coil, reaching a spatial condition which supplied no energy, fell back. In a hundredth of a second it had reached equilibrium, and they were in a weirdly, terribly distorted space. But the triple-ray of the Thessians seemed to sheer off, and miss, no matter how it was directed. And it was painfully weak, for the coil sucked up the energy of whatsoever matter disintegrated in the neighborhood.

Then suddenly the performance was over. And they plunged into artificial space that was black and clean, and not a thing of wavering, struggling energies. Morey, from his control in the *Banderlog*, had succeeded in getting sufficient energy, by using his space distortion coils, to destroy the great projector mechanism. Instantly Arcot, now able to create the artificial space without the destruction of the coils by the struggling ray-feed coil, had thrown them to comparative safety.

Space writhed before they could so much as turn from the instruments. The Thessians had located their artificial space, and reached it with an attraction ray. They already had been withstanding the drain of the enormous fields of the giant planet and the giant sun; the attractive ray was an added strain. Arcot looked at his instruments, and with a grim smile set a single dial. The space about them became black again.

===Chinese===
由于受损线圈的空间应变，思想号不断向近乎无穷远处骤然远离——每当船体随线圈连同其负载趋往另一重空间——又骤然折返——待线圈抵达某个无从供能的空间态而回落之时。百分之一秒内，一切已达平衡，而他们身处的是一片诡谲、极度扭曲的空间。但塞特人的三重射线却仿佛总被荡偏、总落了空，任凭如何瞄准也无济于事。而且那射线微弱得令人心焦，因为线圈把近旁一切衰解物质释放的能量尽数吸走。

忽而之间，这场闹剧戛然而止。他们一头扎进人造空间——那里漆黑而洁净，没有半点摇摆挣扎的能量涟漪。莫里在班德洛格号上操控，凭他的空间畸变线圈终于汲取到足够的能量，毁掉了那座巨大的投射机构。阿科特立时趁势而为——此刻他已能在那只狂乱挣扎的射线馈能线圈不致毁掉线圈的情况下再造人造空间——把众人掷入相对安全的境地。

他们还来不及把目光从仪表上移开，空间已扭动翻腾起来。塞特人找到了他们的人造空间，并用一道引力射线探触而入。他们本就在硬扛巨行星与巨星那庞大引力场的汲取，引力射线又添一重重压。阿科特看了看仪表，嘴角一绷，冷冷一笑，拨动了一个刻度盘。四围的空间重新变作漆黑。

===Original===
“Pulling our energy—merely let ’em pull. They’re pulling on an ocean, not a lake this time. I don’t think they’ll drain those coils very quickly.” He looked at his instruments. “Good for two and a half hours at this rate.

“Morey, you sure did your job then. I was helpless. The controls wouldn’t answer, of course, with that titanic thing flopping its wings, so to speak. What are we going to do?”

Morey stood in the doorway, and from his pocket drew a cigarette, handed it to Arcot, another to each of the others who smoked, and lit them, and his own. “Smoke,” he said, and puffed. “Smoke and think. From our last experience with a minor tragedy, it helps.”

===Chinese===
“想抽我们的能量——随他们抽去。这回他们拽着的是一片大洋，不是一个湖。我看他们抽不空这些线圈，没那么快。”他看了看仪表，“照这个抽法，还能撑两个半钟头。

“莫里，你这活儿干得真漂亮。当时我束手无策——那个庞然大物扑腾着翅膀，控制器自然不听使唤。接下来怎么办？”

莫里站在门口，从衣袋里摸出一支香烟递给阿科特，又给其余抽烟的人一人一支，逐一点燃，也点了自己的。“抽烟，”他说着吸了一口，“边抽边想。上一回遭了场小难，就是这么熬过来的，管用。”

===Original===
“But—this is no minor tragedy, they have burst open the wall of this invulnerable ship, destroyed one of those enormous coils, and can do it again,” exclaimed Zezdon Afthen, exceedingly nervous, so nervous that the normal courage of the man was gone. His too-psychic breeding was against him as a warrior.

“Afthen,” replied Stel Felso Theu calmly, “when our friends have smoked, and thought, the *Thought* will be repaired perfectly, and it will be made invulnerable to that weapon.”

“I hope so, Stel Felso Theu,” smiled Arcot. He was feeling better already. “But do you know what that weapon is, Morey?”

===Chinese===
“可——这可不是什么小难。他们击穿了这艘不坏之舰的舱壁，毁掉了那些巨线圈中的一只，还能再来一次，”泽兹登·阿夫森失声喊道，紧张得厉害——紧张到把这个人素常的胆气都逼没了。他那过度心灵化的天赋血统，做战士终究是吃亏的。

“阿夫森，”斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤从容答道，“等我们的朋友抽完烟、想完事，思想号便会修复如初，而且会让那件武器奈何它不得。”

“但愿如此，斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤，”阿科特微微一笑。他的感觉已经好了不少。“不过，你知道那武器是什么吗，莫里？”

===Original===
“Got some readings on it with the *Banderlog*’s instruments, and I think I do. Twin-ray is right,” replied Morey.

“Hm-hm—so I think. It’s a super-photon. What they do is to use a field somewhat similar to the field we use in making cosmium, except that in theirs, instead of the photons lying side by side, they slide into one another, compounding. They evidently get three photons to go into one. Now, as we know, that size photon doesn’t exist for the excellent reason that it can’t in this space. Space closes in about it. Therefore they have a projected field to accompany it that tends to open out space—and they are using that, not the attractive ray, on us now. The result is that for a distance not too great, the triple-ray exists in normal space—then goes into another. Now the question is how can we stop it? I have an idea—have you any?”

“Yes, but my idea can’t exist in this space either,” grinned Morey.

===Chinese===
“用班德洛格号的仪表测了些读数，我想我知道了。双子射线这条线索是对的，”莫里答道。

“嗯——我也这么想。它是一种超光子（super-photon）。他们的做法，是用一个与我们在制造科斯米姆时所用颇为相似的场；所不同者，他们的场里光子并非并肩排布，而是相互滑入、彼此叠合。他们显然让三个光子并成了一个。而我们都知道，这种尺寸的光子并不存在，理由再充分不过：在这个空间里它无法存在，空间会朝它收拢合围。所以他们配备了一个伴随的投射场，趋势是把空间撑开——如今用来对付我们的正是这个场，而非引力射线。结果便是：在不太远的一段距离内，三重射线存于常态空间——随后便遁入另一重空间。现在的问题是怎么挡住它？我有一个想法——你有什么？”

“有，不过我的想法在这个空间里也一样没法存在，”莫里咧嘴一笑。

===Original===
“I think it can. If it’s what I think, remember it will have a terrific electric field.”

“It’s what you think, then. Come on.” Arcot and Morey went to the calculating room, while Wade took over the ship. But one of the ray-feeds had been destroyed, and they had three more in action, as well as their most important weapon, artificial matter. Wade threw on the time field, and started the emergency lead burner working to recharge the coils that the Thessians were constantly draining. Being in their own peculiar space, they could not draw energy from the stars, and Arcot didn’t want to return to normal space to discharge them, unless necessary.
