“How’s the air pressure in the rest of the ship?” asked Wade.

===Chinese===
“我看能存在。若与我想的一样，记住，它会带着一个强大得骇人的电场。”

“正是我想的那个。走。”阿科特与莫里去了计算室，韦德接管飞船。一套射线馈能装置已被毁掉，好在还有三套在运转，此外还有他们最重要的武器——人工物质。韦德开启时间场，又让应急铅燃料燃烧器运转起来，为被塞特人不断汲取的线圈续充能量。身处自己独有的空间，他们无法从恒星汲取能量；非到万不得已，阿科特也不愿回到常态空间去将它们放电释能。

“船上其余部分的气压怎么样？”韦德问。

===Original===
“Triple normal,” replied Morey. “The Thessian atmosphere leaked in and sent it up terrifically, but when we went into our own space, at the halfway point, a lot leaked out. But the ship is full of water now. It was a bit difficult coming up from the *Banderlog*, and I didn’t want to breathe the air I wasn’t sure of. But let’s work.”

They worked. For eight hours of the time they were now in they continued to work. The supply of lead metal gave out before the end of the fourth hour, and the coils were nearing the end of their resistance. It would soon be necessary for Arcot to return to normal space. So they stopped, their calculations very nearly complete. Throwing all the remaining energy into the coils, they a little more than held the space about them, and moved away from Thett at a speed of about twice that of light. For an hour more Arcot worked, while the ship plowed on. Then they were ready.

As Arcot took over the controls, space reeled once more, and they were alone, far from Thett. The suns of this space were flashing and glowing about them, and the unlimited energy of a universe was at Arcot’s command. But all the remaining atmosphere in the ship had either gone instantaneously in the vacuum, or solidified as the chill of expansion froze it.

===Chinese===
“三倍于常压，”莫里答道，“塞特大气漏进来，把气压顶得吓人，不过我们在半途进入自己的空间时，漏出去了不少。现在船里到处是水。从班德洛格号上来这一路有点费劲，没把握的空气，我实在不想往肺里吸。干活吧。”

他们干了活。在他们此刻所处的时间里，一连干了八个钟头。还不到第四个钟头结束，铅金属便已告罄，线圈的支撑也快到了头。阿科特很快就非得回常态空间不可了。于是他们停下——计算已近完成。他们把残余能量尽数注入线圈，堪堪撑住四围的空间，以约两倍光速的速度驶离塞特星。阿科特又干了一个钟头，飞船破空疾驰。然后，一切就绪。

阿科特接管操控，空间再度一阵天旋地转，四下里只剩他们，远隔塞特星。这个空间的诸太阳在四周闪灼生辉，一个宇宙的无穷能量尽归阿科特调遣。只是船内残余的大气，要么在真空中顷刻散尽，要么在膨胀的酷寒里冻结凝固。

===Original===
To the amazement of the extra-terrestrians, Arcot’s first move was to create a titanic plane of artificial matter, and neatly bisect the *Thought* at the middle! He had thrown all of the controls thus interrupted into neutral, and in the little more than half of the ship which contained the control cabin, was also the artificial matter control. It was busy now. With bewildering speed, with the speed of thought trained to construct, enormous masses of cosmium were appearing beside them in space as Arcot created them from pure energy. Cosmium, relux and some clear cosmium-like lux metal. Ordinary cosmium was reflective, and he wanted something with cosmium’s strength, and the clearness of lux.

In seconds, under Arcot’s flying thought manipulation, a great tube had been welded to the original hull, and the already gigantic ship lengthened by more than five hundred feet! Immediately great artificial matter tools gripped the broken nose-section, clamped it into place, and welded it with cosmium flowing under the inconceivable pressure till it was again a single great hull.

Then the Thessian fleet found them. The coils were charged now, and they could have escaped, but Arcot had to work. The Thessians were attacked with moleculars, cosmics, and a great twin-ray. Arcot could not use his magnet, for it had been among those things severed from the control. He had two ray feeds, and the artificial matter. There were nearly three thousand ships attacking him with a barrage of energy that was inconceivably great, but the cosmium walls merely turned it aside. It took Arcot less than ten seconds to wipe out that fleet of ships! He created a wall of artificial matter at twenty feet from the ship—and another at twenty thousand miles. It was thin, yet it was utterly impenetrable. He swept the two walls together, and forced them against each other until his instruments told him only free energy remained between them. Then he released the outer wall, and a terrific flood of energy swept out.

===Chinese===
令异星同伴们瞠目的是，阿科特的第一步，竟是造出一面巨大的人工物质平面，把思想号齐刷刷拦腰一剖为二！他把由此被截断的所有控制器统统掷回中性位，而载有控制舱的那略过一半的船体里，恰好也有人工物质控制机。此刻它正忙得不可开交。快得令人目乱神迷——那是久经构筑训练的思想的速度——大团大团的科斯米姆在阿科特以纯粹能量创生它们的同时，出现在近旁的空间里。科斯米姆、雷卢克斯，还有一种清澈透明、类似科斯米姆的卢克斯金属。寻常科斯米姆是反光的，他要的是既有科斯米姆的强度、又有卢克斯的透明之物。

几秒之内，在阿科特飞掠般的意念操纵下，一段巨大的筒体已与原船壳焊接为一体，这艘已然庞大的船又加长了五百多英尺！人工物质巨械随即钳住断裂的舰首段，卡入定位，在不可想象的压力下让科斯米姆熔流焊接，直到它重新浑然为一具巨大船壳。

随后塞特舰队发现了他们。线圈此刻已经充能，他们本可以逃走，可阿科特还有活要干。塞特人以分子射线、宇宙射线和一道巨大的双子射线发起了攻击。阿科特用不了他的磁体——它也在被切断的那一部分里。他手中有两套射线馈能装置，还有人工物质。近三千艘战船向他倾泻着大得无法想象的能量弹幕，科斯米姆船壁却只把它荡向一旁。阿科特清灭那支舰队，用了不到十秒钟！他在距船二十英尺处造起一面人工物质之壁——又在两万英里外造起另一面。壁虽薄，却绝对无法穿透。他把两面壁相向推拢，强行对压，直到仪表告诉他两壁之间只剩自由能量。随后他放开外壁，一股狂暴的能量洪流奔涌而出。

===Original===
“I don’t think we’ll be attacked again,” said Morey softly. They were not. Thett had only one other fleet, and had no intention of losing the powers of their generators at this time when they so badly needed them. The strange ship had retired for repairs—very well, they could attack again—and maybe—

Arcot was busy. In the great empty space that had been left, he installed a second collector coil as gigantic as the main artificial matter generator. Then he repaired the broken ray feed, and it, and the companion coil which, with it, had been in the severed nose section, were now in the same relative position to the new collector coil that they had had with relation to the artificial matter coil. Next Arcot built two more ray feeds. Now in the gigantic central power room there loomed two tremendous power collectors, and six smaller ray feed collectors.

His next work was to reconnect the severed connectors and controls. Then he began work on the really new apparatus. Nothing he had constructed so far was more than a duplicate of existing apparatus, and he had been able to do it almost instantly, from memory. Now he must vision something new to his experience, and something that was forced to exist in part in this space, and partly in another. He tried four times before the apparatus had been completed correctly, and the work occupied ten hours. But at last it was done. The *Thought* was ready now for the battle.

===Chinese===
“我看不会再有攻击了，”莫里轻声说。果然没有。塞特星只剩另一支舰队，而这正是他们亟需发电机功率的时候，无意将其折损于此。怪船已退去修理——很好，回头可以再打——说不定——

阿科特忙得正欢。在那截空出的大片舱位里，他装上了第二只集能线圈（collector coil），与主人工物质发生器一般庞大。接着他修复了损坏的射线馈能装置——它连同与它同在被切断舰首段里的那只配对线圈，如今相对于新集能线圈的位置，一如当初相对于人工物质线圈的位置。随后阿科特又造了两套射线馈能装置。至此，庞大的中央动力室里，巍然矗立着两座庞大的功率集能器，以及六座较小的射线馈能集能器。

他的下一步，是把被切断的接线与控制器重新接通。然后，他着手建造那台真正崭新的装置。至此他所造的一切，不过是既有装置的复制，全凭记忆，几乎顷刻可就。如今他却必须在经验之外构想出某种新物，且这新物不得不半存于此空间、半存于另一空间。他试了四次，装置才告正确完成，前后耗了十个钟头。但终于成了。思想号已备好一战。

===Original===
“Got it right at last?” asked Wade. “I hope so.”

“It’s right—tried it a little. I don’t think you noticed it. I’m going down now to give them a nice little dose,” said Arcot grimly. His ship was repaired—but they had caused him plenty of trouble.

“How long have we been out here, their time?” asked Wade.

“About an hour and a half.” The *Thought* had been on the time field at all times save when the Thessian fleet attacked.

===Chinese===
“这回总算弄对了？”韦德问。“但愿如此。”

“成了——试过一小下，想必你们没留意。现在我就下去，请他们美美地受用一剂，”阿科特狠声说道。他的船修好了——可这帮家伙也给他添够了麻烦。

“我们在这外面待了多久，按他们的时间算？”韦德问。

“约莫一个半钟头。”除了塞特舰队来攻的那阵子，思想号始终开着时间场。

===Original===
“I think, Earthman, that you are tired, and should rest, lest you make a tired thought and do great harm,” suggested Zezdon Afthen.

“I want to finish it!” replied Arcot, sharply. He was tired.

In seconds the *Thought* was once more over that fortified station in the mountains—and the triple-ray reached out—and suddenly, about the ship, was a wall of absolute, utter blackness. The triple-ray touched it, and exploded into coruscating, blinding energy. It could not penetrate it. More energy lashed at the wall of blackness as the operators within the sphere-fort turned in the energy of all the generators under their control. The ground about the fort was a great lake of dazzling lava as far as the eye could see, for the triple-ray was releasing its energy, and the wall of black was releasing an equal, and opposing energy!

===Chinese===
“地球人，我看你累了，该歇息了，免得你以疲乏之心运思，酿成大祸，”泽兹登·阿夫森进言道。

“我要干完它！”阿科特声色俱厉。他确实累了。

几秒钟内，思想号重又飞临山中那座设防台站上空——三重射线探出——刹那间，船体四周矗起一面绝对纯粹的黑暗之壁（wall of blackness）。三重射线触上它，轰然迸作万点亮瞎人眼的能量，却穿它不透。球形要塞内的操作者把麾下所有发电机的能量尽数调来，更多能量狂鞭在那面黑暗之壁上。要塞四周的地面，目力所及是一片炫目的熔岩巨湖——三重射线在释放能量，而黑暗之壁正释放着一股势均力敌、迎面对抗的能量！

===Original===
“Stopped!” cried Arcot happily. “Now here is where we give them something to think about. The magnet and the heat!”

He turned the two enormous forces simultaneously on the point where he knew the fort was, though it was invisible behind the wall of black that protected him. From his side, the energy of the spot where all the system of Thett was throwing its forces, was invisible.

Then he released them. Instantly there was a terrific gout of light on that wall of blackness. The ship trembled, and space turned gray about them. The black wall dissolved into grayness in one spot, as a flood of energy beyond comprehension exploded from it. The enormously strong cosmium wall dented as the pressure of the escaping radiation struck it, and turned X-ray hot under the minute percentage it absorbed. The triple-ray bent away, and faded to black as the cosmic force playing about it, actually twisted space beyond all power of its mechanism to overcome. Then, in the tiniest fraction of a second it was over, and again there was blackness and only the brilliant, blinding blue of the cosmium wall testified to its enormous temperature, cooling now far more slowly through green to red.

===Chinese===
“挡住了！”阿科特喜道。“现在，该给他们点值得琢磨的东西了。磁体与热射线！”

他把两股巨力同时指向他确知要塞所在的那个点——尽管隔着护住他的黑壁，那里什么也看不见。而从他这一侧望去，塞特全行星系正倾力灌注其中的那个点上的能量，同样无影无形。

随后他放开了它们。刹那间，那面黑暗之壁上爆出一团骇人的光。船体震颤，四周的空间转为灰白。黑壁在一处溶解成灰，一股超乎理解的能量洪流自那里炸裂而出。坚固无比的科斯米姆壁在逸散辐射的压力下凹陷，又因所吸收的区区一小部分而灼热至放射X射线的地步。三重射线被拗得荡开，黯淡至黑——环绕它嬉游的宇宙之力，硬是把空间扭拧到它的机构万难克服的地步。然后，在极短的一瞬，一切结束，黑暗重临，唯有科斯米姆壁那明亮炫目的湛蓝，昭示着它那骇人的高温——如今冷却徐缓了许多，由蓝转绿，由绿转红。

===Original===
“Lord—you’re right, Zezdon Afthen. I’m going to sleep,” called Arcot. And the ship was suddenly far, far away from Thett. Morey took over, and Arcot slept. First Morey straightened the uninjured wall and ironed out the dents.

“What, Morey, is the wall of Blackness?” asked Stel Felso Theu.

“It’s solid matter. A thing that you never saw before. That wall of matter is made of a double layer of protons lying one against the other. It absorbs absolutely every and all radiation, and because it is solid matter, not tiny sprinklings of matter in empty space, as is the matter of even the densest star, it stops the triple-ray. That matter is nothing but protons; there are no electrons there, and the positive electrical field is inconceivably great, but it is artificial matter, and that electrical field exerts its strain not in pulling and electrifying other bodies, but in holding space open, in keeping it from closing in about that concentrated matter, just as it does about a single proton, except that here the entire field energy is so absorbed.

===Chinese===
“天哪——你说得对，泽兹登·阿夫森。我睡去了，”阿科特喊道。船身倏忽间已远远离开塞特星。莫里接管一切，阿科特酣然睡去。莫里先把已然无恙的那面壁拉直，把凹痕一一熨平。

“莫里，那黑暗之壁究竟是什么？”斯特尔·费尔索·塞尤问。

“它是固态物质。一件你们从未见过的事物。那面物质之壁由两层相互贴靠的质子叠成。它把一切辐射尽数吸收，绝不遗漏；而正因为它是固态物质——不像即便最致密恒星上的物质那样，只是虚空中撒落的点点微尘——它挡得住三重射线。那物质纯粹是质子，其中并无电子，正电场大得无可想象；但它是人工物质，那电场的应变不施于牵引别物、使之带电，而是施于撑住空间、不让空间朝那团浓缩物质收拢合围——正如单个质子周遭的情形，只是在这里，全部场能都被这样占用了。

===Original===
“Arcot was tired, and forgot. He turned his magnet and his heat against it. The heat fought the solid matter with the same energy that created it, and with an energy that had resources as great. The magnet curved space about it, and about us. The result was the terrific energy release you saw, and the hole in the wall. All Thett couldn’t make any impression on it. One of the rays blasted a hole in it,” said Morey with a laugh. For he, too, loved this mighty thing, the almost living ideas of his friend’s brain.

“But it is as bad as the space defense. It works both ways. We can’t send through it but neither can they. Anything we use that attacks them, attacks it, and so destroys it—and it fights.”

“We’re worse off than ever!” said Morey gloomily.

“My friend, you, too, are tired. Sleep, sleep soundly, sleep till I call—sleep!” And Morey slept under Zezdon Afthen’s will, till Torlos carried him gently to his room. Then Afthen let the sleep relax to a natural one. Wade decided he might as well follow under his own power, for now he knew he was tired, and could not overcome Zezdon Afthen, who was not.

===Chinese===
“阿科特累了，一时忘了。他把磁体与热射线对准了它。热射线与那固态物质相搏，用的正是创造它的同一种能量，且后备之储同样无穷。磁体则把它连同我们四周的空间一并拗弯。结果便是你们看见的那场可怖的能量宣泄，还有壁上那个洞。整个塞特星也休想在它上面留下一丝印痕。倒是自己人的一道射线，在它上面轰了个窟窿，”莫里笑着说完。因为他也爱这威灵赫赫的造物——爱他朋友头脑里那些几近活物的念头。

“可它跟空间防御一样糟：双向皆然。我们的东西出不去，他们的也进不来。凡我们用以攻击他们的东西，都会攻击到这面壁本身，从而将它毁掉——而它是会反抗的。”

“我们的处境反倒比先前更糟了！”莫里怏怏道。

“我的朋友，你也累了。睡吧，沉沉地睡，睡到我叫你为止——睡！”莫里便在泽兹登·阿夫森的意志下沉沉睡去，直到托洛斯轻轻把他抱回房间。随后阿夫森让这睡眠松缓为自然睡眠。韦德心想，自己索性也自主入睡的好——他如今自知已倦，而绝拗不过尚无倦意的泽兹登·阿夫森。

---

===Original===
On Thett, the fort was undestroyed, and now floating on its power units in a sea of blazing lava. Within, men were working quickly to install a second set of the new tubes in the molecular motion ray screen, and other men were transmitting the orders of the Sthanto who had come here as the place of actually greatest safety.

“Order all battleships to the nearest power-feed station, and command that all power available be transmitted to the station attacked. I believe it will be this one. There is no limit on the power transmission lines, and we need all possible power,” he commanded his son, now in charge of all land and spatial forces.

“And Ranstud, what happened to that molecular ray screen?”

===Chinese===
塞特星上，要塞并未被毁，此刻正凭自身的功率机组漂浮于一片炽焰翻滚的熔岩之海。要塞之内，一些人正加紧为分子运动射线屏障换装第二组新功率管，另一些人则在传述斯坦托的命令——这位统治者已然亲临此地，把这里当作实际最安全的所在。

“传令所有战列舰驶往最近的力能馈电站（power-feed station），并令凡可调用之功率，一律输往遇袭台站。我认为遇袭的就是这一座。输能线路并无上限，而我们需要一切可能的功率，”他命令儿子——如今统辖全部地面与太空部队的，正是这位公子。

“还有，兰斯图德，那道分子射线屏障出了什么事？”

===Original===
“I do not know. I cannot understand such power.

“But what most worries me is his wall of darkness,” said Ranstud seriously.

“But he was forced to retire for all his wall of darkness, as you saw.

“He can maintain it but a short time, and it was full of holes when he fled.”

===Chinese===
“我不知道。这样的功率，我无法索解。

“而最令我忧心的，是他那道黑暗之壁，”兰斯图德神色凝重。

“可他有那道黑暗之壁，还不是照样被迫退走，你亲眼所见。

“他撑不了多久，逃走时那壁上早已千疮百孔。”

===Original===
“Old Sthanto is much too confident, I believe,” said an assistant working at one of the great boards in the enemy’s fort, to one of his friends. “And I think he has lost his science-knowledge. Any power-man could tell what happened. They tried to use their own big rays against us, and their screen stopped them from going out, just as it stopped ours on the way in. Ours had been working at it for seconds, and hadn’t bothered them. Then for a bare instant their ray touched it—and they retired. That shield of blackness is absolutely new.”

“They have many men on that ship of theirs,” replied his friend, helping to lift the three hundred ton load of a vacuum tube into place, “for it is evident that they built new apparatus, and it is evident their ship was increased in size to contain it. Also the nose was repaired. They probably worked under a time field, for they accomplished an impossible amount of work in the period they were gone.”

Ranstud had come up behind them, and overheard the later part of this conversation. “And what,” he asked suddenly, “did your meters tell you when our ray opened his ship?”

===Chinese===
“老斯坦托过于自信了，我看，”敌方要塞里，一名正伺候于一台巨控台前的助手对同伴说道。“而且他怕是把科学智识丢了。随便哪个掌功率的人都看得出发生了什么。他们想动用自家的大射线来攻我们，他们的屏障却让射线出不去，就像它不让我们的射线进来一样。我们的射线轰了足足几秒钟，也没能奈何他们分毫。随后他们的射线在屏障上只碰了一下——他们便退走了。那面漆黑护盾，绝对是前所未有的东西。”

“他们那艘船上人手一定很多，”他的同伴答道，一边帮忙把一根三百吨重的真空管吊装到位，“很显然，他们新造了装置；也很显然，他们把船加大，才装得下它。船首也修好了。他们多半是开着时间场干活，否则在离开的那段时间里，绝完成不了那么多活计。”

兰斯图德已走到两人身后，听到了这番话的后半截。“那么，”他突然发问，“我们的射线剖开他的船时，你们的仪表说了什么？”

===Original===
“Councilor of Science-wisdom, they told us that our power diminished, and our generators gave off but little power when his power was exceedingly little, we still had much.”

“Have you heard the myth of the source of his power, in the story that he gets it from all the stars of the Island?”

“We have, Great Councilor. And I for one believe it, for he sucked the power from our generators. So might he suck the power from the inconceivably greater generators of the Suns. I believe that we should treat with them, for if they be like the peace-loving fools of Venone, we might win a respite in which to learn their secret.”

===Chinese===
“科学智识评议员（Councilor of Science-wisdom），他们告诉我们：我们的功率在减损，我们的发电机所放出的功率寥寥无几；而当他的功率小得可怜之时，我们却仍保有甚多。”

“他的功率来自何处，那个神话你可曾听说——说他从整个‘空间之岛’的群星汲取功率？”

“听说过，尊贵的评议员。而我个人便信以为真，因为他确实从我们的发电机里吸走了功率。那他自然也吸得走诸恒星——那些大得无可比拟的发电机——的功率。我认为我们应当同他们谈判，因为倘若他们与维诺恩那帮热爱和平的蠢货是一路货色，我们或许能赢得一段喘息，正好窥破他们的秘密。”

===Original===
Ranstud walked away slowly. He agreed, in his heart, but he loved life too well to tell the Sthanto what to do, and he had no intention of sacrificing himself for the possible good of the race.

So they prepared for another attack of the *Thought*, and waited.

===Chinese===
兰斯图德缓缓走开。他心里是赞同的，可他太爱惜性命，犯不上对斯坦托指手画脚；为了种族可能的好处而搭上自己，他可没有这个打算。

于是，他们整备以待，防备思想号的再一次进攻，静静等待着。

## XI / “磁体一笔勾销”

===Original===
“Squadron commander Tharnton speaking: Squadron 73-B of Planetary Guard will follow orders from Dr. Arcot directly. Heading south to Antarctica at maximum speed,” droned the communicator. Under the official tone of command was a note of suppressed rage and determination. “And the squadron commander wishes Dr. Arcot every success in wiping out Antarctica as thoroughly and completely as he destroyed the Arctic base.”

The flight of ships headed south at a speed that heated them white in the air, thin as it was at the hundred mile altitude, yet going higher would have taken unnecessary time, and the white heat meant no discomfort. They reached Antarctica in about ten minutes. The Thessian ships were just entering through great locks in the walls of the dome. At first sight of the terrestrial ships they turned, and shot toward the guard-ships. Their screens were down, for, armored as they were with very heavy relux they expected to be able to overcome the terrestrial thin relux before theirs was seriously impaired.

“Ships will put up screens.” Arcot spoke sharply—a new plan had occurred to him. The moleculars of the Thessians struck glowing screens, and no damage was done. “Ships, in order of number, will lower screen for thirty seconds, and concentrate all moleculars on one ship—the leader. Solar investigator will not join in action.”

===Chinese===
通讯器里传来低沉单调的声音：“这里是中队长桑顿（Tharnton）：行星警卫队（Planetary Guard）第 73-B 中队将直接听从阿科特博士的调度。正以最高速度南下南极洲。”在一板一眼的命令腔底下，压着一股强自克制的怒火与决绝。“中队长期望阿科特博士马到成功，把南极洲铲除得同他摧毁北极基地时一样彻底、一样干净。”

机群向南疾驰，速度之快，即便在一百英里高空那稀薄的空气里，也已把它们灼得白热；再往上飞固然可以少受些阻力，却要白耗时间，何况白热也并不难受。十分钟左右，它们便抵达南极洲。塞特人的飞船正从穹顶（Dome）墙面上巨大的闸门里鱼贯而入。一望见地球人的舰船，它们便掉过头来，朝警卫队的舰船疾冲而去。它们的护盾尚未张开——因为披着一身极厚重的雷卢克斯装甲，它们自忖能在自家装甲大损之前，先吃掉地球人那层薄薄的雷卢克斯。

“各舰升起护盾。”阿科特口吻峻急——他心中已生一计。塞特人的分子射线打在灼亮的护盾上，未能奏功。“各舰按编号次序，轮番放下护盾三十秒，把全部分子射线集中轰击同一艘敌舰——领头的那艘。太阳系调查舰（Solar investigator）不得参战。”

===Original===
The flagship of the squadron lowered its screen, and a tremendous bombardment of rays struck the leading ship practically in one point. The relux glowed, and the opalescence shifted with bewildering, confusing colors. Then the terrestrial ship’s screen was up, before the Thessians could concentrate on the one unprotected ship. Immediately another terrestrial ship opened its screen and bombarded the same ship. Two others followed—and then it was forced to use its screen.

But suddenly a terrestrial ship crashed. Its straining screen had been overworked—and it failed.

Arcot’s magnetic beam went into action. The Thessian ray did not go out—it flickered, dimmed, but was apparently as deadly as ever.

“Shielded—write off the magnet, Morey. That is one asset we lose.”

===Chinese===
中队的旗舰放下护盾，密集得惊人的射线弹幕几乎聚于一点，砸在领头那艘敌舰上。雷卢克斯灼灼放光，乳光幻变出令人目眩神迷的纷乱色彩。没等塞特人腾出手来集中围攻那艘不设防的地球飞船，它的护盾已经升起。紧接着，另一艘地球飞船放下护盾，接手轰击同一艘敌舰。又有两艘相继跟上——到头来，那艘敌舰还是被迫撑起了护盾。

可突然间，一艘地球飞船坠毁了。它苦苦硬撑的护盾早已超负荷——终于垮了下来。

阿科特的磁场束随即出击。塞特人的射线却没有熄灭——它闪烁了几下，暗了一暗，但显然还是致命如故。

“有屏蔽——把磁体一笔勾销吧，莫里。这是我们折损的一项本钱。”

===Original===
Arcot, protected in space, was thinking swiftly. Moleculars—useless. They had to keep their own screens up. Artificial matter—bound in by their own molecular screen! And the magnet had failed them against the protected mechanism of the dome. The ships were not as yet protected, but the dome was.

“Guess the only place we’d be safe is under the ground—way under!” commented Wade dryly.

“Under the ground—Wade, you’re a genius!” Arcot gave a shout of joy, and told Wade to take over the ship.

“Take the ship back into normal space, head for the hill over behind the Dome, and drop behind it. It’s solid rock, and even their rays will take a moment or so to move it. As soon as you get there, drop to the ground, and turn off the screen. No—here, I’ll do it. You just take it there, land on the ground, and shut off the screen. I promise the rest!” Arcot dived for the artificial matter room.

===Chinese===
阿科特身在另一重空间之中，安然无恙，思绪飞转。分子射线——没用，他们不得不张着自己的护盾。人工物质（artificial matter）——会被他们自己的分子护盾挡住！而磁体对付穹顶里那些带防护的机械，也已经失了灵。飞船倒还没有防护，可穹顶有。

“我看咱们唯一保险的地方就是地底下——钻得深深的！”韦德干巴巴地插了一句。

“地底下——韦德，你真是个天才！”阿科特欢呼一声，让韦德接手驾驶。

“把船带回正常空间，朝穹顶后侧那座山飞，兜到山背后去。那是实心的岩石，就算他们的射线，也得花上点工夫才能把它化开。一到那儿就落地，关掉护盾。不——等等，这个我来。你只管把船带到那儿，落在地上，把护盾关掉。剩下的包在我身上！”阿科特一头扎向人工物质室。

===Original===
The ship was suddenly in normal space; its screen up. The dogfight had been ended. The terrestrial ships had been completely defeated. The *Ancient Mariner*’s appearance was a signal for all the moleculars in sight. Ten huge ships, half a dozen small forts and now the unshielded Dome, joined in. Their screen tubes heated up violently in the brief moment it took to dive behind the hill, a tube fused, and blew out. Automatic devices shunted it, another tube took the load—and heated. But their screen was full of holes before they were safe for the moment behind the hill.

Instantly Wade dropped the defective screen. Almost as quickly as the screen vanished, a cylinder of artificial matter surrounded the entire ship. The cylinder was tipped by a perfect cone of the same base diameter. The entire system settled into the solid rock. The rock above cracked and filled in behind them. The ship was suddenly pushed by the base of the cylinder behind them, and drove on through the rock, the cone parting the hard granite ahead. They went perhaps half a mile, then stopped. In the light of the ship’s windows, they could see the faint mistiness of the inconceivably hard, artificial matter, and beyond the slick, polished surface of the rock it was pushing aside. The cone shape was still there.

There was a terrific roar behind them, the rock above cracked, shifted and moved about.

===Chinese===
飞船蓦地回到正常空间；护盾高张。那场缠斗已经收场。地球人的舰船彻底败北。*老水手号*一露面，视野之内所有分子射线便齐齐转向。十艘巨型飞船、六座小型堡垒，还有此刻不设护盾的穹顶，一齐开了火。就在俯冲到山背后的短短一瞬，船上的护盾功率管剧烈升温，一根功率管烧熔、爆裂。自动装置随即把它切出线路，另一根功率管接手负载——也跟着热了起来。可还没等他们暂时躲稳在山背后，护盾已被打得千疮百孔。

韦德当即撤掉失灵的护盾。护盾消失得几乎同一瞬间，一根人工物质圆柱已将整艘船罩住。圆柱前端收成一个底径与它完全相同的规整圆锥。整个构体沉入坚实的岩石。上方的岩石裂开，又在他们身后填合起来。飞船骤然被身后圆柱的底面推动，破岩而进，前端的圆锥劈开坚硬的花岗岩。大约前进了半英里，便停了下来。借着船窗透出的光，他们看得见那硬度不可思议的人工物质隐隐的朦胧微芒，以及它正在推开的那面溜滑如镜的岩壁。锥形依旧完好。

身后传来一声骇人的轰响，头顶的岩石崩裂、挪移、翻搅起来。

===Original===
“Raying the spot where we went down,” Arcot grinned happily.

The cone and cylinder merged, shifted together, and became a sphere. The sphere elongated upward and the *Ancient Mariner* turned in it, till it, too, pointed upward. The sphere became an ellipsoid.

Suddenly the ship was moving, accelerating terrifically. It plowed through the solid rock, and up—into a burst of light. They were *inside* the dome. Great ships were berthed about the floor. Huge machines bulked here and there—barracks for men—everything.

The ellipsoid shrank to a sphere, the sphere grew a protuberance which separated and became a single bar-like cylinder. The cylinder turned, and drove through the great dome wall. A little hole but it whirled rapidly around, sliced the top off neatly and quickly. Again, like a gigantic teapot lid, the whole great structure lifted, settled, and stayed there. Men, scrambling wildly toward ships, suddenly stopped, seemed to blur and their features ran together horribly. They fell—and were dead in an instant as the air disappeared. In another instant they were solid blocks of ice, for the temperature was below the freezing point of carbon dioxide.

===Chinese===
“他们在用射线烧我们下潜的那块地方。”阿科特乐呵呵地咧嘴一笑。

圆锥与圆柱并为一体，一同移形换位，化作一个球体。球体向上拉长，*老水手号*也在其中转了向，直到船首同样指向上方。球体又变作一个椭球。

猛然间，飞船动了，加速度大得骇人。它犁开坚实的岩石，一路向上——冲进一片迸发的光亮。他们已置身穹顶*之内*。地面上泊着一艘艘巨型飞船。庞大的机器四处耸立——士兵的营房——应有尽有。

椭球缩回成球体，球体上鼓出一个凸起，凸起分离出去，化作一根棒一样的圆柱。圆柱转了向，一头钻透巨大的穹顶墙壁。只穿出一个小孔——但那圆柱随即绕着穹顶高速飞旋，利利落落地把整个穹顶削了下来。随后，整座巨大的建筑便像一具硕大无朋的茶壶盖，向上升起、落定，稳稳搁在了那里。发疯般扑向飞船的人们猛地僵住，身影似乎模糊了一瞬，五官可怖地融作一团。他们倒了下去——空气消失的一瞬，他们便已死去。再过一瞬，他们又冻成了硬邦邦的冰块，因为那里的温度已低于二氧化碳的冰点。

===Original===
The giant tamper set to work. The Thessian ships went first. They were all crumpled, battered wrecks in a few seconds of work of the terrible disc.

The dome was destroyed. Arcot tried something else. He put on his control machine the equation of a hyperboloid of two branches, and changed the constants gradually till the two branches came close. Then he forced them against each other. Instantly they fought, fought terribly for existence. A tremendous blast of light and heat exploded into being. The energy of two tons of lead attempted to maintain those two branches. It was not, fortunately, explosive, and it took place over a relux floor. Most of the energy escaped into space. The vast flood of light was visible on Venus, despite the clouds.

But it fused most of Antarctica. It destroyed the last traces of the camp in Antarctica.

“Well—the Squadron was wiped out, I see.” Arcot’s voice was flat as he spoke. The Squadron: twenty ships—four hundred men.

===Chinese===
巨型夯具开工了。塞特人的飞船头一个遭殃。在那可怕圆盘区区几秒的碾压之下，全都成了一堆堆皱瘪破碎的残骸。

穹顶被摧毁了。阿科特又试了点别的东西。他把一个双叶双曲面的方程输入自己的控制机，逐渐改变常数，直到两叶彼此逼近。随即，他强令两叶迎面挤压。刹那间，两叶拼死相抗，为自身的存在而苦苦挣扎。一大股光与热轰然爆现。两吨铅所含的能量竭力想撑住那两叶曲面。所幸这并非爆炸，而且发生在一层雷卢克斯地板上方。绝大部分能量逸散进了太空。那道浩瀚的光洪，尽管隔着云层，在金星上也望得见。

但它把南极洲的大部分都熔成了一片。南极营地的最后一点痕迹，就此荡然无存。

“唉——那支中队算是全灭了，我看见了。”阿科特说话时声音平平。那支中队：二十艘飞船——四百个人。

===Original===
“Yes—but so is the Arctic camp, and the Antarctic camp, as well,” replied Wade.

“What next, Arcot. Shall we go out to intergalactic space at once?” asked Morey, coming up from the power room.

“No, we’ll go back to Vermont, and have the time-field stuff I ordered installed, then go to Sirius, and see what they have. They moved their planets from the gravitation field of Negra, their dead, black star, to the field of Sirius—and I’d like to know how they did it.2 Then—Intergalactia.” He started the ship toward Vermont, while Morey got into communication with the field, and gave them a brief report.

===Chinese===
“是啊——可北极营地，连同南极营地，也一样全灭了。”韦德答道。

“下一步呢，阿科特？咱们这就动身去星系际空间吗？”莫里从动力室上来，问道。

“不，先回佛蒙特，把我订购的时间场装置装上，然后去天狼星，看看他们那边有什么。他们把自己的行星从涅格拉（Negra）——那颗死去的黑星——的引力场，挪进了天狼星的引力场。我很想知道他们是怎么办到的。2 然后——星系际空间。”他驾船朝佛蒙特飞去，莫里则与前线部队取得了联系，向他们简要通报了战况。
